Abstract: Building effective knowledge graphs (KGs) for Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is pivotal for advancing question answering (QA) systems. However, its effectiveness is hindered by a fundamental disconnect: the knowledge graph (KG) construction process is decoupled from its downstream application, yielding suboptimal graph structures. To bridge this gap, we introduce AutoGraph-R1, the first framework to directly optimize KG construction for task performance using Reinforcement Learning (RL). AutoGraph-R1 trains an LLM constructor by framing graph generation as a policy learning problem, where the reward is derived from the graph's functional utility in a RAG pipeline. We design two novel, task-aware reward functions, one for graphs as knowledge carriers and another as knowledge indices. Across multiple QA benchmarks, AutoGraph-R1 consistently enables graph RAG methods to achieve significant performance gains over using task-agnostic baseline graphs. Our work shows it is possible to close the loop between construction and application, shifting the paradigm from building intrinsically ``good'' graphs to building demonstrably ``useful'' ones.

</details>


### [60] [Aligning Stuttered-Speech Research with End-User Needs: Scoping Review, Survey, and Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20535)
*Hawau Olamide Toyin,Mutiah Apampa,Toluwani Aremu,Humaid Alblooshi,Ana Rita Valente,Gonçalo Leal,Zhengjun Yue,Zeerak Talat,Hanan Aldarmaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过文献综述和利益相关者调研（70人），分析口吃语音识别领域的研究现状与用户需求之间的差距，旨在为口吃社区提供更贴合实际需求的技术解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语音识别系统在实际应用中对手口吃语音识别效果不佳，现有的评估方法和研究优先级缺乏系统性地以终端用户需求为导向

Method: 结合文献综述与利益相关者调研：1）对话音识别中口吃语音的论文进行范围审查；2）对70名利益相关者（包括成年口吃者和言语语言病理学家）进行问卷调查

Result: 提出口吃语音研究分类法；识别当前研究方向与利益相关者需求之间的差距

Conclusion: 提出具体指南和方向，以满足口吃社区的真实需求

Abstract: Atypical speech is receiving greater attention in speech technology research, but much of this work unfolds with limited interdisciplinary dialogue. For stuttered speech in particular, it is widely recognised that current speech recognition systems fall short in practice, and current evaluation methods and research priorities are not systematically grounded in end-user experiences and needs. In this work, we analyse these gaps through 1) a scoping review of papers that deal with stuttered speech and 2) a survey of 70 stakeholders, including adults who stutter and speech-language pathologists. By analysing these two perspectives, we propose a taxonomy of stuttered-speech research, identify where current research directions diverge from the needs articulated by stakeholders, and conclude by outlining concrete guidelines and directions towards addressing the real needs of the stuttering community.

</details>


### [61] [Toward Cross-Lingual Quality Classifiers for Multilingual Pretraining Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20549)
*Yassine Turki,Vinko Sabolčec,Bettina Messmer,Martin Jaggi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现嵌入空间中的质量标记具有跨语言一致性，允许高资源语言补贴低资源语言的过滤任务。多语言混合池化策略（特别是结合Q3采样或保留率调优）可显著提升质量过滤效果，尤其对高资源语言效果明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM规模化，数据策展从最大化规模转向优化信噪比进行质量过滤；然而许多语言缺乏足够的高质量原生数据来训练鲁棒的质量分类器。

Method: 评估多种过滤策略，包括跨语言迁移、三分位数采样（Q3）和保留率调优；使用103B tokens训练1B模型进行实验。

Result: 大规模多语言混合池化在排序稳定性和聚合准确率上均优于单语基线，为高资源语言（法语）带来1.2%的聚合归一化准确率提升，低资源语言持平或超越单语基线；但规模本身不能保证稳定性。

Conclusion: 高质量语言的标记可以跨语言迁移以支持低资源语言的质量过滤；大规模多语言混合优于单语基线；但规模本身不能保证稳定性；对于高资源语言，需要通过Q3采样或保留率调优来优化决策边界，才能充分利用多语言信号的潜力。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) scale, data curation has shifted from maximizing volume to optimizing the signal-to-noise ratio by performing quality filtering. However, for many languages, native high quality data is insufficient to train robust quality classifiers. This work investigates the idea that quality markers in embedding space may show cross-lingual consistency, which would allow high-resource languages to subsidize the filtering of low-resource ones. We evaluate various filtering strategies, including cross-lingual transfer, third quartile sampling (Q3), and retention rate tuning. Our results demonstrate that massive multilingual pooling frequently outperforms monolingual baselines in both rank stability and aggregate accuracy for a 1B model trained on 103B tokens, delivering gains for high resource languages (1.2% increase in aggregate normalized accuracy for French) and matching or exceeding monolingual baselines for low-resource languages. However, we find that scale alone does not guarantee stability. Furthermore, for high-resource languages like French, we show that refining the decision boundary through third quartile sampling (Q3) or tuning the retention rate is necessary to fully leverage the multilingual signal.

</details>


### [62] [LayerTracer: A Joint Task-Particle and Vulnerable-Layer Analysis framework for Arbitrary Large Language Model Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20556)
*Yuhang Wu,Qinyuan Liu,Qiuyang Zhao,Qingwei Chong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LayerTracer 是一个架构无关（architecture-agnostic）的端到端分析框架，通过逐层抽取隐状态并经 LM head 映射为词表概率分布，联合定位两类可量化指标：①「任务粒子（task particle）」——目标 token 概率相对增幅 Ratio(l) 最大的层，代表任务推理启动点；②「脆弱层（vulnerable layer）」——对某层核心模块输出施加掩码/噪声扰动后，与原始最终输出分布之间 JS 散度最大的层，代表鲁棒性瓶颈。基于对 Qwen3 系列（0.6B/4B/8B/14B）在 AntSynNET 数据集上的实验，作者发现任务粒子稳定出现在网络后半段（约 > N/2 的深层），且随网络深度呈相对位置依赖；小模型呈明显 U 形脆弱性分布与高 LRS 值，大模型脆弱性曲线趋于平坦、LRS 更低，即层级鲁棒性更强。结论为 Qwen3.5 的 3:1（Full Attention : Linear Attention）混合比例提供了功能性解释，并提出「深度感知的功能对齐」设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 架构生态已从单一 Transformer 走向 Transformer、GatedDeltaNet、Mamba、Linear Attention 等多元混合结构（如 Qwen3.5 采用 3:1 的 Full Attention 与 Linear Attention 循环）。但混合架构中层数划分、模块比例与切换节点的选择主要依赖经验试错而非科学依据，属"黑箱"。已有层级可解释性研究存在四点不足：(1) 多聚焦传统 Transformer 的注意力头与隐状态，难以适配 GateDeltaNet/Mamba 等新结构；(2) 只做单维度的层级表示分析，未将任务起效位置与网络脆弱性联合建模；(3) 缺乏可用于工程实现的通用量化指标，无法直接指导层划分与模块配额；(4) 未阐明参数规模、层级知识利用与鲁棒性之间的内在关联。因此需要一个架构无关、可量化、且能同时回答"任务从哪一层开始执行"和"哪一层最脆弱"的统一分析框架。

Method: 框架分两阶段。阶段一（任务粒子定位）：通过统一加载接口加载任意架构模型，对每层截断前向传播（阻断后续层传播）提取层末 token 隐状态，做归一化后经 lm_head + softmax 映射为词表概率分布，取 Top-10 候选 token；目标 token t* 取最终层输出分布中概率最大的词项；计算相邻层相对概率增幅 Ratio(l) = (P_t(l) − P_t(l−1)) / P_t(l)，取 Ratio 最大的层为任务粒子（任务奇异性）。阶段二（脆弱层识别）：以未扰动的最终层分布 P 为基准，对每层核心处理模块（如 attention 或 state-space block）输出施加统一强度的掩码扰动与加噪，模拟预训练中部分层被训练/参数更新的情形，前向得到扰动分布 Q(l)，计算 JS(P‖Q) = ½KL(P‖M) + ½KL(Q‖M)，M = ½(P+Q)，取 JS 最大者为脆弱层。另提出层级相对稳定性指标 LRS = √[1/(N−1)·Σ(JS(l) − mean JS)²]，衡量各层脆弱性分布的均匀程度。算法 1 给出完整流程：单次参考前向 → 逐层隐状态提取与概率映射 → Ratio 序列计算 → 掩码扰动循环 → 散度排序 → 输出 l_particle 与 l_vuln。

Result: 在 8×A800 服务器上对 Qwen3 的 0.6B、4B、8B、14B 四个规模进行层级分析，实验数据取 AntSynNET 前 500 条样本并划分为十类。结果显示：(1) Ratio 曲线在各规模下均呈现一致的"深层涌现"现象——由可忽略或负值转为显著正突增的拐点一致出现在网络后半段（约 l > N/2），标志模型从低级特征抽象转向主动的任务语义构建；(2) 任务粒子的位置具有相对深度依赖性，而非固定绝对层号，语义整合所需深度随网络总深度成比例变化；(3) Qwen3-0.6B 呈显著 U 形脆弱性分布，浅层与深层对扰动敏感度均明显高于中层，LRS 值偏高，说明结构拓扑脆弱、边界层构成瓶颈；(4) 随参数规模增至 8B 与 14B，脆弱性曲线逐步平滑，深层因临近输出头仍固有敏感，但边界层与中层的极端差异显著减小，LRS 值大幅下降，表明大模型鲁棒性分布更均衡、全局拓扑更稳定，能有效缓冲局部扰动并维持语义流连贯。

Conclusion: 该工作为混合架构设计提供了机制性依据。其一，Qwen3.5 采用 3:1 混合比例的原因在于任务关键推理在深层整合、而边界层在结构上高度脆弱，因此必须在深层锚定高容量 Full Attention 模块以保证决策保真度，这属于功能必要性而非单纯算力权衡。其二，提出面向未来的"深度感知功能对齐"原则：受参数约束的小模型应采用边界保护策略，将可训练的轻量模块限制在鲁棒的中层区域，冻结浅层与深层等敏感层；而脆弱性曲线平坦的大模型则可采用更灵活的交错式混合设计。整体而言，该研究把混合架构设计从启发式试错推进为可量化、指标驱动的优化过程，并为 LLM 结构设计与可解释性研究提供通用支持。

Abstract: Currently, Large Language Models (LLMs) feature a diversified architectural landscape, including traditional Transformer, GateDeltaNet, and Mamba. However, the evolutionary laws of hierarchical representations, task knowledge formation positions, and network robustness bottleneck mechanisms in various LLM architectures remain unclear, posing core challenges for hybrid architecture design and model optimization. This paper proposes LayerTracer, an architecture-agnostic end-to-end analysis framework compatible with any LLM architecture. By extracting hidden states layer-by-layer and mapping them to vocabulary probability distributions, it achieves joint analysis of task particle localization and layer vulnerability quantification. We define the task particle as the key layer where the target token probability first rises significantly, representing the model's task execution starting point, and the vulnerable layer is defined as the layer with the maximum Jensen-Shannon (JS) divergence between output distributions before and after mask perturbation, reflecting its sensitivity to disturbances. Experiments on models of different parameter scales show that task particles mainly appear in the deep layers of the model regardless of parameter size, while larger-parameter models exhibit stronger hierarchical robustness. LayerTracer provides a scientific basis for layer division, module ratio, and gating switching of hybrid architectures, effectively optimizing model performance. It accurately locates task-effective layers and stability bottlenecks, offering universal support for LLM structure design and interpretability research.

</details>


### [63] [LLM StructCore: Schema-Guided Reasoning Condensation and Deterministic Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20560)
*Serhii Zabolotnii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出「LLM StructCore」：面向CL4Health 2026呼吸困难（dyspnea）CRF自动填充任务的「LLM（Stage 1）+ 0-LLM确定性编译器（Stage 2）」两阶段方案。Stage 1用Schema-Guided Reasoning（SGR）提示把临床病历压缩为固定9个领域键的JSON（约400 token），Stage 2用纯Python规则完成键值抽取、条目名规范化（含UMLS别名映射134/134覆盖）、假阳性证据门控、13类受控词表归一化，最终展开为134个条目的提交文件。dev80最佳teacher（Mistral Large 3）macro-F1：EN 0.6543、IT 0.6905；隐藏test200最佳提交（EN）Codabench F1=0.63，落后榜首Aurum 0.68约0.05。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务难点有三：(1) 语言噪声大、输出合同严格（必须严格134条目格式与受控词表），端到端LLM直接抽取134字段常出现JSON格式漂移或幻觉填充导致假阳性(FP)；(2) 官方评分用item级macro-F1，对FP和FN（以及空缺）同样重罚；(3) 关键的任务性质是极端稀疏性——dev集中每文档非unknown条目中位数仅约12/134，unknown是最常见类别，正确预测unknown与正确抽取同等重要。因此作者希望把「临床信息压缩」与「合同执行/词表归一化」彻底解耦：Stage 1只负责稀疏地抽取事实，Stage 2负责所有结构与词表约束。

Method: 数据集：官方NLP-FBK/dyspnea-crf-*（train10/dev80/test200，test200标注隐藏），教师数据来自未标注意大利SGB医院临床记录2,667条。Stage 1（SGR）：提示中固定9个领域顺序Cascade（DEMOGRAPHICS→VITALS→LABS→PROBLEMS→SYMPTOMS→MEDICATIONS→PROCEDURES→UTILIZATION→DISPOSITION）、每域隐含Checklist（Cycle）以提升稀疏条目召回、只输出有明确证据的事实（证据门控/稀疏指令）、域内使用「Key: Value」格式；每键为自由文本，词表路由留给Stage 2。采用multi-slice策略（middle+head_tail，w=4）切分长病历，各窗口独立摘要后合并：PROBLEMS/MEDICATIONS/LABS键做字符串拼接，其余键last-writer-wins。无需语法约束解码即达到100% JSON解析率（对比同一4B模型直接134字段抽取JSON合法率<70%）。教师模型：Mistral Large 3(675B)、Qwen 3.5(397B, 需enable_thinking=false)、Claude Sonnet 4.6（召回优化但因参赛次数限制未提交）。Stage 2（0-LLM，4步确定性算法）：(1) KV抽取，解析各域「Key: Value」行，跳过not stated/unknown；(2) 条目名规范化——精确匹配134列表、大小写/标点归一、70+缩写规则（hr→heart rate、cxr→chest rx）、可选UMLS别名映射（134/134覆盖，最终提交均开启）；(3) 假阳性门控与派生——≥8种药物推poly-pharmacological therapy、35种降压药匹配抗高血压治疗、治疗标志/PMH关键词派生cardiovascular diseases；对高FP代价条目施加强证据正则门控（active neoplasia需癌症词+活动线索、arrhythmia遇NSR阻断、acute coronary syndrome需ACS词汇+支持证据、presence of dyspnea遇显式否定阻断）；(4) 词表归一化到13类受控词表（二值y/n 61项、慢性4类7项、labs 25项、移动度/AVPU/新发3类/持续时间等），最终展开为134项，缺失填unknown。模型对比实验还验证LLM-based Stage 2（Claude直接抽134项）因词表漂移、不可复现、额外成本而劣于确定性编译器。

Result: dev80（开放GT）：EN Mistral Large 3 F1=0.6543（TP236/FP170/FN128）；IT Mistral Large 3 rules_v3e F1=0.6905（TP266/FP198/FN106）；IT v3a F1=0.6900；EN Qwen 3.5 (think=off) F1=0.6411（TP同为236但FP达211）。两个EN teacher的FN均为128，说明Stage 2派生规则存在召回天花板、系统受Stage 1召回限制。train10消融：Gemini Flash teacher F1=0.6763（FN9/FP35）、MedGemma 4B student F1=0.6521（FN22/FP13）、SGR-only baseline F1=0.4384。Multi-slice相对单窗口多召回12–18个TP、代价8–15个FP，收益集中在LABS与MEDICATIONS域。FP门控在dev80 EN上平均每文档丢弃3.2项，四项高风险条目FP从46降到14（约降70%）且保留TP。test200（Codabench）：提交英文Mistral L3→Stage2(det) F1=0.63（距榜首Aurum 0.68差0.05）；无FP门控变体0.61；Claude Sonnet变体0.62（已评测未提交）；意大利v3e变体已备好但因提交次数用尽未提交。dev10跨语言验证（Anthropic召回优化Stage 1）：IT F1=0.9000（TP59/FP9/FN4）vs EN F1=0.8115。误差分析：>85%错误为Stage 1的FN（如foreign body in the airways 11 FN、agitation 7 FN），IT主要FP来源为chest pain 13、cardiovascular diseases 13（治疗标志派生）、level of consciousness 9（AVPU映射错误）、heart failure 8。边缘部署：MedGemma 1.5-4B-it Q5_K_M经llama.cpp在Apple M3 Pro上Stage 1约8秒/文档、Stage 2<10ms，整个dev80评测12分钟内完成。

Conclusion: SGR式9键中间表示 + 确定性编译器的两阶段分解，在极端稀疏的CRF填充任务上兼顾了结构稳定性、词表合规与逐行可解释性：把结构正确性从提示工程转移到代码，使任意后端（含量化4B边缘模型）无需语法约束解码即可100%解析成功，并天然提供「错误在Stage 1还是Stage 2」的调试边界；同时比LLM-based Stage 2更便宜、可复现、零词表漂移。系统与语言无关（IT表现优于EN，因意大利原始病历更结构化且术语与本体更直接对应），最佳提交成绩F1=0.63接近榜首，具备隐私保护型本地部署潜力。局限：总体召回受Stage 1限制，扁平Key:Value难表达「既往/当前」等上下文依赖项；70+缩写映射与英文中心的FP正则门控需人工维护且覆盖有限；仅在CL4Health 2026单一CRF与英/意两种语言上验证。后续方向包括提升Stage 1对高FN条目的召回（域内清单与few-shot）、混合式Stage 2，以及扩展到其他CRF schema与临床域。

Abstract: Automatically filling Case Report Forms (CRFs) from clinical notes is challenging due to noisy language, strict output contracts, and the high cost of false positives. We describe our CL4Health 2026 submission for Dyspnea CRF filling (134 items) using a contract-driven two-stage design grounded in Schema-Guided Reasoning (SGR). The key task property is extreme sparsity: the majority of fields are unknown, and official scoring penalizes both empty values and unsupported predictions. We shift from a single-step "LLM predicts 134 fields" approach to a decomposition where (i) Stage 1 produces a stable SGR-style JSON summary with exactly 9 domain keys, and (ii) Stage 2 is a fully deterministic, 0-LLM compiler that parses the Stage 1 summary, canonicalizes item names, normalizes predictions to the official controlled vocabulary, applies evidence-gated false-positive filters, and expands the output into the required 134-item format. On the dev80 split, the best teacher configuration achieves macro-F1 0.6543 (EN) and 0.6905 (IT); on the hidden test200, the submitted English variant scores 0.63 on Codabench. The pipeline is language-agnostic: Italian results match or exceed English with no language-specific engineering.

</details>


### [64] [Where Reasoning Breaks: Logic-Aware Path Selection by Controlling Logical Connectives in LLMs Reasoning Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20564)
*Seunghyun Park,Yuanyuan Lei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在多步逻辑推理中的脆弱性问题，提出逻辑连接词是高熵分叉点的核心假设，并设计了针对逻辑关键转折点的多层干预框架，在准确率与效率权衡上优于全局推理扩展方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多步逻辑推理中表现脆弱，单个推理步骤的错误会沿推理链传播导致整体失败。研究者发现逻辑连接词是这种结构性脆弱性的主要来源——模型在这些高熵分叉点频繁难以确定正确的逻辑方向。

Method: 提出三层干预框架：(1) 基于梯度的逻辑导向(Gradient-based Logical Steering)，将LLM内部表征引导至有效推理子空间；(2) 局部分支搜索(Localized Branching)，通过定向前瞻搜索消解歧义；(3) 目标迁移偏好优化(Targeted Transition Preference Optimization)，一种外科手术式强化学习目标，在逻辑支点处选择性优化单token偏好。

Result: 通过仅聚焦逻辑关键转换的干预，框架在准确率-效率权衡上优于全局推理时间扩展方法（如束搜索和自洽性）。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了"干预逻辑连接词选择可引导LLM走向更正确的逻辑方向"的假设，为提升LLM结构化推理能力提供了新的针对性优化范式。

Abstract: While LLMs demonstrate impressive reasoning capabilities, they remain fragile in multi-step logical deduction, where a single transition error can propagate through the entire reasoning chain, leading to unstable performance. In this work, we identify logical connectives as primary points of this structural fragility. Through empirical analysis, we show that connective tokens function as high entropy forking points, at which models frequently struggle to determine the correct logical direction. Motivated by this observation, we hypothesize that intervening in logical connective selection can guide LLMs toward more correct logical direction, thereby improving the overall reasoning chain. To validate this hypothesis, we propose a multi-layered framework that intervenes specifically at these logic-critical junctions in the reasoning process. Our framework includes (1) Gradient-based Logical Steering to guide LLMs internal representations towards valid reasoning subspaces, (2) Localized Branching to resolve ambiguity via targeted look-ahead search, and (3) Targeted Transition Preference Optimization, a surgical reinforcement learning objective that selectively optimizes single-token preferences at logical pivots. Crucially, by concentrating intervention solely on logic-critical transitions, our framework achieves a favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-off compared to global inference time scaling methods like beam search and self-consistency.

</details>


### [65] [Ask Only When Needed: Proactive Retrieval from Memory and Skills for Experience-Driven Lifelong Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20572)
*Yuxuan Cai,Jie Zhou,Qin Chen,Liang He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ProactAgent是一种主动检索式的终身学习框架，通过ExpOnEvo实现策略更新与记忆 refinement，并通过ProactRL建模检索为显式策略动作，在SciWorld、AlfWorld和StuLife上分别达到73.50%、71.28%成功率和竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身学习方法将经验检索视为被动操作，仅在任务初始化或完成步骤后触发检索，导致智能体无法在交互过程中识别知识差距并主动检索最相关经验。

Method: 提出ProactAgent框架，包含两个核心组件：1) ExpOnEvo通过策略更新和记忆 refinement实现持续改进；2) ProactRL将检索建模为显式策略动作，通过配对分支过程奖励学习何时检索以及检索什么内容。

Result: 在SciWorld上成功率达73.50%，AlfWorld达71.28%，StuLife达到与闭源模型竞争的性能水平，同时显著降低检索开销。

Conclusion: 主动检索范式可有效解决终身学习中的经验利用问题，结构化经验库结合RL驱动的主动检索策略能够实现更高效的终身学习智能体。

Abstract: Online lifelong learning enables agents to accumulate experience across interactions and continually improve on long-horizon tasks. However, existing methods typically treat retrieval from past experience as a passive operation, triggering it only at task initialization or after completing a step. Consequently, agents often fail to identify knowledge gaps during interaction and proactively retrieve the most useful experience for the current decision. To address this limitation, we present ProactAgent, an experience-driven lifelong learning framework for proactive retrieval over a structured experience base. We first introduce Experience-Enhanced Online Evolution (ExpOnEvo), which enables continual improvement through both policy updates and memory refinement. The experience base organizes historical interactions into typed repositories, including factual memory, episodic memory, and behavioral skills, so that retrieval can provide both relevant evidence and actionable guidance. On top of this, we propose Proactive Reinforcement Learning-based Retrieval (ProactRL), which models retrieval as an explicit policy action and learns when and what to retrieve via paired-branch process rewards. By comparing continuations from identical interaction prefixes with and without retrieval, ProactRL provides step-level supervision for retrieval decisions, encouraging retrieval only when it leads to better task outcomes or higher efficiency. Experiments on SciWorld, AlfWorld, and StuLife show that ProactAgent consistently improves lifelong agent performance, achieving success rates of 73.50\% on SciWorld and 71.28\% on AlfWorld while substantially reducing retrieval overhead, and attains performance competitive with proprietary models on StuLife.

</details>


### [66] [Cooperative Profiles Predict Multi-Agent LLM Team Performance in AI for Science Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20658)
*Shivani Kumar,Adarsh Bharathwaj,David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过对35个开源LLM在六个行为经济学游戏中的表现进行基准测试，发现游戏中的合作行为能够预测多智能体AI系统在科学任务中的表现，合作倾向是LLM的独立可测量属性，可作为多智能体部署前的快速筛查工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统需要协调共享资源（GPU、信用额度等），但尚不清楚这些系统在标准化合作场景中的行为是否能预测其在实际协作任务中的表现。

Method: 对35个开源LLM在六个行为经济学游戏中进行基准测试，然后将游戏中的合作特征与AI-for-Science任务中的表现进行关联分析，控制了多个混淆变量。

Result: 能够在游戏中有效协调、投资于乘法团队生产（而非贪婪策略）的模型，在科学报告的准确性、质量和完成度三个维度上表现更好。控制多个因素后，这些关联依然显著。

Conclusion: 合作倾向是LLM的独立可测量属性，不能简单归因于通用能力。行为经济学游戏框架可作为多智能体系统部署前快速、低成本的"合作适应性"筛查工具。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems built from teams of large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for collaborative scientific reasoning and problem-solving. These systems require agents to coordinate under shared constraints, such as GPUs or credit balances, where cooperative behavior matters. Behavioral economics provides a rich toolkit of games that isolate distinct cooperation mechanisms, yet it remains unknown whether a model's behavior in these stylized settings predicts its performance in realistic collaborative tasks. Here, we benchmark 35 open-weight LLMs across six behavioral economics games and show that game-derived cooperative profiles robustly predict downstream performance in AI-for-Science tasks, where teams of LLM agents collaboratively analyze data, build models, and produce scientific reports under shared budget constraints. Models that effectively coordinate games and invest in multiplicative team production (rather than greedy strategies) produce better scientific reports across three outcomes, accuracy, quality, and completion. These associations hold after controlling for multiple factors, indicating that cooperative disposition is a distinct, measurable property of LLMs not reducible to general ability. Our behavioral games framework thus offers a fast and inexpensive diagnostic for screening cooperative fitness before costly multi-agent deployment.

</details>


### [67] [ORPHEAS: A Cross-Lingual Greek-English Embedding Model for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20666)
*Ioannis E. Livieris,Athanasios Koursaris,Alexandra Apostolopoulou,Konstantinos Kanaris Dimitris Tsakalidis,George Domalis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ORPHEAS is a specialized Greek-English embedding model for bilingual retrieval-augmented generation, trained using knowledge graph-based fine-tuning on multi-domain corpus, achieving superior performance over state-of-the-art multilingual models while maintaining cross-lingual capability.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing multilingual embedding models distribute representational capacity across many languages, limiting optimization for Greek and failing to encode morphological complexity and domain-specific terminology.

Method: Propose ORPHEAS, a specialized Greek-English embedding model trained with a high-quality dataset generated by a knowledge graph-based fine-tuning methodology on a diverse multi-domain corpus.

Result: Outperforms state-of-the-art multilingual embedding models on both monolingual and cross-lingual retrieval benchmarks.

Conclusion: Domain-specialized fine-tuning on morphologically complex languages does not compromise cross-lingual retrieval capability.

Abstract: Effective retrieval-augmented generation across bilingual Greek--English applications requires embedding models capable of capturing both domain-specific semantic relationships and cross-lingual semantic alignment. Existing multilingual embedding models distribute their representational capacity across numerous languages, limiting their optimization for Greek and failing to encode the morphological complexity and domain-specific terminological structures inherent in Greek text. In this work, we propose ORPHEAS, a specialized Greek--English embedding model for bilingual retrieval-augmented generation. ORPHEAS is trained with a high quality dataset generated by a knowledge graph-based fine-tuning methodology which is applied to a diverse multi-domain corpus, which enables language-agnostic semantic representations. The numerical experiments across monolingual and cross-lingual retrieval benchmarks reveal that ORPHEAS outperforms state-of-the-art multilingual embedding models, demonstrating that domain-specialized fine-tuning on morphologically complex languages does not compromise cross-lingual retrieval capability.

</details>


### [68] [Intersectional Fairness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20677)
*Chaima Boufaied,Ronnie De Souza Santos,Ann Barcomb*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了六种大语言模型在交叉人口属性上的公平性问题。研究发现：现代LLM在歧义语境中表现良好但导致公平指标信息量不足；在消歧语境中，准确率受刻板印象影响，种族-性别交叉维度偏见最显著；各组结果分布仍不均匀，重复运行中响应一致性也存在变异。最终表明，没有LLM能在交叉场景中实现持续可靠的公平行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益部署于社会敏感场景，但关于其在交叉人口属性上的公平性和偏见问题缺乏系统评估。

Method: 使用六个LLM、两个基准数据集的歧义和消歧语境，通过偏差评分、子群公平指标、准确率和多轮一致性分析进行系统评估。

Result: ①歧义语境下指标信息量有限；②消歧语境中准确率受刻板印象对齐影响；③种族-性别交叉处方向性偏见最强；④各交叉组结果分布仍不均匀；⑤重复运行中响应一致性存在变异。

Conclusion: 当前LLM表现出的能力部分源于刻板印象一致的线索，没有模型能在交叉场景中实现持续可靠的公平行为。评估需超越准确率，结合偏差、子群公平和一致性指标。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in socially sensitive settings, raising concerns about fairness and biases, particularly across intersectional demographic attributes. In this paper, we systematically evaluate intersectional fairness in six LLMs using ambiguous and disambiguated contexts from two benchmark datasets. We assess LLM behavior using bias scores, subgroup fairness metrics, accuracy, and consistency through multi-run analysis across contexts and negative and non-negative question polarities. Our results show that while modern LLMs generally perform well in ambiguous contexts, this limits the informativeness of fairness metrics due to sparse non-unknown predictions. In disambiguated contexts, LLM accuracy is influenced by stereotype alignment, with models being more accurate when the correct answer reinforces a stereotype than when it contradicts it. This pattern is especially pronounced in race-gender intersections, where directional bias toward stereotypes is stronger. Subgroup fairness metrics further indicate that, despite low observed disparity in some cases, outcome distributions remain uneven across intersectional groups. Across repeated runs, responses also vary in consistency, including stereotype-aligned responses. Overall, our findings show that apparent model competence is partly associated with stereotype-consistent cues, and no evaluated LLM achieves consistently reliable or fair behavior across intersectional settings. These findings highlight the need for evaluation beyond accuracy, emphasizing the importance of combining bias, subgroup fairness, and consistency metrics across intersectional groups, contexts, and repeated runs.

</details>


### [69] [Exploiting LLM-as-a-Judge Disposition on Free Text Legal QA via Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20726)
*Mohamed Hesham Elganayni,Runsheng Chen,Sebastian Nagl,Matthias Grabmair*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探索了LLM-as-a-Judge评估中提示词优化和评判者选择的系统性影响，发现自动优化方法（ProTeGi）优于人工设计，且宽松评判者产生的提示词泛化能力更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge范式在法律问答评估中日益重要，但提示词设计（人工设计vs自动优化）和评判者选择对评估效果的影响尚不明确。

Method: 在LEXam基准上，使用ProTeGi方法对两个评判模型（Qwen3-32B、DeepSeek-V3）进行提示词优化，跨四个任务模型测试，并验证跨评判者的迁移效果。

Result: 自动优化持续优于基线；宽松评判者带来更高且更稳定的提升；宽松反馈优化的提示词向严格评判者的迁移效果优于反向迁移。

Conclusion: 算法优化提示词可超越人工设计；评判者在优化过程中的倾向性（宽松/严格）会显著影响提示词的泛化能力，宽松评判者产生更通用的提示词，而严格评判者易产生过拟合于特定评判者的提示词。

Abstract: This work explores the role of prompt design and judge selection in LLM-as-a-Judge evaluations of free text legal question answering. We examine whether automatic task prompt optimization improves over human-centered design, whether optimization effectiveness varies by judge feedback style, and whether optimized prompts transfer across judges. We systematically address these questions on the LEXam benchmark by optimizing task prompts using the ProTeGi method with feedback from two judges (Qwen3-32B, DeepSeek-V3) across four task models, and then testing cross-judge transfer. Automatic optimization consistently outperforms the baseline, with lenient judge feedback yielding higher and more consistent gains than strict judge feedback. Prompts optimized with lenient feedback transfer better to strict judges than the reverse direction. Analysis reveals that lenient judges provide permissive feedback, yielding prompts with broader applicability, whereas strict judges produce restrictive feedback, leading to judge-specific overfitting. Our findings demonstrate algorithmically optimizing prompts on training data can outperform human-centered prompt design and that judges' dispositions during optimization shape prompt generalizability. Code and optimized prompts are available at https://github.com/TUMLegalTech/icail2026-llm-judge-gaming.

</details>


### [70] [RespondeoQA: a Benchmark for Bilingual Latin-English Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20738)
*Marisa Hudspeth,Patrick J. Burns,Brendan O'Connor*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个以拉丁语为中心的问答基准数据集RespondeoQA，包含约7,800个问答对，涵盖多种问题类型，并对LLaMa 3、Qwen QwQ和o3-mini三种大语言模型进行了评估，发现技能导向问题对所有模型都较具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏针对拉丁语这一专门语言文化领域的问答基准数据集，现有大语言模型在该领域的能力尚待系统评估。

Method: 从拉丁语教学资源（包括考试、Quizbowl风格问答和教科书，跨度从1800年代至今）中自动提取、清洗问题，并进行人工审核，最终构建多样化的问答对数据集。

Result: 三个大模型在技能导向问题上表现均较差；推理模型（QwQ、o3-mini）在韵律划分和文学手法任务上表现更好，但整体提升有限；QwQ在拉丁语提问时表现稍好，而LLaMa 3和o3-mini的表现更依赖于任务类型。

Conclusion: RespondeoQA为评估大语言模型在专门语言文化领域的能力提供了新资源，且其创建流程可轻松适配于其他语言。

Abstract: We introduce a benchmark dataset for question answering and translation in bilingual Latin and English settings, containing about 7,800 question-answer pairs. The questions are drawn from Latin pedagogical sources, including exams, quizbowl-style trivia, and textbooks ranging from the 1800s to the present. After automated extraction, cleaning, and manual review, the dataset covers a diverse range of question types: knowledge- and skill-based, multihop reasoning, constrained translation, and mixed language pairs. To our knowledge, this is the first QA benchmark centered on Latin. As a case study, we evaluate three large language models -- LLaMa 3, Qwen QwQ, and OpenAI's o3-mini -- finding that all perform worse on skill-oriented questions. Although the reasoning models perform better on scansion and literary-device tasks, they offer limited improvement overall. QwQ performs slightly better on questions asked in Latin, but LLaMa3 and o3-mini are more task dependent. This dataset provides a new resource for assessing model capabilities in a specialized linguistic and cultural domain, and the creation process can be easily adapted for other languages. The dataset is available at: https://github.com/slanglab/RespondeoQA

</details>


### [71] [Working Memory Constraints Scaffold Learning in Transformers under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20789)
*Pranava Madhyastha,Dagmar Adamcova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将人类工作记忆（容量限制、时间衰减、首因/近因效应）的认知约束直接"硬编码"进 GPT-2 的自注意力机制中，从零训练于 BabyLM 的 10M/100M 词发展性数据，评估 BLiMP 语法判断与人类阅读时间/ERP 等心理测量数据的对齐。结论：固定窗口注意力是最有效的归纳偏置，在低数据条件下显著提升语法准确率（68% vs 基线 61%），并使惊讶值更好地预测人类认知努力；同时促使注意力头出现可解释的功能特化与更早的句法结构线性可解码性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Transformer 的自注意力可近乎均匀地访问整个上下文，缺少对人类语言加工中工作记忆（WM）容量有限、时间衰减、首因与近因效应等关键认知约束的架构体现。虽然 GPT-2 类模型已被证明与人类阅读时间/神经活动高度相关，但这种成功可能是"尽管存在架构鸿沟"而非"因为存在"的结果。已有工作多把约束置于外围（预训练后加衰减偏置、推理时截断上下文、ALiBi 等软位置偏置仅"劝阻"而非"禁止"长程注意力、无约束模型中涌现的首近效应脆弱且尺度敏感）。本文因此提出核心问题：把人类工作记忆约束直接整合进架构、且从训练开始就生效，能否既使行为更类人，又提升学习效率？作者预期（零假设）强约束会损害模型性能，实际结果与该预期相反。

Method: 在 HuggingFace GPT-2 small（12 层、768 维、12 头、124M 参数、上下文 1024）上改造 GPT2Attention 模块，实现四类认知启发的注意力变体：(1) 固定窗口注意力：对位置 i 仅允许访问 [max(0,i-W+1), i]，用 -∞ 掩码实现硬截断，W∈{4,5,7,9}（依据 Cowan 2001 的 4 chunk 上限与 Miller 1956 的"神奇数字七±二"）；(2) 首因-近因注意力：按序列首/尾指数衰减并归一化得 p_i、r_i，加权合成位置偏置 b_j 加到 key 位置（初值 0.5 的可学习权重），并单独做 primacy-only / recency-only 消融；(3) 指数衰减注意力：a'=(1-α)a + α·e^{-|i-j|·λ}，采用 De Varda & Marelli (2024) 在 Provo 上网格搜索的最优 λ=82.86、α=0.37；(4) 逻辑斯蒂衰减注意力：以心理测量函数 w=1/(1+e^{k(d-m)}) 乘性调制，k=0.4、m=12.0，形成"近邻维持—快速跌落"的软硬混合。数据为 BabyLM 的 Strict-Small（10M 词）与 Strict（100M 词）训练集，并分别训练对应的 GPT-2 分词器。训练：AdamW、lr 5e-5、batch 64、weight decay 0.01、5 epochs、梯度裁剪 1.0，3 次运行取平均。评估：(a) BLiMP 最小对立对语法判断（按现象类别与子任务细分）；(b) 心理测量基准（1725 句的眼动指标如 FFD/Gaze Duration/Go-Past/RBTT、自定速阅读时间、ERP 成分 N400/P600/LAN/ELAN），以 ΔLog-Likelihood 衡量惊讶值相对仅含词长与词频的基线模型的增量解释力；(c) 内部表征分析：注意力头可视化 + 基于 Hewitt & Manning (2019)/Someya et al. (2025) 的结构探针（线性投影 B∈R^{64×768} 逼近依存树距离，最小生成树恢复结构，报告 UUAS，按 nsubj/dobj/prep/attr/root 五类关系与 0–11 逐层报告）。

Result: 1) BLiMP（10M）：所有改造模型（除 primacy/recency 单独消融外）显著优于基线（p≤0.001），基线约 61%，固定窗口约 68%。100M 时基线升至约 71%，差距收窄，指数衰减与首近偏置与基线无显著差异，但最优受限模型仍保持小幅显著优势。2) 现象层面：形态一致、限定词-名词一致、不规则形式、反身代词一致（均依赖局部依赖）表现最好，固定窗口模型尤为突出；论元结构与约束/绑定（Binding）在固定窗口 W=5 上显著优于基线，W=5 模型在论元结构上甚至接近 GPT2-large 预训练模型；省略（Ellipsis）对数据量高度敏感（10M 33–54% → 100M 65–78%）；海岛效应与 NPI 许可对所有模型均接近随机，且受限模型无优势——因为线性局部性不是捕捉抽象结构/逻辑辖域的正确抽象。窗口大小内部比较：W=5 综合最稳健，W=4 局部强但中等非局部依赖退化，W=7/9 与 5 相当但在强局部任务偶有退化。3) 心理测量对齐（10M）：基线 ΔLog-Likelihood ≈3.2，而固定窗口 7/9 及首近偏置接近其两倍；100M 时基线提升、各模型差距收窄，但所有模型（含基线）在 100M 的对齐度低于 10M，提示语言建模目标与人类理解在规模化下趋于分离。4) 内部表征：固定窗口 W=5 模型在首层即锐利聚焦前一词 token，L2H0/H1 聚焦 SVO 结构（'trophy','fit','suitcase'），L2H2 专精动词、L2H3 专精名词，类似 18–36 月龄儿童电报式言语；基线各头弥散、无功能分化。结构探针显示固定窗口模型在五类关系上 UUAS 全面更高，nsubj/dobj 在中间层约高 0.05，且结构更早出现（第 2 层即相当于基线第 4–5 层），与注意力分析结论一致。

Conclusion: 与人类工作记忆功能相符的架构约束是一种强归纳偏置，能带来数据效率提升、对人类理解过程更好的预测对齐，以及更具语言可解释性的内部结构；收益在数据受限（最接近人类语言习得条件）时最显著，而所有模型的心理测量对齐度随训练数据增加而退化，与"语言建模目标与人类理解在规模化下分离"的证据一致。这与当代追求更长上下文、更弱归纳偏置的趋势相抵触，说明"硬"认知约束能主动搭建学习脚手架而非阻碍学习。作者明确承认的局限：研究限于 10M/100M 词与英语；WM 实现是位置索引式刚性缓冲的简化，未建模相似性干扰与内容寻址检索；线性局部性可能妨碍需要全局抽象结构的现象（海岛效应）；纯文本单模态范式限制了认知合理性；消融与超参未充分调优（衰减机制是否弱于硬窗口无法定论）；探针为间接证据。开放方向：可基于结构距离（而非线性距离）的认知约束、干扰与内容寻址式 WM 机制、跨语言（形态格标记、自由语序、中心后置）与多模态验证，以及按 binder-bindee 线性距离分层的受控最小对分析。

Abstract: We investigate the integration of human-like working memory constraints into the Transformer architecture and implement several cognitively inspired attention variants, including fixed-width windows based and temporal decay based attention mechanisms. Our modified GPT-2 models are trained from scratch on developmentally plausible datasets (10M and 100M words). Performance is evaluated on grammatical judgment tasks (BLiMP) and alignment with human reading time data. Our results indicate that these cognitively-inspired constraints, particularly fixed-width attention, can significantly improve grammatical accuracy especially when training data is scarce. These constrained models also tend to show a stronger alignment with human processing metrics. The findings suggest that such constraints may serve as a beneficial inductive bias, guiding models towards more robust linguistic representations, especially in data-limited settings.

</details>


### [72] [Can "AI" Be a Doctor? A Study of Empathy, Readability, and Alignment in Clinical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20791)
*Mariano Barone,Francesco Di Serio,Roberto Moio,Marco Postiglione,Giuseppe Riccio,Antonio Romano,Vincenzo Moscato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究量化评估了医疗场景中LLM与临床医师沟通质量的差距，发现基线模型在情感极性和语言复杂度上与医师存在显著偏差，虽共情提示可缓解但无法弥补语义保真度缺陷；协作式重写策略表现最优，提示LLM更适合作为医患沟通的辅助增强工具而非独立替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在医疗领域的应用日益广泛，但其与临床沟通标准的对齐程度缺乏量化评估，需要系统性地探究模型输出与专业医师之间的差距。

Method: 多维度评估框架，从语义保真度、可读性、情感共鸣三个维度对通用型与领域专用LLM进行系统比较；涵盖结构化医学解释与真实医患交互场景；采用双重利益相关者评估（医生与患者）。

Result: 基线模型情感极性偏激（大模型负面情感达43-45%，vs医生37%）；大模型语言复杂度显著更高（GPT-5/Claude的FKGL达16.91-17.60，vs医生11.47-12.50）；共情提示可降低极端负面情感及复杂度（GPT-5降低6.87 FKGL），但未能提升语义保真度；协作重写整体对齐效果最佳；改写配置语义相似度最高（均值0.93）；患者一致偏好重写变体。

Conclusion: LLMs最有效地定位为协作式沟通增强工具，而非临床专业知识的替代品；无任何模型在认知标准上超越医生，而协作重写变体始终获得患者青睐。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in healthcare, yet their communicative alignment with clinical standards remains insufficiently quantified. We conduct a multidimensional evaluation of general-purpose and domain-specialized LLMs across structured medical explanations and real-world physician-patient interactions, analyzing semantic fidelity, readability, and affective resonance. Baseline models amplify affective polarity relative to physicians (Very Negative: 43.14-45.10% vs. 37.25%) and, in larger architectures such as GPT-5 and Claude, produce substantially higher linguistic complexity (FKGL up to 16.91-17.60 vs. 11.47-12.50 in physician-authored responses). Empathy-oriented prompting reduces extreme negativity and lowers grade-level complexity (up to -6.87 FKGL points for GPT-5) but does not significantly increase semantic fidelity. Collaborative rewriting yields the strongest overall alignment. Rephrase configurations achieve the highest semantic similarity to physician answers (up to mean = 0.93) while consistently improving readability and reducing affective extremity. Dual stakeholder evaluation shows that no model surpasses physicians on epistemic criteria, whereas patients consistently prefer rewritten variants for clarity and emotional tone. These findings suggest that LLMs function most effectively as collaborative communication enhancers rather than replacements for clinical expertise.

</details>


### [73] [Convergent Evolution: How Different Language Models Learn Similar Number Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20817)
*Deqing Fu,Tianyi Zhou,Mikhail Belkin,Vatsal Sharan,Robin Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究了语言模型如何学习表示数字，发现不同模型（Transformer、Linear RNN、LSTM、词嵌入）都能学习到具有周期T=2,5,10尖峰的傅里叶域特征，但只有部分模型能学习到几何可分离的特征用于线性分类模T运算。论文证明傅里叶域稀疏性是几何可分离的必要非充分条件，并发现数据、架构、优化器和分词器都会影响几何可分离特征的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型如何表示数字这一基本语义单元？不同架构的模型是否通过相同机制学习数字表示？傅里叶域的周期性与几何可分离性之间存在什么关系？这些问题的解答有助于理解语言模型的归纳偏置和表征学习机制。

Method: 理论分析：证明傅里叶域稀疏性是mod-T几何可分离的必要非充分条件；实证研究：对比不同架构（Transformer、Linear RNN、LSTM、词嵌入）、不同训练信号（自然语言数据、合成加法问题）、不同分词器对数字特征学习的影响。

Result: 发现数字特征存在两层 hierarchy：（1）所有模型都学习到周期T的傅里叶域尖峰特征；（2）只有部分模型进一步学习到几何可分离特征。几何可分离特征可通过两种途径习得：自然语言中的互补共现信号（如文本-数字共现、跨数字交互），或多token加法问题（而非单token加法）。

Conclusion: 特征学习存在趋同进化现象：多样的模型从不同的训练信号中学习到相似的特征。傅里叶域稀疏性本身不足以保证几何可分离性，模型需要更丰富的监督信号才能习得可用的数值推理能力。

Abstract: Language models trained on natural text learn to represent numbers using periodic features with dominant periods at $T=2, 5, 10$. In this paper, we identify a two-tiered hierarchy of these features: while Transformers, Linear RNNs, LSTMs, and classical word embeddings trained in different ways all learn features that have period-$T$ spikes in the Fourier domain, only some learn geometrically separable features that can be used to linearly classify a number mod-$T$. To explain this incongruity, we prove that Fourier domain sparsity is necessary but not sufficient for mod-$T$ geometric separability. Empirically, we investigate when model training yields geometrically separable features, finding that the data, architecture, optimizer, and tokenizer all play key roles. In particular, we identify two different routes through which models can acquire geometrically separable features: they can learn them from complementary co-occurrence signals in general language data, including text-number co-occurrence and cross-number interaction, or from multi-token (but not single-token) addition problems. Overall, our results highlight the phenomenon of convergent evolution in feature learning: A diverse range of models learn similar features from different training signals.

</details>


### [74] [Parallel-SFT: Improving Zero-Shot Cross-Programming-Language Transfer for Code RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20835)
*Zhaofeng Wu,Shiqi Wang,Boya Peng,Anuj Goyal,Melanie Kambadur,Sebastian Ruder,Yoon Kim,Chloe Bi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究零样本跨编程语言迁移的代码强化学习问题，发现Llama-3.1在源语言上的RL训练无法提升目标语言性能，甚至可能降低性能。提出Parallel-SFT方法，通过在SFT阶段引入多语言等价代码（parallel programs）来改善迁移能力，使模型形成以功能为中心的隐空间，从而提升跨语言泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型在主流编程语言（如C++、Python）上表现优异，但在资源较少的编程语言上能力受限。核心假设是大部分编程技能具有通用性，从一种语言学到的能力应能迁移到其他语言。

Method: 1) 提出零样本跨编程语言迁移任务；2) 发现仅在源语言上进行RL训练会损害或无法提升目标语言性能；3) 提出Parallel-SFT策略，在SFT阶段的数据混合中加入"parallel programs"（功能等价的跨语言代码），之后再进行RL训练。

Result: Parallel-SFT显著提升了跨语言迁移能力，使RL训练后的模型能更好地泛化到未见的编程语言。

Conclusion: Parallel-SFT通过引入跨语言等价代码，使模型学习到更以功能为中心的隐空间表示，功能等价的程序在不同语言间形成更紧密的聚类，从而解释了迁移能力提升的原因。

Abstract: Modern language models demonstrate impressive coding capabilities in common programming languages (PLs), such as C++ and Python, but their performance in lower-resource PLs is often limited by training data availability. In principle, however, most programming skills are universal across PLs, so the capability acquired in one PL should transfer to others. In this work, we propose the task of zero-shot cross-programming-language transfer for code RL. We find that, for Llama-3.1, RL training for code generation in a source PL fails to improve, and sometimes even degrades, the performance on other target PLs. To address this, we hypothesize that effective RL transfer requires a generalizable SFT initialization before RL. We thus propose **Parallel-SFT**, an SFT strategy that incorporates "parallel programs" -- functionally equivalent code implemented in multiple PLs -- into the data mixture. We demonstrate that this improves transferability: when we subsequently perform RL on our Parallel-SFT model, we observe better generalization to unseen PLs. Analysis of the model internal representations reveals that Parallel-SFT leads to a more functionality-centric latent space, where equivalent programs across PLs are more tightly clustered, which we hypothesize to contribute to the improved transferability.

</details>


### [75] [SpeechParaling-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Paralinguistic-Aware Speech Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20842)
*Ruohan Liu,Shukang Yin,Tao Wang,Dong Zhang,Weiji Zhuang,Shuhuai Ren,Ran He,Caifeng Shan,Chaoyou Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpeechParaling-Bench是一个针对大音频-语言模型(LALMs)的副语言学特征评估基准，将特征覆盖从50个扩展到100+个，包含1000+英中平行语音查询，设计三类渐进难度的任务，并开发基于LALM的成对比较评估管道以缓解主观性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LALMs的副语言学特征评估受限于粗糙的特征覆盖范围和评估固有的主观性，缺乏全面、可靠的评测基准。

Method: 1) 提出SpeechParaling-Bench基准，将副语言学特征覆盖从<50扩展至>100个细粒度特征；2) 构建1000+英中平行语音查询；3) 设计三类渐进难度任务：细粒度控制、句内变化、上下文适应；4) 开发基于LALM的成对比较评估管道，以相对偏好替代绝对评分。

Result: 实验揭示当前LALMs存在显著不足：即使领先的商业模型在综合静态控制和动态调制副语言学特征方面表现欠佳；在情境对话中，43.3%的错误源于未能正确理解副语言学提示。

Conclusion: 当前LALMs在副语言学建模方面存在重大局限，亟需开发更鲁棒的副语言学建模能力以实现类人语音助手。

Abstract: Paralinguistic cues are essential for natural human-computer interaction, yet their evaluation in Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) remains limited by coarse feature coverage and the inherent subjectivity of assessment. To address these challenges, we introduce SpeechParaling-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for paralinguistic-aware speech generation. It expands existing coverage from fewer than 50 to over 100 fine-grained features, supported by more than 1,000 English-Chinese parallel speech queries, and is organized into three progressively challenging tasks: fine-grained control, intra-utterance variation, and context-aware adaptation. To enable reliable evaluation, we further develop a pairwise comparison pipeline, in which candidate responses are evaluated against a fixed baseline by an LALM-based judge. By framing evaluation as relative preference rather than absolute scoring, this approach mitigates subjectivity and yields more stable and scalable assessments without costly human annotation. Extensive experiments reveal substantial limitations in current LALMs. Even leading proprietary models struggle with comprehensive static control and dynamic modulation of paralinguistic features, while failure to correctly interpret paralinguistic cues accounts for 43.3% of errors in situational dialogue. These findings underscore the need for more robust paralinguistic modeling toward human-aligned voice assistants.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [76] [Blossom VI: A Practical Minimum Weight Perfect Matching Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20351)
*Pavel Arkhipov,Vladimir Kolmogorov*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了最小权完美匹配（MWPM）的新实用算法 Blossom VI。其核心思想是：把原始—对偶算法的每个原始阶段（primal phase）视为在零松弛（tight/zero-slack）子图上求解无权最大匹配问题，用cherry tree/cherry blossom 代替传统交替树/花，并在原始阶段结束时一次性收缩整个 cherry blossom，而不是像 Blossom V 那样在阶段内反复收缩嵌套的传统花。实现（C++）在 9 个基准族（最大约 10^7 条边）上测试：在 Blossom V 出现超线性时间的困难族上显著更快（如 TSP 实例 lrb744710 从 120.3 秒降到 2.45 秒），在 Blossom V 已近线性扩展的族上最多慢约 2 倍。实验分析表明加速主要来自更浅的花嵌套深度和避免昂贵的展开（Expand）级联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小权完美匹配是组合优化中的基础问题，也是 Christofides 旅行商近似算法、平面图最大割的多项式算法、量子纠错等应用的关键子程序。自 2009 年起 Blossom V 一直是领先的通用实用实现，在许多自然图族上几乎线性时间运行，但在困难的结构化图上会出现明显减速（Blossom V 原论文中最重 TSP 实例即有此类现象）。理论上复杂度更优的算法并不必然在实践中更快，因此需要一个能保持实践效率、同时突破 Blossom V 瓶颈的实用实现。

Method: 1）框架：Edmonds 最小权完美匹配的 LP 对偶表述（节点约束 + 花约束），采用原始—对偶迭代，原始阶段固定对偶变量、只用零松弛边改进匹配，对偶阶段调整对偶变量使新紧边出现；不采用原 cherry-tree 论文的 metagraph 递归增强流程。

2）关键观察：每个原始阶段等价于在零松弛子图上求无权最大基数匹配，因此可复用基于 cherry tree 的高效无权最大匹配算法（Kotlov–Vainshtein 等），用 cherry tree（plus/minus/± 节点、receptacle 概念）维护搜索森林。

3）核心差异：不在原始阶段中即时收缩奇环，而是把中间嵌套结构保持为隐式，在原始阶段结束时一次性将每个非平凡 cherry blossom 收缩为超节点（Shrink），从而得到更浅的收缩层次、避免昂贵的展开级联。原始阶段操作包括 Grow-out、Grow-in、Augment、Expand；Shrink 只在其他原始操作都无法执行时进行；随后所有 cherry blossom 被收缩，森林退化为普通交替森林，可沿用 Blossom IV/V 的双变量 δ 的对偶更新（按树维护辅助图与优先队列、连通分量策略）。

4）正确性论证（附录 A）：给出主算法保持的不变量（对偶可行、匹配边与父边均紧、对偶变量为正的奇集内部匹配恰缺一个未匹配点）；证明每个 cherry blossom 都可由一串标准传统花收缩“传统实现”（归纳构造奇交替紧环），从而 Blossom VI 的终态可归约为传统多树花算法的合法状态，保证终止性与最优性；并给出 receptacle 旋转引理（Lemma 3.2）的完整新证明（作者认为原文献证明的终止性论证可能不完备）。

5）工程实现（C++）：近似分数匹配初始化（交替树超过阈值 t=100 即放弃增广）、连通分量式对偶更新（LP 最小费用流方案在树数少时试用但收益不明显）、松弛与对偶变量的惰性更新、blossom 父指针路径压缩、配对堆（intrusive pairing heap）与惰性堆销毁、maybe_has_zero_slack 标志优化 E0 遍历、超节点邻接边向量的连续存储、不维护树的孩子仅维护树节点列表等。

Result: 在 9 个实例族（稠密/稀疏随机、Delaunay 大/小权重、几何大/小权重、maxcut 大/小权重、TSP）上与 Blossom V 2.05 对比，单线程、500 秒超时、每例运行 3 次取均值（波动 <5%）。结果：稠密随机、稀疏随机、Delaunay-small-weights、geometric-small-weights、maxcut-small-weights 族上 Blossom VI 明显更快；Delaunay-big-weights 与 geometric-big-weights 族上大致持平；maxcut-big-weights 族上约慢 2 倍；在 Blossom V 超线性困难的族（稀疏随机、Delaunay/几何/maxcut 的小权重族及 TSP）上 Blossom V 多达 500 秒超时，而 Blossom VI 仍只需数十秒。TSP：sra104815 0.23s vs 0.20s、ara238025 0.83s vs 8.19s、lra498378 1.99s vs 8.57s、lrb744710 2.45s vs 120.27s。统计指标显示：困难实例上 Blossom VI 的最大超节点嵌套深度比 Blossom V 小 1~2 个数量级，且 Blossom V 的运行时间在由 Expand 主导的实例上占 76%~97%（展开需作废超节点所有关联边及自环的头尾指针，深层花会引发展开级联），这解释了主要加速；随机图族上 Blossom V 几乎全部时间花在初始化（其分数初始化几乎直接求得最优），Blossom VI 使用更轻量的近似分数初始化因而更快；小权重族上 Blossom VI 收缩的超节点更少。

Conclusion: Blossom VI 通过“把每个原始阶段视为无权最大匹配 + 用 cherry blossom 隐式表示中间奇集并在阶段末统一收缩”的设计，显著降低了超节点嵌套深度并避免了昂贵的展开级联，在 Blossom V 表现超线性的困难实例族上取得大幅加速，同时在近线性族上保持竞争力（最差约慢 2 倍）。作者认为 Blossom VI 是目前对 Blossom V 的实质性改进，并可通过多树分数初始化等方式继续优化；代码已开源于 GitHub（Paul566/blossom-vi）。

Abstract: We implement an algorithm for solving the minimum weight perfect matching problem. Our code significantly outperforms the current state-of-the-art Blossom V algorithm on those families of instances where Blossom V takes superlinear time. In practice, our implementation shows almost-linear runtime on every family of instances on which we have tested it.
  Our algorithm relies on solving the maximum-cardinality unweighted matching problems during its primal phase. Following the state-of-the-art cherry blossom algorithm, we use cherry trees instead of traditional alternating trees and cherry blossoms instead of traditional blossoms. We shrink cherry blossoms rather than traditional blossoms into supernodes. This strategy allows us to deal with much shallower supernodes.

</details>


### [77] [Nearly Optimal Bounds for Computing Decision Tree Splits in Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20394)
*Hoang Ta,Hoa T. Vu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据流中决策树分裂的近似问题，对回归和分类任务分别给出了接近最优的空间界。回归任务得到单轮算法，空间复杂度为Õ(M²/ε)；分类任务得到单轮算法，空间复杂度为Õ(1/ε)。两类问题均提供了匹配的空间下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树学习是机器学习基础任务，数据流模型下空间效率至关重要。现有方法存在两轮限制或空间次优的问题，需要设计单轮且空间最优的算法。

Method: 利用损失函数的Lipschitz性质，结合水库采样(reservoir sampling)和支持范围查询的Count-Min草图，通过精心设计的数据结构实现单轮空间高效算法。

Result: 回归：单轮算法达到Õ(M²/ε)空间，改进原两轮算法；分类：单轮算法达到Õ(1/ε)空间，改进原Õ(1/ε²)算法；两类问题均给出匹配的空间下界Ω(M²/ε)和Ω(1/ε)。

Conclusion: 在数据流模型下，决策树分裂近似问题已达到接近最优的空间-精度权衡，为该领域建立了理论基础。

Abstract: We establish nearly optimal upper and lower bounds for approximating decision tree splits in data streams. For regression with labels in the range $\{0,1,\ldots,M\}$, we give a one-pass algorithm using $\tilde{O}(M^2/ε)$ space that outputs a split within additive $ε$ error of the optimal split, improving upon the two-pass algorithm of Pham et al. (ISIT 2025). Furthermore, we provide a matching one-pass lower bound showing that $Ω(M^2/ε)$ space is indeed necessary.
  For classification, we also obtain a one-pass algorithm using $\tilde{O}(1/ε)$ space for approximating the optimal Gini split, improving upon the previous $\tilde{O}(1/ε^2)$-space algorithm. We complement these results with matching space lower bounds: $Ω(1/ε)$ for Gini impurity and $Ω(1/ε)$ for misclassification (which matches the upper bound obtained by sampling).
  Our algorithms exploit the Lipschitz property of the loss functions and use reservoir sampling along with Count--Min sketches with range queries. Our lower bounds follow from careful reductions from the INDEX problem.

</details>


### [78] [Cluster Vertex Deletion on Chordal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20457)
*Yixin Cao,Peng Li*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 提出一种在弦图上多项式时间算法解决cluster vertex deletion问题，使用基于团树的动态规划和亚模集合函数最小化，解决了多个近期文献中提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决弦图上cluster vertex deletion问题的多项式时间算法这一开放问题，该问题此前被多个研究团队在2018-2024年间反复提出，涉及到理论计算机科学、数学规划和算法领域。

Method: 在弦图的团树(clique tree)上进行动态规划，将最优子问题值的计算规约为亚模集合函数(submodular set function)的最小化问题。

Result: 成功设计出弦图上cluster vertex deletion问题的多项式时间算法。

Conclusion: 该算法解决了弦图上cluster vertex deletion问题的计算复杂性这一悬而未决的理论问题，具有重要的理论贡献。

Abstract: We present a polynomial-time algorithm for the cluster vertex deletion problem on chordal graphs, resolving an open question posed in different contexts by Cao et al. [Theoretical Computer Science, 2018], Aprile et al. [Mathematical Programming, 2023], Chakraborty et al. [Discrete Applied Mathematics, 2024], and Hsieh et al. [Algorithmica, 2024]. We use dynamic programming over clique trees and reduce the computation of the optimal subproblem value to the minimization of a submodular set function.

</details>


### [79] [Fully Dynamic Algorithms for Coloring Triangle-Free Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20648)
*Sepehr Assadi,Helia Yazdanyar*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文给出了一种动态算法，可在边插入删除条件下维护三角自由图（triangle-free graph）的 O(Δ/lnΔ) 着色，其中 Δ 为最大度。算法随机化，摊销更新时间为 Δ^{o(1)}·log n，高概率抵御自适应对手。核心创新在于将熵压缩法（entropy compression method）引入动态图算法场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Johansson 的经典结果证明三角自由图可 O(Δ/lnΔ) 着色且多项式时间可找，但此前无动态维护算法。本文旨在将这一最优上界拓展至边动态更新的场景，填补文献空白。

Method: 核心工具为熵压缩法——一种源自组合学（尤其是 Lovász局部引理构造性证明）的分析方法。算法设计为随机化，通过追踪更新过程中的"熵余量"证明摊销复杂度界。方法论上，将静态分析中的熵压缩技术适配至动态对抗场景。

Result: 对 n 顶点图，算法以高概率实现摊销更新时间 O(Δ^{o(1)}·log n)，保持 O(Δ/lnΔ) 颜色数。算法可抵御自适应 adversary（即对手可根据历史查询调整插入/删除操作）。

Conclusion: 给出了三角自由图动态着色问题的最优（与静态最优一致）彩色数保证及多对数级更新时间。熵压缩法在动态算法中的成功应用表明该技术具有普适性，有望推广至其他动态组合问题。

Abstract: A celebrated result of Johansson in graph theory states that every triangle-free graph of maximum degree $Δ$ can be properly colored with $O(Δ/\lnΔ)$ colors, improving upon the "greedy bound" of $Δ+1$ coloring in general graphs. This coloring can also be found in polynomial time.
  We present an algorithm for maintaining an $O(Δ/\lnΔ)$ coloring of a dynamically changing triangle-free graph that undergoes edge insertions and deletions. The algorithm is randomized and on $n$-vertex graphs has amortized update time of $Δ^{o(1)}\log{n}$ per update with high probability, even against an adaptive adversary.
  A key to the analysis of our algorithm is an application of the entropy compression method that to our knowledge is new in the context of dynamic algorithms. This technique appears general and is likely to find other applications in dynamic problems and thus can be of its own independent interest.

</details>


### [80] [Designing Approximate Binary Trees for Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20786)
*Leon Kellerhals,Mitja Krebs,André Nichterlein,Stefan Schmid*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 针对"Tree in Binary Tree (TBT)"问题：给定一棵树 G，在与 G 相同顶点集上构造一棵二叉树 H，最小化 G 中相邻顶点在 H 中的距离之和（cost_H = Σ_{uv∈E(G)} dist_H(u,v)）。本文给出该问题首个常数因子近似——线性时间的 4-近似算法，作者称这是该问题的第一个常数因子近似结果（此前仅有针对带 Steiner 点/三叉树/带宽/割宽的变体结果）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 问题源于"需求感知网络设计"（demand-aware network design）：在光交换技术（如数据中心可重构光网络）下，网络物理拓扑可动态调整以贴合通信需求。G 作为需求图（单位权需求），目标是设计宿主网络 H 以最小化加权通信距离。限制 G 为树具有实际意义：机器学习等并行/分布式负载的通信模式呈树状，真实网络拓扑也广泛为树。学术动机上，该问题属于"线性布局/嵌入"问题的变体（cutwidth、bandwidth、linear arrangement cost 均已研究半个多世纪），其中带宽与割宽的二叉树嵌入在 1980 年代已有结果（Monien、Simonson），但线性排列代价的二叉树版本此前无人研究，其计算复杂度亦未解决。

Method: 两阶段算法：

阶段一 Bracket Builder（对应变体 TBT-SN，允许 Steiner 点）：任意固定 G 的根，对每个顶点 v 用其 G 中的孩子构造完全二叉树 T'_v（内节点为 Steiner 点），并把 v 连接到 T'_v 的根形成 T_v。得到 cost_H(v) ≤ c_v(⌈log c_v⌉+1)（Lemma 3），配合 Lemma 2 的下界可证 3-近似（Lemma 4），且 Steiner 点数 O(n)。

下界（Lemma 2）：对最大度 Δ ≥ 3 的图 H，从 v 出发 BFS 直到发现 v 在 G 中的所有孩子得到 T_v，最优情形为 (Δ−1) 叉完全树，推出 cost_H(v) ≥ c_v·h_v + Δh_v/(Δ−2) − Δ((Δ−1)^{h_v}−1)/(Δ−2)²，其中 h_v = 1+⌊log_{Δ−1}⌈c_v/Δ⌉⌋；当 c_v ≥ Δ(Δ−1) 时可简化为 cost_H(v) ≥ c_v(log c_v/log(Δ−1) − 4)。

阶段二 Tournament Runner（消除所有 Steiner 点）：把每个 T_v 看作 v 的孩子之间的淘汰赛，Steiner 点对应一场"比赛"；胜者（c_x ≤ c_y 者，平局任意）取代 Steiner 点晋级，败者接管胜者原有的子树并被计费。关键点：胜者晋级使其到每个孩子的距离 +1，代价 ≤ c_x ≤ c_y，完全由败者承担，且每个节点最多输一次，故总增量 ≤ Σ_v c_v = n−1（Lemma 6）。也可直接构造：按度数递增把 v 的孩子排成完全二叉树（度数小者更靠近根）。

另外第 2 节论证 BST 路线不可行：若 G 为路径并按键交替赋键 1, n/2+1, 2, n/2+2, …，任何二叉搜索树成本为 Ω(n log n)，而与路径同构的二叉树成本仅 n−1，故 BST 只能给 Ω(log n) 近似。

Result: Theorem 1：TBT 存在线性时间的 4-近似。具体为 cost ≤ 3·cost_OPT + (n−1) ≤ 4·cost_OPT（因任意 H 的成本至少 |E(G)| = n−1）；Steiner 点 O(n) 个、比赛 O(n) 场、每场 O(1) 时间，故整体线性时间。附录表 1 给出 UB/LB 比值随 c_u 的变化，比值在 c_u 较小时最高约 3（低度顶点是当前分析的瓶颈），随 c_u 增大趋近 2（c_u ≥ 64 时 ≤ 2.1，c_u = 120+ 时约 1.74）。作者还指出该问题的计算复杂度仍未解决（既未证明 NP-hard 也未找到多项式精确算法）。

Conclusion: 本文给出了 TBT 的首个常数因子（4）线性时间近似算法，方法简洁：先允许 Steiner 点做平衡化，再以"淘汰赛"视角消除 Steiner 点并用精巧的计费（charging）方案控制额外代价。作者认为算法优雅简洁，但近似因子仍有改进空间，指出三个方向：(1) Bracket Builder 的上界假设所有叶子都在最底层，当 c_v 非 2 的幂时部分叶子位于次底层，可收紧；(2) 让最终胜者获得第二个孩子可降低成本；(3) 第二阶段分析未计入胜者向其 G 中父亲靠近所带来的收益，作者猜想可将额外代价从 n−1 降到 G 的叶子数。再结合对低度顶点的分类讨论（当前分析瓶颈，见附录表 1）有望降低近似因子，但可能牺牲算法的优雅性与增加分析复杂度。另一开放问题：在标准复杂度假设下能否改进近似因子的下界（目前为 1），以及 TBT 的精确计算复杂度仍是未解问题。

Abstract: We study the following problem that is motivated by demand-aware network design: Given a tree~$G$, the task is to find a binary tree~$H$ on the same vertex set. The objective is to minimize the sum of distances in~$H$ between vertex pairs that are adjacent in~$G$. We present a linear-time factor-4 approximation for this problem.

</details>


### [81] [Dynamic Construction of the Lovász Local Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20836)
*Bernhard Haeupler,Slobodan Mitrović,Srikkanth Ramachandran,Wen-Horng Sheu,Robert Tarjan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了大量局部搜索算法可以在完全动态设置下直接扩展到自适应对抗模型，实现每个更新操作 amortized Õ(1) 次局部搜索步骤。通过 witness-tree 和 entropy compression 技术，分析了局部重采样或回溯过程的收敛性，并应用于边着色等约束满足问题的动态维护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部搜索算法在静态实例上表现出色（通常只需亚线性修复步骤），但将其扩展到动态设置面临挑战——对手可以自适应地添加或删除约束。核心问题：如何在这种对抗性扰动下保持算法的收敛效率？此前该领域的动态算法（如 Christiansen SODA '26 的边着色）在某些参数 regime 下需要指数级运行时间。

Method: 利用 Moser (2009) 引入的 witness-tree 和 entropy compression 技术框架，将静态局部搜索算法的分析推广到动态对抗模型。核心思路：对违反约束的变量进行重采样或回溯，通过 witness-tree 结构追踪算法的执行历史，并借助 entropy compression 证明算法效率。

Result: 证明了简单的局部搜索过程在完全动态设置下仍保持 near-linear 的总重采样步骤数，即每个更新操作的 amortized Õ(1) 局部搜索步骤。具体应用：对于最大度 Δ = poly(log n) 和常数 ε，可维护 (1+ε)Δ-边着色，amortized 更新时间为 poly(log n)。

Conclusion: 结果具有广泛适用性，将多种局部搜索算法的静态收敛保证扩展到动态对抗设置。显著改进了此前该领域的动态算法复杂度（如边着色问题从指数时间提升到多项式时间），证明了许多已有的局部搜索算法无需修改即可在动态环境中保持高效。

Abstract: This paper proves that a wide class of local search algorithms extend as is to the fully dynamic setting with an adaptive adversary, achieving an amortized $\tilde{O}(1)$ number of local-search steps per update.
  A breakthrough by Moser (2009) introduced the witness-tree and entropy compression techniques for analyzing local resampling processes for the Lovász Local Lemma. These methods have since been generalized and expanded to analyze a wide variety of local search algorithms that can efficiently find solutions to many important local constraint satisfaction problems. These algorithms either extend a partial valid assignment and backtrack by unassigning variables when constraints become violated, or they iteratively fix violated constraints by resampling their variables. These local resampling or backtracking procedures are incredibly flexible, practical, and simple to specify and implement. Yet, they can be shown to be extremely efficient on static instances, typically performing only (sub)-linear number of fixing steps. The main technical challenge lies in proving conditions that guarantee such rapid convergence.
  This paper extends these convergence results to fully dynamic settings, where an adaptive adversary may add or remove constraints. We prove that applying the same simple local search procedures to fix old or newly introduced violations leads to a total number of resampling steps near-linear in the number of adversarial updates.
  Our result is very general and yields several immediate corollaries. For example, letting $Δ$ denote the maximum degree, for a constant $ε$ and $Δ= \text{poly}(\log n)$, we can maintain a $(1+ε) Δ$-edge coloring in $\text{poly}(\log n)$ amortized update time against an adaptive adversary. The prior work for this regime has exponential running time in $\sqrt{\log n}$ [Christiansen, SODA '26].

</details>

### [82] [3DPipe: A Pipelined GPU Framework for Scalable Generalized Spatial Join over Polyhedral Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19982)
*Lyuheng Yuan,Da Yan,Akhlaque Ahmad,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出3DPipe，一个用于多面体对象可扩展3D空间连接的流水线GPU框架。通过在过滤和精炼两阶段利用GPU并行、多层剪枝策略减少候选集、分块流式处理超越GPU内存限制，以及流水线执行重叠各计算环节，3DPipe实现了最高9.0倍于TDBase的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LiDAR目标检测、3D数字病理等应用的快速发展，3D数据量急剧增长，对3D数据集的空间连接操作需求日益迫切。然而，现有空间连接技术主要针对2D数据设计，3D空间连接研究尚不充分，计算成本高昂。

Method: 3DPipe采用流水线GPU框架实现3D空间连接，包含三个关键技术：1) 在过滤和精炼两阶段均充分利用GPU并行计算能力；2) 多层剪枝策略有效减少候选集；3) 分块流式处理突破GPU内存限制。系统通过流水线执行重叠CPU数据准备、主机-设备数据传输和GPU计算三个环节。

Result: 实验表明，3DPipe相比当前最先进的GPU解决方案TDBase实现了最高9.0倍的加速比，同时保持了优异的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 3DPipe为多面体对象的大规模3D空间连接提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案，在性能上显著优于现有GPU解决方案，并具有良好可扩展性。该工作填补了3D空间连接研究中GPU加速的空白，为LiDAR目标检测、3D数字病理等应用提供了重要的基础设施支撑。

Abstract: Spatial join is a fundamental operation in spatial databases. With the rapid growth of 3D data in applications such as LiDAR-based object detection and 3D digital pathology, there is an increasing need to support spatial join over 3D datasets. However, existing techniques are largely designed for 2D data, leaving 3D spatial join underexplored and computationally expensive. We present 3DPipe, a pipelined GPU framework for scalable spatial join over polyhedral objects. 3DPipe exploits GPU parallelism across both filtering and refinement stages, incorporates a multi-level pruning strategy for efficient candidate reduction, and employs chunked streaming to handle datasets exceeding GPU memory. Its pipelined execution overlaps CPU data preparation, host-device data transfer, and GPU computation to improve throughput. Experiments show that 3DPipe achieves up to 9.0$\times$ speedup over the state-of-the-art GPU solution, TDBase, while maintaining excellent scalability. 3DPipe is open-sourced at https://github.com/lyuheng/3dpipe.

</details>


### [83] [Worst-Case Optimal GPU Datalog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20073)
*Yihao Sun,Kunting Qi,Thomas Gilray,Sidharth Kumar,Kristopher Micinski*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SRDatalog 是首个基于最坏情况最优连接（WCOJ）的 GPU Datalog 引擎。它用扁平列式存储（SoA）+ 两阶段确定性内存分配（Count/Materialize）规避二进制连接的中间结果内存爆炸与静态 WCOJ 系统的索引重建开销；再用根层直方图引导的负载均衡、结构化辅助关系拆分（helper-relation splitting）应对键倾斜与深层扇出失衡，并用基于 CALM 定理的流序（phase-aligned 多 CUDA 流）规则复用提升 SM 占用率。在真实程序分析负载上相对等价算力成本的 CPU 引擎取得约 21×–47× 的几何平均加速，相对 GPU 基线（cuMatch、VFLog）分别达 2.1×–4.0× 与 2.3×–7.1×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU Datalog 的动机在于 Datalog 本身是访存受限（memory-bound）且具备巨大数据并行性，现有 GPU 引擎（GDlog、VFLog 等）通过 SIMT 友好的二进制连接取得对 CPU 的加速。但生产级程序分析负载（DOOP、ddisasm、polonius 等）包含大量 6–8 元递归规则，二进制分解会把多路连接拆成 k−1 次二元连接并物化中间结果，受 AGM 界约束会产生时间与空间上的超线性膨胀，导致与连接顺序无关的 OOM 失败。WCOJ 虽能避免该渐近膨胀，但其「一次一个属性」的交集模型在幂律度数分布下映射到 SIMT 硬件时出现严重负载倾斜：最外层变量绑定到高枢纽（如 java.lang.Object）时单个 thread group 承担绝大部分交集空间，其余 SM 空转。已有补救各有结构性缺陷：静态 WCOJ（cuMatch、HoneyComb）依赖 CoCo trie/网格分区等不可变层级索引，半朴素求值每轮注入 delta 需持续重建索引（微基准中重建占 merge 时间高达 93%）；动态方案（ALFTJ）用无锁工作窃取队列，在写密集负载下产生 SIMT 不友好的原子序列化，且无法先验确定输出偏移，必须用竞争性全局原子计数器分配写位置，破坏合并写带宽。作者据此提出三条架构硬性要求（imperatives）：两阶段 count-and-materialize 产生确定性写偏移；扁平有序列存储以支持廉价 delta 合并；启动期（launch-time）负载均衡器在不引入运行时协调的前提下化解最外层倾斜。

Method: 方法（SRDatalog 架构与三项关键机制）：
1. 编译与执行流水线：AOT 编译器把 Horn 子句降为分阶段 CUDA 内核，单次半朴素迭代按 Histogram → Count → Materialize → Compute Delta → Build Index（radix sort）→ Merge 顺序执行。
2. 两阶段 WCOJ（Imperative 1）：Count 内核执行多路列交集但不物化结果，产出 thread-group 级输出基数直方图；对该直方图做全局前缀和，一次性批量分配显存并算出每线程确定性写偏移；Materialize 内核重放交集逻辑并以合并写方式输出，从而避免工作窃取队列的原子竞争。
3. 扁平列式存储与 head/body 分区（Imperative 2）：所有关系以 Structure-of-Arrays 形式按查询计划规定的列序做字典序（基数序）排序存放，共享前缀的元组连续存储；借助协作式 fast-skip 在裸列上模拟 trie 式多路导航，无需维护 CoCo trie 等辅助索引。每个关系物理划分为轻量 head 缓冲与大体量有序 body，delta 合并仅写入 head，只有当 head 超过容量阈值时才触发一次 GPU 路径合并（path merge）将 head 冲刷进 body，从而吸收半朴素长尾迭代的写放大。
4. 根层直方图引导的负载均衡（Imperative 3）：直方图内核一次性扫描最外层连接列，统计每个根键的扇出（fan-out）并做前缀和，把整个连接空间展平为一维工作单元序列；内核启动时按 ⌈T/p⌉ 的连续切片把一维空间分配给各 thread block，重键因此可被多个 block 切分；线程用二分查找前缀数组把全局工作单元号反解为 (key, i1, i2) 三元组，直接点查列数组，无需辅助索引、迭代器状态或跨 warp 协调。直方图增量维护：仅对新 delta 计算并与基关系直方图在 merge 阶段合并，避免每轮全量重算。
5. 结构化辅助关系拆分：针对六层嵌套的 CallGraphEdge 规则，仅把导致灾难性内层倾斜的子句（MethodLookup、HeapAllocation_Type，变量 sn/dsc/t）抽出为独立 HelpNT 关系，使原本埋藏的倾斜键提升到顶层可被直方图均衡；其余 Reachable、Instruction_Method 子句保持融合，最终仍以 5 路 WCOJ 内核执行，兼顾倾斜治理与渐近内存效率。
6. 流序规则并行（§6）：依据 CALM 定理的单调性保证同层内不同规则可并发求值且收敛确定；编译器按流水线阶段（Count → Scan → Resize → Materialize）对独立规则分组，跨多个 CUDA 流并发派发，重叠计算内核与显存分配停顿，摊薄内核启动开销，并在深层嵌套造成的高寄存器压力下以独立活跃 warp 提升占用率；与 FlowLog 的全异步 tuple-at-a-time 流式不同，后者会产生散乱非合并写、破坏 GPU 带宽利用，故只限迭代内阶段对齐并发。

Result: 实验设置：GPU 侧为单张 NVIDIA RTX 6000 Ada (48 GB)；CPU 基线为云实例 AMD EPYC 9655 32 核 / 256 GB DDR5，两者按商品云租赁价格（约 $1/小时）对齐，构成「等价成本」基线。对比对象为 cuMatch、VFLog（GPU）与 Soufflé、FlowLog、Ascent（CPU）。
主要结果：
- 端到端（7 类负载、17 个数据集，计时排除数据加载）：SRDatalog 全部领先，对 Ascent 几何平均 21×、对 FlowLog 14×、对 Soufflé 26×；doop/ddisasm/polonius 等复杂多路负载上 3×–51×；Andersen 与 CSPA 类非线性传递闭包因浅不动点 + 密集交集达 32×–214×；galen 最高 74×（对 Soufflé）。
- 对 GPU WCOJ（cuMatch，LSQB SF=100，Q6/Q7/Q9）：2.1×–4.0×，Q9 从 3.07 s 降至 0.78 s（4.0×），Q6 从 12.85 s 降至 3.64 s（3.5×），主要归因于扁平列存避免了网格分区结构的指针追踪与分配开销。
- 对 GPU Datalog（VFLog，Same Generation 基准，4 图）：2.3×–7.1×，vsp_finan 513 次迭代从 35.5 s 降至 5.01 s；亦超过 Lobster 报告的 2×–3× 加速。
- 消融（直方图均衡，Nsight Systems 测内核时间）：1.1×–35.8×；HeapAllocHelper 达 35.8×（因 Object/Exception 等基类主导子类型层次，拆分后倾斜提升至根层被直方图吸收）；虚分派规则 3.1×–3.3×（只解决外层倾斜，VarPointsTo 内层倾斜仍存）；CastTo 1.8×（外层单键占 75.5% 但绝对量小，160 ms 基线）；VPT_LoadField 仅 1.1×，因 delta 优先策略使根层直方图收益中性。
- 消融（流并行调度）：DOOP 五个数据集 1.27×–1.66×（batik 1.66×，设备执行 3.0 s 压缩到 2.3 s 墙钟，硬件并发因子 1.30×），galen 1.23×；andersen/ddisasm/polonius 差异在 ±3% 以内，因其主 SCC 规则稀疏（≤3 条）或单条递归规则已自行饱和 SM。
- GDlog/VFLog 因在多路程序分析拓扑上必然 OOM，未纳入主表。

Conclusion: 结论：SRDatalog 通过在 GPU 上以 WCOJ 取代二进制连接，配合扁平列存、两阶段确定性物化、根层直方图均衡与辅助关系拆分，消除了既有 GPU Datalog 的中间结果显存耗尽与静态 WCOJ 的索引重建税，并借助 CALM 定理下的阶段对齐多流调度缓解长尾迭代的低占用率问题，从而首次实现复杂多路递归程序分析负载在单 GPU 上的端到端执行，成为当时最快、扩展性最好的 GPU Datalog 引擎。作者同时指出若干局限与后续方向：残余的内层变量倾斜尚未解决（CastTo、VPT_LoadField 显示根层单层均衡不足），拟通过现代 GPU 的 Cluster Launch Control 等特性做跨 SM 工作再分配；内层倾斜与长尾并发的收益在规则稀疏负载上有限；未来计划构建 GPU 原生、由代价模型驱动的查询规划器以自动寻找实例最优（instance-optimal）的变量序；此外本文正文存在实验数字不一致（摘要称 21×–47×，正文为 21×/14×/26×，且 ascent/flowlog/soufflé 加速比在各数据集间波动较大），arXiv 模板元数据（会议、ISBN、日期、作者邮箱）明显为占位符未填写，且未提供开源代码链接，复现性信息不足。

Abstract: Datalog is a declarative logic-programming language used for complex analytic reasoning workloads such as program analysis and graph analytics. Datalog's popularity is due to its unique price-point, marrying logic-defined specification with the potential for massive data parallelism. While traditional engines are CPU-based, the memory-bound nature of Datalog has led to increasing interest in leveraging GPUs. These engines beat CPU-based engines by operationalizing iterated relational joins via SIMT-friendly join algorithms. Unfortunately, all existing GPU Datalog engines are built on binary joins, which are inadequate for the complex multi-way queries arising in production systems such as DOOP and ddisasm. For these queries, binary decomposition can incur the AGM bound asymptotic blowup in time and space, leading to OOM failures regardless of join order. Worst-Case Optimal Joins (WCOJ) avoid this blowup, but their attribute-at-a-time intersections map poorly to SIMT hardware under key skew, causing severe load imbalance across Streaming Multiprocessors (SMs). We present SRDatalog, the first GPU Datalog engine based on WCOJ. SRDatalog uses flat columnar storage and two-phase deterministic memory allocation to avoid the OOM failures of binary joins and the index-rebuild overheads of static WCOJ systems. To mitigate skew and hide hardware stalls, SRDatalog further employs root-level histogram-guided load balancing, structural helper-relation splitting, and stream-aligned rule multiplexing. On real-world program-analysis workloads, SRDatalog achieves geometric-mean speedups of 21x to 47x.

</details>


### [84] [A GPU-Accelerated Framework for Multi-Attribute Range Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20121)
*Zhonggen Li,Haoran Yu,Yifan Zhu,Yunjun Gao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Garfield是一个GPU加速的多属性范围过滤ANNS框架，通过GMG轻量索引和硬件感知执行管道解决现有方案的索引膨胀和CPU计算瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范围过滤近似最近邻搜索（RFANNS）对现代向量数据库日益重要，但现有方案存在严重索引膨胀和高构建开销问题，且仅依赖CPU进行索引和查询处理，未能利用GPU强大算力。

Method: 1) 提出GMG索引：对数据进行单元划分，构建局部图索引，添加常数级跨单元边，保证线性存储和索引复杂度；2) 集群引导排序策略：重排查询相关单元，支持GPU上高效的逐单元遍历，跨单元复用候选项作为入口点；3) 单元导向核外管道：动态调度单元以最小化批次中活跃查询数，重叠GPU计算与CPU-GPU索引流传输。

Result: 索引体积缩小4.4倍，吞吐量提升119.8倍，优于现有最优RFANNS方法。

Conclusion: Garfield通过轻量级索引设计和GPU感知执行管道，有效克服了现有RFANNS方案的索引膨胀和高构建开销瓶颈。GMG索引的常数级跨单元边设计保证了线性存储和索引开销，集群引导排序策略实现了高效的GPU逐单元遍历，单元导向的核外管道则支持超GPU内存数据集的处理。

Abstract: Range-filtered approximate nearest neighbor search (RFANNS) is increasingly critical for modern vector databases. However, existing solutions suffer from severe index inflation and construction overhead. Furthermore, they rely exclusively on CPUs for the heavy indexing and query processing, failing to leverage the powerful computational capabilities of GPUs. In this paper, we present Garfield, a GPU-accelerated framework for multi-attribute range filtered ANNS that overcomes these bottlenecks through designing a lightweight index structure and hardware-aware execution pipeline. Garfield introduces the GMG index, which partitions data into cells and builds local graph indexes. By adding a constant number of cross-cell edges, it guarantees linear storage and indexing overhead. For queries, Garfield utilizes a cluster-guided ordering strategy that reorders query-relevant cells, enabling a highly efficient cell-by-cell traversal on the GPU that aggressively reuses candidates as entry points across cells. To handle datasets exceeding GPU memory, Garfield features a cell-oriented out-of-core pipeline. It dynamically schedules cells to minimize the number of active queries per batch and overlaps GPU computation with CPU-to-GPU index streaming. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Garfield reduces index size by 4.4x, while delivering 119.8x higher throughput than state-of-the-art RFANNS methods.

</details>


### [85] [An Agentic Approach to Metadata Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20144)
*Jiani Zhang,Sercan O. Arik,Cosmin Arad,Fatma Ozcan,Alon Halevy*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出Metadata Reasoner，一种用于数据源发现的智能体方法。通过结合表格搜索引擎和自主元数据推理，在真实数据集KramaBench上达到83.16% F1-score，比现有最佳方法提升32个百分点；在含噪声的BIRD数据集上保持85.5% F1-score和99%低质量数据规避率，证明了其有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-driven autonomous agents performing complex multi-step tasks face a critical bottleneck in discovering relevant data sources, compounded by the sheer volume of available data and the nuanced semantics requiring consideration of many aspects.

Method: The Metadata Reasoner is an agentic approach combining: (1) a table-search engine for candidate retrieval, and (2) autonomous metadata consultation to evaluate whether candidates meet task requirements, with goal of finding sufficient yet minimal data source sets.

Result: KramaBench: 83.16% average F1-score (32 percentage points above baselines); BIRD benchmark: 85.5% average F1-score in noisy environment, 99% success rate in avoiding low-quality data.

Conclusion: The Metadata Reasoner demonstrates a substantial improvement over existing methods, achieving 32 percentage points higher F1-score on real-world data and maintaining high robustness (85.5% F1, 99% success rate) in noisy environments with redundant/low-quality tables.

Abstract: As LLM-driven autonomous agents evolve to perform complex, multi-step tasks that require integrating multiple datasets, the problem of discovering relevant data sources becomes a key bottleneck. Beyond the challenge posed by the sheer volume of available data sources, data-source selection is difficult because the semantics of data are extremely nuanced and require considering many aspects of the data. To address this, we introduce the Metadata Reasoner, an agentic approach to metadata reasoning, designed to identify a small set of data sources that are both sufficient and minimal for a given analytical task. The Metadata Reasoner leverages a table-search engine to retrieve candidate tables, and then autonomously consults various aspects of the available metadata to determine whether the candidates fit the requirements of the task. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the Metadata Reasoner through a series of empirical studies. Evaluated on the real-world KramaBench datasets for data selection, our approach achieves an average F1-score of 83.16%, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines by a substantial margin of 32 percentage points. Furthermore, evaluations on a newly-created synthetic benchmark based on the BIRD data lake reveal that the Metadata Reasoner is highly robust against redundant and low-quality tables that may be in the data lake. In this noisy environment, it maintains an average of 85.5% F1-score for selecting the right datasets and demonstrates a 99% success rate in avoiding low-quality data.

</details>


### [86] [Pre-Execution Query Slot-Time Prediction in Cloud Data Warehouses: A Feature-Scoped Machine Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20145)
*Prashant Kumar Pathak*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出基于机器学习的方法，在查询执行前预测BigQuery计算槽时间，通过融合SQL操作符复杂度评分、数据卷特征和查询文本TF-IDF特征，实现比基线方法30-37%的误差降低。但对于长尾查询(>=20分钟)效果不佳，可能因运行时因素不可观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云数据仓库按槽时间计费，但查询成本高度可变且难以预估，导致预算超支和调度性能下降。传统静态查询规划器启发式方法无法捕获复杂的SQL结构、数据偏斜和工作负载竞争等问题。

Method: 1) 仅使用执行前可观测信号：SQL操作符成本导出的查询复杂度评分、计划器估算的数据量特征和工作负载元数据、查询文本的文本特征；2) 使用HistGradientBoostingRegressor对数变换后的槽时间建模；3) 文本特征采用TF-IDF + TruncatedSVD-512管道；4) 刻意排除运行时因素（槽池利用率、缓存状态、实际偏斜）；5) 训练集：7个部署环境的749个查询；测试集：2个保留环境的746个查询。

Result: 全工作负载MAE 1.17槽分钟，RMSE 4.71，可解释方差74%；成本显著查询(>=0.01分钟)MAE 3.10 vs 预测均值4.95 vs 预测中位数4.54，误差降低30-37%；长尾查询(>=20分钟)性能未优于平凡基线。

Conclusion: 长尾查询性能不足支持假设：其主要由当前特征范围外的运行时因素主导。提出复杂度路由双模型架构作为实用改进方向，并识别出弥补长尾差距的未来研究方向。

Abstract: Cloud data warehouses bill compute based on slot-time consumed. In shared multi-tenant environments, query cost is highly variable and hard to estimate before execution, causing budget overruns and degraded scheduling. Static query-planner heuristics fail to capture complex SQL structure, data skew, and workload contention. We present a feature-scoped machine learning approach that predicts BigQuery slot-time before execution using only pre-execution observable signals: a structured query complexity score derived from SQL operator costs, data volume features from planner estimates and workload metadata, and textual features from query text. We deliberately exclude runtime factors (slot-pool utilization, cache state, realized skew) unknowable at submission. The model uses a HistGradientBoostingRegressor trained on log-transformed slot-time, with a TF-IDF + TruncatedSVD-512 text pipeline fused with numeric and categorical features. Trained on 749 queries across seven deployment environments and evaluated out-of-distribution on 746 queries from two held-out environments, the model achieves MAE 1.17 slot-minutes, RMSE 4.71, and 74% explained variance on the full workload. On cost-significant queries (slot-time >= 0.01 min, N=282) the model achieves MAE 3.10 versus 4.95 for a predict-mean baseline and 4.54 for predict-median, a 30-37% reduction. On long-tail queries (>= 20 min, N=22) the model does not outperform trivial baselines, consistent with the hypothesis that long-tail queries are dominated by unobserved runtime factors outside the current feature scope. A complexity-routed dual-model architecture is described as a practical refinement, and directions for closing the long-tail gap are identified as future work.

</details>


### [87] [Estimating Power-Law Exponent with Edge Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20274)
*Adam Tan,Mohamed Hefny,Keval Vora*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出在边级差分隐私(edge-DP)下直接对幂律指数 α 估计所需的低维充分统计量 (T_disc, N) 加噪、而非先发布带噪度分布直方图再做拟合的方法；在中心化与本地两种模型下给出离散近似与似然数值优化两类变体（共6种算法），在9个数据集上验证其精度与稳定性显著优于 Hay et al. (2009) 的直方图拟合基线（中心化 NO 的平均 l1 误差约 0.005%，基线约 15.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多真实网络的度分布近似服从幂律 P(d)∝d^{-α}，α（尺度参数/幂律指数）是概括图结构并指导图算法与系统优化的重要标量。当图中包含敏感关系数据时，需要在不泄露任何单条边信息的前提下估计 α。已有的图 DP 工作只提供通用的结构统计发布，并不直接针对幂律指数估计。主流基线做法（Hay et al., 2009）是先发布带噪的度分布直方图、再对其做幂律拟合，但该“发布直方图→拟合”流程中每个度桶都需加噪，叠加平滑、分箱与投影会严重扭曲度分布尾部，使 α 的估计方差大、误差高；为估计单个标量参数而发布整个直方图在效率上也不合理。

Method: 问题设定：无向简单图 G=(V,E)，度分布取 d∈[d_min,d_max] 上的截断离散幂律 P(d|α)=d^{-α}/Z(α)，Z(α)=Σ_{d=dmin}^{dmax} d^{-α}（由 Hurwitz ζ 函数计算）；尾部度数多重集 D' 的规模 N=|D'|。α 的充分统计量是低维对 (T,N)，其中 T=Σ ln d_i；Clauset et al. (2009) 的离散近似估计量为 α̂=1+N/T_disc，T_disc=Σ ln(d_i/(d_min−0.5))。

中心化算法（Alg.1–3）：证明单条边仅影响两个端点，得 ΔT_disc ≤ 2ln((d_min+1)/d_min)（d_min=1 时约 1.386，随 d_min 增大而减小）、ΔN ≤ 2；用 Laplace 机制分别对 T_disc 与 N 加噪，预算 ε=ε_t+ε_n（实验取 ε/2），再重组。Alg.1 直接代入闭式估计量 α̂=1+Ñ/T̃_disc；Alg.2 利用恒等式 Σln d_i = T_disc + N·ln(d_min−0.5)，在噪声统计量上数值优化精确离散对数似然 ℓ(α;T,N)=−αT−N ln Z(α)（Alg.3 给出对数似然打分函数）；拟合与优化均为后处理，无额外隐私损失。d_max 若公开可免加噪，取保守值（如 |V|）对 MLE 影响极小。

本地算法（Alg.4–5）：无可信策展人，每节点本地加噪。方案一“度释放”：d̃_v = d_v + Lap(2/ε)，因每条边被两端点各用一次故预算 ε/2 拆分，聚合时对 d̃_v ≥ d_min 的节点计 N 并累加 ln(d̃_v/(d_min−0.5))；方案二“对数统计量释放”：c̃_v = ln(d_v/(d_min−0.5)) + Lap(2ln((d_min+1)/d_min)/ε)，按 c̃_v ≥ ln(d_min/(d_min−0.5)) 判尾。两种释放方式各自与离散近似（Option A）或数值优化（Option B）组合，得到 DA/DR、DA/LR、NO/DR、NO/LR 四个 LEDP 变体。实验：6 个真实图（wiki、enron、brightkite、ego-twitter、gplus、stanford，SNAP 无向化）+ 3 个合成图（Inc-Powerlaw 生成器，α=2/2.5/3）；默认 ε=1.0，另测 ε∈[0.1,5]；d_min∈{1,3}（d_min=5,10 时非私有 MLE 越界故弃用）；指标为相对非私有 α 的 l1 误差，重复 20 次取均值与标准差；基线 Base 为 Hay et al. 的 edge-DP 直方图发布 + 幂律拟合。

Result: 中心化（Table 4）：d_min=1 时 Base 平均/最差 l1 = 15.88%/76.70%，DA 为 9.57%/17.69%，NO 仅 0.0049%/0.0989%；d_min=3 时 Base 21.79%/89.80%，DA 5.28%/9.47%，NO 0.0066%/0.1115%。NO 的平均误差比 DA 低约三个数量级、最差误差低约两个数量级，且在每个数据集上均最优；DA 在聚合意义上优于 Base（仅 d_min=1 的三个合成图上 Base 更优）。

本地（Table 5）：d_min=1 时 DA/LR 8.72%、DA/DR 9.14%、NO/LR 6.26%、NO/DR 1.03%（最差 3.31%）；d_min=3 时分别为 4.61%、5.94%、3.06%、1.63%。四个本地变体的最差误差均低于 Base，NO/DR 整体最强。

四项发现：(1) 直接对子分量/充分统计量加噪一致优于度分布直方图拟合（尾部不被破坏、仅扰动低维统计量）；(2) 数值优化优于闭式离散近似（DA 本身有近似偏差，NO 优化精确离散似然）；(3) 在 LDP 中释放方式与估计器存在交互：NO 偏好度释放（NO/DR 优于 NO/LR），DA 偏好对数统计量释放（DA/LR 优于 DA/DR）；(4) d_min 影响依赖方法：中心化 DA 随 d_min 增大在所有数据集改善、NO 多变差；本地 DA/LR、DA/DR、NO/LR 多改善而 NO/DR 多变差。

隐私预算敏感性：中心化曲线（尤其 NO）在 ε=0.1~5 区间近乎平坦（NO 平均 l1 约 1.7e-4%→0），本地变体随 ε 增大显著改善；中心化 NO 在整个区间内绝对误差低于所有本地变体。局部小预算下存在失效风险：ego-twitter 上 ε=0.1 时 NO/LR 全部运行优化到 α<0 被截断而无有效估计。

Conclusion: 本文提出并系统评估了在边级差分隐私下估计图幂律指数 α 的方法族：直接私有化 α 估计所需的低维充分统计量 (T_disc, N)，避免传统“先发布带噪度直方图再拟合”流程中对分布尾部的扭曲，从而在精度与稳定性上均优于基线；中心化与本地两类模型、离散近似与数值优化两种估计器的交叉组合共产生 2+4 个算法。结论是数值优化（尤其中心化 NO）精度最高，本地场景下 NO/DR 综合最优，d_min 的选择需与估计器匹配。未来工作包括：用随机响应仅发布 1 比特尾节点指示位以更精确估计尾规模 N；将 LDP 与“友谊悖论”采样结合以在部分参与/通信受限场景下获得过度代表高阶节点的度观测，并刻画其隐私—效用权衡。

Abstract: Many real-world graphs have degree distributions that are well approximated by a power-law, and the corresponding scaling parameter $α$ provides a compact summary of that structure which is useful for graph analysis and system optimization. When graphs contain sensitive relationship data, $α$ must be estimated without revealing information about individual edges. This paper studies power-law exponent estimation under edge differential privacy. Instead of first releasing a noisy degree distribution and then fitting a power-law model, we propose privatizing only the low-dimensional sufficient statistics needed to estimate $α$, thereby avoiding the high distortion introduced by traditional approaches. Using these released statistics, we support both discrete approximation and likelihood-based numerical optimization for efficient parameter estimation. We develop edge-DP algorithms for both centralized and local DP models, compare degree release and log-statistic release in the local setting, and evaluate the resulting methods on various graph datasets across multiple privacy budgets and tail-cutoff settings.

</details>


### [88] [Making TransactionIsolation Checking Practical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20587)
*Jian Zhang,Shuai Mu,Cheng Tan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出 Boomslang——首个通用的事务隔离级别检查框架，通过类编译器的"前端-后端分离"流水线（envelope tracing → 抽象语义图 ASG → 含"叠加态(superposition)"的低级 IR → SMT 约束 → 求解），在无需了解数据库内部实现的前提下支持任意操作类型（put/get/delete/scan/iterator）与可扩展的隔离级别模块化定义；实验表明其可用 271–386 行代码复现 Cobra/Viper/PolySI 三个已有检查器且性能相当或更优，并新支持 RR、RC、PL-2+、CS、PL-FCV 五个此前无法检查的隔离级别，发现 TiDB 新 bug、审计 JuiceFS 元数据层、支持 MariaDB 厂商特有语义，并实证确认了时间戳反演陷阱这一理论预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隔离级别检查是验证事务数据库正确性的关键，但现有工具各有硬伤：黑盒检查器（Cobra、Viper、PolySI）通常只支持读/写两类操作，无法处理范围查询与迭代器，且每个工具只针对单一隔离级别，无法扩展；白盒检查器（Emme）需要解析 CDC 日志等数据库内部信息，门槛高、也无法复用扩展。真实场景还提出四类挑战：(1) 真实工作负载（如 JuiceFS 元数据层）使用迭代器、范围查询、物理删除等完整算子集，且未返回的键是"从未插入""已被删除"还是"被迭代器漏掉"本质不可判定；(2) 厂商特有语义偏离教科书定义（如 MariaDB 的 SELECT FOR UPDATE 读取最新版本，违反 RR 的快照一致性），现有检查器会误报；(3) 现有检查器假设"写值唯一"和"墓碑删除"，而真实系统存在重复值与物理删除，部分 bug（MariaDB）仅在重复值下才暴露；(4) 实际系统提供的是组合保证（如 RC 下的 read-your-own-writes），由于 RC 中不存在反依赖，无法简单"关掉"某类依赖来打补丁。因此需要一个统一、通用、可扩展且对非专家友好的检查框架，且必须保持可靠（soundness）与完备（completeness），而不是像 Elle 等那样为性能牺牲二者之一。

Method: Boomslang 采用类编译器的模块化流水线，把 trace 视为"程序"、隔离级别视为"语言"：(1) 追踪——envelope tracing，仅在数据库调用前后包装 log_input/log_output，不修改应用逻辑也不依赖数据库内部，配合 ctx 传递用户提示；(2) 解析——构建抽象语义图 ASG，含三部分：known graph（按目标隔离级别定制节点的依赖图）、write history（每个 key 的写集合 wSet 与部分可见的版本序 verOrder）、read lineage（按 (key,value) 索引的读取记录，含候选写者集合 candWs、操作类型与版本集 verSet）；(3) 语义分析——降级为低级 IR = 简单有向图 + 边上一阶逻辑表达式 + 核心新抽象 superposition（⟨possibility_1,…,possibility_n⟩，表示"已知但未知"的依赖，真实情形中恰有一个为真），依赖生成 pass 逐类产出 wr/ww/rw/pwr/prw 等依赖，唯一候选写者产生已知边，多个候选则生成 superposition；用户可注册自定义模块（可访问只读 ASG 与 hints）以支持会话序、实时序、厂商语义等；(4) 优化与约束生成——三项优化：可达性剪枝（预计算传递闭包，命中路径即消解 superposition）、拓扑优先（按拓扑序优先尝试更可能的可能性）、unsat search（把 trace 切成子 trace 分别求解以快速定位小 unsat core），并把每个 superposition 编码为"恰有一个为真"的互斥析取；(5) 求解——默认用原生支持图原语的 MonoSAT，亦可编译为经典 SMT 子句交由 Z3，返回 sat/unsat 加反例。实现为 Java，约 14K 行（ASG 0.6K、ASG→IR 1.7K、优化 1.1K、IR→SMT 1.9K）。

Result: 功能上，Boomslang 是已有工作的超集：(1) 实现 8 个检查器（SSER/SER/RR/SI/RC/PL-2+/CS/PL-FCV），每个 <370 行，其中 SER/PL-2+/CS/PL-FCV 共享同一份 ASG→IR 代码；(2) 以 271/281/386 行分别复现 Cobra（16.6K）、Viper（2.9K）、PolySI（3.4K）的核心逻辑，代码量约减少 9 倍，且性能相当或更优，跨 checker 共享优化（拓扑优先默认开启）；(3) 统一框架下重做 Viper 与 PolySI 的 SI 编码对比，发现 Viper 编码实际略快于 PolySI，说明此前的性能差距源于实现差异而非编码本身。性能上：与 Cobra（10K txns）比总体相当或更好，仅无 superposition 的 TPC-C 因通用性的边记账开销略慢；与 Viper/PolySI（50K txns）比，Viper 在 BlindW-C/RUBiS/Twitter 上均触发 600s 超时，Boomslang 全面快于 PolySI（Twitter 117.2s vs 234.6s，RUBiS 78.9s vs 109.4s），TPC-C 上二者均 OOM；与 Elle（ListAppend-J）性能相当，仅 SMT 编码有额外斜率开销。消融显示无优化时超时（>600s），拓扑优先使问题变得可解，可达性剪枝进一步提速；运行时间随 superposition 数量超线性增长。可扩展性上：MariaDB 厂商语义仅需 85 行前端改动（把物理事务切分为带新视图的逻辑子事务），三种 RYOW 策略仅需 38 行。应用上：在 TiDB 8.5.4 发现新 bug（pessimistic 模式下 RR 中 UPDATE 后读不到自身写入，已被官方确认为中等严重度），稳定捕获 PostgreSQL 12.3/MariaDB 10.6 的已知并发 bug（含仅在重复值下出现的异常，以及现有检查器无法刻画的含范围查询的 G2 环），审计 JuiceFS 元数据层（现有检查器无法支持其 Get/Set/Iter/Delete 全 API），并在 Tapir 上找到"可串行化但不严格可串行化"的真实 trace，实证确认 timestamp inversion pitfall；对 15 条已知真实违规 trace 全部在 26s 内检出。规模上可处理至 80K 事务（SI 内存约 40GB）。

Conclusion: Boomslang 证明了一个通用、可扩展、实用的隔离级别检查框架是可行的：通过 ASG + superposition 的抽象，把"任意算子、缺失信息、厂商语义、组合隔离保证"统一到同一 IR 中，使前端扩展新隔离级别、后端共享优化成为可能，同时也为不同检查器提供了公平对比的平台。其局限在于：支持未修改数据库与任意算子意味着理论上面对 NP-Complete 问题，无法保证在有界时间内判定所有 trace；不支持完整 SQL 语义（如何高效把丰富 SQL 语义翻译为 SMT 约束仍是开放问题）；unsat search 目前采用随机切分 trace，更优的切分策略是未来工作。该工作为并发测试预言机、数据完整性审计、外包数据库验证等场景提供了通用基础设施，并有望打破"没有统一框架就没人做专用检查器、没有用例就没人建框架"的循环。

Abstract: Checking whether database transactions adhere to isolation levels is a crucial yet challenging problem. We present Boomslang, the first general-purpose checking framework capable of verifying configurations that were previously uncheckable.
