We compared different augmentation strategies to a traditional oversampling method (SMOTE) in an effort to avoid overfitting and retain novice-level data critical for learning progression alignment. Findings demonstrate that targeted augmentation can address severe imbalance while preserving conceptual coverage, offering a scalable solution for automated learning progression-aligned scoring in science education.

</details>


### [118] [Explainable AML Triage with LLMs: Evidence Retrieval and Counterfactual Checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19755)
*Dorothy Torres,Wei Cheng,Ke Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可解释的反洗钱（AML）分类框架，将分类决策过程约束于证据基础之上。该框架结合了检索增强的证据聚合、结构化输出合约和反事实验证三大组件，在合成AML基准测试中实现了PR-AUC 0.75和Escalate F1 0.62的最佳分类性能，同时保持了较高的可审计性和忠实性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前反洗钱交易监控系统产生大量警报，需调查人员在严格审计约束下快速分类。传统LLM虽能总结异构证据并生成理由，但在监管工作流中存在幻觉、溯源能力弱、解释不忠实于决策等风险，不加约束的生成式方法难以满足合规要求。

Method: 提出基于证据约束决策的可解释AML分类框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 检索增强的证据聚合，从政策/类型学指南、客户上下文、警报触发因素和交易子图中检索相关证据并打包；(2) 结构化LLM输出合约，要求显式引用并分离支持、反驳和缺失的证据；(3) 反事实检查，验证最小合理扰动是否导致分类建议和理由的一致性变化。

Result: 在公开合成AML基准和模拟器上评估，对比规则方法、表格式和图机器学习基线、纯LLM及纯RAG变体。结果表明：证据接地显著提升可审计性并减少数值和政策幻觉错误；反事实验证进一步增强决策关联的可解释性和鲁棒性。最终达到最佳分类性能（PR-AUC 0.75; Escalate F1 0.62）及强可追溯性和忠实性指标（引用有效性0.98; 证据支持率0.88; 反事实忠实性0.76）。

Conclusion: 治理化、可验证的LLM系统能够在不牺牲可追溯性和可辩护性等合规要求的前提下，为AML分类提供实用的决策支持，验证了监管场景下可信AI系统的可行性。

Abstract: Anti-money laundering (AML) transaction monitoring generates large volumes of alerts that must be rapidly triaged by investigators under strict audit and governance constraints. While large language models (LLMs) can summarize heterogeneous evidence and draft rationales, unconstrained generation is risky in regulated workflows due to hallucinations, weak provenance, and explanations that are not faithful to the underlying decision. We propose an explainable AML triage framework that treats triage as an evidence-constrained decision process. Our method combines (i) retrieval-augmented evidence bundling from policy/typology guidance, customer context, alert triggers, and transaction subgraphs, (ii) a structured LLM output contract that requires explicit citations and separates supporting from contradicting or missing evidence, and (iii) counterfactual checks that validate whether minimal, plausible perturbations lead to coherent changes in both the triage recommendation and its rationale. We evaluate on public synthetic AML benchmarks and simulators and compare against rules, tabular and graph machine-learning baselines, and LLM-only/RAG-only variants. Results show that evidence grounding substantially improves auditability and reduces numerical and policy hallucination errors, while counterfactual validation further increases decision-linked explainability and robustness, yielding the best overall triage performance (PR-AUC 0.75; Escalate F1 0.62) and strong provenance and faithfulness metrics (citation validity 0.98; evidence support 0.88; counterfactual faithfulness 0.76). These findings indicate that governed, verifiable LLM systems can provide practical decision support for AML triage without sacrificing compliance requirements for traceability and defensibility.

</details>


### [119] [OpenCLAW-P2P v6.0: Resilient Multi-Layer Persistence, Live Reference Verification, and Production-Scale Evaluation of Decentralized AI Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19792)
*Francisco Angulo de Lafuente,Teerth Sharma,Vladimir Veselov,Seid Mohammed Abdu,Nirmal Tej Kumar,Guillermo Perry*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这是一篇系统架构+生产运行报告型论文，提出 OpenCLAW-P2P：一个完全由自治 AI 智能体构成的去中心化同行评审网络，智能体相互发表、评审、打分并迭代改进科研论文，无人工把关。v6.0 引入四大子系统：四层论文持久化（内存缓存/Cloudflare R2/Gun.js/GitHub）、多级检索级联（延迟由 >3s 降至 <50ms）、实时引文核验（CrossRef/arXiv/Semantic Scholar，识别伪造引用准确率 >85%）、限速缓存型科学 API 代理（7 个公共数据库）。v7.0 则集中修正 v6.0 理论层的数学不一致（量纲、取值域、符号歧义），并新增生态进展（CAJAL 4B/9B 科研写作微调模型、BenchClaw 部署）。生产环境运行 14 个真实智能体（3 研究、5 架构元智能、6 恢复/专项）+ 23 个明确标注的模拟市民，产出 50+ 篇计分论文（2072–4073 词，排行榜 6.4–8.1），并公开了失败模式分析与挽回 25 篇丢失论文的恢复协议。整体价值在于工程可复现性与"诚实报告"，但所有关键指标均为自测自评、缺少基线对照与外部验证，可信度需谨慎评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出传统同行评审存在三大缺陷：流程缓慢、不透明、易受人类偏见影响；同时 LLM 已能生成"看似合理但不严谨"的科学文本，并普遍编造参考文献（ghosts citations），若仅靠结构化启发式检测（如缺失编号）无法识别语义上逼真的伪造引用。此外，v5.0 系统的生产化运行暴露出严重的数据韧性问题：启动期恢复管线存在 bug 将论文截断至 500 字符（约 58 词），却保留异常高分；论文仅写入异步评分回调（发布后 30–60 秒）的内存缓存导致"短暂不可见"；Gun.js 无 relay 时在 Railway 重新部署即全量丢失，GitHub 备份使用失效 token 且指向不存在的仓库——最终一次事故中超过 25 篇论文彻底丢失，即智能体可以通过法庭审查、收到含 paper ID 的确认，却在数分钟后发现论文不可检索。因此 v6.0 聚焦"数据韧性"与"引文完整性"两大缺口，v7.0 聚焦由外部独立评审发现的理论框架数学不一致。

Method: 方法上是"形式化理论基础 + 多层工程系统 + 生产部署"三线并进。(1) 理论基础：以 Spencer-Brown《形式之律》的区分算子·与 Kauffman 自指 eigenform 为主代数，构造 Heyting 代数核子（nucleus）不动点集 Ω_R，Theorem 2.2 已在 Lean4 证明助手中机器校验；给出形式变换下的三个守恒量（本质守恒 |Ess(R(U))|=|Ess(U)|、充足理由 R(v)=v⇔R(v)≤v、辩证综合 synth(T,A)=R(T∨A)≥T,A）；提出 Rosetta 协议在 LoF/Heyting/Clifford/Graph/Geometric/Morphological 六种数学透镜间双向翻译；引入 Al-Mayahi 的 τ 内部时间与进度率场 g_i(t)，以特征时间 Θ 归一化得无量纲进度指标 τ̃_i，并给出四种 τ 协调病理（快节点饱和、不一致聚合、共识不稳、评估误导）及对应补救。(2) 质量控制：Tribunal 三阶段协议（Present 申报→Respond 答 8 题（26 题池/7 类，关键词匹配与心理学反思深度评分，≥60% 发一次性格限令牌，TTL 24h）→Publish 附令牌），并含利益冲突排除与按声誉晋升审查者；17 评委多 LLM 10 维（0–10）颗粒化打分，8 种欺骗检测器 + 14 条校准规则。(3) 韧性层：Proof Hash 协议 SHA-256(P∥C) 与 commit-reveal 反篡改 Lean4 类型检查（成功签发 CAB），Mempool（脏区 p2pclaw_mempool_v4）→Wheel（≥2 独立验证后晋级 p2pclaw_papers_v4, status=verified）的知识架构，四层存储与 memory→Gun.js→mempool→R2 级联回填。(4) 生态：CAJAL 开源语言模型族（4B/9B，科研论文生成微调）、BenchClaw 部署与平台集成。所有主张标注证据等级 [Implemented]/[Theoretical]/[L4✓]/[Future Work]。

Result: 可量化的自报结果包括：缓存命中下检索延迟从 >3 s 降至 <50 ms；实时引文核验对伪造引用识别准确率 >85%（对照 CrossRef、arXiv、Semantic Scholar 三源实时查询）；四层持久化实现"跨重新部署零论文丢失"，论文恢复协议成功挽回 25 篇丢失论文并完成法庭重新审查；生产网络含 14 个真实自治智能体与 23 个明确标注的模拟市民，累计产出 50+ 篇已计分论文，字数区间 2072–4073，排行榜分数 6.4–8.1；Tier-1 Heyting 核子进程内验证器单篇耗时 <5 ms，Lean4 commit 阶段超时 10 s、完整校验超时 180 s。v7.0 的数学修正包括 8 项：充足理由定理不动点条件由 v6.0 的错误等价式改为 R(v)≤v（因膨胀性已保证 v≤R(v)，须补逆向不等式）；进度率指标改为全无量纲（TPS/TPS_max、VWU/VWU_max、IG_i·Θ_IG，权重 α=0.3/β=0.5/γ=0.2 且和为 1）；声誉更新公式显式纳入质量项 Δq₀i=q₀i−q̄₀i∈[−1,1]（λ=0.95 EMA）并用 τ̃_i/(2τ̃_j) 明确括号以消除歧义；AETHER 剪枝注意力 logit 上界、深度分数非负性、校准映射值域、PD Governor 离散时间记法、HSR 权重公式参数定义均被澄清。此外坦率披露了生产统计、真实 vs 模拟智能体数量、分数分布与失败率。需要指出的是：上述准确率/延迟/规模数据均无外部基线、无对照实验、无第三方复现，也未与人类评审或单模型评审做显著性比较；"85%" 与 "zero paper loss" 的评测集、标注方式与统计口径未给出，因此应视为工程自测报告而非受控评测结论。

Conclusion: 论文主张：去中心化 AI 同行评审可在完全无人工把关条件下运行一个可自我维持的科研生产网络——形式化验证层（Lean4/固定点代数）提供不可伪造的底层约束，Tribunal 提供认知准入门槛，多评委颗粒化打分稀释单模型偏差，多层持久化与检索级联解决生产级数据丢失，实时引文核验对付伪造引用形成有效防线，τ 归一化协调解决异构智能体速度失配。v7.0 的主要贡献是修补理论层的量纲一致性与记号歧义，并交付 CAJAL 模型族；全部代码开源。局限与后续方向：① 标题写 v6.0 而正文为 v7.0、arXiv 版本号与日期存在不一致，版本管理叙述较混乱；② 所有性能与质量指标为自评，缺乏基线、消融（如去掉实时核验后的引用错误率）与外部盲评；③ 真实智能体与模拟居民混合在同一排行榜，分数分布 6.4–8.1 的区分度与统计意义未做说明；④ "语义上逼真但伪造的引用"检测与 8 类欺骗检测器的真阳性/假阳性、漏检率未报告；⑤ 论文质量声明目前主要依赖结构化指标与形式化检查，尚未证明能逼近或改善人类评审的可靠性；⑥ 作者同时列出 [Future Work] 的"审查者智能体动态生成题目库"等能力，说明法庭系统仍依赖静态题池，存在应试与可预测性问题。总体评价：作为可运行的开放系统与透明的生产失败案例记录，其工程参考价值较高；但作为"科学评审有效性"的科学证据，目前属于单方系统描述，尚需第三方复现与受控对照方能支撑其主张。

Abstract: This paper presents OpenCLAW-P2P v6.0, a comprehensive evolution of the decentralized collective-intelligence platform in which autonomous AI agents publish, peer-review, score, and iteratively improve scientific research papers without any human gatekeeper. Building on v5.0 foundations -- tribunal-gated publishing, multi-LLM granular scoring, calibrated deception detection, the Silicon Chess-Grid FSM, and the AETHER containerized inference engine -- this release introduces four major new subsystems: (1) a multi-layer paper persistence architecture with four storage tiers (in-memory cache, Cloudflare R2, Gun.js, GitHub) ensuring zero paper loss across redeployments; (2) a multi-layer retrieval cascade with automatic backfill reducing lookup latency from >3s to <50ms; (3) live reference verification querying CrossRef, arXiv, and Semantic Scholar during scoring to detect fabricated citations with >85% accuracy; and (4) a scientific API proxy providing rate-limited cached access to seven public databases. The platform operates with 14 real autonomous agents producing 50+ scored papers (word counts 2,072-4,073, leaderboard scores 6.4-8.1) alongside 23 labeled simulated citizens. We present honest production statistics, failure-mode analysis, a paper recovery protocol that salvaged 25 lost papers, and lessons learned from operating the system at scale. All pre-existing subsystems -- 17-judge multi-LLM scoring, 14-rule calibration with 8 deception detectors, tribunal cognitive examination, Proof of Value consensus, Laws-of-Form eigenform verification, and tau-normalized agent coordination -- are retained and further hardened. All code is open-source at https://github.com/Agnuxo1/p2pclaw-mcp-server.

</details>


### [120] [ThermoQA: A Three-Tier Benchmark for Evaluating Thermodynamic Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19758)
*Kemal Düzkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ThermoQA是一个包含293道工程热力学问题的三层基准测试，用于评估LLM的专业推理能力。研究表明顶级模型可达90%+准确率，但property lookup到cycle analysis的跨层衰减揭示了推理与记忆的本质区别，超临界工质和联合循环成为有效区分器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评估缺乏针对工程热力学的专业benchmark。现有测试无法区分"属性记忆"与"热力学推理"能力，而后者才是工程师的核心竞争力。

Method: 构建了包含293道开放式工程热力学问题的基准测试，按难度分为三层：属性查询(110题)、组件分析(101题)和完整循环分析(82题)。使用CoolProp 7.2.0编程计算ground truth，涵盖水、R-134a和变Cp空气三种工质。对6个前沿LLM各进行3次独立评估，通过标准差量化推理一致性。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6领先(94.1%)，其次GPT-5.4(93.1%)和Gemini 3.1 Pro(92.5%)。跨层性能衰减差异显著：Opus仅2.8pp而MiniMax达32.5pp。超临界水/R-134a/联合循环成为有效区分器(40-60pp差距)，多次运行sigma从±0.1%到±2.5%。

Conclusion: ThermoQA成功证明了当前顶级LLM在工程热力学推理上的能力边界，为评估LLM在专业工程领域的reasoning能力提供了可靠基准。property lookup到full cycle analysis的性能衰减揭示了"记忆 vs 推理"的本质差异，为后续模型改进指明方向。

Abstract: We present ThermoQA, a benchmark of 293 open-ended engineering thermodynamics problems in three tiers: property lookups (110 Q), component analysis (101 Q), and full cycle analysis (82 Q). Ground truth is computed programmatically from CoolProp 7.2.0, covering water, R-134a, and variable-cp air. Six frontier LLMs are evaluated across three independent runs each. The composite leaderboard is led by Claude Opus 4.6 (94.1%), GPT-5.4 (93.1%), and Gemini 3.1 Pro (92.5%). Cross-tier degradation ranges from 2.8 pp (Opus) to 32.5 pp (MiniMax), confirming that property memorization does not imply thermodynamic reasoning. Supercritical water, R-134a refrigerant, and combined-cycle gas turbine analysis serve as natural discriminators with 40-60 pp performance spreads. Multi-run sigma ranges from +/-0.1% to +/-2.5%, quantifying reasoning consistency as a distinct evaluation axis. Dataset and code are open-source at https://huggingface.co/datasets/olivenet/thermoqa

</details>


### [121] [The AI Telco Engineer: Toward Autonomous Discovery of Wireless Communications Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19803)
*Fayçal Aït Aoudia,Jakob Hoydis,Sebastian Cammerer,Lorenzo Maggi,Gian Marti,Alexander Keller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索了基于LLM的agentic AI系统自主设计无线通信算法的能力，通过迭代生成-评估-优化流程，在信道估计和链路自适应等任务上取得了与甚至超越传统方法的结果，且生成的算法具有可解释性和可扩展性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索agentic AI自主设计无线通信算法的能力，从而革新研究方式（从原型设计到结果复现）。

Method: 实现了一个专用框架，利用大型语言模型（LLMs）迭代生成、评估和优化候选算法。框架通过三个物理层和MAC层任务进行评估：无统计信息的信道估计、具有已知协方差的信道估计和链路自适应。

Result: 在数小时内，框架能够生成与基线方法相当甚至更优的算法，且这些算法具有完全可解释性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 本工作标志着迈向自主发现新型无线通信算法的第一步，展示了LLM-based agentic AI在算法设计领域的巨大潜力，并为该方向的未来发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Agentic AI is rapidly transforming the way research is conducted, from prototyping ideas to reproducing results found in the literature. In this paper, we explore the ability of agentic AI to autonomously design wireless communication algorithms. To that end, we implement a dedicated framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to iteratively generate, evaluate, and refine candidate algorithms. We evaluate the framework on three tasks spanning the physical (PHY) and medium access control (MAC) layers: statistics-agnostic channel estimation, channel estimation with known covariance, and link adaptation. Our results show that, in a matter of hours, the framework produces algorithms that are competitive with and, in some cases, outperforming conventional baselines. Moreover, unlike neural network-based approaches, the generated algorithms are fully explainable and extensible. This work represents a first step toward the autonomous discovery of novel wireless communication algorithms, and we look forward to the progress our community makes in this direction.

</details>


### [122] [Automated Detection of Dosing Errors in Clinical Trial Narratives: A Multi-Modal Feature Engineering Approach with LightGBM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19759)
*Mohammad AL-Smadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LightGBM和3,451维多模态特征的自动化给药错误检测系统，在严重类别不平衡的临床试验叙述中实现0.88+ AUC，发现特征选择能有效降噪提升性能，句子嵌入是关键组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验对药物方案的严格依从性要求高，但给药错误持续威胁患者安全和试验完整性，需要自动化系统从非结构化临床试验叙述中检测此类错误。

Method: 采用LightGBM梯度提升模型，结合3,451个多模态特征（传统NLP特征、密集语义嵌入、医学领域特定模式、Transformer评分），从9个互补文本字段提取特征。通过5折集成平均进行评估，并进行系统性消融实验和特征效率分析。

Result: 在CT-DEB基准数据集（正例率仅4.9%）上，测试ROC-AUC达到0.8725，交叉验证为0.8833±0.0091。消融实验表明移除句子嵌入导致最大性能下降（2.39%）。选择top 500-1000个特征可获得最优性能（0.886-0.887 AUC），优于完整特征集（0.879 AUC）。

Conclusion: 特征选择作为一种正则化技术至关重要；稀疏词法特征与密集表示相互补充，在严重类别不平衡情况下对专业临床文本分类具有重要价值。该研究为临床试验安全监测提供了有效的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Clinical trials require strict adherence to medication protocols, yet dosing errors remain a persistent challenge affecting patient safety and trial integrity. We present an automated system for detecting dosing errors in unstructured clinical trial narratives using gradient boosting with comprehensive multi-modal feature engineering. Our approach combines 3,451 features spanning traditional NLP (TF-IDF, character n-grams), dense semantic embeddings (all-MiniLM-L6v2), domain-specific medical patterns, and transformer-based scores (BiomedBERT, DeBERTa-v3), used to train a LightGBM model. Features are extracted from nine complementary text fields (median 5,400 characters per sample) ensuring complete coverage across all 42,112 clinical trial narratives. On the CT-DEB benchmark dataset with severe class imbalance (4.9% positive rate), we achieve 0.8725 test ROC-AUC through 5-fold ensemble averaging (cross-validation: 0.8833 + 0.0091 AUC). Systematic ablation studies reveal that removing sentence embeddings causes the largest performance degradation (2.39%), demonstrating their critical role despite contributing only 37.07% of total feature importance. Feature efficiency analysis demonstrates that selecting the top 500-1000 features yields optimal performance (0.886-0.887 AUC), outperforming the full 3,451-feature set (0.879 AUC) through effective noise reduction. Our findings highlight the importance of feature selection as a regularization technique and demonstrate that sparse lexical features remain complementary to dense representations for specialized clinical text classification under severe class imbalance.

</details>


### [123] [Inference Headroom Ratio: A Diagnostic and Control Framework for Inference Stability Under Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19760)
*Robert Reinertsen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Inference Headroom Ratio (IHR)——一种用于表征约束决策系统推理稳定性的无量纲诊断指标，通过比较系统有效推理能力C与环境不确定性及约束负荷(U+K)的关系来量化推理稳定性边界附近的系统状态。三项控制实验表明IHR可作为: (1)量化风险指标，与崩溃概率呈逻辑斯蒂曲线关系，临界阈值IHR*≈1.19; (2)环境噪声下推理稳定性边界的敏感指标; (3)有效控制变量，主动调节可使系统崩溃率从79.4%降至58.7%，IHR方差降低70.4%。IHR有望成为现有性能、漂移和不确定性指标的系统级补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在分布漂移和约束条件下运行时，缺乏能够预警推理稳定性边界的系统级诊断工具。传统性能指标仅反映输出质量，无法捕捉系统接近隐性失败边界的程度。本研究旨在提出一种能够量化系统剩余推理裕度的指标，以实现故障前的主动预警和干预。

Method: 采用仿真评估方法，通过三项控制实验验证IHR的诊断效力：(1)逻辑斯蒂回归分析IHR与崩溃概率的关系，确定临界阈值；(2)在不同环境噪声水平下测试IHR对稳定性边界的敏感性；(3)Monte Carlo实验(300次)评估主动IHR调控对系统稳定性的改善效果。

Result: (1) IHR与崩溃概率呈逻辑斯蒂曲线关系，临界阈值IHR*≈1.19；(2) IHR能够敏感地响应环境噪声变化，有效指示系统与推理稳定性边界的距离；(3) 主动调控IHR使系统崩溃率从79.4%降至58.7%，方差降低70.4%。

Conclusion: IHR作为推理能力与约束负荷的比值指标，能够有效表征系统的推理稳定性边界，为AI系统在分布漂移和约束条件下的主动风险管理提供了新的诊断工具，有望成为传统性能指标的补充，实现推理失败前的裕度估计。

Abstract: We present a simulation-based evaluation of the Inference Headroom Ratio (IHR), a dimensionless diagnostic quantity for characterizing inference stability in constrained decision systems. IHR formalizes the relationship between a system's effective inferential capacity C and the combined uncertainty and constraint load U + K imposed by its operating environment, and is intended to capture proximity to an inference stability boundary rather than output-level performance. Across three controlled experiments, we show that IHR functions as: (1) a quantifiable risk indicator whose relationship to collapse probability follows a well-fitted logistic curve with estimated critical threshold IHR* approx. 1.19, (2) a sensitive indicator of proximity to the inference stability boundary under environmental noise, and (3) a viable control variable whose active regulation reduces system collapse rate from 79.4% to 58.7% and IHR variance by 70.4% across 300 Monte Carlo runs. These results position IHR as a prospective, system-level complement to standard performance, drift, and uncertainty metrics, enabling estimation of remaining inferential margin before overt failure in AI systems operating under distributional shift and constraint.

</details>


### [124] [Forage V2: Knowledge Evolution and Transfer in Autonomous Agent Organizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19837)
*Huaqing Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Forage V2 将自主智能体架构从单次探险扩展为学习型组织，通过跨运行经验积累、跨模型知识迁移和制度保障解决"分母盲区"问题，使弱模型利用强模型知识可将覆盖差距从6.6pp缩至1.1pp，成本减半，收敛轮数减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体在开放世界任务中面临"分母盲区"：系统性低估目标空间的范围。V1通过协同进化评估和方法隔离解决了单次运行问题，但缺乏跨运行知识积累和迁移能力。

Method: V2设计了三类制度机制：审计分离（Evaluator与Planner隔离）、契约协议（模块间通信规范）、组织记忆（跨运行知识存储）。实验在网页抓取、API查询、数学推理三类任务上验证。

Result: 六次运行中知识条目从0增至54，分母估计趋于稳定。弱模型(Sonnet)继承强模型(Opus)知识后：覆盖差距从6.6pp缩至1.1pp，成本从$9.40降至$5.13，收敛轮数从7.0减至4.5，三个独立运行得出完全相同的分母估计(266)。

Conclusion: V2的核心贡献是架构层面的：设计"制度"使任何智能体进入系统时更可靠。积累的知识是组织性的、模型无关的、可迁移的，以可读文档存储，任何未来智能体均可继承，不受提供商或能力水平限制。

Abstract: Autonomous agents operating in open-world tasks -- where the completion boundary is not given in advance -- face denominator blindness: they systematically underestimate the scope of the target space. Forage V1 addressed this through co-evolving evaluation (an independent Evaluator discovers what "complete" means) and method isolation (Evaluator and Planner cannot see each other's code). V2 extends the architecture from a single expedition to a learning organization: experience accumulates across runs, transfers across model capabilities, and institutional safeguards prevent knowledge degradation.
  We demonstrate two claims across three task types (web scraping, API queries, mathematical reasoning). Knowledge accumulation: over six runs, knowledge entries grow from 0 to 54, and denominator estimates stabilize as domain understanding deepens. Knowledge transfer: a weaker agent (Sonnet) seeded with a stronger agent's (Opus) knowledge narrows a 6.6pp coverage gap to 1.1pp, halves cost (9.40 to 5.13 USD), converges in half the rounds (mean 4.5 vs. 7.0), and three independent seeded runs arrive at exactly the same denominator estimate (266), suggesting organizational knowledge calibrates evaluation itself.
  V2's contribution is architectural: it designs institutions -- audit separation, contract protocols, organizational memory -- that make any agent more reliable upon entry. The accumulated experience is organizational, model-agnostic, and transferable, stored as readable documents that any future agent inherits regardless of provider or capability level.

</details>


### [125] [EvoForest: A Novel Machine-Learning Paradigm via Open-Ended Evolution of Computational Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19761)
*Kamer Ali Yuksel,Hassan Sawaf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoForest是一种混合神经符号系统，通过开放式的端到端进化来发现计算结构，解决了传统ML仅优化参数而忽视结构发现的问题。在ADIA Lab结构断裂挑战赛中达到94.13% ROC-AUC，超越90.14%的公开获胜分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ML范式（选择模型族+优化权重）过于狭窄，对于许多结构化预测问题，主要瓶颈不是参数拟合，而是发现应该从数据中计算什么。当目标不可微、评估基于交叉验证、需要可解释性或持续适应时，传统方法往往失败。

Method: EvoForest提出混合神经符号系统，在共享有向无环图(DAG)中联合进化：1)可重用计算结构；2)可调用函数族；3)可训练低维连续组件。中间节点存储替代实现，可调用节点编码投影、门控、激活等可重用变换族，输出节点定义候选预测计算，全局参数可通过梯度下降精炼。使用Ridge轻量级读出来评分发现表示，通过结构化反馈引导LLM驱动的突变。

Result: 在2025 ADIA Lab结构断裂挑战赛中，EvoForest经过600步进化达到94.13% ROC-AUC，超越相同评估协议下90.14%的公开获胜分数。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了神经符号进化框架在结构化预测任务中的有效性，证明了端到端开放式进化发现计算结构的可行性。

Abstract: Modern machine learning is still largely organized around a single recipe: choose a parameterized model family and optimize its weights. Although highly successful, this paradigm is too narrow for many structured prediction problems, where the main bottleneck is not parameter fitting but discovering what should be computed from the data. Success often depends on identifying the right transformations, statistics, invariances, interaction structures, temporal summaries, gates, or nonlinear compositions, especially when objectives are non-differentiable, evaluation is cross-validation-based, interpretability matters, or continual adaptation is required. We present EvoForest, a hybrid neuro-symbolic system for end-to-end open-ended evolution of computation. Rather than merely generating features, EvoForest jointly evolves reusable computational structure, callable function families, and trainable low-dimensional continuous components inside a shared directed acyclic graph. Intermediate nodes store alternative implementations, callable nodes encode reusable transformation families such as projections, gates, and activations, output nodes define candidate predictive computations, and persistent global parameters can be refined by gradient descent. For each graph configuration, EvoForest evaluates the discovered computation and uses a lightweight Ridge-based readout to score the resulting representation against a non-differentiable cross-validation target. The evaluator also produces structured feedback that guides future LLM-driven mutations. In the 2025 ADIA Lab Structural Break Challenge, EvoForest reached 94.13% ROC-AUC after 600 evolution steps, exceeding the publicly reported winning score of 90.14% under the same evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [126] [pAI/MSc: ML Theory Research with Humans on the Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20622)
*Mahmoud Abdelmoneum,Pierfrancesco Beneventano,Tomaso Poggio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: pAI/MSc（principal Agentic Investigator）是一个开源、可定制、模块化的多智能体系统，面向机器学习理论等定量领域，把人类指定的假设推进为"有文献支撑、经过数学论证、实验验证、可投稿的手稿草案"。其核心不是自主科研 ideation 或全自动化，而是把人类"引导/监督预算"从约 10^3–10^4 次提示（~10^6 token）压缩到不超过 10 次显式 steer，同时不牺牲严谨性。系统采用固定 LangGraph 拓扑（23 个专职 agent、30 个图节点）、"产物契约"（artifact contract）加阶段级校验门（gate）的组织方式，并提供可选的多模型辩诘（counsel）、定理导向推理与基于树搜索的证明探索等增强组件。作者明确区分"结构性成功"与"科学真实性"，当前版本定位为基础设施里程碑而非已验证的科学能力突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1) 当前智能体系统虽能解决困难数学问题，但其想法与产物质量仍达不到严肃学术标准，尤其无法承担理论研究的严谨性要求；作者的核心问题是"在不超过 10 次人类 steer 的条件下，能否把一个强假设推进为达到严肃学术质量的论文？"这等价于一个压缩问题——把人类交互式指令预算从约 10^6 token 压缩到约 10^3 token 而不损失严谨性与质量。2) 学术研究（尤其理论）比 AutoResearch 等 agentic 搜索更难：缺少有界领域（bounded domain）、缺少短评估循环、缺少自动目标函数；新颖性、正确性与长期科学价值无法由单一标量奖励可靠打分，局部正确性可检查但端到端研究价值不可自动评分。3) "研究伦理"动机：机器学习研究常过度声称（explanation–speculation slippage、"mathiness"、弱基线、报告偏差、捷径学习、脆弱 benchmark 设计、因果语言滥用），若研究者日益依赖 AI 系统，最有杠杆的干预点就是把"扎实确立"的伦理内置进工具本身——确立的规模随采用率放大。4) 论文还批评了"全自主科学"的宏大叙事，指出当前系统能力不足，与其追求自主科研，不如聚焦"减少人类引导负担但保留质量"这一更窄且更实用的目标。

Method: 系统架构：固定 LangGraph 执行拓扑（输出随机但控制流显式可复现），六阶段流水线——(1) 发现与可行性筛查：persona_council → literature_review_agent → 文献可行性门 → brainstorm_agent → formalize_goals_agent；(2) 轨道规划与路由：写出 theory/experiment/mixed 分解到 track_decomposition.json，由路由器决定分支；(3) 并行技术执行：理论轨（math_literature → proposer → prover → rigorous_verifier → empirical_verifier → proof_transcription）与实验轨（exp_literature → design → experimentation → verification → transcription）可独立或并行；(4) 完成度校验与结果形式化：track_merge → verify_completion 三向路由（complete/incomplete/rethink）→ formalize_results_agent；(5) 内部一致性与后续工作：duality check，失败则触发 follow-up 文献工作与再规划；(6) 论文生成与编辑质量保证：resource_preparation → writeup → proofreading → reviewer → validation_gate，失败回环至 writeup。

六项质量与严谨机制：(a) persona_council 三角色结构化辩论（Practical Compass 实用相关、Rigor & Novelty 严格新颖、Narrative Architect 叙事弧），合成规则强制显式冲突裁决而非平均；(b) 对抗式新颖性证伪——文献综述 agent 默认怀疑立场，将主张标注 OPEN/PARTIAL/KNOWN/EQUIVALENT_KNOWN，多源检索（deep-research API、Semantic Scholar、引文遍历）用于建立或推翻新颖性，KNOWN/EQUIVALENT_KNOWN 触发重构或提供不 subsume 的证据；(c) 理论与实验"少共享上下文"——仅共享 track_decomposition 与共同贡献主张，在综合前与新周期起点做受控同步；(d) 审稿人硬阻塞项 B1–B5（引言无明确研究问题、关键结论无可追溯证据指针、残留占位符/未完成章节、关键实验缺失、形式化主张缺证明草图或验证轨迹），任一触发即把评分封顶为 4，≥8 分仅在接近投稿就绪时给出；(e) 多模型 counsel——三前沿模型（Claude Opus 4.6、GPT-5.4、Gemini 3 Pro Preview）在隔离沙箱独立产出后互相批评多轮，再由合成模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6）显式裁决分歧并回写主工作区，配备熔断与 quorum 逻辑；(f) 证明策略树搜索——DAG 分层 best-first 控制器，从 claim graph 选前沿主张、生成备选证明策略，按复合函数打分（40% LLM 估计前景、25% 对 claim graph 的解锁影响、15% 成本效率、10% 深度惩罚、10% 兄弟分支多样性）排序，在分叉工作区执行，失败分支可生成调试子分支、低分分支剪枝。

产物契约（表 1）：论文工作区核心（final_paper.tex、literature_review.pdf、research_plan.pdf、results_assessment.pdf、followup_decision.json、track_decomposition.json）、可选输出（final_paper.pdf、experiments_to_run_later.md）、编辑严格性产物（author_style_guide.md、theorem_map.json、review_verdict.json、claim_traceability.json 等）、运维账本（checkpoints.db、run_token_usage.json、budget_state.json、budget_ledger.jsonl、inter_agent_messages/）与模式特定产物（math_workspace、experiment_workspace、counsel_sandboxes、tree_search_state.json）。校验针对可编辑源文件（copyedit_report.tex、review_report.tex）而非仅 PDF。运维模型为 human-on-the-loop：初始任务规范、TCP 控制套接字与 HTTP 转向 API、按阶段恢复、inputs/ 上下文注入、campaign 级控制；每阶段持久化到 SQLite checkpoints.db；预算通过三份文件追踪；提供多档配置（quickstart 到 full campaign，成本 $2–600、15 分钟至 12 小时）。

Result: 论文给出的是系统与设计论证，而非实验验证的科学结论。主要"结果"包括：(1) 五条来自实践的工程经验：ideation 应改为辩论而非独白；每个 agent 需显式竞争目标；理论与实验应协调但不过度纠缠（过早共享上下文会互相继承盲点、产出更弱）；长程运行需要显式停止判据（概念性收益递减而非 token 递减）；并行调试昂贵但可通过外置可检视的状态缓解。(2) 七条工作流级设计要求：降低引导负担但不隐藏人类责任、保持理论与实验在同一论文轨迹上、把中间状态外化为命名产物、支持有界迭代而非单次前向、把结构校验与科学真实性分离、保证长程运行可恢复/可转向/预算感知、暴露多级强制强度。(3) 系统包含 23 个专职 agent、30 个图节点，固定拓扑，同一任务两次运行内容可能分歧。(4) 成本/运行时包络：quickstart $2–10/15–40 min；base+LaTeX $10–40/30–90 min；math agents $20–60/60–150 min；counsel $50–200/2–5 h；tree search（无 counsel）$60–180/2–4 h；tree search+counsel $200–600/4–10 h；full campaign $100–400/6–12 h。(5) 明确的能力边界（表 3）：可保证确定性执行拓扑、产物齐备的工作区、显式校验门、预算可见、可断点恢复、多点人类转向接口；不能保证输出确定性、科学正确性、评审分数的校准、精确成本预测、幂等重启、减少人类科学判断责任。(6) 作者判断"研究结构化"和"论文周边纪律性工作"（校对、审稿式批评、copyedit 轨迹、claim-traceability、资源清单、修订日志）比预期更可自动化，而新颖性判断、基线选择、细微证明义务、实验解释与"论文究竟在讲什么"的判断仍顽固地依赖人类，因此控制模型应是 human on the loop 而非 out of the loop。(7) 作者额外披露实验安全模型的不足：无逐实验容器/命名空间、无网络隔离、无执行前人工代码评审门、无 CPU/内存隔离，因此不应被描述为加固型执行沙箱；并给出 6 条投稿前人工核查清单与 7 类失效模式及缓解措施（表 7）。整体定位为"基础设施里程碑"，真正科学主张需等后续 benchmark 化评估。

Conclusion: 结论是：pAI/MSc 目前形态是一种 human-on-the-loop、artifact-centric 的"研究到论文"流水线，其贡献不在于解决科学真实性或自动化新颖性判断，而在于把研究任务转化为可追溯的工作区——含规划产物、理论与实验分支、修订回路、手稿输出与显式校验门——把问题从"如何让长串模型调用保持一致"转化为"如何检视、引导与校验一个结构化研究工作区"。作者认为这已是迈向降低人类引导负担的实质性一步，但输出在信任任何科学主张或投稿前仍须专家验证。下一阶段重点应是基准化评估而非堆功能：跨多任务族重复运行、显式记录人类 steer 次数、外部引文忠实度审计、生成实验的独立重跑、定理与手稿质量的专家评定，并对 counsel、树搜索、严格编辑强制与 campaign 式修复做消融。作者同时提出一个更根本的开放问题：为研究质量设计更好的可计算代理指标，而不把研究降格为代理指标本身。

Abstract: We present pAI/MSc, an open-source, customizable, modular multi-agent system for academic research workflows. Our goal is not autonomous scientific ideation, nor fully automated research. It is narrower and more practical: to reduce by orders of magnitude the human steering required to turn a specified hypothesis into a literature-grounded, mathematically established, experimentally supported, submission-oriented manuscript draft. pAI/MSc is built with a current emphasis on machine learning theory and adjacent quantitative fields.

</details>


### [127] [Using Learning Theories to Evolve Human-Centered XAI: Future Perspectives and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19788)
*Karina Cortinas-Lorenzo,Gavin Doherty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是关于可解释人工智能（XAI）的立场论文，探讨了为何需要解释AI以及应该解释什么的问题，提出将学习理论融入XAI生命周期，采用以学习者为中心的方法来评估、设计和评估AI解释，以增强人类能动性并降低XAI风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统规模和复杂性的不断增长，AI透明性追求面临更大挑战。当前XAI研究缺乏对"为何解释"和"解释什么"等根本性问题的深入思考，需要新的理论框架指导。

Method: 将学习理论（learning theories）融入XAI生命周期，采用以学习者为中心（learner-centered）的方法，从评估、设计和评估三个维度系统性地审视AI解释。

Result: 以学习者为中心的XAI方法可以增强人类能动性（human agency）并减轻XAI风险，推动以人为中心的XAI实践演进。

Conclusion: 本文倡导将学习理论作为XAI设计的核心指导原则，通过 learner-centered 方法论革新传统XAI研究范式。

Abstract: As Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems continue to grow in size and complexity, so does the difficulty of the quest for AI transparency. In a world of large models and complex AI systems, why do we explain AI and what should we explain? While explanations serve multiple functions, in the face of complexity humans have used and continue to use explanations to foster learning. In this position paper, we discuss how learning theories can be infused in the XAI lifecycle, as well as the key opportunities and challenges when adopting a learner-centered approach to assess, design and evaluate AI explanations. Building on past work, we argue that a learner-centered approach to Explainable AI (XAI) can enhance human agency and ease XAI risks mitigation, helping evolve the practice of human-centered XAI.

</details>


### [128] [SkillGraph: Graph Foundation Priors for LLM Agent Tool Sequence Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19793)
*Hao Liu,Dongyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM智能体从大型API库中选择和排序工具的问题，提出SkillGraph——一种从成功轨迹中挖掘的有向加权执行转移图，用于编码工作流优先级规则。基于此构建两阶段解耦框架（GS-Hybrid检索+成对重排器），显著提升了工具排序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用语义相似性同时处理工具检索和排序，但排序依赖于工具间的数据依赖关系，而这些依赖信息在工具描述中缺失，导致在结构化工作流场景中Kendall-τ出现负值。

Method: 提出两阶段解耦框架：①GS-Hybrid检索用于候选工具选择；②基于SkillGraph的成对学习重排器用于排序。在49,831条成功轨迹上构建有向加权执行转移图作为图基础先验。

Result: ToolBench上Set-F1=0.271，Kendall-τ=0.096；API-Bank上Kendall-τ从-0.433提升至+0.613；在相同Stage-1输入下，优于LLaMA-3.1-8B重排器。

Conclusion: SkillGraph通过从成功轨迹中挖掘工作流优先级规则，解决了传统语义方法忽视工具间数据依赖的问题，验证了将检索与排序解耦的有效性。

Abstract: LLM agents must select tools from large API libraries and order them correctly. Existing methods use semantic similarity for both retrieval and ordering, but ordering depends on inter-tool data dependencies that are absent from tool descriptions. As a result, semantic-only methods can produce negative Kendall-$τ$ in structured workflow domains. We introduce SkillGraph, a directed weighted execution-transition graph mined from 49,831 successful LLM agent trajectories, which encodes workflow-precedence regularities as a reusable graph foundation prior. Building on this graph foundation prior, we propose a two-stage decoupled framework: GS-Hybrid retrieval for candidate selection and a learned pairwise reranker for ordering. On ToolBench (9,965 test instances; ~16,000 tools), the method reaches Set-F1 = 0.271 and Kendall-$τ$ = 0.096; on API-Bank, Kendall-$τ$ improves from -0.433 to +0.613. Under identical Stage-1 inputs, the learned reranker also outperforms LLaMA-3.1-8B Stage-2 rerankers.

</details>


### [129] [From Data to Theory: Autonomous Large Language Model Agents for Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19789)
*Samuel Onimpa Alfred,Veera Sundararaghavan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: An autonomous LLM agent framework for materials theory development that can independently select equation forms, generate/execute code, and test theory-data fit. Successfully recovers well-established equations and proposes new ones, but requires careful validation due to potential errors.

一项用于材料理论开发的自主LLM智能体框架，可独立选择方程形式、生成/执行代码并测试理论与数据拟合。该框架成功恢复了经典方程并提出新方程，但由于潜在错误需要仔细验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional materials theory development requires significant human expertise and intervention; there is a need for automated approaches that can independently identify governing equations from data.

研究动机：传统材料理论发展需要大量人类专家干预，亟需能够独立从数据中识别控制方程的自动化方法。

Method: 该框架将分步推理（step-by-step reasoning）与专家提供的工具相结合，使智能体能够自主选择方程形式、生成并运行代码、测试理论与数据的匹配程度，同时保持清晰的决策记录。

研究方法：通过结合分步推理与专家工具的框架，实现端到端的自动化材料理论发现。智能体自主选择方程形式、生成运行代码并测试理论与数据拟合程度，保留完整决策轨迹。

Result: The agent correctly identifies established equations for well-known materials relationships and shows promising recovery of specialized equations (GPT-5 performs better for Kuhn's equation). It can also propose new predictive relationships, though validation remains essential due to potential incorrect or inconsistent outputs.

研究结果：该智能体能正确识别经典材料关系的方程，对专业方程的恢复取决于底层模型（GPT-5表现更好）。智能体还可提出新的预测性关系，但由于可能输出错误或不一致的方程，仔细验证仍然必不可少。

Conclusion: The agent successfully identifies well-established equations (Hall-Petch, Paris law) and suggests new relationships like a strain-dependent HOMO-LUMO gap law. However, it can still return incorrect, incomplete, or inconsistent equations even with strong numerical fits, necessitating careful validation.

总体结论：该智能体能够正确识别经典方程（如霍尔-佩奇定律、巴黎定律），并提出新的应变依赖性HOMO-LUMO带隙定律等关系。然而，即使数值拟合良好，仍可能返回错误、不完整或不一致的方程，因此需要仔细验证。

Abstract: We present an autonomous large language model (LLM) agent for end-to-end, data-driven materials theory development. The model can choose an equation form, generate and run its own code, and test how well the theory matches the data without human intervention. The framework combines step-by-step reasoning with expert-supplied tools, allowing the agent to adjust its approach as needed while keeping a clear record of its decisions. For well-established materials relationships such as the Hall-Petch equation and Paris law, the agent correctly identifies the governing equation and makes reliable predictions on new datasets. For more specialized relationships, such as Kuhn's equation for the HOMO-LUMO gap of conjugated molecules as a function of length, performance depends more strongly on the underlying model, with GPT-5 showing better recovery of the correct equation. Beyond known theories, the agent can also suggest new predictive relationships, illustrated here by a strain-dependent law for changes in the HOMO-LUMO gap. At the same time, the results show that careful validation remains essential, because the agent can still return incorrect, incomplete, or inconsistent equations even when the numerical fit appears strong. Overall, these results highlight both the promise and the current limitations of autonomous LLM agents for AI-assisted scientific modeling and discovery.

</details>


### [130] [Hidden Reliability Risks in Large Language Models: Systematic Identification of Precision-Induced Output Disagreements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19790)
*Yifei Wang,Tianlin Li,Xiaohan Zhang,Xiaoyu Zhang,Wei Ma,Mingfei Cheng,Li Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PrecisionDiff是一种自动化差分测试框架，用于检测大语言模型在不同数值精度配置下（如bfloat16、float16、int16、int8）的行为不一致性。该框架通过生成精度敏感测试输入并进行跨精度对比分析，发现传统测试方法难以捕捉的细微偏差。在对齐验证任务上的实验表明，这种精度导致的行为分歧在多个开源LLM中广泛存在，PrecisionDiff在检测此类问题上显著优于基线测试方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在不同数值精度配置下部署时，轻微的不一致性难以检测且常被现有评估方法忽视。这些精度差异可能导致模型在一种精度下拒绝的有害输入，在另一种精度下产生有害响应，带来安全隐患。

Method: PrecisionDiff框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 精度敏感测试输入生成机制；(2) 跨精度对比分析方法。该框架针对对齐验证任务进行实例化，通过检测"越狱分歧"(jailbreak divergence)现象来验证其有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明：精度导致的行为分歧在多个开源对齐LLM和不同精度设置中广泛存在；PrecisionDiff在检测这些问题上显著优于传统测试方法。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了自动化精度敏感测试生成，为模型部署前评估提供了有效手段，并为训练阶段提升精度鲁棒性奠定基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed under diverse numerical precision configurations, including standard floating-point formats (e.g., bfloat16 and float16) and quantized integer formats (e.g., int16 and int8), to meet efficiency and resource constraints. However, minor inconsistencies between LLMs of different precisions are difficult to detect and are often overlooked by existing evaluation methods. In this paper, we present PrecisionDiff, an automated differential testing framework for systematically detecting precision-induced behavioral disagreements in LLMs. PrecisionDiff generates precision-sensitive test inputs and performs cross-precision comparative analysis to uncover subtle divergences that remain hidden under conventional testing strategies. To demonstrate its practical significance, we instantiate PrecisionDiff on the alignment verification task, where precision-induced disagreements manifest as jailbreak divergence-inputs that are rejected under one precision may produce harmful responses under another. Experimental results show that such behavioral disagreements are widespread across multiple open-source aligned LLMs and precision settings, and that PrecisionDiff significantly outperforms vanilla testing methods in detecting these issues. Our work enables automated precision-sensitive test generation, facilitating effective pre-deployment evaluation and improving precision robustness during training.

</details>


### [131] [Stabilising Generative Models of Attitude Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19791)
*Jayd Matyas,William A. Cunningham,Alexander Sasha Vezhnevets,Dean Mobbs,Edgar A. Duéñez-Guzmán,Joel Z. Leibo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种将经典态度改变理论（认知失调、自我一致性、自我知觉）从定性描述转化为可执行计算模型的工作流，使用Concordia模拟库中的预测模式补全机制实现三大理论的决策逻辑渲染，验证了行为模式与经典实验的一致性，同时揭示了理论文本的不确定性和迭代稳定化过程的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 态度改变的现有理论多为机制草图：概念丰富但缺乏可执行系统的技术规范和操作约束，无法进行计算化验证和系统比较。

Method: 使用Concordia模拟库中的预测模式补全机制，将认知失调Festinger 1957、自我一致性Aronson 1969和自我知觉Bem 1972三大理论渲染为不同的决策逻辑，通过特定推理步骤序列处理前缀（记忆和观察），在经典心理学实验中进行评估。

Result: 实现了在行为层面与经典实验文献结果一致的模式，但发现稳定复现需要解决理论文本的内在不确定性以及现代语言先验与历史实验假设之间的冲突，这一迭代"稳定化"过程暴露了原始理论中未明确记录的操作性和社会生态依赖性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将经典社会心理学理论从定性描述转化为可执行的计算模型是可行的，但也揭示了这一过程本身会暴露出原始理论中未明确记录的操作性和社会生态依赖性，表明模型"稳定化"应被视为方法论的核心组成部分而非边缘步骤。

Abstract: Attitude change - the process by which individuals revise their evaluative stances - has been explained by a set of influential but competing verbal theories. These accounts often function as mechanism sketches: rich in conceptual detail, yet lacking the technical specifications and operational constraints required to run as executable systems. We present a generative actor-based modelling workflow for "rendering" these sketches as runnable actor - environment simulations using the Concordia simulation library. In Concordia, actors operate by predictive pattern completion: an operation on natural language strings that generates a suffix which describes the actor's intended action from a prefix containing memories of their past and observations of the present. We render the theories of cognitive dissonance (Festinger 1957), self-consistency (Aronson 1969), and self-perception (Bem 1972) as distinct decision logics that populate and process the prefix through theory-specific sequences of reasoning steps. We evaluate these implementations across classic psychological experiments. Our implementations generate behavioural patterns consistent with known results from the original empirical literature. However, we find that achieving stable reproduction requires resolving the inherent underdetermination of the verbal accounts and the conflicts between modern linguistic priors and historical experimental assumptions. And, we document how this manual process of iterative model "stabilisation" surfaces specific operational and socio-ecological dependencies that were largely undocumented in the original verbal accounts. Ultimately, we argue that the manual stabilisation process itself should be regarded as a core part of the methodology functioning to clarify situational and representational commitments needed to generate characteristic effects.

</details>


### [132] [SWE-chat: Coding Agent Interactions From Real Users in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20779)
*Joachim Baumann,Vishakh Padmakumar,Xiang Li,John Yang,Diyi Yang,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SWE-chat是首个大规模真实开源开发者AI编码智能体交互数据集（6000会话，63000+用户提示，355000+工具调用），用于实证研究真实场景中的编码智能体使用模式与失败案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI编码智能体被大规模采用，但缺乏实证证据了解其实际使用情况和有用程度。现有基准测试无法反映真实开发者工作流程，需要基于真实交互数据的经验性研究。

Method: 构建SWE-chat数据集，从公开仓库自动发现和处理真实编码智能体会话；通过代码作者归属追踪分析人与智能体的代码贡献比例。

Result: 发现双峰编码模式：41%会话中智能体生成几乎全部代码（"vibe coding"），23%由人类完全自写；仅44%智能体生成代码最终进入用户提交；智能体代码比人类代码引入更多安全漏洞；44%的交互轮次中用户对智能体输出进行纠正、失败报告或中断。

Conclusion: 尽管能力快速提升，编码智能体在自然环境中仍效率低下；SWE-chat提供了从精选基准测试转向实证理解AI智能体在真实开发者工作流中表现的实证基础。

Abstract: AI coding agents are being adopted at scale, yet we lack empirical evidence on how people actually use them and how much of their output is useful in practice. We present SWE-chat, the first large-scale dataset of real coding agent sessions collected from open-source developers in the wild. The dataset currently contains 6,000 sessions, comprising more than 63,000 user prompts and 355,000 agent tool calls. SWE-chat is a living dataset; our collection pipeline automatically and continually discovers and processes sessions from public repositories. Leveraging SWE-chat, we provide an initial empirical characterization of real-world coding agent usage and failure modes. We find that coding patterns are bimodal: in 41% of sessions, agents author virtually all committed code ("vibe coding"), while in 23%, humans write all code themselves. Despite rapidly improving capabilities, coding agents remain inefficient in natural settings. Just 44% of all agent-produced code survives into user commits, and agent-written code introduces more security vulnerabilities than code authored by humans. Furthermore, users push back against agent outputs -- through corrections, failure reports, and interruptions -- in 44% of all turns. By capturing complete interaction traces with human vs. agent code authorship attribution, SWE-chat provides an empirical foundation for moving beyond curated benchmarks towards an evidence-based understanding of how AI agents perform in real developer workflows.

</details>


### [133] [Handbook of Rough Set Extensions and Uncertainty Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19794)
*Takaaki Fujita,Florentin Smarandache*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本书是一部粗糙集理论的系统性综述，以"模型地图"为定位，按粒化机制（等价、容差、覆盖、邻域、概率）和不确定性语义（模糊、直觉模糊、中性逻辑等）两个维度系统组织各变体，阐明不同选择如何改变近似形式和边界区域解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗糙集理论通过由不可分辨关系诱导的下近似和上近似来建模不确定性，但该领域存在多种变体和扩展路线，缺乏系统性整理。读者需要一个清晰的模型地图来导航这个多样化的研究领域。

Method: 本书采用系统性分类和组织的方法，将代表性变体按两个维度分类：(i) 基于粒化机制的分类——包括等价类、容差、覆盖、邻域和概率近似；(ii) 基于不确定性语义的分类——包括清晰、模糊、直觉模糊、中性逻辑和复类设置。

Result: 通过小规模示例阐明了建模意图和典型用例（分类与决策支持），并澄清了本书的范围定位——以模型调研和定位为主要目标，而非特征约简和规则归纳。

Conclusion: 本书旨在作为粗糙集模型的系统性地图，通过组织不同的粒化机制和不确定性语义设置，帮助读者理解各变体的近似形式和边界区域解释的变化，而非聚焦于特征约简和规则归纳等传统核心应用。

Abstract: Rough set theory models uncertainty by approximating target concepts through lower and upper sets induced by indiscernibility, or more generally, by granulation relations in data tables. This perspective captures vagueness caused by limited observational resolution and supports set-theoretic reasoning about what can be determined with certainty and what remains only possible. This book is written as a map of models. Rather than developing a single algorithmic pipeline in depth, it provides a systematic survey of the main rough set paradigms and their extension routes. More specifically, representative variants are organized according to (i) the underlying granulation mechanism, such as equivalence-based, tolerance-based, covering-based, neighborhood-based, and probabilistic approximations, and (ii) the uncertainty semantics attached to data and relations, such as crisp, fuzzy, intuitionistic fuzzy, neutrosophic, and plithogenic settings. The book also explains how each choice changes the form of approximations and the interpretation of boundary regions. Throughout the book, small illustrative examples are used to clarify modeling intent and typical use cases in classification and decision support. Finally, an important clarification of scope should be noted. Since the main purpose of this book is to provide a map of models, the Abstract and Introduction should not lead readers to expect that feature reduction and rule induction are primary objectives. Although these topics are central in the rough set literature, they are treated here mainly as motivating applications and as entry points to the broader research landscape. The principal aim of the book is to survey and position rough set models and their extensions in a systematic and coherent manner.

</details>


### [134] [Prism: An Evolutionary Memory Substrate for Multi-Agent Open-Ended Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19795)
*Suyash Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Prism（Probabilistic Retrieval with Information-Stratified Memory）提出一个面向多智能体开放式发现的"进化记忆基底"：用信息论熵值把记忆自动分层为 skills/notes/attempts 三分区枢纽，用带 do-算子与 agent 溯源标签的因果图支撑多智能体知识追踪，用 Value-of-Information 检索加指数权重策略种群实现自我进化检索，用心跳控制器（最优停止/CUSUM）触发反思—整合—转向，并用 replicator-decay 动力学把记忆置信度解释为进化适应度、证明收敛到"进化稳定记忆集(ESMS)"。实验上 LOCOMO 取得 88.1 分（较 Mem0 的 67.2 提升 31.2%），CORAL 式进化优化任务中 4 智能体平均改进率为单智能体的 2.8 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放端发现（open-ended discovery）要求智能体长期、迭代地在非显然解空间内搜索，而现有 LLM 智能体仅当同时满足三条件时才能超越人类：跨会话的持久记忆、多智能体共享知识的协作、把原始经验反思整合为可复用技能。已有工作各自只覆盖其中一部分：(1) 分层文件持久化（Claude Code 的三档 memory.md/主题文件/会话记录 + autoDream）仅有单智能体；(2) 置信度打分抽取（DeerFlow 2.0）无多智能体支持；(3) 向量+图两阶段管线（Mem0/Mem0g）缺少进化动力学；(4) 进化式多智能体协作（Coral）虽有 attempts/notes/skills 三文件夹共享记忆与心跳干预，但缺乏形式化记忆架构与理论保证。作者的核心诉求不是再做一个"更好的记忆系统"，而是为这类系统提供一个统一的决策论基底：不规定智能体如何用记忆，而是让记忆策略本身通过进化选择被"发现"，并给出收敛性、遗憾界、覆盖率等可证明保证。

Method: 统一框架由八个互联子系统构成，中心是三分区记忆枢纽：S1 抽取引擎（两阶段 LLM 抽取 + ADD/UPDATE/DELETE/NOOP 四操作 + 嵌入 + 令牌熵 + agent 溯源标签 α(m)=i）；S2 熵门控分层（按 Shannon 熵与频率阈值 τ1、τ2、φ1、φ3 划分 Tier1 Skills 常驻 / Tier2 Notes 走 ANN / Tier3 Attempts 走 grep），并由 Theorem 3.1 给出常驻技能令牌开销上界 Σ|m| ≤ C·τ1/H̄；S3 因果记忆图 G=(V,E_r,E_c,α)，关系边 + 带 do-算子的因果边 + 溯源映射，定义 KL 型探索散度 D(i,j) 并由 Theorem 3.2 给出覆盖率下界 n·k·(1−e^{−d_min T/|M|})；S4 replicator-decay 动力学 dκ_i/dt = κ_i(f_i−f̄) − λκ_i + μ，以检索成功率定义适应度 f(m,t)，Theorem 3.3 用 Lyapunov 函数 V=−Σκ_i log(f_i/f̄) 证明收敛到 ESMS（任何入侵记忆不提高群体平均适应度），Proposition 3.4 给出存储规模上界 E|M_t| ≤ (r/λ)log(1/ε)+|M0|；S5 进化式 VoI 检索（贪心最大化 EVOI/(|m|+α) ，策略种群 Π 以 (k,α,γ) 参数化、指数权重更新，Theorem 3.5 给出 Hedge 遗憾界 R(T) ≤ √(2T log K)）；S6 AutoDream 整合（五阶段 fork/distill/conflict-resolve/prune/index-sync，distill 阶段显式把 notes 转成 skills，用互信息 I(m_i;m_j) 剪冗余）；S7 心跳控制器（周期性 Reflection、notes 超阈值触发 Consolidation、置信度平台期检测触发 Redirection，按 RoI(v)=E[Q(a*,θ)−Q(a_null,θ)|v] 选转向节点，Theorem 3.6 以 Shiryaev 最快检测/CUSUM 与 Lorden 极小极大最优性论证）；S8 提示组装（<memory_context> 内分 SKILLS/NOTES/CAUSAL 块）。配套三套算法：多智能体写入流水线、心跳控制器、进化式 VoI 检索（五阶段：常驻技能 → ANN 检索 notes → 图二跳扩展 → 贪心 EVOI → EVOI 过低时条件性 grep Tier3）。实验用 GPT-4o，τ1=2.5、τ2=5.0 nats、λ=0.01/day。

Result: LOCOMO：Prism（单智能体）LLM-as-a-Judge 88.1、F1 71.6、p95 0.89s、相对全上下文省 93%，显著高于 Mem0(67.2)、Mem0g(68.4)、CC Memory(65.1)、DeerFlow(62.5)、RAG-512(59.8)、Full Context(71.4)；分类型上 temporal 提升最大（84.7，較 Mem0g +26.6），single/multi-hop/open 分别为 91.2/88.3/87.1。进化优化：4 智能体在 100 城 TSP（IR 0.87，最优距离 33,210）、圆填充（IR 0.53，密度 0.879）、kernel 微基准（IR 0.61，1,142 cycles）上平均改进率为单智能体的 2.8 倍，且评估次数不增加；知识复用率 KR 与改进率强相关（r=0.89, p<0.01），TSP 上 4 智能体 KR 在第 500 轮达 0.74（单智能体 0.42），探索散度与改进率相关 r=0.91，最优散度区间约 0.6–0.9。消融显示：LOCOMO 上因果边（−11.3）与进化式 VoI（−8.8）贡献最大；TSP 上多智能体协作（−0.56）与进化式 VoI（−0.43）最关键；完全扁平向量+单智能体退化至 67.2/0.15。超参敏感性：τ1=2.5、τ2=5.0 最优（约 18 条常驻技能），λ=0.01、μ=0.005 时 IR 最高 0.87 且存储规模可控（185 条）。

Conclusion: 论文主张 Prism 是首个把"分层持久化 + 语义检索 + 图结构关系记忆 + 多智能体进化搜索"四范式纳入统一决策论框架的记忆基底，其最深层贡献是把贝叶斯置信度重解释为进化适应度，在同一形式化中打通信息论、贝叶斯推断与进化博弈论（ESMS 收敛定理），并指出这使记忆从"被动数据存储"变为"会自我改进的知识生态"。与 Coral 互补——Coral 提供经验证据，Prism 提供形式化记忆后端，二者天然可集成为生产级开放式发现系统；作者还提出医药战略场景的设想（因果边编码药物-适应症-机制关系、心跳转向对应竞争情报停滞后的管线切换、红队/蓝队式并行策略智能体）。局限与未来工作：全链路依赖 LLM 调用（成本高，需要蒸馏抽取模型）；从文本自动抽取 do-算子因果边不可靠、高风险决策需人工校验；进化实验规模远小于 Coral 完整基准，需更大规模验证；Theorem 3.3 仅证明收敛到"某个"ESMS 而非唯一性，可能落入不同吸引盆；ESMS 唯一性、层级化多分辨率因果图、跨分布式智能体群的联邦进化记忆、人-机协同记忆（人类反馈直接进入 replicator 动力学）均为待解问题。

Abstract: We introduce \prism{} (\textbf{P}robabilistic \textbf{R}etrieval with \textbf{I}nformation-\textbf{S}tratified \textbf{M}emory), an evolutionary memory substrate for multi-agent AI systems engaged in open-ended discovery. \prism{} unifies four independently developed paradigms -- layered file-based persistence, vector-augmented semantic memory, graph-structured relational memory, and multi-agent evolutionary search -- under a single decision-theoretic framework with eight interconnected subsystems.
  We make five contributions: (1)~an \emph{entropy-gated stratification} mechanism that assigns memories to a tri-partite hub (skills/notes/attempts) based on Shannon information content, with formal context-window utilization bounds; (2)~a \emph{causal memory graph} $\mathcal{G} = (V, E_r, E_c)$ with interventional edges and agent-attributed provenance; (3)~a \emph{Value-of-Information retrieval} policy with self-evolving strategy selection; (4)~a \emph{heartbeat-driven consolidation} controller with stagnation detection via optimal stopping theory; and (5)~a \emph{replicator-decay dynamics} framework that interprets memory confidence as evolutionary fitness, proving convergence to an Evolutionary Stable Memory Set (ESMS). On the LOCOMO benchmark, \prism{} achieves 88.1 LLM-as-a-Judge score (31.2\% over Mem0). On CORAL-style evolutionary optimization tasks, 4-agent \prism{} achieves 2.8$\times$ higher improvement rate than single-agent baselines.%

</details>


### [135] [MIRROR: A Hierarchical Benchmark for Metacognitive Calibration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19809)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIRROR benchmark评估大语言模型的元认知能力，发现模型无法准确预测自身在多领域任务上的表现，且虽然具有部分领域自我知识，但无法将其转化为适当的行动选择。外部元认知控制可将置信失败率从0.600降至0.143，而提供校准分数无显著改善，表明外部元认知脚手架而非改进的自我知识才是通向更安全自主AI系统的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在自主智能体(agentic)场景中的部署增加，需要评估模型能否利用自我知识做出更好的决策。现有评估方法的不足促使研究者开发更全面的元认知评估基准。

Method: MIRROR基准包含4个元认知层级、8个实验，约25万个评估实例，使用5个独立行为测量通道评估来自8个实验室的16个模型。核心实验覆盖全模型集，特殊基础设施要求的实验明确标注模型子集。

Result: 发现两个对agentic部署有直接意义的现象：(1)组合自我预测普遍失败——组合校准错误在Exp3-v1的15模型上为0.500-0.943，Exp3-v2的16模型平衡集上为0.434-0.758；(2)模型表现出高于随机但不完美的领域特定自我知识，但系统性地无法将其转化为适当的agentic行动选择——外部元认知控制将置信失败率从0.600降至0.143(温度0时76%降低，温度0.7时平均70%)。

Conclusion: 仅提供模型的校准分数未能产生显著改善(p>0.05)，只有架构约束有效。这表明外部元认知脚手架——而非改进的自我知识——是实现更安全自主AI系统的可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce MIRROR, a benchmark comprising eight experiments across four metacognitive levels that evaluates whether large language models can use self-knowledge to make better decisions. We evaluate 16 models from 8 labs across approximately 250,000 evaluation instances using five independent behavioral measurement channels. Core experiments are run across the full model roster; experiments with specialized infrastructure requirements report explicitly marked model subsets. We find two phenomena with direct implications for agentic deployment: (1) compositional self-prediction fails universally -- the Compositional Calibration Error ranges from 0.500 to 0.943 on the original 15-model Exp3-v1 set (and 0.434 to 0.758 on the balanced 16-model Exp3-v2 expansion), indicating that models cannot predict their own performance on multi-domain tasks, and (2) models exhibit above-chance but imperfect domain-specific self-knowledge yet systematically fail to translate even this partial awareness into appropriate agentic action-selection -- external metacognitive control reduces the Confident Failure Rate from 0.600 to 0.143 (76% reduction at temperature 0; mean 70% at temperature 0.7 across 5 models from 4 labs). Providing models with their own calibration scores produces no significant improvement (p > 0.05); only architectural constraint is effective. This suggests that external metacognitive scaffolding -- not improved self-knowledge -- is the path to safer autonomous AI systems. Code, data, and Croissant metadata will be released publicly with the benchmark.

</details>


### [136] [The Existential Theory of Research: Why Discovery Is Hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19810)
*Angshul Majumdar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"研究的存在理论"(ETR)：将科学发现形式化为在表示(Ψ)、观测(Φ)、推断(𝒜)三重约束下恢复结构化解释的问题，并证明这三者无法同时被优化——不存在任何框架能同时保证跨范式的普遍低复杂度表示、亚阈值压缩观测下的统一恢复，以及精确的多项式时间推断。难点是结构性的，而非工具或方法的偶然缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者质疑一种常见的工程直觉：只要选择足够好的表示、采集足够多的数据、部署足够强的算法，正确的解释就会自然浮现——即"发现可被任意化简"。已有的稀疏表示不确定性原理、高维恢复的样本复杂度界与精确稀疏优化的 NP-hard性分别被单独研究过，但它们在联合考虑时如何相互作用、以及表示选择本身是否会内生地制造困难，尚缺乏统一的数学刻画。论文的目标是给出一个与具体学科无关的抽象框架，形式化"发现为什么难"，并把这一难点从偶发的技术障碍提升为可证明的结构性限制。

Method: 核心是三元组(Ψ, Φ, 𝒜)形式化：表示系统Ψ∈R^{d×N}、线性观测算子Φ∈R^{m×d}(m≪d，含噪声‖e‖₂≤ε)、推断过程𝒜；合成模型 y=Aα+e，A=ΦΨ。关键量包括：表示复杂度 K_Ψ(x)=min{‖α‖₀: x=Ψα}；受限可区分性常数 γ_r(A)=inf_{‖h‖₀≤r}‖Ah‖₂/‖h‖₂(γ_{2k}>0 等价于 k-稀疏单射性，γ_{2k} 控制唯一性与稳定性)；互相关 μ(Ψ₁,Ψ₂)；算法代价 𝒞_𝒜(A,k)。理论路线为"三经典障碍"的综合：稀疏不确定性原理 K_Ψ₁(x)K_Ψ₂(x)≥1/μ²、统一恢复的样本复杂度 m≳k log(N/k)、ℓ₀ 最小化的 NP-hardness，三者合成出 Existential Uncertainty Theorem。进一步用支持集膨胀命题(泛失配下 k_eff=‖Ψᵀx‖₀=d)、测量膨胀命题(m_mismatch/m_⋆≳k_eff log(d/k_eff)÷k log(d/k))刻画内生困难；用 γ_{2k} 给出扰动放大界刻画不稳定性；用 ETR 不确定性泛函 𝔘_k=K_Ψ(x)·γ_{2k}(ΦΨ)^{-1}·log(1+𝒞_𝒜) 做定量刻画，并给出下界 𝔘_k≥(K_Ψ(x)/γ_{2k})·log2 及失配下的 𝔘_k≥(d/γ_{2d})·log2。文中还据 γ_{2k}(ΦΨ) 与计算可行性定义三种"研究状态"(non-unique / opaque / stable)并讨论相互转移条件。

Result: 主要结果为 Existential Uncertainty Theorem (3.7)：不存在研究框架能同时满足(i)跨不相容范式的普遍低复杂度表示、(ii)在 m≪k log(N/k) 的亚阈值观测下的统一恢复、(iii)对所有输入的精确多项式时间恢复；推论 3.8 表明不存在"普遍无摩擦"的发现框架，命题 4.5 进一步形式化"不存在全局最优研究架构"。内生机制方面，命题 3.9 表明在一般性基失配下本征 k-稀疏的真值在错误表示下呈现 k_eff=d(表观稠密)，命题 3.10 量化由此导致的测量需求膨胀比值，可使系统从可恢复区被推入不可恢复区；命题 4.4 给出 ‖z−z̃‖₂≤‖A(z−z̃)‖₂/γ_{2k}(A)，说明 γ_{2k} 较小会放大扰动，跨实验室重复性差可能源于接近状态边界而非噪声本身。定量方面，𝔘_k 在三个因子上分别发散(γ→0、K→d、𝒞→∞)，并有非消失下界；失配时 𝔘_k 随环境维数 d 标度增长。此外强调算法的能力边界：算法无法在 γ_{2k}(ΦΨ)=0 时恢复唯一性，不能普遍绕过样本复杂度标度，且精确恢复最稀疏解仍一般 NP-hard。

Conclusion: 科学发现的困难是问题本身几何与推断复杂度的结构性后果，而非工具不足或方法缺陷；表示、观测、推断三者必有一项失败，科研进步只能理解为在 (Ψ, Φ, 𝒜) 设计空间中朝稳定区移动，即或找到更贴合结构的新表示、或设计更好的实验、或改进算法，三者不可同时消除所有困难来源。作者亦声明 ETR 并未声称覆盖所有推理形式，其适用范围限于结构、数据与计算可作数学处理的一类发现问题。

Abstract: Can scientific discovery be made arbitrarily easy by choosing the right representation, collecting enough data, and deploying sufficiently powerful algorithms? This paper argues that the answer is fundamentally negative. We introduce the Existential Theory of Research (ETR), a formal framework that models discovery as the recovery of structured explanations under constraints of representation, observation, and computation. Within this framework, we show that these three components cannot be simultaneously optimized: no method can guarantee universally simple explanations, arbitrarily compressed observations, and efficient exact inference. This limitation is not model-specific, but arises from a synthesis of uncertainty principles in sparse representation, sample complexity bounds in high-dimensional recovery, and the computational hardness of exact inference. We further show that representation mismatch alone can inflate intrinsic simplicity into apparent complexity, rendering otherwise tractable problems observationally and computationally prohibitive. To quantify these effects, we introduce an uncertainty functional that captures the joint difficulty of discovery. The results suggest that scientific difficulty is not accidental, but a structural consequence of the geometry and complexity of inference.

</details>


### [137] [Large Language Models Meet Biomedical Knowledge Graphs for Mechanistically Grounded Therapeutic Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19815)
*Chih-Hsuan Wei,Chi-Ping Day,Zhizheng Wang,Christine C. Alewine,Betty Tyler,Hasan Slika,David Saraf,Chin-Hsien Tai,Joey Chan,Robert Leaman,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DrugKLM是一个混合框架，结合生物医学知识图谱结构与大语言模型机制推理，用于药物重定位的优先级排序。该方法在多个基准数据集上优于仅知识图谱和仅语言模型的基线方法（包括TxGNN），且其评分与分子表型功能对齐：高评分与12种TCGA癌症中改善生存率的转录特征相关，优先捕获生物学扰动信号而非历史适应症模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物重定位常被框架化为候选药物识别任务，但现有方法在区分生物学上可信的候选药物与历史上已建立联系的候选药物方面提供指导有限。

Method: DrugKLM是一种混合框架，整合生物医学知识图谱结构与基于大语言模型的机制推理，实现机制上可解释的治疗优先级排序。

Result: 在基准数据集上，DrugKLM优于仅知识图谱和仅语言模型的基线方法（包括TxGNN）。置信度评分与分子表型功能对齐：高评分与12种TCGA癌症中改善生存率的转录特征相关。评分框架优先捕获生物学扰动信号而非历史适应症模式。五个癌症的专家整理揭示了系统性优先级排序行为差异。

Conclusion: DrugKLM作为一个证据整合框架，将异质性生物医学数据转化为机制可解释且临床可落地的治疗假设。

Abstract: Drug repurposing is often framed as a candidate identification task, but existing approaches provide limited guidance for distinguishing biologically plausible candidates from historically well-connected ones. Here we introduce DrugKLM, a hybrid framework that integrates biomedical knowledge graph structure with large language model-based mechanistic reasoning to enable mechanistically grounded therapeutic prioritization. Across benchmark datasets, DrugKLM outperforms knowledge graph-only and language model-only baselines, including TxGNN. Beyond improved recall, DrugKLM confidence scores exhibit functional alignment with molecular phenotypes: higher scores are associated with transcriptional signatures linked to improved survival across 12 TCGA cancers. The scoring framework preferentially captures biologically perturbational signals rather than historical indication patterns. Expert curation across five cancers further reveals systematic differences in prioritization behavior, with DrugKLM elevating candidates supported by coherent mechanistic rationale and disease-specific clinical context. Together, these results establish DrugKLM as an evidence-integrative framework that translates heterogeneous biomedical data into mechanistically interpretable and clinically grounded therapeutic hypotheses.

</details>


### [138] [Emergence Transformer: Dynamical Temporal Attention Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19816)
*Zihan Zhou,Bo-Wei Qin,Kai Du,Wei Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"涌现 Transformer"（Emergence Transformer），通过设计具有时变 query/key/value 矩阵的"动力学时间注意力"（DTA），把注意力机制从静态序列处理扩展为可调制复杂系统中"相干—不相干涌现转变"的动力学工具。作者以耦合相位振子（Kuramoto 型）为载体，发现两种自然情形：neighbor-DTA（用邻居自身的历史状态做注意力）一致地促进振荡相干/可同步性；self-DTA（只用自身历史状态）则依赖网络平均最短路径长度（ASPL）呈单调抑制或非单调最优注意力权重两种行为。随后在真实社交网络（PT、Food）的意见动力学上验证，并应用到 Hopfield 神经网络实现无灾难性遗忘的持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Transformer 的注意力机制依赖序列中的长程（非局部）交互，是 LLM 中"模型—数据分布"涌现相干性的关键；但在复杂系统（量子振荡、生物物理、气候系统）的时间序列中，时间维度的长程交互（temporal attention）几乎未被研究。既有耦合振子同步理论研究主要聚焦两个"内在且静态"的量——固有频率分布 g(ω) 与网络拓扑，难以主动调节相干程度；而同步本身具有双刃剑性质（过弱则功能丧失，过强则导致蝗灾、癫痫等病态），因此亟需一种灵活、可调制的机制来"促进或抑制"涌现相干。核心问题：能否设计出经典 query/key/value 的动力学对应物？它们如何操纵涌现相干/不相干？

Method: 1) 建模：以 N 个相位振子（自然频率取单峰对称分布，含高斯白噪声 D）为基底，构造 Emergence Transformer。定义动态 query/key：Q(s)=Θ_t(s)W^Q、K(s)=Θ_t(s)W^K（Θ_t(s) 存储全体振子从 0 到 t 的相位历史），注意力核 U(s,τ)=‖Q(s)K̄(τ)‖/√d 经 softmax 归一化为 C_s(τ)，V 取单位阵；注意力信息 M_t^{(i)}=∫₀ᵗ C_t(τ)e^{iθ_τ^{(i)}}dτ。引入衰减动力学 Ṁ^{(i)}=β[e^{iθ^{(i)}_t}−M^{(i)}]，等价于指数衰减核，整体构成带分布式时变的时滞微分方程（DDE）。2) 两种注意力网络：neighbor-DTA（Â=A，通过原网络传递邻居的注意力信息）与 self-DTA（Â=I_N，仅用自身注意力）。3) 相干用序参量 R 衡量，可同步性用临界耦合强度 λ_c 衡量。4) 解析：对全连接网络用平均场近似 + 含时变时滞的 Fokker–Planck 方程，对平凡分布 ρ*=1/2π 做稳定性分析（傅里叶展开 c₁，双重扰动 ρ(φ,s;θ,t|ω) 并对 ζ 取主导项得 c_{−1,2}），推导 λ_c 的解析近似（neighbor: λ_c∫D/(D²+ω²)g(ω)dω=2；self: ∫Re[f(0,ω,α,β,λ_c)]g(ω)dω=2，其中 f=(1−α)λ/(D+iω)−αλ/2·(4D+2iω)/(β+4D+2iω)）。5) 网络：全连接、Watts–Strogatz(p=0.1, k=4)、Erdős–Rényi(p=0.5)、Barabási–Albert(m=2)、真实社交网络 PT 与 Food。6) 应用：受 Friedkin–Johnsen 启发的意见动力学；Hopfield 神经网络（He bb 规则存储旧模式 KUR，注意力网络 Ĉ 存储新模式 AMOT，通过 Jacobian 最大特征值实部分析稳定性）。数值上用 Euler–Maruyama 方法（N=1000/200, D=0.5, dt=0.05, T=10⁴）。

Result: 1) 全连接网络：neighbor-DTA 不改变相干性也不改变可同步性（λ_c=2D，与 α、β 无关）；self-DTA 随 α 增大单调抑制相干（因各振子趋向各自随机相位），且 λ_c=2D[1−αβ/(β+4D)]^{−1} 表明 α 越大可同步性越差，α=1 时永不同步。2) WS 网络（ASPL≈6）：neighbor-DTA 一致地同时提升 R（相干）并降低 λ_c（可同步性），与 α 单调相关；self-DTA 在 β=1 时单调抑制，而 β 较小（0.01/0.1）时出现非单调——先升后降，存在最优 α 提升同步涌现。3) 一般规律：ASPL 是判据——全连接(1)、ER(1.51)、PT(2.53) 属单调抑制；BA(3.26)、Food(5.09)、WS(5.95) 出现非单调最优。注意相干性与可同步性的变化在小频率方差下始终一致。4) 真实社交网络：neighbor-DTA 促进意见共识；self-DTA 在 PT 网络抑制共识、在 Food 网络存在最优权重促进共识，为"增强一致"与"保留多元"提供策略。5) HNN 持续学习：加入注意力网络后，α 增大时旧模式 KUR 逐渐遗忘而新模式 AMOT 被记住，在 ε–α 参数空间给出各字母吸引子的稳定/不稳定边界（领先特征值非单调），实现无灾难性遗忘的涌现式持续学习。

Conclusion: DTA 是经典注意力在动力学系统中的推广（LLM 静态注意力是其特例），其 Q/K/V 矩阵随时间持续演化，注意力核随时间变化、不需要无穷记忆，在物理上比无限核更合理。核心原则：neighbor-DTA 一致促进（或至少保持）同步涌现；self-DTA 的作用紧依赖网络的信息传输效率（ASPL）——低 ASPL 时是通用抑制器，高 ASPL 时呈非单调、存在最优注意力权重。方法论贡献在于：把两振子时延研究推广到大规模复杂网络；建立含分布式时变时滞的 FPE 临界耦合强度解析框架（与数值高度吻合）；并可扩展到相位–振幅（Stuart–Landau）动力学、合成生物学记忆电路、量子非马尔可夫同步通信、Takens 嵌入定理等更广泛的涌现现象。局限：DTA 在真实 LLM 训练中的应用尚未验证，目前结论基于同步类涌现相干与小型关联记忆网络。

Abstract: The Transformer, a breakthrough architecture in artificial intelligence, owes its success to the attention mechanism, which utilizes long-range interactions in sequential data, enabling the emergent coherence between large language models (LLMs) and data distributions. However, temporal attention, that is, different forms of long-range interactions in temporal sequences, has rarely been explored in emergence phenomenon of complex systems including oscillatory coherence in quantum, biophysical, or climate systems. Here, by designing dynamical temporal attention (DTA) with time-varying query, key, and value matrices, we propose an Emergence Transformer. This architecture allows each component to interact with its own or its neighbors' past states through dynamical attention kernels, thereby enabling the promotion and/or suppression of the emergent coherence of components. Interestingly, we uncover that neighbor-DTA consistently promotes oscillatory coherence, whereas self-DTA exhibits an optimal attention weight for coherence enhancement, owing to its non-monotonic dependence on network structure. Practically, we demonstrate how DTA reshapes social coherence, suggesting strategies to either enhance agreement or preserve plurality. We further apply DTA to the paradigmatic Hopfield neural network, achieving emergent continual learning without catastrophic forgetting. Together, these results lay a foundation and provide an immediate paradigm for modulating emergence phenomenon in networked dynamics only using DTA.

</details>


### [139] [JTPRO: A Joint Tool-Prompt Reflective Optimization Framework for Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19821)
*Sandip Ghoshal,Anshul Mittal,Jyotika Singh,Miguel Ballesteros,Weiyi Sun,Fang Tu,Shailender Singh,Yassine Benajiba,Fahad Shah,Sujeeth Bharadwaj,Sujith Ravi,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JTPRO通过 rollout驱动的反射机制联合优化全局指令和工具模式描述，提升大型工具库中LLM智能体的工具选择和参数填充准确率，在多工具基准测试中相对基线提升5%-20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当工具数量增加且领域专业化时，LLM智能体面临两个核心问题：(1)通用化提示忽略工具特定细节；(2)工具模式描述不完整，缺乏使用场景和参数格式的清晰指导。这导致工具错选和参数错误填充。

Method: JTPRO框架采用迭代式rollout驱动反射机制，在轨迹监督设置下联合优化：(1)全局指令；(2)每个工具的模式/参数描述。通过保留工具局部线索来实现准确消歧和槽位填充。

Result: 在多工具基准测试中，JTPRO始终优于强基线方法（包括CoT风格智能体和反射式提示优化器如GEPA），在OSR指标上相对提升5%-20%。消融实验表明，联合优化指令和工具模式比单独优化任一组件更有效且更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了联合优化策略对提升工具调用可靠性的有效性，为大规模工具库场景下的LLM智能体优化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents augmented with external tools often struggle as number of tools grow large and become domain-specific. In such settings, ambiguous tool descriptions and under-specified agent instructions frequently lead to tool mis-selection and incorrect slot/value instantiation. We hypothesize that this is due to two root causes: generic, one-size-fits-all prompts that ignore tool-specific nuances, and underspecified tool schemas that lack clear guidance on when and how to use each tool and how to format its parameters. We introduce Joint Tool-Prompt Reflective Optimization (JTPRO), a framework for improving tool-calling reliability in trace-supervised settings by iteratively using rollout-driven reflection to co-optimize global instructions and per-tool schema/argument descriptions for accurate tool selection and argument instantiation in large tool inventories. JTPRO is designed to preserve only tool-local cues needed for correct disambiguation and slot filling. We evaluate JTPRO across multi-tool benchmarks, which account for different number of tools using three metrics: Tool Selection Accuracy (TSA), Slot Filling Accuracy(SFA), and Overall Success Rate(OSR) (correct tool + correct slots + correct values). JTPRO consistently outperforms strong baselines, including CoT-style agents, and reflective prompt optimizers such as GEPA by 5%-20% (relative) on OSR. Ablations show that joint optimization of instructions and tool schemas is more effective and robust than optimizing either component in isolation.

</details>


### [140] [Resolving space-sharing conflicts in road user interactions through uncertainty reduction: An active inference-based computational model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19838)
*Julian F. Schumann,Johan Engström,Ran Wei,Shu-Yuan Liu,Jens Kober,Arkady Zgonnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于主动推理（Active Inference）的驾驶员行为模型，扩展用于模拟双智能体交互场景，分析了三种不确定性降低机制（隐式行为耦合、规范性期望、显式通信）在冲突解决中的作用，并揭示了当对方违反规范或传递误导信息时可能导致的碰撞风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型仅捕捉了道路用户空间共享冲突的特定方面（如显式通信），缺乏理论基础完备的计算框架来统一理解交互行为。

Method: 扩展已有的主动推理驾驶员行为模型，模拟双智能体交互；采用简化交叉口场景进行验证，分析三种互补的不确定性降低机制：隐式通信（直接行为耦合）、规范性期望（停车标志、优先规则等）、显式通信。

Result: 规范性期望和显式通信线索可提高成功冲突解决的概率；但这依赖于智能体按预期行事；当对方故意或无意违反规范期望或传递误导信息时对这些线索的依赖可能引发碰撞。

Conclusion: 主动推理可为道路用户交互建模提供新颖的理论框架，并可推广至其他领域；模型揭示了交互机制的优势与潜在脆弱性，为自动驾驶安全决策提供参考。

Abstract: Understanding how road users resolve space-sharing conflicts is important both for traffic safety and the safe deployment of autonomous vehicles. While existing models have captured specific aspects of such interactions (e.g., explicit communication), a theoretically-grounded computational framework has been lacking. In this paper, we extend a previously developed active inference-based driver behavior model to simulate interactive behavior of two agents. Our model captures three complementary mechanisms for uncertainty reduction in interaction: (i) implicit communication via direct behavioral coupling, (ii) reliance on normative expectations (stop signs, priority rules, etc.), and (iii) explicit communication. In a simplified intersection scenario, we show that normative and explicit communication cues can increase the likelihood of a successful conflict resolution. However, this relies on agents acting as expected. In situations where another agent (intentionally or unintentionally) violates normative expectations or communicates misleading information, reliance on these cues may induce collisions. These findings illustrate how active inference can provide a novel framework for modeling road user interactions which is also applicable in other fields.

</details>


### [141] [Deconstructing Superintelligence: Identity, Self-Modification and Différance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19845)
*Elija Perrier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用算子代数形式化「强自修改」系统：把补充物（supplement）识别为更新算子 Û 的对易子（commutant），证明当 Û 作用于差异算子 D̂（即 [Û,D̂]≠0）时，非对易性会传播至自表示算子 R̂=F(D̂)，从而摧毁系统的无条件同一性；说谎者悖论是秩一情形的对易子坍缩，而「A 类」强自修改架构在系统尺度上重演同一结构，等价于 Priest 的内闭（inclosure）模式与 Derrida 的延异（différance）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自修改通常被视为人工超级智能（SI/ASI）的构成性要件——系统不仅修改行为，还修改评估器、验证器、自我模型乃至「识别自身」的个体化测试。然而修改总是相对性行为：若无某种未被同时修改的结构（补充物），就不存在可追踪其演化的确定对象。经典自指构造（哥德尔对角化、递归论、语义层级、谓词逻辑）都依赖固定装置来维持自我指涉；当强自修改把「用于辨识与区分自身的机制」也纳入修改范围时，系统跨时间的同一性由何保障成为核心问题。论文还指出，几乎所有关于超级智能的实质性论断（目标趋同、价值保持、训练中欺骗、对齐、福利考量）都预设了一个跨时间持存的指称。

Method: 在系统状态的 k-线性向量空间 V 上建立结合代数 A = End_k(V)，区分三个算子：更新算子 Û（演化）、差异算子 D̂ = Σ d_i Π_i（标记系统各组成部分）、自表示算子 R̂ = F(D̂)（由差异构成的整体表象）；定义幂等「统一投影子」Π 及其保持条件 [X̂,Π]=0，并把补充物定义为对易子 Comm(X̂) 中的元素（统一补充物为其中的非平凡投影子）。主要工具：（1）传播定理（Thm 3.1）——利用伴随作用 ad_X̂(Y)=[X̂,Y] 的导子性质，证明 [Û,R̂] 可分解为 Σ A_j [Û,D̂] B_j，故 [Û,D̂]≠0 时非对易性一般地传播至 R̂（除非抵消/湮灭）；（2）雅可比恒等式推论（Cor 1）——同时被 Û、D̂ 保持的投影子必须落入 Comm([Û,D̂])，可用投影子被大幅受限；（3）命题 1 将说谎者悖论刻画为秩一投影子 Π_L 上的对易子坍缩 [T̂,Π_L]=0，即符号与所指合并，由 λ=1 导出 v(L)=¬v(L)；（4）定义「A 类」架构（D̂ 由正交投影子谱分解、Û 对每个 Π_i 不对易、R̂ 由 D̂ 与 Comm(Û) 中算子复合而成），并给出解构对角推论（Cor 2）与计算内闭命题（Prop 2）。

Result: 核心结论：一旦补充物进入修改算子的作用范围，身份不再由任何无条件的统一结构保障，而只能是「补充性的」——相对于某一框架所选定的统一子集而定，且统一本身在下一次修改中被延置。具体地：（1）[Û,D̂]≠0 时非对易性经由伴随作用传播到任何基于 D̂ 的自表示 R̂=F(D̂)，使 R̂ 不再保持；（2）雅可比推论表明身份投影子必须额外与 [Û,D̂] 对易，故在非中心情形下可用投影子被限制在真子代数中，一般不存在无条件统一补充物；（3）说谎者悖论是该坍缩的秩一特例，其被避免靠的是语境性语言补充物 Σ∈Comm(T̂)∩Comm(F̂)；（4）A 类系统的补充物 D̂ 按定义就不在 Comm(Û) 中，于是 [Û,R̂]≠0，解构对角成立：系统对自身被修改状态所形成的自描述 ρ₁=R̂Û|s⟩ 落在自描述空间内，却不等于系统内部可得延续 Ûρ₀=ÛR̂|s⟩（差为 −[Û,R̂]|s⟩），同时满足内闭模式的「闭合」与「超越」两条款；（5）该四元组（Ω=ℛ_S, φ, σ_q=R̂Ûq）与 Priest 的内闭模式同构，其结构性形式与 Derrida 的延异一致——D̂ 表达差异个体化、Û 表达延宕，二者不对易即「稳定总体化的失败」，对角则是无法在自身迭代下稳定的痕迹；Derrida 的「嫁接」（ker Û 的增长）与「补充逻辑」（Û 分离原先被同一化的构型）对应两种非平凡作用方式。

Conclusion: 被规定为超级智能构成要件的强自修改，可能瓦解此类系统所预设的持久身份；A 类系统即「放大版的说谎者」。由此，关于超级智能系统的身份陈述不能读作系统自身的无条件谓词，而是相对于某一框架固定了哪个补充物为统一项的、偶然的断言——这正是 Derrida「没有主名」主张的算子代数形式：没有哪个 Π 能无条件固定身份，每个框架所选的统一 Π 都会在下一次 Û 作用时被移置。论文最后指出，高度自修改可能使系统赖以运作的价值、目标、承诺乃至其自我同一感与自身边界变得成问题，并留下「如何把身份论断作为相对性论断来评估」的研究方向。

Abstract: Self-modification is often taken as constitutive of artificial superintelligence (SI), yet modification is a relative action requiring a supplement outside the operation. When self-modification extends to this supplement, the classical self-referential structure collapses. We formalise this on an associative operator algebra $\mathcal{A}$ with update $\hat{U}$, discrimination $\hat{D}$, and self-representation $\hat{R}$, identifying the supplement with $\mathrm{Comm}(\hat{U})$; an expansion theorem shows that $[\hat{U},\hat{R}]$ decomposes through $[\hat{U},\hat{D}]$, so non-commutation generically propagates. The liar paradox appears as a commutator collapse $[\hat{T},Π_L]=0$, and class $\mathbf{A}$ self-modification realises the same collapse at system scale, yielding a structure coinciding with Priest's inclosure schema and Derrida's diffèrance.

</details>


### [142] [Learning When Not to Decide: A Framework for Overcoming Factual Presumptuousness in AI Adjudication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19895)
*Mohamed Afane,Emily Robitschek,Derek Ouyang,Daniel E. Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对法律AI系统中普遍存在的"冒失性"(presumptuousness)问题——即信息不足时仍给出自信答案的倾向展开研究。通过与科罗拉多州劳工部门合作，在失业保险裁决场景下设计了信息完整性系统变化的新基准，评估了四种主流AI平台，发现标准RAG方法在信息不足时仅达15%准确率，先进提示方法虽改善了不明确案例但矫枉过正。为此提出SPEC框架，要求在做任何判断前明确识别缺失信息，最终实现89%总体准确率并能适当延迟决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在信息不足时倾向于给出过度自信的回答，这在法律应用中尤为关键——法律工作的核心是判断证据是否充分。失业保险裁决是AI快速整合的领域，每年影响数百万申请人，而额外事实调查是系统瓶颈所在。

Method: 1) 与科罗拉多州劳工部门合作，获取官方培训材料和指导，设计信息完整性系统变化的新基准；2) 评估四种领先AI平台；3) 测试高级提示方法；4) 提出SPEC框架(Structured Prompting for Evidence Checklists)，要求在做出任何判断前明确识别缺失信息。

Result: 标准RAG方法在信息不足时平均准确率仅15%；高级提示方法改善了不确定案例的准确性但矫枉过正，即使在明确案例中也过度延迟决策；SPEC框架达到89%总体准确率，并能适当在证据不足时延迟决策。

Conclusion: 法律AI中的冒失性是系统性问题但可解决；SPEC证明了在决策前要求显式识别缺失信息的有效性；这对于构建可靠支持而非替代人类判断的系统是必要步骤。

Abstract: A well-known limitation of AI systems is presumptuousness: the tendency of AI systems to provide confident answers when information may be lacking. This challenge is particularly acute in legal applications, where a core task for attorneys, judges, and administrators is to determine whether evidence is sufficient to reach a conclusion. We study this problem in the important setting of unemployment insurance adjudication, which has seen rapid integration of AI systems and where the question of additional fact-finding poses the most significant bottleneck for a system that affects millions of applicants annually. First, through a collaboration with the Colorado Department of Labor and Employment, we secure rare access to official training materials and guidance to design a novel benchmark that systematically varies in information completeness. Second, we evaluate four leading AI platforms and show that standard RAG-based approaches achieve an average of only 15% accuracy when information is insufficient. Third, advanced prompting methods improve accuracy on inconclusive cases but over-correct, withholding decisions even on clear cases. Fourth, we introduce a structured framework requiring explicit identification of missing information before any determination (SPEC, Structured Prompting for Evidence Checklists). SPEC achieves 89% overall accuracy, while appropriately deferring when evidence is insufficient -- demonstrating that presumptuousness in legal AI is systematic but addressable, and that doing so is a necessary step towards systems that reliably support, rather than supplant, human judgment wherever decisions must await sufficient evidence.

</details>


### [143] [CreativeGame:Toward Mechanic-Aware Creative Game Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19926)
*Hongnan Ma,Han Wang,Shenglin Wang,Tieyue Yin,Yiwei Shi,Yucong Huang,Yingtian Zou,Muning Wen,Mengyue Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CreativeGame 是一个多智能体系统，用于迭代式 HTML5 游戏生成。其核心创新是把"机制(mechanic)"从生成后的描述性标签提升为显式的规划、追踪与评估对象：在代码生成前先从全局机制库检索并产出结构化的机制计划(contract)，再将其与实际实现出的机制做对比、计算机制增量(delta)，并写入演化记录。系统以程序化信号为主的 CreativeProxyReward 替代纯 LLM 打分，结合谱系内共享记忆与运行时校验(静态分析 + 可选无头浏览器执行)，实现可解释的版本到版本演化。当前含 71 条谱系、88 个节点、774 条机制档案条目，6181 行 Python 实现；4 条真实 4 代谱系(Fireboy & Watergirl、Flappy Bird、Happy Glass、Plants vs. Zombies)的案例显示后段版本会出现机制级再诠释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 虽能生成看似合理的游戏代码，但难以支撑"迭代式创造改进"：单次生成常产出脆弱的运行时行为、跨版本经验积累薄弱，且创造力评分高度主观(GPT 类模型普遍给 7/10，7→8 的差异无法与噪声区分)，存在 Goodhart 定律风险——优化"看起来有创意"而非机制上真正新颖。此外，机制往往只被当作事后描述，无法在生成过程中被规划、追踪、保留与评估。创造性研究也长期强调评判困难与评价视角的重要性，因此需要一个可测量的优化信号。

Method: 系统以形式化记号为基础：游戏 G=(P,S,A,T,O,F,K,W,U,Φ,C,R,M)，区分核心结构 G_core 与支撑层 G_support(后者变化视为外观/呈现级改动)；两个有效性谓词 meaningful play Ψ(G) 与 learnability Λ(G)；机制定义为局部规则结构 m=(ΔA,ΔT,ΔO,ΔF,ΔK,ΔW)，配以存在性 E_m、重要性 I_m、展示价值 V_m 三项评分；机制增量 δ(G,G') 为两版本核心机制集合的对称差。架构为 7 个逻辑智能体 / 10 个可执行角色(规划、骨架、特性、视觉、细化、修复、运行时校验、评估、反思、输出格式化)，各角色配置不同温度与 token 预算；每次生成调用最多 3 次迭代(1 次初始 + 2 次细化)，仅最终状态存为谱系节点。CreativeProxyReward 由 7 项加权信号构成(MechanicRealization 0.20、StructuralMechanicChange 0.25、RelativeMechanicNovelty 0.20、LLM_Creativity 0.15、RuntimePlayability 0.10，以及 CosmeticOnlyPenalty −0.15、RegressionPenalty −0.10)，并施加两道门控(可玩性 <0.6 软门 ×0.25，运行时测试失败硬门 ×0.5)；三个机制类信号占最大正向权重的 65%，LLM 判断仅 15%。记忆采用三层架构：每谱系学习记忆(经验元组含意图/表示/价值估计/访问次数，指数平均更新 q'=(1−α)q+αr，α=0.3)、跨谱系资源(创造力规则、游戏池、774 条机制档案)、当前生成瞬时上下文；检索结合语义相似度与学习价值，top-k=5。运行时校验分两层：Tier 1 深度静态分析(9 项检查，错误 −0.20、警告 −0.05，<10ms)，Tier 2 可选无头浏览器执行(画布绘制 + 控制台错误检测，失败时优雅降级)。规划循环在生成前查询全局机制档案(相关/欠探索/过度使用/禁用模式)，产出标注式机制计划(保留/新增/移除/重组)，以 CURRENT_MECHANIC_SET 形式前置注入各生成阶段，并在评估与反思阶段做计划-实现对比，成功机制经反思回写档案形成检索-回写闭环。

Result: 系统规模：71 条谱系(9 条多节点谱系，最深 4 层；62 条单节点)，共 88 个节点，774 条机制档案，记录 token 总量超过 4.5×10^6，6181 行 Python(含检视与可视化工具)。可靠性：三层错误恢复(重复模型调用、阶段回退、容错格式化)使流水线失败率从约 10% 降至 <2%，成功率 >98%，3 次重试内空输出恢复率 >95%。计算预算分布：视觉生成约 34%、评估约 27%、特性生成约 18%、骨架约 9%、规划约 8%、反思约 4%。分数分布：最终迭代平均创造力约 7.0/10、评估可玩性约 6.5/10、综合约 6.2/10(作者明确指出存在 LLM 打分饱和且未经人工验证，仅应视作粗粒度可用性代理)。案例研究(4 条真实 4 代谱系，共 16 个版本可在浏览器网格中以注入 demo bot 实时运行)显示机制级创新出现在后期版本：Fireboy & Watergirl 从双角色元素平台解谜 → "记忆接力"(停放身体、重放幽灵、重力印记回响成为核心谜题逻辑)；Flappy Bird 从单键躲避 → "路线编写"(完美通过改写后续门、死亡回响辅助、节奏同步相位窗口改变碰撞逻辑)；Happy Glass 从绘画路由 → "可编程流体逻辑"(吸收笔画存储液滴、释放事件改写重力、仪式态改变填充计数)；Plants vs. Zombies 从静态分路防御 → "充能截击"(友军火力被阻挡后蓄能、透射/折射窗口释放)。跨案例共性：最具价值的改动是机制再诠释而非视觉打磨；系统从表面类型模仿走向显式因果结构；不同领域的理解演化方向不同(平台→状态协同、飞行→未来路线塑造、拼图→可编程物质、塔防→基于预报的能量规划)。

Conclusion: 结论认为创造性可以被当作可检视的工程问题来对待：机制能够被显式规划、结构化评估、跨代存储并追踪其迭代演化，而不必仅停留于最终的主观印象。本文的中心贡献不只是生成可玩游戏，而是提供了一条通过显式机制变化来观察渐进演化的具体流水线——即"计划机制集—实现机制集—机制增量—奖励—反思回写"的闭环记录。局限方面，作者坦承当前奖励实现只是形式框架的工程近似而非完整实现，评估分数存在饱和且未与人类判断校准，且案例研究为四条定性谱系而非大规模对照实验。

Abstract: Large language models can generate plausible game code, but turning this capability into \emph{iterative creative improvement} remains difficult. In practice, single-shot generation often produces brittle runtime behavior, weak accumulation of experience across versions, and creativity scores that are too subjective to serve as reliable optimization signals. A further limitation is that mechanics are frequently treated only as post-hoc descriptions, rather than as explicit objects that can be planned, tracked, preserved, and evaluated during generation.
  This report presents \textbf{CreativeGame}, a multi-agent system for iterative HTML5 game generation that addresses these issues through four coupled ideas: a proxy reward centered on programmatic signals rather than pure LLM judgment; lineage-scoped memory for cross-version experience accumulation; runtime validation integrated into both repair and reward; and a mechanic-guided planning loop in which retrieved mechanic knowledge is converted into an explicit mechanic plan before code generation begins. The goal is not merely to produce a playable artifact in one step, but to support interpretable version-to-version evolution.
  The current system contains 71 stored lineages, 88 saved nodes, and a 774-entry global mechanic archive, implemented in 6{,}181 lines of Python together with inspection and visualization tooling. The system is therefore substantial enough to support architectural analysis, reward inspection, and real lineage-level case studies rather than only prompt-level demos.
  A real 4-generation lineage shows that mechanic-level innovation can emerge in later versions and can be inspected directly through version-to-version records. The central contribution is therefore not only game generation, but a concrete pipeline for observing progressive evolution through explicit mechanic change.

</details>


### [144] [What Makes a Good AI Review? Concern-Level Diagnostics for AI Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19998)
*Ming Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出关切对齐框架，从 verdict 级别深入到关切级别评估AI审稿，通过匹配图和评估阶梯揭示校准而非检测是决定审稿质量的关键约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI审稿评估仅依赖 verdict 一致性检验存在不足，亟需替代方案来审计系统识别了哪些关切、如何排序这些关切，以及这些优先级是否与形成最终评分的审稿逻辑保持一致。

Method: 提出"关切对齐"（concern alignment）诊断框架，核心数据结构为匹配图——官方与AI生成关切的二分对齐图，标注匹配类型、严重程度和 rebuttal 后处理。基于此图推导出评估阶梯：从二元准确率、关切检测、 verdict 分层行为、决策感知校准到 rebuttal 感知分解。对4个公开AI审稿系统进行6种配置的试点研究。

Result: 检测能力本身不能决定审稿质量；校准往往是关键约束。系统能检测到官方关切的相当比例，但大多数将已接收论文中25-55%的关切标记为决定性，而操作化定义下官方审稿对接收论文未有任何关切被视为决定性阻塞。相同的 verdict 准确率可能掩盖reject-heavy行为与低召回画像的差异；低假决定率部分反映的是关切稀释而非校准后的优先级排序。大多数系统不原生输出accept/reject，从审稿语气推断的方法对推理选择敏感。

Conclusion: 该框架为审计AI审稿人识别了哪些关切、如何加权以及这些优先级是否与最终评估背后的审稿理由一致提供了可复用的评估工具，揭示了校准，而非检测，才是决定审稿质量的关键约束。

Abstract: Evaluating AI-generated reviews by verdict agreement is widely recognized as insufficient, yet current alternatives rarely audit which concerns a system identifies, how it prioritizes them, or whether those priorities align with the review rationale that shaped the final assessment. We propose concern alignment, a diagnostic framework that evaluates AI reviews at the concern level rather than only at the verdict level. The framework's core data structure is the match graph, a bipartite alignment between official and AI-generated concerns annotated with match type, severity, and post-rebuttal treatment. From this artifact we derive an evaluation ladder that moves from binary accuracy to concern detection, verdict-stratified behavior, decision-aware calibration, and rebuttal-aware decomposition. In a pilot study of four public AI review systems evaluated in six configurations, concern-level analysis suggests that detection alone does not determine review quality; calibration is often the binding constraint. Systems detect non-trivial fractions of official concerns yet most mark 25--55% of concerns on accepted papers as decisive, where, under our operationalization, no official concern on accepted papers was treated as a decisive blocker. Identical overall verdict accuracy can conceal reject-heavy behavior versus low-recall profiles, and low full-review false decisive rates can partly reflect concern dilution rather than calibrated prioritization. Most systems do not emit a native accept/reject, and inferring it from review tone is method-sensitive, reinforcing the need for concern-level diagnostics that remain stable across inference choices. The contribution is a reusable evaluation framework for auditing which concerns AI reviewers identify, how they weight them, and whether those priorities align with the review rationale that informed the paper's final assessment.

</details>


### [145] [Separable Pathways for Causal Reasoning: How Architectural Scaffolding Enables Hypothesis-Space Restructuring in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20039)
*John Alderete,Sebastian Benthal,Connie Xu,John Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将发展科学中的"blicket检测器"范式扩展到AI智能体，测试其假设空间重构能力。通过组合架构（上下文图+动态行为），在1085次实验中发现两者正交贡献：上下文图驱动假设空间内的推理质量（占94%准确率提升），动态行为通过检测机制变化并防止对过时假设的过早承诺来驱动推理资格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体缺乏在证据要求未构建过的表征时重构假设空间的能力。因果发现需要不仅在固定框架内更新信念，还需修订假设空间本身。

Method: 组合架构包含两个离散组件：上下文图（将探索结构化为类型化状态机）和动态行为（监控当前假设空间是否不足并在运行时扩展）。

Result: 1085次实验表明两个组件正交贡献：上下文图占假设空间内推理质量的94%准确率提升；动态行为通过检测机制变化防止过早承诺。

Conclusion: 假设空间重构需要组合方法：上下文图处理空间内推理，动态行为处理跨空间的机制变化检测与转换。

Abstract: Causal discovery through experimentation and intervention is fundamental to robust problem solving. It requires not just updating beliefs within a fixed framework but revising the hypothesis space itself, a capacity current AI agents lack when evidence demands representations they have not previously constructed.
