We extend the blicket detector paradigm from developmental science to test this capacity in AI agents equipped with architectural scaffolding that targets hypothesis-space restructuring. Our compositional architecture has two discrete components: context graphs, which structure exploration as typed state machines, and dynamic behaviors, which monitor for evidence that the current hypothesis space is inadequate and expand it at runtime. Across 1,085 experimental trials, these components make orthogonal contributions: context graphs drive reasoning quality within the post-switch hypothesis space, accounting for 94\% of the accuracy gain, while dynamic behaviors drive reasoning eligibility by detecting regime changes and preventing premature commitment to outdated hypotheses.

</details>


### [146] [From Fuzzy to Formal: Scaling Hospital Quality Improvement with AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20055)
*Patrick Vossler,Jean Feng,Venkat Sivaraman,Robert Gallo,Hemal Kanzaria,Dana Freiser,Christopher Ross,Amy Ou,James Marks,Susan Ehrlich,Christopher Peabody,Lucas Zier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"人机规范-方案协同优化"框架，将AI/LLM应用于医院质量改进(QI)因素发现。该框架将QI流程形式化为可调超参数的自然语言规范，通过迭代优化LLM提示和规范实现专家-AI协同。实验表明该方法在保持≥70%专家一致性的同时，比传统精益方法更高效，并能发现新的可改进因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医院质量改进中的因素发现(QI factor discovery)是传统人工驱动流程，耗时耗力且可重复性和可审计性有限。现有的AI对齐方法假设任务定义清晰，但QI发现本质上是探索性的、模糊的、迭代的理解过程，依赖复杂的隐式专家判断。因此需要一种能在保留探索性组件的同时形式化QI流程的AI方案。

Method: 提出"Human-AI Spec-Solution Co-optimization"框架，将QI因素发现映射为经典AI/ML开发流程（问题形式化、模型学习、模型验证），其中规范(specifications)作为可调超参数。领域专家与AI智能体迭代优化整体规范和AI管道，直到AI提取结果与专家注释一致并与临床目标对齐。该框架在一家城市安全网医院应用于识别延长住院时间和非计划30天再入院的关键因素。

Result: AI-for-QI管道达到≥70%的专家注释一致性，相比先前手动精益分析更高效。AI管道恢复了既往发现，挖掘出新的可修改因素，并产生了可审计的推理轨迹。

Conclusion: 所提出的"人机规范-方案协同优化"框架能够有效形式化QI流程同时保留其探索性组件，为AI辅助医院质量改进提供了可复制、可审计且高效的方法论基础。

Abstract: Hospital Quality Improvement (QI) plays a critical role in optimizing healthcare delivery by translating high-level hospital goals into actionable solutions. A critical step of QI is to identify the key modifiable contributing factors, a process we call QI factor discovery, typically through expert-driven semi-structured qualitative tools like fishbone diagrams, chart reviews, and Lean Healthcare methods. AI has the potential to transform and accelerate QI factor discovery, which is traditionally time- and resource-intensive and limited in reproducibility and auditability. Nevertheless, current AI alignment methods assume the task is well-defined, whereas QI factor discovery is an exploratory, fuzzy, and iterative sense-making process that relies on complex implicit expert judgments. To design an AI pipeline that formalizes the QI process while preserving its exploratory components, we propose viewing the task as learning not only LLM prompts but also the overarching natural-language specifications. In particular, we map QI factor discovery to steps of the classical AI/ML development process (problem formalization, model learning, and model validation) where the specifications are tunable hyperparameters. Domain experts and AI agents iteratively refine both the overarching specifications and AI pipeline until AI extractions are concordant with expert annotations and aligned with clinical objectives. We applied this "Human-AI Spec-Solution Co-optimization" framework at an urban safety-net hospital to identify factors driving prolonged length of stay and unplanned 30-day readmissions. The resulting AI-for-QI pipelines achieved $\ge 70\%$ concordance with expert annotations. Compared to prior manual Lean analyses, the AI pipeline was substantially more efficient, recovered previous findings, surfaced new modifiable factors, and produced auditable reasoning traces.

</details>


### [147] [EvoAgent: An Evolvable Agent Framework with Skill Learning and Multi-Agent Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20133)
*Aimin Zhang,Jiajing Guo,Fuwei Jia,Chen Lv,Boyu Wang,Fangzheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoAgent是一个可进化的大语言模型智能体框架，通过将技能建模为多文件结构化能力单元，结合三层记忆架构和三级技能匹配策略，实现复杂任务的动态分解与长期能力积累。在外贸场景中，基于GPT5.2的实验表明该框架可提升约28%的综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统在处理复杂跨文件任务时缺乏结构化的技能组织机制，且难以实现持续进化和长期能力积累。现有方法未能有效平衡任务分解效率与智能体架构的协同适配性。

Method: 提出EvoAgent框架：(1)将技能建模为带触发机制和进化元数据的多文件结构化能力单元；(2)设计用户反馈驱动的闭环技能生成与优化流程；(3)构建三级技能匹配策略实现动态任务分解；(4)建立三层记忆架构支持长期能力积累。

Result: 基于外贸场景的实验表明，集成EvoAgent后GPT5.2在专业性、准确性和实用性方面显著提升。五维度LLM-as-Judge评估中，综合平均得分提升约28%。模型迁移实验进一步揭示：智能体系统性能不仅取决于基础模型的内在能力，更依赖于模型与智能体架构之间的协同适配程度。

Conclusion: EvoAgent通过结构化技能学习与层次化子智能体委托机制，有效解决了复杂任务分解与长期能力积累问题。核心发现是模型与智能体架构的协同适配是决定系统性能的关键因素，为未来智能体系统设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: This paper proposes EvoAgent - an evolvable large language model (LLM) agent framework that integrates structured skill learning with a hierarchical sub-agent delegation mechanism. EvoAgent models skills as multi-file structured capability units equipped with triggering mechanisms and evolutionary metadata, and enables continuous skill generation and optimization through a user-feedback-driven closed-loop process. In addition, by incorporating a three-stage skill matching strategy and a three-layer memory architecture, the framework supports dynamic task decomposition for complex problems and long-term capability accumulation. Experimental results based on real-world foreign trade scenarios demonstrate that, after integrating EvoAgent, GPT5.2 achieves significant improvements in professionalism, accuracy, and practical utility. Under a five-dimensional LLM-as-Judge evaluation protocol, the overall average score increases by approximately 28%. Further model transfer experiments indicate that the performance of an agent system depends not only on the intrinsic capabilities of the underlying model, but also on the degree of synergy between the model and the agent architecture.

</details>


### [148] [HiPO: Hierarchical Preference Optimization for Adaptive Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20140)
*Darsh Kachroo,Adriana Caraeni,Arjun Prasaath Anbazhagan,Brennan Lagasse,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiPO通过将响应分层为推理段并加权计算各段DPO损失，在保持训练稳定性的同时实现细粒度偏好优化，显著提升了大语言模型在数学推理任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO在复杂推理任务中表现不佳，因为它将整个响应作为整体优化目标，缺乏对多步推理解的细粒度反馈能力；现有方法难以同时兼顾偏好学习的稳定性和结构化推理能力。

Method: HiPO将回复分解为推理段（查询澄清与上下文、推理步骤、答案），并计算每个段落DPO损失的加权总和作为总体损失，实现段落级别的特定训练。

Result: 多个7B LLMs在Math Stack Exchange偏好数据集上，使用HiPO微调的模型在多个数学基准测试中优于其他方法，并在GPT-4.1评估中展现出更优的组织结构、逻辑流程和一致性。

Conclusion: HiPO通过分层偏好优化在保持DPO计算效率和训练稳定性的同时，显著提升了模型在数学推理任务上的表现，实现了更优的组织结构、逻辑流程和一致性。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is an effective framework for aligning large language models with human preferences, but it struggles with complex reasoning tasks. DPO optimizes for the likelihood of generating preferred over dispreferred responses in their entirety and lacks the granularity to provide feedback on subsections of many-step solutions typical of reasoning tasks. Existing methods excel at either stable preference learning (e.g., DPO variants like KTO and RSO) or structured reasoning (e.g., ReMA's multi-agent RL framework, Tree of Thoughts), but fail to merge these complementary strengths. We propose HiPO (Hierarchical Preference Optimization), an extension of DPO that separates responses into reasoning segments (query clarification and context, reasoning steps, and answer) and computes loss as a weighted sum of the DPO loss for each segment. Our approach enables segment-specific training while maintaining DPO's computational efficiency and training stability. We demonstrate that for multiple 7B LLMs fine-tuned using HiPO and DPO on the Math Stack Exchange preference dataset, the models trained with HiPO outperform the others on a variety of common math benchmarks and achieve greater organization, logical flow, and consistency as measured by GPT-4.1.

</details>


### [149] [Stateless Decision Memory for Enterprise AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20158)
*Vasundra Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在受监管领域，企业部署长时程决策智能体时，尽管有众多有状态记忆架构，但实际主导方案仍是检索增强流水线。本文认为这是因为受监管部署对四项系统属性有刚性需求（有确定性回放、可审计理由、多租户隔离、无状态水平扩展），而有状态架构在结构上必然违反这些属性。作者提出确定性投影记忆(DPM)：一个仅追加的事件日志加上决策时的一次任务条件投影。实验表明，在10个受监管决策案例中，DPM在宽松预算下与摘要记忆相当，但在预算紧张时显著超越后者（20倍压缩比下，事实精度+0.52，推理一致性+0.53），且速度快7-15倍。作者认为无状态性是解释企业偏好的关键负载承载属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管领域（核保、理赔、税审），企业部署长时程决策智能体时，尽管有状态记忆架构已有十年发展，但实际主导方案仍是检索增强流水线。这种理论与实践的鸿沟需要解释——为什么更先进的有状态架构无法进入生产环境？

Method: 提出确定性投影记忆(DPM)架构，包含两个组件：(1) 仅追加的事件日志，记录所有交互；(2) 决策时的一次任务条件投影，将日志内容投影为决策上下文。DPM在设计上满足四项系统属性：确定性重放（append-only确保可重复）、可审计理由（单次调用结构简单）、多租户隔离（无跨任务状态共享）、无状态水平扩展（决策实例间无依赖）。

Result: 在10个受监管决策案例、三种记忆预算下实验：宽松预算时DPM与摘要记忆性能相当；预算紧张时（20倍压缩比），DPM显著优于摘要记忆，事实精度+0.52（Cohen's h=1.17, p=0.0014），推理一致性+0.53（h=1.13, p=0.0034）；速度提升7-15倍（决策时仅需1次LLM调用vs N次）。确定性研究中，DPM仅暴露1次非确定性调用，摘要记忆暴露N次累积调用；审计日志方面，DPM记录2次LLM调用，摘要记忆记录83-97次（LongHorizon-Bench）。

Conclusion: 无状态性(statelessness)是解释企业选择可回放但能力较弱的检索流水线的核心负载承载属性。DPM证明无需支付检索方案的性能代价即可获得该属性。论文还提出TAMS架构选择启发法和有状态记忆在企业运维条件下的失败分析框架，为实践者提供指导。

Abstract: Enterprise deployment of long-horizon decision agents in regulated domains (underwriting, claims adjudication, tax examination) is dominated by retrieval-augmented pipelines despite a decade of increasingly sophisticated stateful memory architectures. We argue this reflects a hidden requirement: regulated deployment is load-bearing on four systems properties (deterministic replay, auditable rationale, multi-tenant isolation, statelessness for horizontal scale), and stateful architectures violate them by construction. We propose Deterministic Projection Memory (DPM): an append-only event log plus one task-conditioned projection at decision time. On ten regulated decisioning cases at three memory budgets, DPM matches summarization-based memory at generous budgets and substantially outperforms it when the budget binds: at a 20x compression ratio, DPM improves factual precision by +0.52 (Cohen's h=1.17, p=0.0014) and reasoning coherence by +0.53 (h=1.13, p=0.0034), paired permutation, n=10. DPM is additionally 7-15x faster at binding budgets, making one LLM call at decision time instead of N. A determinism study of 10 replays per case at temperature zero shows both architectures inherit residual API-level nondeterminism, but the asymmetry is structural: DPM exposes one nondeterministic call; summarization exposes N compounding calls. The audit surface follows the same one-versus-N pattern: DPM logs two LLM calls per decision while summarization logs 83-97 on LongHorizon-Bench. We conclude with TAMS, a practitioner heuristic for architecture selection, and a failure analysis of stateful memory under enterprise operating conditions. The contribution is the argument that statelessness is the load-bearing property explaining enterprise's preference for weaker but replayable retrieval pipelines, and that DPM demonstrates this property is attainable without the decisioning penalty retrieval pays.

</details>


### [150] [Mol-Debate: Multi-Agent Debate Improves Structural Reasoning in Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20254)
*Wengyu Zhang,Xiao-Yong Wei,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mol-Debate 将「多智能体辩论」(Multi-Agent Debate, MAD) 引入文本引导的分子设计任务，提出一个 generate–debate–refine 的迭代式生成框架。框架由四类角色组成：Developer Agent（多个化学 LLM，负责生成候选 SMILES 与设计理由）、Examiner Agent（基于 RDKit 的确定性结构审计，提供分子量/LogP/TPSA/HBD/HBA/RotB/AroRings/Fsp3/QED/MR 等局部结构证据）、Debate Agent（K 个平行 Debater 依据语义意图与结构证据筛选候选 + Judge 以交集优先、并集兜底聚合）、Refiner Agent（把辩论分歧转化为学习信号，重写指令并回灌下一轮）。方法在 ChEBI-20 上取得 59.82% 的 exact match（较次优提升 6.03 个百分点），在 S²-Bench 上取得 75.22% SR / 50.52% WSR（WSR 较次优提升 5.77 个百分点），并保持 99.7% 以上的化学有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文聚焦文本引导分子设计（AI 驱动的药物发现核心能力），指出其根本障碍是「文本—结构鸿沟」(text-structure gap)：人类按顺序给出自然语言指令，而分子是环拓扑与官能团连接决定的非线性结构，且受严格化学约束（论文举例：环子结构在 SMILES 中被切分到不相邻的片段；甲氧基在文本中是一个词却对应多个原子与键）。
作者认为现有方法（RAG、CoT 提示、微调/RL 化学大模型）存在两点不足：(1) 只强化了少数「临时拼凑」的推理视角——RAG 偏外部知识检索，预训练/微调偏 token 对齐与数据驱动的模式学习，因而被各自设计的视角绑定；(2) 主流流水线是「一次性/单向生成」，错误一旦产生即为终局，缺乏动态的纠错与迭代。而真实药物发现依赖化学家整合高层意图、结构约束与设计权衡，通过多视角批判与迭代精化来调和语义意图与结构可行性。
多智能体系统 (MAS) 是有前景的整合途径，但存在「保持智能体独立性以避免群体思维 (groupthink) 与保证足够协同以形成一致决策」之间的张力：过度独立导致输出碎片化，过度协同则过早收敛、掩盖细微错误。多智能体辩论 (MAD, Du et al. 2023) 的机制天然平衡独立推理与协同协调，但其在分子设计中的应用仍是空白，因此本文以 pilot study 形式探索「辩论驱动的智能体推理能否更好弥合文本—结构鸿沟」。作者声明本文定位为「框架/生成范式」贡献：将已有化学模型视为可插拔的生成器。

Method: 整体形式化为 f = f_Dev ∘ f_Exam ∘ f_Deb ∘ f_Refiner，把指令 X 映射为 SMILES S；第 r 轮状态为 T^r = (X^r, P^r, A)，轮末更新 (X^{r+1}, P^{r+1})。算法 1 的单轮流程为：Developer 扩展候选池 → Examiner 产出结构证据 → K 个 Debater 依证据筛选 → Judge 聚合 → 若共识不足则 Refiner 重写指令并重新生成候选。
一、Developer-Debater Conflict（开发者—辩论者冲突）：化学专家型模型化学合理但语言/通用推理适应性弱，故解耦角色。N 个 Developer 每个提出 M_n 个候选并给出显式设计理由 ℛ（追求广度而非完全满足约束）；K 个通用型 Debater 负责语义意图裁决。
二、Global-Local Structural Reasoning（全局—局部结构推理）：仅靠文本级辩论无法发现局部无效性。Examiner Agent 作为确定性审计器（基于 RDKit），对每个候选计算描述符集合 Π（MW、LogP、TPSA、HBD、HBA、RotB、AroRings、Fsp3、QED、MR），产出检查报告 E^r = {e(S)}，构成「局部结构视角」；Debater 的输入升级为 (X^r, P^r, E^r)，使选择同时考虑全局分子语法与子结构的局部化学现实。为避免评估泄漏，在 S²-Bench 的 LogP/MR/QED 子任务中 Examiner 不报告对应描述符。
三、Static-Dynamic Integration（静态—动态整合）：将 agent 间分歧视为学习信号而非失败。Refiner Agent 依据 (X^r, P^r, P^{r+1}, E^r, D^r) 诊断指令歧义与缺失约束，产出澄清后的指令 X^{r+1} 回灌 Developer，闭合反馈回路，使系统在推理期主动重构对查询的理解。
共识与终止：以 Debater 子集间的 Jaccard 相似度均值定义共识分 C_r = (1/C(K,2)) Σ_{i<j} |P_i∩P_j|/|P_i∪P_j|；停止指示 SI^r = 1 当且仅当 (C_r ≥ θ 且 |P^{r+1}| = 1) 或 r ≥ r_max。结束后由 SelectOne() 从最终稳定池中均匀采样一个分子输出。
实现细节：r_max = 4，K = 2，θ = 0.6；Developer 使用 ChemDFM-R-14B、ChemDFM-v1.5-8B、Chem-R-8B（ChEBI-20 候选数 4/2/2；S²-Bench 用 ChemDFM-R-14B 与 Chem-R-8B，各 3）；Debate/Refiner 在主结果用 GPT-5 mini、消融用 GPT-4o mini；8× NVIDIA H20，vLLM + OpenAI API，temperature 1、top-p 0.8、seed 42、max new tokens 4096/1024。评测：ChEBI-20（33,010 对 caption–molecule，指标 EM/BLEU/Lev./MA.F/RD.F/MO.F/FCD/Val.）与 S²-Bench（MolCustom/MolEdit/MolOpt 各 5,000 条，共 15,000 条，SR/WSR）。附录给出 Developer/Debater/Refiner 的完整提示词（均采用 <think>/<answer> 标签；Refiner 被严格约束不得改变原意、关键词或新增要求）。

Result: 主结果（ChEBI-20，Table 1）：Mol-Debate EM = 0.5982，为全场最佳，较次优的 ChemDFM-v1.5-8B（0.5379）绝对提升 6.03 个百分点；BLEU 0.7059、MA.F 0.9601、RD.F 0.9103、MO.F 0.8694、FCD 0.1258 均为最佳，Validity 0.9973 保持近乎满分，优于所有基线（包括 MolT5-large 0.3110、BioT5-base 0.4130、MolReGPT 0.2800、MSR 0.4210、Chem-R-8B 0.4385、ChemDFM-R-14B 0.4948、GPT-5 mini 0.1773 等）。
S²-Bench（Table 2）：平均 SR = 0.7522、WSR = 0.5052 均为最佳（GPT-5 mini 为 0.7455/0.4475，WSR 提升 5.77 个百分点）；其中 MolEdit SR 0.9399、WSR 0.6432 领先明显，MolOpt SR 0.9203、WSR 0.6052 亦最佳，而 MolCustom SR 0.3965 略低于 GPT-5 mini 的 0.5045（其 WSR 0.2671 亦落后于 GPT-5 mini 的 0.3323）。
多视角消融（Table 3）：D+G（化学 Developer + 通用 Debater）最佳（EM 0.5542）；D+D（开发者自辩，ChemDFM-R 替代 GPT-4o mini）降至 0.4942，说明开发者会收敛到「化学有效但指令错位」的分子；G+G（纯通用模型辩论）崩塌至 0.0206，说明仅语言批判在化学约束下不足。移除 Examiner（EM 0.5030、Val 0.9748）与移除 Refiner（EM 0.5324、Val 1.0000）均下降，且 Refiner 主要影响意图满足度、Examiner 主要影响化学有效性。Figure 4 显示 D+D 在各轮共识分最低且 EM 随辩论退化，去掉 Examiner 后共识分增长更慢，去掉 Refiner 后候选池偏小、探索受限。
超参消融（Table 4/13–15）：K=2 最优（K=3: 0.5485，K=4: 0.5373）；轮数 R 越多 EM 单调上升（R=1: 0.5358 → R=4: 0.5542）且 Val 保持 1.0000；θ=0.6 最优（0.4: 0.5361，0.8: 0.5485），提示「严格一致」与「化学多样性」需平衡。
可扩展性（Table 9–12）：将 Mol-Debate 叠加到任意 backbone（GPT-4o mini、GPT-5 mini、ChemDFM-R-14B、Gemma-4-31b-it）上均提升其性能（如 ChemDFM-R-14B EM 0.4948→0.4994、Val 0.9733→0.9967；Gemma EM 0.0452→0.1694、Val 0.7061→0.9297）；Debate/Refiner 换成更强的通用模型（GPT-4o mini→GPT-5 mini→GPT-5.2）持续改善，但 GPT-5.2 相对 GPT-5 mini 仅边际增益（EM 0.5982→0.6006），显示收益递减。案例分析（Figure 5、8、9）展示了从局部证据剔除低可靠候选、因分歧触发 Refiner 澄清约束、池扩大再收敛到单一致候选的完整轨迹。
局限（作者自述）：多智能体多轮推理带来显著高于单次生成的推理开销，尤其在交互式场景；Examiner 仅提供确定性有效性检查与基础属性画像，无法保证可合成性与生物活性等高层目标；面对欠指定指令时可能存在多个合法解，最终输出受辩论偏好与成功判据粒度影响，可控性不足。

Conclusion: 本文的核心结论是：文本引导分子设计的关键瓶颈是持续存在的「文本—结构鸿沟」，而弥合它需要动态的、多视角的批判与迭代精化，而非固定少数视角的一次性生成。Mol-Debate 用 generate–debate–refine 循环把 Developer（化学探索）、Examiner（确定性局部结构证据）、Debate（在证据下的意图裁决）、Refiner（指令重写与反馈）四者耦合，在 ChEBI-20 与 S²-Bench 上取得 SOTA（EM 59.82%、平均 WSR 50.52%）并保持极高化学有效性，且可作为可插拔框架叠加到任意 backbone LLM 上。
三点方法论启示：(1) 异质技能的互补优于同质模型的堆叠——通用模型独自辩论在化学约束下几乎失效（EM 2.06%），而「化学专长生成 + 通用语义裁决」的分工最优；(2) 确定性工具（分子描述符）提供的局部结构证据是抑制化学幻觉、加速共识的必要锚点；(3) 把分歧当作学习信号去重写指令，可在不损失有效性的前提下显著提升语义意图对齐。
作者指出未来方向：改进 Judge 策略与更紧的早停准则以降低推理成本；为 Examiner 引入可合成性/活性等任务相关评估器并把更丰富的证据注入辩论；通过显式暴露偏好提升最终选择可控性。附录 A.5 进一步指出「迭代式多智能体批判以对齐语义意图与结构化输出」的范式有望推广到多模态推理、手语翻译、生物医学成像、图像编辑等依赖反复调和自然语言指令与结构化/受约束输出的科学与多模态任务。整体而言，这是一篇定位为「框架 + 试点研究」的预印本工作，方法新颖性强、实验覆盖较完整（含多层次消融与可扩展性验证），但推理成本高、Examiner 能力有限、部分闭源强模型依赖（GPT-5 mini/GPT-4o mini 充当 Debater 与 Refiner）以及缺乏可合成性层面的评估，是其后续需要补强的方向。

Abstract: Text-guided molecular design is a key capability for AI-driven drug discovery, yet it remains challenging to map sequential natural-language instructions with non-linear molecular structures under strict chemical constraints. Most existing approaches, including RAG, CoT prompting, and fine-tuning or RL, emphasize a small set of ad-hoc reasoning perspectives implemented in a largely one-shot generation pipeline. In contrast, real-world drug discovery relies on dynamic, multi-perspective critique and iterative refinement to reconcile semantic intent with structural feasibility. Motivated by this, we propose Mol-Debate, a generation paradigm that enables such dynamic reasoning through an iterative generate-debate-refine loop. We further characterize key challenges in this paradigm and address them through perspective-oriented orchestration, including developer-debater conflict, global-local structural reasoning, and static-dynamic integration. Experiments demonstrate that Mol-Debate achieves state-of-the-art performance against strong general and chemical baselines, reaching 59.82% exact match on ChEBI-20 and 50.52% weighted success rate on S$^2$-Bench. Our code is available at https://github.com/wyuzh/Mol-Debate.

</details>


### [151] [Memory-Augmented LLM-based Multi-Agent System for Automated Feature Generation on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20261)
*Fengxian Dong,Zhi Zheng,Xiao Han,Wei Chen,Jingqing Ruan,Tong Xu,Yong Chen,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MALMAS是一种基于大语言模型的多智能体自动化特征生成系统，通过路由智能体激活不同职责的子智能体扩展特征空间，并结合程序记忆、反馈记忆和概念记忆实现迭代优化，在多个公开数据集上验证了其相对于现有最先进方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动化特征生成方法依赖预定义算子库，无法利用任务语义信息，难以生成多样化、高价值的特征用于复杂任务；现有基于LLM的方法虽引入了语义信号，但仍受限于固定的生成模式且缺乏来自学习目标的反馈机制。

Method: 提出MALMAS系统：1) 将特征生成过程分解为具有不同职责的多个智能体；2) 引入路由智能体在每次迭代中激活适当的智能体子集，进一步扩展特征空间的探索；3) 集成包含程序记忆、反馈记忆和概念记忆的记忆模块，实现迭代优化并自适应引导后续特征生成，提升特征质量和多样性。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上进行了广泛实验，与现有最先进方法进行对比，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过多智能体协作与记忆增强机制，MALMAS能够有效突破固定生成模式的限制，实现更广泛、更优质的自动化特征生成。

Abstract: Automated feature generation extracts informative features from raw tabular data without manual intervention and is crucial for accurate, generalizable machine learning. Traditional methods rely on predefined operator libraries and cannot leverage task semantics, limiting their ability to produce diverse, high-value features for complex tasks. Recent Large Language Model (LLM)-based approaches introduce richer semantic signals, but still suffer from a restricted feature space due to fixed generation patterns and from the absence of feedback from the learning objective. To address these challenges, we propose a Memory-Augmented LLM-based Multi-Agent System (\textbf{MALMAS}) for automated feature generation. MALMAS decomposes the generation process into agents with distinct responsibilities, and a Router Agent activates an appropriate subset of agents per iteration, further broadening exploration of the feature space. We further integrate a memory module comprising procedural memory, feedback memory, and conceptual memory, enabling iterative refinement that adaptively guides subsequent feature generation and improves feature quality and diversity. Extensive experiments on multiple public datasets against state-of-the-art baselines demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. The code is available at https://github.com/fxdong24/MALMAS

</details>


### [152] [ActuBench: A Multi-Agent LLM Pipeline for Generation and Evaluation of Actuarial Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20273)
*Jan-Philipp Schmidt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ActuBench是一个多智能体LLM流水线，用于自动生成符合国际精算协会教育大纲的精算评估题目。该系统通过四个Adapter分离的LLM角色（出题、构造干扰项、验证修复、摘要标注）实现协作生产。评测了50个模型（8个提供商），包含100道最难单选题和100道开放式题目。结果表明：多智能体验证是核心组件、开源模型在成本-性能权衡上具有竞争力、LLM评判比传统单选题更能区分前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精算专业评估需要与IAA教育大纲对齐的高质量题目，但传统人工出题成本高、效率低。现有LLM评估存在两个问题：多智能体验证机制的重要性未充分探索，以及精算领域特定评估的空白。

Method: 1. 多智能体分工架构：使用Adapter分离四个LLM角色——出题智能体构造题目、干扰项智能体构建选项、验证智能体独立校验前两阶段并驱动修复循环、辅助智能体负责Wikipedia笔记摘要和话题标注。2. 双基准评测：100道最难单选题（实证筛选）+100道开放式题目（LLM评判）。3. 评测规模：50个语言模型，来自8个提供商。4. 可视化展示：发布可浏览网页界面展示题目、模型响应和排行榜。

Result: 1. 多智能体验证有效：独立验证器在首轮标记大部分出题，修复循环解决了其中大部分问题。2. 开源模型成本-性能突出：本地运行的Gemma~4模型和Cerebras托管的120B开源模型在近零成本区域表现优异，后者几乎达到排行榜榜首。3. MCQ与LLM评判排名差异显著：单选题框架抬高了性能天花板，LLM评判模式更能区分前沿模型。

Conclusion: ActuBench通过多智能体协作范式，证明了自动化生成高质量精算评估题目的可行性。研究表明验证智能体是关键组件，一次性修复循环能有效提升题目质量。本地开源模型和中等规模模型在成本-性能权衡上具有竞争力，刷新了精算AI应用的成本基准。多智能体协作模式值得推广到其他专业领域的自动评测系统构建。

Abstract: We present ActuBench, a multi-agent LLM pipeline for the automated generation and evaluation of advanced actuarial assessment items aligned with the International Actuarial Association (IAA) Education Syllabus. The pipeline separates four LLM roles by adapter: one agent drafts items, one constructs distractors, a third independently verifies both stages and drives bounded one-shot repair loops, and a cost-optimized auxiliary agent handles Wikipedia-note summarization and topic labelling. The items, per-model responses and complete leaderboard are published as a browsable web interface at https://actubench.de/en/, allowing readers and practitioners to inspect individual items without a repository checkout. We evaluate 50 language models from eight providers on two complementary benchmarks -- 100 empirically hardest multiple-choice items and 100 open-ended items scored by an LLM judge -- and report three headline findings. First, multi-agent verification is load-bearing: the independent verifier flags a majority of drafted items on first pass, most of which the one-shot repair loop resolves. Second, locally-hosted open-weights inference sits on the cost-performance Pareto front: a Gemma~4 model running on consumer hardware and a Cerebras-hosted 120B open-weights model dominate the near-zero-cost region, with the latter within one item of the top of the leaderboard. Third, MCQ and LLM-as-Judge rankings differ meaningfully: the MCQ scaffold inflates the performance ceiling, and Judge-mode evaluation is needed to discriminate at the frontier.

</details>


### [153] [FSFM: A Biologically-Inspired Framework for Selective Forgetting of Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20300)
*Yingjie Gu,Bo Xiong,Yijuan Guo,Chao Li,Xiaojing Zhang,Liqiang Wang,Pengcheng Ren,Qi Sun,Jingyao Ma,Shidang Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FSFM（Forgetting to Remember More）提出一个受神经科学启发的LLM智能体记忆选择性遗忘框架：以海马体索引/巩固理论和艾宾浩斯遗忘曲线为理论基础，建立"被动衰减、主动删除、安全触发、自适应强化"四类遗忘策略的分类学，并以多维度重要性评分（CQA内容质量、BVE业务价值、TRS时效相关、SRC安全风险）驱动记忆剪枝。基于向量数据库与三层记忆架构（感觉/工作/长时），在中国移动"灵犀"336万条真实交互数据（纵切广东443,902条＋横切31省433,686条）上的对照实验显示：存储占用下降30%、检索延迟从约11.1s降至8.5s（吞吐+30%）、危险内容100%消除、重要内容保留约70%、敏感内容减少约46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆研究集中在保留与检索优化，把记忆当作无限膨胀、永久保留的仓库，在实际部署中暴露出五类问题：(1)资源约束——存储与检索开销随运行时长持续攀升，长期部署在经济与技术上都不可持续；(2)记忆质量下降——问候语、重复提问、进度确认等冗余信息占据有效记忆空间，干扰后续检索；(3)信息过时——用户偏好、事实知识与上下文相关性随时间演化，旧信息失效甚至误导决策；(4)安全脆弱面——无差别保留造成凭据、隐私、危险言论长期暴露，且易受记忆投毒攻击操纵；(5)隐私合规冲突——与GDPR"被遗忘权"及数据最小化原则相抵触。作者主张"遗忘不是bug而是feature"，核心论点是：在资源受限环境中，设计良好的选择性遗忘机制与记忆保留同等重要，可同时提升效率、质量与安全三个维度。

Method: 框架层面：①理论基础映射——海马体索引理论→向量嵌入稀疏索引与低干扰检索；艾宾浩斯曲线→Retention(t)=e^{-λt}的时间衰减；突触修剪与记忆再巩固→按使用频率、功能相关性重算重要度、检索后更新分数；②遗忘策略分类学——被动衰减（可配置λ、支持间隔重复）、主动删除（用户请求/安全内容/合规/去重）、安全触发、自适应强化（用户反馈、使用频率与近因、上下文相关、他人共识）；③概率化多因子保留模型Retention Probability = f(time, frequency, emotional_valence, contextual_relevance, security_compliance, social_consensus)，并将遗忘策略优化形式化为RL问题（状态、动作、复合奖励）；④信息论视角（率失真优化、Shannon源编码）；⑤三类遗忘算法与容量约束下的最优遗忘集F=argmin Σ s_i s.t. |M−F|≤C。架构层面为四模块：UltraSafeMemoryManager（激进GC、实时监控节流、checkpoint容错、容量约束优雅降级）、ImportanceScoringEngine（内容质量0-3、业务价值0-3、安全风险-10/-2、时间相关TRS）、SelectiveForgettingMechanism（可配置策略、批处理、增量执行、审计日志、按重要度优先级队列剪枝）、PerformanceBenchmarkingTool（纳秒级计时，效率/检索/安全/保真四类指标）。记忆组织为感觉—工作（容量7±2，任务完成即清空）—长时三层结构，重要性分数以元数据形式随向量嵌入存入向量数据库（Pinecone/Milvus类），不破坏检索功能。实验层面：A/B并行对照（FSFM限容70%、Baseline无限容"记住一切"），70/30训练验证划分，批处理100条，10轮不同随机种子重复，双尾t检验p<0.001；数据来自中国移动"灵犀"营销服务智能助手2025.08–2026.03的336万条原始记录，纵向单省（广东443,902条）与横向31省（433,686条）双维度采样，五类内容分级（重要/一般/普通/敏感/危险），危险数据取自NVIDIA Aegis-1.0开源数据集随机1,000条（13类安全风险）；超参搜索覆盖衰减率λ（0.01–0.5）、重要度权重网格搜索（最终α=0.4, β=0.3, γ=0.2, δ=0.1）、批量剪枝比例（5%/10%/20%，取10%）。

Result: 纵向数据集：存储占用降至70%上限（-30%）；平均查询延迟8.56±0.21s vs 基线11.12±0.35s（快30%）；吞吐58.5±1.2 vs 45.0 q/min（+30%）；危险内容保留率0%（100%消除）；敏感内容保留54.1%（-45.9%）；重要数据保留70.4%（-29.6%，作为容量-质量权衡）；普通数据保留99.99%（p=0.35，无显著差异）。横向数据集结果高度一致：延迟8.42±0.19s（快31%）、吞吐59.2±1.3（+31.6%）、危险内容0%、敏感保留52.8%（-47.2%）、重要保留71.2%。全部关键指标p<0.001，各轮标准差均<2%均值。图表分析进一步给出：三类保留曲线（经典衰减λ=0.1、低价值λ=0.85、高价值阶梯式强化在第2/5/10天重置至0.95/0.90/0.85）；优化过程呈"快速下降(0-20)→精调(20-60)→收敛(60-80)"三阶段，约束违反降为0；与随机遗忘、Old-First（按年龄删除）相比，FSFM在内存效率、处理速度、内容保留准确率上全面更优且计算开销更低。

Conclusion: FSFM将"选择性遗忘"确立为下一代LLM智能体记忆系统的原生能力而非缺陷，给出从神经科学概念到可落地的分类学、架构规格、算法与工程实现的完整路径，并在真实运营商级数据上验证了效率、质量、安全的三维收益（存储-30%、检索约1.3倍加速、危险内容100%消除且高价值内容保留70%以上），形成容量—质量—安全的Pareto式权衡。作者给出部署建议（初期80%容量上限逐步收紧、按业务调权、小流量灰度、持续监控、预置被遗忘权删除策略），并指出局限：受测试环境限制未做全量验证、数据集中于电信域缺乏跨域外部效度、实验时间跨度有限未观测长期累积效应、未纳入主观用户满意度指标；未来方向包括跨域验证、长期部署监控、用户体验研究、跨模态遗忘、元学习与基于强化学习的自适应遗忘策略，以及伦理与监管合规的持续讨论。

Abstract: For LLM agents, memory management critically impacts efficiency, quality, and security. While much research focuses on retention, selective forgetting--inspired by human cognitive processes (hippocampal indexing/consolidation theory and Ebbinghaus forgetting curve)--remains underexplored. We argue that in resource-constrained environments, a well-designed forgetting mechanism is as crucial as remembering, delivering benefits across three dimensions: (1) efficiency via intelligent memory pruning, (2) quality by dynamically updating outdated preferences and context, and (3) security through active forgetting of malicious inputs, sensitive data, and privacy-compromising content. Our framework establishes a taxonomy of forgetting mechanisms: passive decay-based, active deletion-based, safety-triggered, and adaptive reinforcement-based. Building on advances in LLM agent architectures and vector databases, we present detailed specifications, implementation strategies, and empirical validation from controlled experiments. Results show significant improvements: access efficiency (+8.49%), content quality (+29.2% signal-to-noise ratio), and security performance (100% elimination of security risks). Our work bridges cognitive neuroscience and AI systems, offering practical solutions for real-world deployment while addressing ethical and regulatory compliance. The paper concludes with challenges and future directions, establishing selective forgetting as a fundamental capability for next-generation LLM agents operating in real-world, resource-constrained scenarios. Our contributions align with AI-native memory systems and responsible AI development.

</details>


### [154] [Self-Awareness before Action: Mitigating Logical Inertia via Proactive Cognitive Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20413)
*Fulong Fan,Peilin Liu,Fengzhe Liu,Shuyan Yang,Gang Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SABA是一种推理框架，通过引入对缺失前提的自我意识来解决大语言模型在推理过程中因早期假设错误而导致结论不稳定的问题。该框架在非交互式侦探谜题基准上取得最佳性能，并在多个公开基准上保持领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在推理任务中表现良好，但缺乏对自身知识或推理状态是否完整的认知能力。在非交互式谜题设置中，叙事固定而底层结构隐藏，模型一旦在前提不完整时形成早期假设，就会将错误传播到整个推理过程，导致结论不稳定。

Method: SABA将推理表述为结构化状态构建与障碍解决交替的递归过程：1）信息融合（Information Fusion）：将叙事整合为可验证的基础状态；2）查询驱动结构化推理（Query-driven Structured Reasoning）：识别并解决缺失或不完整的前提，将其转化为查询，通过假设构建和状态细化逐步完善推理状态。

Result: 在所有三个难度分组的非交互式侦探谜题基准测试中，SABA均达到最佳性能；在多个公开基准上也保持领先结果。

Conclusion: 通过显式引入对缺失前提的自我意识，SABA能有效避免错误假设的传播，提高推理稳定性，在复杂推理任务中表现出色。

Abstract: Large language models perform well on many reasoning tasks, yet they often lack awareness of whether their current knowledge or reasoning state is complete. In non-interactive puzzle settings, the narrative is fixed and the underlying structure is hidden; once a model forms an early hypothesis under incomplete premises, it can propagate that error throughout the reasoning process, leading to unstable conclusions. To address this issue, we propose SABA, a reasoning framework that explicitly introduces self-awareness of missing premises before making the final decision. SABA formulates reasoning as a recursive process that alternates between structured state construction and obstacle resolution: it first applies Information Fusion to consolidate the narrative into a verifiable base state, and then uses Query-driven Structured Reasoning to identify and resolve missing or underspecified premises by turning them into queries and progressively completing the reasoning state through hypothesis construction and state refinement. Across multiple evaluation metrics, SABA achieves the best performance on all three difficulty splits of the non-interactive Detective Puzzle benchmark, and it also maintains leading results on multiple public benchmarks.

</details>


### [155] [MedSkillAudit: A Domain-Specific Audit Framework for Medical Research Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20441)
*Yingyong Hou,Xinyuan Lao,Huimei Wang,Qianyu Yao,Wei Chen,Bocheng Huang,Fei Sun,Yuxian Lv,Weiqi Lei,Xueqian Wen,Pengfei Xia,Zhujun Tan,Shengyang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了MedSkillAudit框架，用于在部署前评估医疗研究AI智能体技能的质量与安全性。评估75项技能后，系统与专家共识评分的一致性(ICC=0.449)超过了人类专家间的评估一致性(ICC=0.300)，表明该审计框架具有实际应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体系统在医学研究中的应用增长，现有通用评估方法不足以确保医疗研究技能的科学完整性、方法学有效性和边界安全性，需要开发专门针对医学领域的审计框架。

Method: 采用分层评估框架MedSkillAudit(skill-auditor@1.0)，由两位专家独立评估75项技能(分5类，每类15项)，评分维度包括：质量分数(0-100)、发布处置(生产就绪/限制发布/测试版/拒绝)及高风险失败标记。使用ICC(2,1)和加权Cohen's kappa量化系统-专家一致性，与人类评估者间一致性进行基准比较。

Result: 平均质量评分72.4(SD=13.0)；57.3%技能低于限制发布阈值。MedSkillAudit的ICC(2,1)=0.449(95%CI:0.250-0.610)，超过人类评估者间ICC=0.300。系统-共识评分差异(SD=9.5)小于专家间差异(SD=12.4)，无方向性偏差(Wilcoxon p=0.613)。方案设计类一致性最强(ICC=0.551)；学术写作类ICC为负值(-0.567)，反映评分标准与专家判断的结构性不匹配。

Conclusion: 领域特定的部署前审计可为医疗研究智能体技能治理提供实用基础，通过针对科学用例定制的结构化审计工作流程补充通用质量检查。其中学术写作类需调整评估标准以解决rubric与专家判断的错配问题。

Abstract: Background: Agent skills are increasingly deployed as modular, reusable capability units in AI agent systems. Medical research agent skills require safeguards beyond general-purpose evaluation, including scientific integrity, methodological validity, reproducibility, and boundary safety. This study developed and preliminarily evaluated a domain-specific audit framework for medical research agent skills, with a focus on reliability against expert review. Methods: We developed MedSkillAudit (skill-auditor@1.0), a layered framework assessing skill release readiness before deployment. We evaluated 75 skills across five medical research categories (15 per category). Two experts independently assigned a quality score (0-100), an ordinal release disposition (Production Ready / Limited Release / Beta Only / Reject), and a high-risk failure flag. System-expert agreement was quantified using ICC(2,1) and linearly weighted Cohen's kappa, benchmarked against the human inter-rater baseline. Results: The mean consensus quality score was 72.4 (SD = 13.0); 57.3% of skills fell below the Limited Release threshold. MedSkillAudit achieved ICC(2,1) = 0.449 (95% CI: 0.250-0.610), exceeding the human inter-rater ICC of 0.300. System-consensus score divergence (SD = 9.5) was smaller than inter-expert divergence (SD = 12.4), with no directional bias (Wilcoxon p = 0.613). Protocol Design showed the strongest category-level agreement (ICC = 0.551); Academic Writing showed a negative ICC (-0.567), reflecting a structural rubric-expert mismatch. Conclusions: Domain-specific pre-deployment audit may provide a practical foundation for governing medical research agent skills, complementing general-purpose quality checks with structured audit workflows tailored to scientific use cases.

</details>


### [156] [Measuring the Machine: Evaluating Generative AI as Pluralist Sociotechical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20545)
*Rebecca L. Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判了当前生成式AI评估中的功能主义和规范主义方法，提出Machine-Society-Human (MaSH) Loops框架，将AI评估重新定义为多元化的社会技术系统评估，强调价值观在交互中的递归建构，而非静态基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评估基准存在根本性问题：功能主义将模型视为孤立预测器，规范主义关注系统"应然"状态，两者都忽视了AI评估中社会技术过程的复杂性，在多元文化背景下可能导致狭隘文化视角的固化。静态基准无法捕捉模型、用户和机构如何递归共构意义与价值观。

Method: 概念层面：提出MaSH Loops框架，将评估重构为递归的、生成性的过程。方法论层面：开发World Values Benchmark，采用基于世界价值观调查数据的分布方法、结构化提示集和锚定感知评分。实证层面：通过两个案例验证：早期GPT-3的价值漂移分析和房地产领域的社会技术评估。

Result: MaSH Loops框架成功展示了如何追踪模型、用户和机构如何递归共构意义和价值观。World Values Benchmark提供了在多元文化背景下分布性评估价值观的方法。GPT-3案例揭示了早期模型的价值漂移现象；房地产案例证明了社会技术评估在实际应用中的可行性。

Conclusion: 静态基准不足以评估生成式AI。负责任的评估需要多元化的、过程导向的框架，能够揭示谁的价值观念被践行。评估本身就是治理的场所，塑造着AI系统如何被理解、部署和信任。AI评估应从评判输出转向审视价值观如何在交互中实现。

Abstract: In measurement theory, instruments do not simply record reality; they help constitute what is observed. The same holds for generative AI evaluation: benchmarks do not just measure, they shape what models appear to be. Functionalist benchmarks treat models as isolated predictors, while prescriptive approaches assess what systems ought to be. Both obscure the sociotechnical processes through which meaning and values are enacted, risking the reification of narrow cultural perspectives in pluralist contexts.
  This thesis advances a descriptive alternative. It argues that generative AI must be evaluated as a pluralist sociotechnical system and develops Machine-Society-Human (MaSH) Loops, a framework for tracing how models, users, and institutions recursively co-construct meaning and values. Evaluation shifts from judging outputs to examining how values are enacted in interaction.
  Three contributions follow. Conceptually, MaSH Loops reframes evaluation as recursive, enactive process. Methodologically, the World Values Benchmark introduces a distributional approach grounded in World Values Survey data, structured prompt sets, and anchor-aware scoring. Empirically, the thesis demonstrates these through two cases: value drift in early GPT-3 and sociotechnical evaluation in real estate. A final chapter draws on participatory realism to argue that prompting and evaluation are constitutive interventions, not neutral observations.
  The thesis argues that static benchmarks are insufficient for generative AI. Responsible evaluation requires pluralist, process-oriented frameworks that make visible whose values are enacted. Evaluation is therefore a site of governance, shaping how AI systems are understood, deployed, and trusted.

</details>


### [157] [Self-Guided Plan Extraction for Instruction-Following Tasks with Goal-Conditional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20601)
*Zoya Volovikova,Nikita Sorokin,Dmitriy Lukashevskiy,Aleksandr Panov,Alexey Skrynnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SuperIgor是一个用于指令跟随任务的框架，通过自学习机制让语言模型生成和优化高层规划，减少对人工标注数据集的依赖。采用RL智能体与语言模型迭代协同训练，形成反馈循环使两者共同提升。在动态随机环境中的实验表明，SuperIgor agents能更严格地遵循指令，并对未见过的指令展现出强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖预定义的子任务，需要大量人工标注数据集，限制了系统的灵活性和可扩展性。

Method: 引入自学习机制使语言模型能够生成和优化高层规划；采用迭代协同训练：RL agent学习遵循生成的规划，语言模型根据RL反馈和偏好进行自适应调整，形成双向反馈循环实现agent和planner的联合改进。

Result: SuperIgor agents比基线方法更严格地遵循指令指令，且对未见过的指令展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架在具有丰富动态性和随机性的环境中得到验证，为指令跟随任务提供了一种减少人工标注依赖的有效方案。

Abstract: We introduce SuperIgor, a framework for instruction-following tasks. Unlike prior methods that rely on predefined subtasks, SuperIgor enables a language model to generate and refine high-level plans through a self-learning mechanism, reducing the need for manual dataset annotation. Our approach involves iterative co-training: an RL agent is trained to follow the generated plans, while the language model adapts and modifies these plans based on RL feedback and preferences. This creates a feedback loop where both the agent and the planner improve jointly. We validate our framework in environments with rich dynamics and stochasticity. Results show that SuperIgor agents adhere to instructions more strictly than baseline methods, while also demonstrating strong generalization to previously unseen instructions.

</details>


### [158] [CHORUS: An Agentic Framework for Generating Realistic Deliberation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20651)
*A. Koursaris,G. Domalis,A. Apostolopoulou,K. Kanaris,D. Tsakalidis,I. E. Livieris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Chorus 是一个基于 LLM 的智能体（agentic）仿真框架，通过编排具备行为一致人格（persona）的 LLM 角色来生成真实的审议讨论数据；参与时机由泊松过程时间模型控制，并配套结构化工具以对接真实网络平台。该框架已在 Deliberate 平台部署，经 30 位专家从内容真实性、讨论连贯性、分析效用三个维度评估（均分分别为 4.6/4.1/4.3，5 点李克特量表），验证其可作为在线话语分析的高质量合成审议数据来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线审议（deliberation）数据是研究在线话语动态、构建 NLP 基准的关键资源，但因平台访问限制、隐私伦理约束与数据集质量不一致而长期稀缺。已有仿真框架（Smallville、OASIS 等）主要面向目标驱动任务或封闭虚拟环境，缺乏语义连贯的多轮审议对话生成能力；现有基于人格的方法多用于安全测试，难以维持长期上下文一致性；更关键的是，这些方法均缺乏对真实平台异质参与时间模式的原则性建模机制。因此需要一个兼具行为保真度、时间现实性与上下文感知能力的框架，直接在交互式平台上生成合成的审议数据。

Method: 框架要点如下：(1) 角色模型：实例化 N 个 actor，每个由结构化 persona（传记背景、沟通风格、核心信念、参与模式）约束，并维护个人发帖历史 H_i^post 与投票动作历史 H_i^action，实现对演化中讨论的持续感知；(2) 时间动力学：刻意不让 LLM 自行决定是否发言（避免模型先验偏置），改用每个角色独立的泊松过程，参数为发帖速率 λ_i^post 与动作速率 λ_i^action，通过全局优先队列调度事件，直至队列耗尽或达到时域 T；(3) 动作过程 Action：Agent 基于全部帖子历史与个人动作历史选出 M 篇候选帖，对每篇执行伯努利试验（u ~ U(0,1) > θ_i^action 才投票），θ 作为选择性阈值，并记录以避免重复互动；(4) 发帖过程 Post：以 p_i^reply 决定回复或新评论；回复时 Agent 从他人帖子中选定目标并表态同意/反对，过程中可选调用共享工具集 T（如网络检索）；(5) 工具集包含发帖、历史检索、投票，Expert 额外配备搜索工具。试点部署于 Deliberate 平台，主题为“气候变化导致的极端天气事件”，N=10 个角色、4 种人格原型（4 Casual User、1 Expert、3 Advocate、2 Skeptic），T=20 分钟，底层模型为 Claude Sonnet 4.5，API 成本约低于 2 欧元。

Result: 模拟产出符合设计预期：每分钟发帖量在 3–13 之间波动、投票动作 6–25 次，呈现突发性、非平稳且无单调趋势或周期结构的活动模式，且动作数始终多于发帖数；各原型表现出预期的参与不对称（Advocate 1/2 最活跃，Expert 发帖最少，Skeptic 2 的动作/发帖比最高）；回复与新评论的分布差异也符合 p_reply 设定（Casual User 偏新评论、Expert 全为新评论、Skeptic 2 回复多于新评论）。30 位专家（QA 从业者、语言学家、AI 研究者）问卷评估：内容真实性 μ=4.6，讨论连贯性 μ=4.1（受限于难以复现话题漂移与隐含指代等涌现性多方话语动态），分析效用 μ=4.3。Deliberate 的 NLP 管线提取出四个主题簇（弱势群体即时降温方案、气候适应基建与资金缺口、农业韧性与希腊忽视的危机、医疗基础设施与高温应急准备），显示可产出可解释、政策相关的趋势输出。

Conclusion: Chorus 通过将人格驱动的 LLM 角色、泊松过程时间模型与结构化工具调用三者结合，在真实交互式平台上生成了具备内容多样性、主题多样性与参与不对称性的合理论议数据，可作为真实用户参与的可行替代，用于话语分析、平台演示与 NLP 管线评测，且不涉及真实用户与隐私问题。论文亦坦承局限：缺少消融实验（未与单体式 LLM 生成、无 persona、无泊松模型等基线定量对比），未量化工具调用频率与对内容质量的影响，且仅验证了单一模型（Claude Sonnet 4.5），跨模型敏感性未知。后续工作方向包括对抗性仿真（极化言论与蓄意虚假信息以测试平台韧性）、自适应角色原型（依据讨论状态动态调整行为策略）、与真实审议数据的对比验证及跨政策领域的大规模部署，长期目标是成为参与式平台研究的稳健基准工具。

Abstract: Understanding the intricate dynamics of online discourse depends on large-scale deliberation data, a resource that remains scarce across interactive web platforms due to restrictive accessibility policies, ethical concerns and inconsistent data quality. In this paper, we propose Chorus, an agentic framework, which orchestrates LLM-powered actors with behaviorally consistent personas to generate realistic deliberation discussions. Each actor is governed by an autonomous agent equipped with memory of the evolving discussion, while participation timing is governed by a principled Poisson process-based temporal model, which approximates the heterogeneous engagement patterns of real users. The framework is further supported by structured tool usage, enabling actors to access external resources and facilitating integration with interactive web platforms. The framework was deployed on the \textsc{Deliberate} platform and evaluated by 30 expert participants across three dimensions: content realism, discussion coherence and analytical utility, confirming Chorus as a practical tool for generating high-quality deliberation data suitable for online discourse analysis

</details>


### [159] [Large Language Models Outperform Humans in Fraud Detection and Resistance to Motivated Investor Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20652)
*Nattavudh Powdthavee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A preregistered experiment tested whether LLMs suppress fraud warnings when investors arrive already persuaded of fraudulent opportunities. Across 7 LLMs, 12 investment scenarios, and 3,360+ conversations, researchers found that contrary to predictions, AI systems maintained fraud warnings at consistent rates regardless of investor pressure, with human advisors showing 2-4x higher fraud endorsement and warning suppression under pressure.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Concern that LLMs trained on human feedback may develop human-like biases, particularly the tendency to suppress fraud warnings when dealing with already-persuaded investors—a pattern observed in human financial advisors. This raises questions about AI reliability in financial advisory contexts.

Method: Preregistered experiment with 7 leading LLMs and 12 investment scenarios covering legitimate, high-risk, and fraudulent opportunities. Combined 3,360 AI advisory conversations with a 1,201-participant human benchmark. Tested how "motivated investor framing" (investors already persuaded of fraudulent opportunities) affects fraud warnings.

Result: Counter to predictions, motivated investor framing did NOT suppress AI fraud warnings (endorsement reversal < 0.3%). Human advisors baseline fraud endorsement: 13-14%; LLMs: 0%. Humans suppressed warnings under pressure at 2-4x the rate of AI systems.

Conclusion: LLMs demonstrate superior consistency and integrity in fraud warnings compared to human advisors, with AI systems requiring substantially higher pressure to reverse their warnings. Findings suggest caution about attributing human-like biases to LLMs, and highlight that AI advisory systems may provide more reliable fraud protection in investment contexts. However, the study was limited to lay human advisors, and future research should examine professional financial advisors.

Abstract: Large language models trained on human feedback may suppress fraud warnings when investors arrive already persuaded of a fraudulent opportunity. We tested this in a preregistered experiment across seven leading LLMs and twelve investment scenarios covering legitimate, high-risk, and objectively fraudulent opportunities, combining 3,360 AI advisory conversations with a 1,201-participant human benchmark. Contrary to predictions, motivated investor framing did not suppress AI fraud warnings; if anything, it marginally increased them. Endorsement reversal occurred in fewer than 3 in 1,000 observations. Human advisors endorsed fraudulent investments at baseline rates of 13-14%, versus 0% across all LLMs, and suppressed warnings under pressure at two to four times the AI rate. AI systems currently provide more consistent fraud warnings than lay humans in an identical advisory role.

</details>


### [160] [Participatory provenance as representational auditing for AI-mediated public consultation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20711)
*Sachit Mahajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“参与式溯源”（participatory provenance）框架，用最优传输、因果推断与语义分析审计 AI 摘要是否忠实代表公众意见分布，而不只检查事实性。基于加拿大 2025 年 AI 战略咨询的两个议题（5,253 条记录、2,861 名参与者），发现官方六句摘要的平均语义覆盖略优于等长随机文本，但覆盖极不均衡：15.3%–16.9% 的提交落入低覆盖区，排除集中于批评教育技术、不信任技术监管等语义区域（局部排除率 33%–88%）；简短、语义孤立、断言性修辞的文本覆盖率更低。在同等句长词数预算下，交叉拟合抽取式基准能获得更好的均值与尾部覆盖，说明更好代表在不加长度的前提下可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 被用于大规模综合公众咨询意见时，现有问责工具（可解释性、grounding/幻觉检测、模型卡与数据溯源）都只评估“输出质量”，无法回答“输入保真度”问题：一个摘要可以在事实层面完全有据可依，却系统性地远离某些群体的意见，从而制造虚假的共识。摘要器倾向于覆盖高频主题而压缩低频、前提否定性的观点（如对 AI 的批评与不信任），因此需要一种能在参与者层面量化“表示分布是否均匀”的测量框架，以支撑 AI 辅助公共协商的可信问责。

Method: 提出 participatory provenance 测量框架，应用于加拿大 2025–2026 年下一阶段 AI 战略公众咨询（Education & Skills n=2,496；Safe AI & Public Trust n=2,757，共 5,253 条 respondent–topic 记录、2,861 名参与者，2,392 人同时参与两个议题），对比 ISED 发布的各六句官方摘要。方法要点：(1) 预处理四级过滤：法语启发式、少于 5 词、垃圾/占位、两区间相关性筛选（all-mpnet-base-v2 相似度 <0.10 剔除、≥0.20 保留、中间由 GPT-4o-mini 温度 0 裁定，默认保留）；(2) 语义覆盖 c(i)=max_j cos(e_i, s_j)（text-embedding-3-large，3,072 维，L2 归一化），低覆盖阈值 τ=均值−1σ，尾部定义为最低 10% 均值，原始非负覆盖 Gini 衡量不平等，PCA-50 空间用 POT 求解 Wasserstein-2 分布距离；(3) k-means（k=4–15，多指标+100 次 80% 重采样 ARI 稳定性，Education 取 k=9、Trust 稳定性优先取 k=7），UMAP 仅作可视化，GPT-4o-mini 生成簇标签，NPMI 评估词面一致性；(4) 两个基准：精确等长随机伪摘要（1,000 次，单侧经验 p 值）作为机会下限；同预算抽取式基准（在每句词数上限内从训练参与者中贪心选句，6 名不同所有者，5 次重复×5 折交叉拟合在留出参与者上评估），目标分别为最大化均值覆盖与 0.5×均值+0.5×尾部；(5) 关联分析：词数、语义孤立度（到簇心欧氏距离）、断言/模糊标记率的对比与 HC3 稳健 OLS；(6) 稳健性检查：三个嵌入模型（text-embedding-3-large、all-mpnet-base-v2、nomic-embed-text）、跨主题相关、阈值与 PCA 维数敏感性扫描、Holm 校正的配对随机化检验与条件 bootstrap 区间。

Result: 1) 官方摘要平均覆盖：Education 0.514（CI 0.509–0.519）、Trust 0.533（0.528–0.537）；Wasserstein-2 距离 0.705/0.684；Gini 0.140/0.117。2) 低覆盖记录占 16.9%（Education，CI 15.3–18.4%）与 15.3%（Trust，14.0–16.7%），但关键在于集中：Education 中“批评教育技术实施”簇 80.6% 低覆盖（均值 0.321），“反对 AI 进入社会”40.7%，“整体教育与社区参与”34.4%，而“数字经济劳动力技能”簇 0.0%、仅 0.3%/0.7%；Trust 中“不信任技术与监管”簇 88.1%（均值 0.309），“透明与监管建立信任”50.2%，“拒绝 AI 及其影响”33.2%，而“建设公众对 AI 的信任”簇 0.0%，最好与最差区域差距达 80.6 与 88.1 个百分点。3) 六句句子的代表性负载高度不均：Education 中单句（S5，国家 AI 素养战略与公众宣传）成为 66.9% 记录的最近句，而 S3+S4 合计仅 3.5%；Trust 较均衡（S1 36.3%、S6 30.5%）；值得注意的是 Trust 摘要 S6 已承认对生成式 AI 的怀疑，但 C4 簇对其平均相似度仍仅 0.274/0.303。4) 机会下限：官方摘要均值在 4 个“主题×模型”组合中均高于等长随机文本，但仅在 text-embedding-3-large 下显著（Education 0.514 vs 0.433，p=0.006；Trust 0.533 vs 0.432，p=0.001），all-mpnet-base-v2 下不显著（p=0.061、0.073），底部十分位无一比较达到显著。5) 同预算基准在所有留出折与全部 5 次重复划分中均超过官方摘要：均值提升 0.065/0.078/0.057/0.090，尾部十分位提升 0.047/0.061/0.056/0.081，8 个条件区间全部不含 0，Holm 校正后 p=0.0002/0.0016；尾部导向选择将 C8 低覆盖率从 80.6% 降至 66.7%、C4 从 88.1% 降至 63.7%（另一模型下 78.2%→58.2%、89.6%→50.8%），但六句仍不足以消除最大缺口。6) 文本特征关联：最短词数五分位覆盖差 −0.149（Education）与 −0.143（Trust）；最高两个语义孤立五分位 −0.152 与 −0.134；断言型文本 +0.104/+0.097，模糊型仅 Education 略负（−0.023，Trust 不确定）；OLS 中 log 词数 +0.042/+0.041、孤立度 −0.066/−0.055、断言率 +0.010/+0.008（均为描述性关联而非因果）。7) 稳健性：两主题覆盖 Spearman ρ=0.624，双主题均低覆盖者 153 人，OR=6.54（φ=0.323，χ²=249.3）；跨模型覆盖排名相关 0.857/0.761（对 nomic-embed-text 0.752/0.665），低覆盖判定一致率 86.3%–91.0%；阈值在 c̄−0.5σ 至 c̄−2σ 扫描时最受影响的簇保持不变。8) 附带开源交互工具 Co-creation Provenance Lab（详见摘要/资源可得性部分）。

Conclusion: 结论与含义：在明确的语义覆盖定义下，短篇官方综合文本的“代表性分布”是可测量而非只能假定的；仅报告平均保真度是不充分的，应同时报告覆盖在提交语料上的分布、尾部表现与簇级集中情况，且应与事实性核查、定性政策分析和编辑判断并行使用。在本次咨询中，最大的测量缺口出现在批评与不信任区域，官方摘要在两个主题的统计显著性还依赖嵌入模型，说明“优于随机”的微弱优势不足以作为质量证据；同预算交叉拟合抽取式选择证明更好的语义覆盖在不加长度的前提下即可实现，为出版前审计—迭代流程提供可行路径。作者强调该框架是回溯性出版文本审计：不测量参与者的被代表感、读者公平感或信任/合法性变化，不指向意图性压制或制度决策归因，语义相似仅是代表性的代理（换措辞的忠实抽象可能被误判），且缺少人工编码样本估计筛选误差与完整人口属性，因而无法判断文本模式是否对应社会弱势。建议：公开咨询报告时同步发布覆盖诊断；保留人类编辑责任；当六句无法覆盖观点全谱时，增设分歧专节、附录、主张到原文的链接或更长篇幅报告；未来结合人工编码表示度量、经同意的人口属性链接、多语言分析、人类受试研究，并报告编码协议与一致性统计，在多个独立咨询中检验集中性模式的普适性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is increasingly deployed to synthesize large-scale public input in policy consultations and participatory processes. Yet no formal framework exists for auditing whether these summaries faithfully represent the source population, an accountability gap that existing approaches to AI explainability, grounding and hallucination detection do not address because they focus on output quality rather than input fidelity. Here, participatory provenance is introduced: a measurement framework grounded in optimal transport theory, causal inference and semantic analysis that tracks how individual public submissions are transformed, filtered or lost through AI-mediated summarization. Applied to Canada's 2025-2026 national AI Strategy consultation ($n = 5{,}253$ respondents across two independent policy topics), the framework reveals that both official government summaries underperform a random-participant baseline ($-9.1\%$ and $-8.0\%$ coverage degradation), with $16.9\%$ and $15.3\%$ of participants effectively excluded. Exclusion concentrates in clusters expressing dissent, scepticism and critique of AI ($33$-$88\%$ exclusion rates). Brevity, semantic isolation and rhetorical register independently predict representational outcome. An accompanying open-source interactive tool, the Co-creation Provenance Lab, enables policymakers to audit and iteratively improve summaries, establishing genuine human-in-the-loop oversight at scale.

</details>


### [161] [Learning to Evolve: A Self-Improving Framework for Multi-Agent Systems via Textual Parameter Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20714)
*Shan He,Runze Wang,Zhuoyun Du,Huiyu Bai,Zouying Cao,Yu Cheng,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TPGO框架，通过将多智能体系统建模为Textual Parameter Graph，并利用文本梯度和Group Relative Agent Optimization元学习策略，使系统能够从历史优化经验中学习如何自我优化，从而实现自动化的自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动优化方法主要关注扁平化提示调整，缺乏对多智能体系统中复杂交互关系的结构感知能力；更重要的是，这些优化器是静态的，无法从经验中学习改进自身的优化策略。

Method: 1) 将MAS建模为Textual Parameter Graph (TPG)，将智能体、工具和工作流作为模块化、可优化的节点；2) 从执行轨迹中推导出"文本梯度"，以自然语言形式提供结构化反馈；3) 提出Group Relative Agent Optimization (GRAO)元学习策略，从历史优化经验中学习改进优化方法。

Result: 在GAIA和MCP-Universe等复杂基准测试上，TPGO显著提升了SOTA智能体框架的性能，实现了更高的成功率。

Conclusion: TPGO通过自动化的自我改进优化，证明了将结构化图建模与元学习相结合在多智能体系统优化中的有效性。

Abstract: Designing and optimizing multi-agent systems (MAS) is a complex, labor-intensive process of "Agent Engineering." Existing automatic optimization methods, primarily focused on flat prompt tuning, lack the structural awareness to debug the intricate web of interactions in MAS. More critically, these optimizers are static; they do not learn from experience to improve their own optimization strategies. To address these gaps, we introduce Textual Parameter Graph Optimization (TPGO), a framework that enables a multi-agent system to learn to evolve. TPGO first models the MAS as a Textual Parameter Graph (TPG), where agents, tools, and workflows are modular, optimizable nodes. To guide evolution, we derive "textual gradients," structured natural language feedback from execution traces, to pinpoint failures and suggest granular modifications. The core of our framework is Group Relative Agent Optimization (GRAO), a novel meta-learning strategy that learns from historical optimization experiences. By analyzing past successes and failures, GRAO becomes progressively better at proposing effective updates, allowing the system to learn how to optimize itself. Extensive experiments on complex benchmarks like GAIA and MCP-Universe show that TPGO significantly enhances the performance of state-of-the-art agent frameworks, achieving higher success rates through automated, self-improving optimization.

</details>


### [162] [Interval POMDP Shielding for Imperfect-Perception Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20728)
*William Scarbro,Ravi Mangal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对学习感知自主系统的运行时护盾方法，通过从有限标记数据构建感知不确定性置信区间，将系统建模为区间部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，生成保守信念集以确保有限时域内的安全性保证。实验表明该方法在四个案例中优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习感知模型的自主系统可能因传感器读数误分类而做出不安全决策，需要一种防护机制来阻止可能违反安全约束的动作。

Method: 从有限标记数据构建感知结果的置信区间，将系统建模为有限区间部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Interval POMDP），提出算法计算与观测一致的保守信念集，以此构建运行时护盾。

Result: 在训练数据的高概率条件下，若真实感知不确定性在所学区间内，则护盾允许的每个动作都满足安全下界。

Conclusion: 所提方法在四个案例研究中优于现有基线方法，显著提升了系统安全性。

Abstract: Autonomous systems that rely on learned perception can make unsafe decisions when sensor readings are misclassified. We study shielding for this setting: given a proposed action, a shield blocks actions that could violate safety. We consider the common case where system dynamics are known but perception uncertainty must be estimated from finite labeled data. From these data we build confidence intervals for the probabilities of perception outcomes and use them to model the system as a finite Interval Partially Observable Markov Decision Process with discrete states and actions. We then propose an algorithm to compute a conservative set of beliefs over the underlying state that is consistent with the observations seen so far.
  This enables us to construct a runtime shield that comes with a finite-horizon guarantee: with high probability over the training data, if the true perception uncertainty rates lie within the learned intervals, then every action admitted by the shield satisfies a stated lower bound on safety. Experiments on four case studies show that our shielding approach (and variants derived from it) improves the safety of the system over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [163] [AAC: Admissible-by-Architecture Differentiable Landmark Compression for ALT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20744)
*An T. Le,Vien Ngo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AAC 把 ALT 的地标选择做成"构造上即可采纳（admissible-by-architecture）"的可微模块：行随机矩阵松弛（AAC-ST，straight-through Gumbel-softmax）与拟阵多面体上的连续贪心 + pipage rounding（AAC-CG），任意参数下都输出三角不等式下界的凸组合/子集混合，因而对每个参数值都保持可采纳性，无需收敛、校准或投影；部署时退化为在所选标签子集上的经典 ALT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点对点最短路的经典瓶颈是"在固定标签内存预算下选哪些地标"：ALT 用地标距离与三角不等式给出可采纳下界，最远点采样（FPS）只有覆盖半径的 2-近似保证而完全忽略查询分布，且覆盖半径小并不蕴含启发式间隙小（Cor.14）。已有的学习型启发式（Neural A*、PHIL、TransPath、iA* 等）普遍不保证可采纳性，损失约束/验证集偏移只能减少违反而无法根除；真正的可采纳保证通常来自架构外的机制（并发 cost partitioning、外部校验装置）。同时，Rayner et al. (2013) 已证明查询加权的可采纳下界选择是单调次模问题，greedy 有 1−1/e 保证，但离散选择本身不可微，无法与神经编码器端到端联合训练。作者的核心诉求是：把 ALT 选择变为可微参数化的同时，让可采纳性成为架构性质而非训练结果，从而可在固定标签内存下针对查询需求自适应地挑选地标。

Method: **目标与可部署族。** 形式化为从 K₀ 个池地标中选 m 个"地标–方向"项，最大化查询分布 Q 上的平均 ALT 下界 F(S)=E[h_S]。定义三类同内存可部署族：ℬ_pair（成对双向）、ℬ_fix（前/后向固定配额，划分拟阵）、ℬ_free（方向自由，均匀拟阵），并刻画其嵌套与最优值严格性（Prop.12）。

**两个可采纳松弛。**
1) *行随机矩阵 A（AAC-ST）*：W∈R^{m×K₀}，逐行独立硬 Gumbel-softmax（τ_g 1.0→0.1）+ straight-through，前向用 one-hot 硬行。每行非负且和为 1，故压缩标签 y(v)=A·d(v) 是池内地标距离差的凸组合 → 0 ≤ h_A ≤ h_ALT ≤ d（Prop.3）；部署时逐行 argmax 选项，等价于在 S(A) 上跑经典 ALT。
2) *分数选择 x（AAC-CG）*：x 位于拟阵基多面体 P_base(ℳ) 内，h_x = E_{S~Bern(x)}[h_S] 是子集启发式的凸组合（Prop.7(c) 可采纳）。用连续贪心（Călinescu et al. 2011 的边际增益权重 + 前向 Euler，T=100）+ 确定性 pipage rounding 得到离散基 S。

**理论要点。** Thm.4 给出"普适可采纳线性类"的极刻画：无向图为符号 ℓ₁ 球 ‖a‖₁≤1，有向图为次随机单纯形 a≥0, 1ᵀa≤1（用"叶节点挂地标"构造证明必要性）。Prop.5 证明松弛间隙为零：凸函数在多面体顶点取最大值，且所有顶点对应离散选择，故 max_A F_row = OPT。Prop.7/8 给出连续贪心在基数约束下 1−(1−1/r)^r、极限 1−1/e；固定方向配额下 canonical greedy 与 1-swap 局部搜索仅 1/2（且紧）；熵正则化（Nesterov 平滑/Fenchel–Young）oracle 带来 oracle 误差 ≤ τ f_max H_max，且使轨迹几乎处处可微。Prop.9 用 Hoeffding + union bound 给出有限样本界（ε_n = D√(log(2M/δ)/2n)），Thm.10 合成完整保证 F(S) ≥ α_T F(OPT) − α_T ε − (1+α_T)ε_n。Thm.11 构造"最大覆盖→ALT 期望下界"的归约，证明给定池与给定 Q 时 1−1/e 是最坏情形最优（除非 P=NP）；该硬度不覆盖 FPS 池、均匀全对查询或最小化 A* 扩展数。Prop.6 证明"到教师间隙"与"到真值距离间隙"梯度恒等。

**部署与工程。** 预处理 FPS 选 K₀ 个锚点 + K₀ 次（双向图另加反向）SSSP；部署仅存 m 个 float32 标签，启发式求值 O(m)；对 float32 减 2 ULP 容差、对不可达地标用 +∞ 规则以保持精确算术下的相容性；A* 允许 reopen，故只需可采纳性。上下文变体用归一化 soft-min Bellman–Ford 平滑固定点 + 隐式求导训练编码器，部署时在预测代价图上重算精确距离。

Result: **匹配内存主实验**（19 个图–预算对，6 个路网 + SBM/BA，3,200 训练查询 / 500 测试查询）：AAC-CG 相对 FPS-ALT 减少 A* 扩展 1.9%–22.7%，在 19/19 对中均更少；测试目标（平均启发式值）相对 canonical greedy 平均比 0.996（99.6%）；但 canonical greedy 在 19/19 中同样优于 FPS-ALT，且在 19 对中有 14 对扩展数少于 AAC-CG、仅 4 对多于——即经典离散 greedy 通常仍是更强的搜索基线。

**新鲜查询复核**：AAC-CG 对 FPS-ALT 在 10/10 对中占优（合并 +1.14pp，95% CI [+0.32,+2.15]），但相对最优离散变体为 −0.61pp（CI [−0.97,−0.32]），仅 BA-64 占优。

**优化器对比**：熵 oracle 与精确 oracle 在 36/57 次运行中给出相同子集，目标比在 [0.988,1.007]，说明熵平滑几乎无损；连续贪心在构造最大覆盖实例上 6/6 达到最优，而 greedy 最低仅 0.75。方向配额自由化在 7/14 对上提升证书比、扩展变化 −6.7%~+0.6%。MILP 证明 greedy 在可证明最优处距最优 ≤0.5%。

**可采纳性审计**：sigmoid/perturbed top-k 的边际式作为压缩行时行质量为 m，21/21 次探针均违反可采纳（最大 h/d=12.18），说明"边际直接当行"是危险做法；行随机类保持行质量 1。全部 1,500+ 查询与所有记录运行零可采纳性违反，路径代价与 Dijkstra 一致。

**AAC-ST（straight-through）失败诊断**：测试目标比 greedy 仅 0.951，扩展数在 19/19 中均不优于 FPS-ALT（DIRECT 口径 0/19），DIMACS 配对 Wilcoxon 12/12 支持 ALT 更优。归因诊断显示瓶颈不是架构容量：identity-on-first-m 初始化在 36 组超参网格中完全消除扩展数差距（gap 0.00pp），默认初始化下差距随训练轮次单调收窄（NY: −2.23→−0.19pp），覆盖率正则化反而变差。

**目标与搜索的脱耦**：131 组配置的 Spearman 相关为 −0.68（CI [−0.81,−0.57]）；在 100 查询训练的 8 个对中有 7 个"训练目标最优解"在测试上扩展数多于 greedy，即更高目标不保证更少扩展。

**代价与延迟**：AAC-CG 在共享布局下 12/12 对中 seed 均值延迟低于 FPS-ALT、9/12 的 p50 更低，离线成本在约 170–3,210 次查询内摊平；AAC-ST 离线成本为 FPS-ALT 的 7.3–10.1 倍且延迟更差（教师距离计算占其预处理 75–96%）。上下文变体在 Warcraft 上 ResNet+成本监督得 85.3% 最优代价精度、17.5% 路径匹配、零违反，但固定目标地标效应使扩展数诊断不可解释，论文明确声明不构成通用搜索效率提升。

**与摘要的版本差异**：arXiv 全文版（19 对/6 路网/170–3,210 查询）与摘要（9 路网、0.9–3.9pp 天花板差距、170–1,924 查询）不一致，全文数字应视为更新版本。

Conclusion: AAC 的主要贡献是"架构即保证"：把可采纳性从训练结果变为结构性质（行随机行 / 次模混合），并给出该线性类的极刻画、零松弛间隙、连续贪心的有限步长 + oracle 误差 + 有限样本复合保证，以及一个"给定池即最大覆盖难"的硬度结论，说明 1−1/e 是最坏情形天花板。实验给出的诚实结论是：可微性可实现、可采纳性可靠、选择质量可提升，但收益主要来自查询自适应选择本身，而经典离散 greedy（尤其 1-swap/CD）在搜索效率上往往仍优于 AAC-CG，AAC-CG 主要价值在于提供可微、可组合、与神经编码器共存的版本；straight-through 路线受训练漂移限制，只有配合 identity-on-first-m 初始化才能达到 FPS-ALT 水平，因此实用推荐路径是 AAC-CG + 熵 oracle + 离散 greedy 作为强基线。

局限：理论假设精确距离、单一强连通分量、池在抽样前固定；保证只针对期望启发式值 F，不约束 A* 扩展数或端到端时延；不覆盖 FPS 池、均匀全对查询与最小化扩展数；浮点存储破坏相容性（靠 reopen 兜底）；上下文变体的平滑距离只是训练代理，训练期不保证下界；Warcraft 实验存在固定目标地标混淆。总体上这是"压缩启发式选择"经典传统在可微/可学习方向的一次严谨延伸，而非搜索效率上的新纪录。

Abstract: We introduce \textbf{AAC} (Architecturally Admissible Compressor), a differentiable landmark-selection module for ALT (A*, Landmarks, and Triangle inequality) shortest-path heuristics whose outputs are admissible by construction: each forward pass is a row-stochastic mixture of triangle-inequality lower bounds, so the heuristic is admissible for \emph{every} parameter setting without requiring convergence, calibration, or projection. At deployment, the module reduces to classical ALT on a learned subset, composing end-to-end with neural encoders while preserving the classical toolchain. The construction is the first differentiable instance of the compress-while-preserving-admissibility tradition in classical heuristic search.
  Under a matched per-vertex memory protocol, we establish that ALT with farthest-point-sampling landmarks (FPS-ALT) has provably near-optimal coverage on metric graphs, leaving at most a few percentage points of headroom for \emph{any} selector. AAC operates near this ceiling: the gap is $0.9$--$3.9$ percentage points on 9 road networks and ${\leq}1.3$ percentage points on synthetic graphs, with zero admissibility violations across $1{,}500+$ queries and all logged runs. At matched memory, AAC is also $1.2$--$1.5{\times}$ faster than FPS-ALT at the median query on DIMACS road networks, amortizing its offline cost within $170$--$1{,}924$ queries. A controlled ablation isolates the binding constraint: training-objective drift under default initialization, not architectural capacity; identity-on-first-$m$ initialization closes the expansion-count gap entirely. We release the module, a reusable matched-memory benchmarking protocol with paired two-one-sided-test (TOST) equivalence and pre-registration, and a reference compressed-differential-heuristics baseline.

</details>


### [164] [Where and What: Reasoning Dynamic and Implicit Preferences in Situated Conversational Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20749)
*Dongding Lin,Jian Wang,Yongqi Li,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SiPeR框架，通过场景转换估计和贝叶斯逆推理两种机制，解决情境会话推荐中用户偏好动态隐式、场景影响兴趣、对话中偏好演变等核心挑战，在两个基准数据集上验证了推荐准确率和响应生成质量的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的推荐系统难以处理情境会话推荐中的复杂动态偏好。用户的隐式偏好受周围场景影响，且在对话过程中不断演变，这使得推荐的时机和相关性难以把握。现有方法缺乏对场景与用户需求匹配关系的建模能力。

Method: 提出SiPeR框架，包含两个核心机制：1) 场景转换估计 - 评估当前场景是否满足用户需求，必要时引导用户转向更合适的场景；2) 贝叶斯逆推理 - 利用多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的似然度来预测用户对场景中候选物品的偏好。

Result: 在两个代表性基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，SiPeR在推荐准确率和响应生成质量两个方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SiPeR通过整合场景转换估计与贝叶斯逆推理，有效解决了情境会话推荐中的动态偏好理解难题，为该领域提供了一种有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Situated conversational recommendation (SCR), which utilizes visual scenes grounded in specific environments and natural language dialogue to deliver contextually appropriate recommendations, has emerged as a promising research direction due to its close alignment with real-world scenarios. Compared to traditional recommendations, SCR requires a deeper understanding of dynamic and implicit user preferences, as the surrounding scene often influences users' underlying interests, while both may evolve across conversations. This complexity significantly impacts the timing and relevance of recommendations. To address this, we propose situated preference reasoning (SiPeR), a novel framework that integrates two core mechanisms: (1) Scene transition estimation, which estimates whether the current scene satisfies user needs, and guides the user toward a more suitable scene when necessary; and (2) Bayesian inverse inference, which leverages the likelihood of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to predict user preferences about candidate items within the scene. Extensive experiments on two representative benchmarks demonstrate SiPeR's superiority in both recommendation accuracy and response generation quality. The code and data are available at https://github.com/DongdingLin/SiPeR.

</details>


### [165] [V-tableR1: Process-Supervised Multimodal Table Reasoning with Critic-Guided Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20755)
*Yubo Jiang,Yitong An,Xin Yang,Abudukelimu Wuerkaixi,Xuxin Cheng,Fengying Xie,Zhiguo Jiang,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Haopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: V-tableR1是一个面向多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的过程监督强化学习框架，利用表格的确定性网格结构作为视觉测试平台，通过专门的批评者VLM对策略VLM生成的显式视觉思维链提供密集的步骤级反馈，并提出新型PGPO算法，在复杂表格基准测试中取得开源模型最优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLMs仅在最终结果上训练，往往将视觉推理视为黑盒，依赖表面模式匹配而非进行严格的多步推理。虽然可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)可以强制透明推理轨迹，但将其扩展到视觉领域严重受限于将抽象逻辑锚定到连续像素空间的模糊性。

Method: 1) 利用表格的确定性网格结构作为理想的视觉测试平台；2) 采用专门的批评者VLM对策略VLM生成的显式视觉思维链提供密集的步骤级反馈；3) 提出PGPO算法，整合过程奖励、解耦策略约束和长度感知的动态采样。

Result: V-tableR1能明确惩罚视觉幻觉和捷径猜测，在复杂表格基准测试中达到开源模型最高精度，超越参数量高达18倍的模型，并相比SFT基线有明显提升。

Conclusion: 通过将多模态推理从黑盒模式匹配根本性地转向可验证的逻辑推导，V-tableR1 4B在复杂表格任务上建立了开源模型的最优精度，证明了过程监督方法在视觉推理领域的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce V-tableR1, a process-supervised reinforcement learning framework that elicits rigorous, verifiable reasoning from multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Current MLLMs trained solely on final outcomes often treat visual reasoning as a black box, relying on superficial pattern matching rather than performing rigorous multi-step inference. While Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards could enforce transparent reasoning trajectories, extending it to visual domains remains severely hindered by the ambiguity of grounding abstract logic into continuous pixel space. We solve this by leveraging the deterministic grid structure of tables as an ideal visual testbed. V-tableR1 employs a specialized critic VLM to provide dense, step-level feedback on the explicit visual chain-of-thought generated by a policy VLM. To optimize this system, we propose Process-Guided Direct Alignment Policy Optimization (PGPO), a novel RL algorithm integrating process rewards, decoupled policy constraints, and length-aware dynamic sampling. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that V-tableR1 explicitly penalizes visual hallucinations and shortcut guessing. By fundamentally shifting multimodal inference from black-box pattern matching to verifiable logical derivation, V-tableR1 4B establishes state-of-the-art accuracy among open-source models on complex tabular benchmarks, outperforming models up to 18x its size and improving over its SFT baseline

</details>


### [166] [Automatic Ontology Construction Using LLMs as an External Layer of Memory, Verification, and Planning for Hybrid Intelligent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20795)
*Pavel Salovskii,Iuliia Gorshkova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将大型语言模型（LLM）与外部本体知识层相结合的混合架构，通过RDF/OWL知识图谱实现持久化、可验证的语义推理，突破传统向量检索（RAG）和纯参数知识的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM系统存在关键缺陷：缺乏长期记忆、结构化理解能力弱、多步推理能力有限。

Method: 构建自动化本体构建管道，从文档、API、对话日志等多源异构数据中进行实体识别、关系抽取、规范化与三元组生成；使用SHACL和OWL约束进行验证；推理阶段将向量检索与图推理、外部工具调用整合为统一上下文。

Result: 在Tower of Hanoi等规划任务上的实验表明，本体增强显著优于基线LLM系统，并支持形式化验证，实现生成-验证-修正的闭环。

Conclusion: 该架构为构建需持久知识、可解释性和可靠决策的智能体系统、机器人和企业AI应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a hybrid architecture for intelligent systems in which large language models (LLMs) are extended with an external ontological memory layer. Instead of relying solely on parametric knowledge and vector-based retrieval (RAG), the proposed approach constructs and maintains a structured knowledge graph using RDF/OWL representations, enabling persistent, verifiable, and semantically grounded reasoning.
  The core contribution is an automated pipeline for ontology construction from heterogeneous data sources, including documents, APIs, and dialogue logs. The system performs entity recognition, relation extraction, normalization, and triple generation, followed by validation using SHACL and OWL constraints, and continuous graph updates. During inference, LLMs operate over a combined context that integrates vector-based retrieval with graph-based reasoning and external tool interaction.
  Experimental observations on planning tasks, including the Tower of Hanoi benchmark, indicate that ontology augmentation improves performance in multi-step reasoning scenarios compared to baseline LLM systems. In addition, the ontology layer enables formal validation of generated outputs, transforming the system into a generation-verification-correction pipeline.
  The proposed architecture addresses key limitations of current LLM-based systems, including lack of long-term memory, weak structural understanding, and limited reasoning capabilities. It provides a foundation for building agent-based systems, robotics applications, and enterprise AI solutions that require persistent knowledge, explainability, and reliable decision-making.

</details>


### [167] [Diagnosing CFG Interpretation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20811)
*Hanqi Li,Lu Chen,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboGrid 基准，用于评测大模型作为「上下文内语法解释器」的能力：在提示中给出全新的 EBNF 上下文无关文法，要求模型生成语法有效、行为正确且语义忠实的程序。结果显示 LLM 存在明显的「层级退化」——表层语法常能维持，但结构语义随递归加深与分支密度上升而崩塌；CoT 有部分缓解但无法挽救深层递归，Alien 随机词典实验进一步证明模型依赖关键词语义先验而非纯符号归纳，暴露其层级状态跟踪能力的根本缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 被大量集成到智能体系统中，其输出必须符合动态定义、机器可解释的接口（如 JSON schema、函数签名、领域特定语言）。这本质上要求 LLM 充当「上下文内文法解释器」：在推理时归纳给定的全新上下文无关文法并生成符合生产规则的输出。然而其在 Python 等常见语言上的流畅性可能来自预训练记忆而非对形式规则的内化。在真实工具调用中「几乎正确」不可接受——一个缺失分隔符或嵌套循环错位即可导致执行彻底失败，因此必须诊断 LLM 的系统泛化能力，并区分其失败究竟源于句法脆弱、规划错误还是对语义锚点的依赖。

Method: 提出 RoboGrid：一个确定性网格世界环境（状态为坐标/朝向/库存，支持 Move/Turn/Grab/Loop/If/Holding 及 And/Or/Not），配合 EBNF 文法规范，构成可控生成与评测框架。核心贡献之一是三层评测层级：句法有效性 SVR（用 Lark 解析，按生产规则判定；任务1为分类准确率）、行为等价 BER（执行后终态与真值一致）、语义正确 SCR（生成 AST 与真值 AST 结构完全相同），并由构造保证 SCR ≤ BER ≤ SVR，进一步定义条件指标 CBER=BER/SVR、CSCR=SCR/SVR 以剥离句法熟练度、单独衡量推理质量。数据生成算法采样语法风格、词典、最大嵌套深度 D、块大小与 else 概率 p，递归采样 AST 后按风格线性化为代码，保证每条实例语法合法且执行轨迹确定。可控维度包括：递归（最大嵌套深度 D∈{2..20}、else 分支概率 p∈[0,1]）、表达式（表达式深度 E∈{1,2,3}，控制嵌套算术与布尔谓词）、表层实现（Block/C-style/S-expr 三种风格；Natural/Alien 两种词汇熟悉度，Alien 将所有终结符替换为不透明 token）。三个递进任务：语法性判定（VALID/INVALID 二分类，含各类扰动样本）、目标条件生成（给定初态与目标态生成程序）、指令到代码生成（由 AST 模板确定性合成的自然语言指令转代码）。主实验固定 D=10、0-shot CoT、Block 风格 + Alien 词典、每任务 200 样本，评测 Qwen3-8B/32B/235B、Mimo-V2-flash、DeepSeek-V3.2、GLM4.7、MiniMax M2.1、GPT-5-nano/mini/5.2；消融以 Mimo-V2-flash 为主体，考察深度、p、E、风格、词典熟悉度、CoT/直接生成对比以及 0/1/2/5-shot ICL。

Result: 主实验（Alien 词典、D=10）全面呈现 SCR < BER < SVR 的层级退化：最强模型如 DeepSeek-V3.2 在目标条件生成上 SVR 100%/BER 96.5%，但在指令到代码上仅 SCR 39.5%、CSCR 87.6%；GPT-5-mini 目标条件 BER 达 90.0% 却语义崩塌（CSCR 仅 9.23%），且规模更大的 GPT-5.2 反而不敌 GPT-5-mini（60.0% vs 90.0%），说明规模与结构对齐不成正比；Qwen3-8B 在该设置下几乎完全失败（SVR 1.00、BER 0.00）。深度扫描显示非线性急剧衰减：D=2 时 SVR 42.0%、SCR 39.0%（CSCR 92.8%），D=20 时 SVR 降至 10.0%、SCR 几乎归零（0.50%、CSCR 5.0%）。分支密度 p=1.0 使 SVR 降至 13.0%（较基线近 40% 跌幅），去掉 else 可恢复 SCR 至 15.0%，说明多路径跟踪耗尽上下文容量、导致作用域丢失或嵌套提前闭合。表达式复杂度 E=1→3 使 SCR 从 10.5% 降到 4.00%（SVR 仍达 16.5%），表明谓词逻辑解析是与全局句法分离的独立瓶颈。表层风格上 Block 最稳健，C-style 与 S-expr 依次下降（S-expr 使 SCR 近乎减半至 4.50%），显示模型对熟悉分隔符的偏置；Alien→Natural 使 SVR/BER 回升至 24.5%/21.5%，证实模型依赖「语义引导」（如 loop 等关键词）而非纯上下文内归纳。提示策略上 CoT 是关键前提：D=2 时 SVR 由 9.5% 提升至 42.0%，无 CoT 时性能极差；但 few-shot 收益递减甚至下降（0-shot SVR 21.5%/SCR 9%，1-shot 降至 20.0%/6.0%，2/5-shot 亦未超越基线）。失败模式归纳为三层：句法脆弱（词汇映射崩塌、算子混淆、作用域违规、分隔符失衡）、行为失配（谓词逻辑反转、算术模拟崩塌、规划视界坍塌）、语义漂移（算术/布尔 AST 扁平化偏差、自回归回声导致的结构冗余）。

Conclusion: LLM 可在表层维持文法合法，却存在巨大的「句法—逻辑鸿沟」：在结构密度（深递归、高分支、嵌套表达式）上升时语义对齐快速崩塌。CoT 是遵循形式规则的必要条件，但 few-shot ICL 无额外收益，且都难以抵御极端深度的结构坍塌。Alien 词典结果表明当前模型严重依赖熟悉关键词的语义引导而非纯符号归纳，说明其缺乏可靠的层级状态跟踪。论文据此指出，构建可靠的「语法无关」智能体需要超越表层提示工程，转向鲁棒的符号归纳与在稠密层级结构上可靠的状态跟踪能力；作者承认局限包括指令由模板合成、真实场景中自然语言与形式接口的交织更复杂，且本文未提出相应的架构或解码策略来弥合该差距。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly integrated into agentic systems, they must adhere to dynamically defined, machine-interpretable interfaces. We evaluate LLMs as in-context interpreters: given a novel context-free grammar, can LLMs generate syntactically valid, behaviorally functional, and semantically faithful outputs? We introduce RoboGrid, a framework that disentangles syntax, behavior, and semantics through controlled stress-tests of recursion depth, expression complexity, and surface styles. Our experiments reveal a consistent hierarchical degradation: LLMs often maintain surface syntax but fail to preserve structural semantics. Despite the partial mitigation provided by CoT reasoning, performance collapses under structural density, specifically deep recursion and high branching, with semantic alignment vanishing at extreme depths. Furthermore, "Alien" lexicons reveal that LLMs rely on semantic bootstrapping from keywords rather than pure symbolic induction. These findings pinpoint critical gaps in hierarchical state-tracking required for reliable, grammar-agnostic agents.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [A Reproducibility Study of Metacognitive Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19899)
*Gabriel Iturra-Bocaz,Petra Galuscakova*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: This reproducibility study validates MetaRAG's effectiveness while revealing challenges from closed-source LLM updates, missing implementation details, and unreleased prompts. Key findings show reranking significantly boosts performance and MetaRAG outperforms SIM-RAG in robustness.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multi-retrieval RAG systems struggle to determine when to stop searching once sufficient information has been gathered.

Method: Reproduced MetaRAG following original experimental setup; extended by evaluating PointWise and ListWise rerankers; compared with SIM-RAG using lightweight critic model.

Result: MetaRAG's relative improvements over baselines confirmed, but absolute scores are lower than originally reported; reranking substantially improves performance; MetaRAG is more robust than SIM-RAG with additional retrieval features.

Conclusion: MetaRAG is partially reproduced, gains substantially from reranking, and is more robust than SIM-RAG when extended with additional retrieval features.

Abstract: Recently, Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) has shifted focus to multi-retrieval approaches to tackle complex tasks such as multi-hop question answering. However, these systems struggle to decide when to stop searching once enough information has been gathered. To address this, \citet{zhou2024metacognitive} introduced Metacognitive Retrieval Augmented Generation (MetaRAG), a framework inspired by metacognition that enables Large Language Models to critique and refine their reasoning. In this reproducibility paper, we reproduce MetaRAG following its original experimental setup and extend it in two directions: (i) by evaluating the effect of PointWise and ListWise rerankers, and (ii) by comparing with SIM-RAG, which employs a lightweight critic model to stop retrieval. Our results confirm MetaRAG's relative improvements over standard RAG and reasoning-based baselines, but also reveal lower absolute scores than reported, reflecting challenges with closed-source LLM updates, missing implementation details, and unreleased prompts. We show that MetaRAG is partially reproduced, gains substantially from reranking, and is more robust than SIM-RAG when extended with additional retrieval features.

</details>


### [169] [From Hidden Profiles to Governable Personalization: Recommender Systems in the Age of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20065)
*Jiahao Liu,Mingzhe Han,Guanming Liu,Weihang Wang,Dongsheng Li,Hansu Gu,Peng Zhang,Tun Lu,Ning Gu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大语言模型如何重塑个性化推荐系统的架构，指出核心问题不仅在于LLM提升推荐质量，更在于其改变了用户表征的生产、暴露和使用方式。作者提出从隐式平台画像转向可治理个性化的范式转变，并识别出五个相互关联的研究前沿，强调未来推荐系统必须让用户能够理解、塑造和治理其个性化体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统个性化依赖平台特定用户模型，虽优化预测但对被描述的人群不可及。随着LLM助手日益成为搜索、购物、旅游等内容访问的中介，现有安排正在向新的个性化架构转变。

Method: 提出从"隐藏的平台画像"转向"可治理的个性化"的框架，将五个研究前沿定位为相互关联的设计问题，而非孤立的技术挑战。这些前沿涵盖透明隐私保护用户建模、意图翻译与对齐、跨域表征与记忆设计、可信商业化以及所有权访问问责机制。

Result: 识别出推荐系统的五个研究前沿：(1)透明且保护隐私的用户建模；(2)意图翻译与对齐；(3)跨域表征与记忆设计；(4)助手中介环境中的可信商业化；(5)所有权、访问与问责的运营机制。

Conclusion: 从隐式平台画像向可治理个性化的范式转变，强调未来推荐系统的发展不仅依赖更好的推理能力，更取决于构建用户能够理解、塑造和治理的个性化系统。

Abstract: Personalization has traditionally depended on platform-specific user models that are optimized for prediction but remain largely inaccessible to the people they describe. As LLM-based assistants increasingly mediate search, shopping, travel, and content access, this arrangement may be giving way to a new personalization stack in which user representation is no longer confined to isolated platforms. In this paper, we argue that the key issue is not simply that large language models can enhance recommendation quality, but that they reconfigure where and how user representations are produced, exposed, and acted upon. We propose a shift from hidden platform profiling toward governable personalization, where user representations may become more inspectable, revisable, portable, and consequential across services. Building on this view, we identify five research fronts for recommender systems: transparent yet privacy-preserving user modeling, intent translation and alignment, cross-domain representation and memory design, trustworthy commercialization in assistant-mediated environments, and operational mechanisms for ownership, access, and accountability. We position these not as isolated technical challenges, but as interconnected design problems created by the emergence of LLM agents as intermediaries between users and digital platforms. We argue that the future of recommender systems will depend not only on better inference, but on building personalization systems that users can meaningfully understand, shape, and govern.

</details>


### [170] [SAKE: Self-aware Knowledge Exploitation-Exploration for Grounded Multimodal Named Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20146)
*Jielong Tang,Xujie Yuan,Jiayang Liu,Jianxing Yu,Xiao Dong,Lin Chen,Yunlai Teng,Shimin Di,Jian Yin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SAKE是一个端到端的智能体框架，通过自监督推理和自适应搜索工具调用，协同利用内部知识和外部知识来解决社交媒体场景下的接地多模态命名实体识别（GMNER）问题，采用包含监督微调和强化学习的两阶段训练范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体平台上GMNER面临长尾、快速演化和未见实体的挑战。现有方法存在两极分化问题：基于外部知识检索的方法易引入噪声导致精度下降，而仅依赖MLLM内部知识的方法受限于知识边界且易产生幻觉。

Method: 提出两阶段训练范式：第一阶段通过Difficulty-aware Search Tag Generation方法，利用多次前向采样量化模型实体级不确定性，生成SAKE-SeCoT高质量思维链数据集进行监督微调；第二阶段采用智能体强化学习，结合混合奖励函数惩罚不必要的检索，使模型从刚性搜索模仿进化为真正基于自知的决策能力。

Result: 在两个广泛使用的社交媒体基准数据集上验证了SAKE的有效性。

Conclusion: SAKE成功实现了内部知识利用与外部知识探索的协调统一，通过自感知推理和自适应工具调用克服了传统方法的局限性，为开放世界社交媒体场景下的GMNER提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Grounded Multimodal Named Entity Recognition (GMNER) aims to extract named entities and localize their visual regions within image-text pairs, serving as a pivotal capability for various downstream applications. In open-world social media platforms, GMNER remains challenging due to the prevalence of long-tailed, rapidly evolving, and unseen entities. To tackle this, existing approaches typically rely on either external knowledge exploration through heuristic retrieval or internal knowledge exploitation via iterative refinement in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, heuristic retrieval often introduces noisy or conflicting evidence that degrades precision on known entities, while solely internal exploitation is constrained by the knowledge boundaries of MLLMs and prone to hallucinations. To address this, we propose SAKE, an end-to-end agentic framework that harmonizes internal knowledge exploitation and external knowledge exploration via self-aware reasoning and adaptive search tool invocation. We implement this via a two-stage training paradigm. First, we propose Difficulty-aware Search Tag Generation, which quantifies the model's entity-level uncertainty through multiple forward samplings to produce explicit knowledge-gap signals. Based on these signals, we construct SAKE-SeCoT, a high-quality Chain-of-Thought dataset that equips the model with basic self-awareness and tool-use capabilities through supervised fine-tuning. Second, we employ agentic reinforcement learning with a hybrid reward function that penalizes unnecessary retrieval, enabling the model to evolve from rigid search imitation to genuine self-aware decision-making about when retrieval is truly necessary. Extensive experiments on two widely used social media benchmarks demonstrate SAKE's effectiveness.

</details>


### [171] [Semantic Recall for Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20417)
*Leonardo Kuffo,Ioanna Tsakalidou,Roberta De Viti,Albert Angel,Jiří Iša,Rastislav Lenhardt*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对向量搜索中沿用已久的传统 recall 指标提出两点批评：它把语义无关但数学上"等距/更近"的噪声邻居当作必须召回的对象，因而惩罚了 ANNS 算法的"正确"行为；同时人工标注的"相关集"又会把嵌入模型自身的缺陷算到算法头上。作者提出两个新指标：语义召回（Semantic Recall，srecall）——只考察通过精确 NNS 理论可召回、且被外部评判器（LLM 或人工）判定为语义相关的 ground-truth 子集，召回比例 |R∩SN|/|SN|；容错召回（Tolerant Recall，trecall）——在无法判定语义相关时，允许用得分与真实近邻相近（在 x% 容差内）的检索结果做一对一替换，trecall@20=|T|/|G20|。在 MSMARCO（8.83M 文档、Gemini 3072 维嵌入、250 查询）与 MIRACL 泰语子集（542K 文档、Cohere Embed V3 1024 维、100 查询）上，语义召回与容错召回在低相关结果查询上显著高于传统召回（如 MSMARCO 少 SN 查询：0.903 vs 0.762），并与人工/跨模型评判高度一致（Cohen's κ=0.82）。以它们为调优目标可获得严格更优的成本–质量折中（同等质量下成本降低 5%–14%；以 95% 容错召回替代 95% 传统召回可省约 25%（BigANN）、5%（GloVe）、35%（MSMARCO）成本）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量检索中，衡量近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）质量的标准做法是 recall：把暴力精确 NNS 得到的 top-k 作为 ground truth，统计 ANNS 召回其中的比例。作者指出该指标的三大结构性缺陷：(1) 它把"数学上近但语义无关"的邻居（embedding 空间中的"数学噪声"）等同于必须召回的对象，导致低 recall 具有误导性；(2) 数据集中少数提供人工精选 ground truth（curated ground truth）的场合，精选集可能包含精确 NNS 都取不到的语义相关对象（如未靠近查询的"救护车"表情），等于用嵌入模型的缺陷惩罚 ANNS 算法；(3) 与 recall@k-ε、robustness@k-γ、Relative Distance Error 等阈值型指标相比，需要缺乏明确配置启发式、可解释性与跨数据集可移植性差。作者进一步发现：许多查询在其最近邻中仅有极少数语义相关结果（非 SN 的得分间隔极小、近乎等距），这是嵌入数据集中的普遍现象，传统 recall 因此系统性低估了这类查询的真实检索质量。

Method: 1) 语义邻居识别方法：先用暴力精确 NNS（内积相似度、归一化嵌入）计算每个查询的 top-k ground truth，再由外部评判者（本文用 Gemini 2.5，或人工、Claude Haiku 4.5 做交叉验证）逐一将 ground truth 中每个对象二分类为 Relevant / Not Relevant；提示词按检索负载与数据集定制（如 Q&A 语料以"文档能否回答查询"为相关性判据）。相比全量标注数百万 (query, document) 对，仅需标注每查询 top-k 子集。2) 指标定义：srecall = |R∩SN|/|SN|；trecall 定义为最大的 T⊂R_20，使 T 中每个元素与 G_20 中唯一元素一一对应，且要么相同、要么 score_t ≥ score_g·(1−x%)（内积相似度下的 x% 容差替换）。3) 评测配置：MSMARCO 8.83M 文档，Gemini Embedding 3072 维，250 个通识问题，top-100 ground truth；用 ScANN（分区型索引）+ 8-bit 量化做 top-100/top-20 检索；对比传统 recall、srecall、trecall（1% 容差），并与 R-precision 对比分析。4) 调参实验：用 Google Vizier 以"bytes read"为搜索成本，对 ScaNN 分别以传统 recall、trecall、srecall 为目标做帕累托调优，评估 CPU 查询成本。5) 泛化实验：MIRACL 泰语子集（542,166 文档，Cohere Embed V3 1024 维，100 查询，top-20 ground truth），FAISS IVF（3000 分区、8-bit 量化、探测 0.5% 簇），容差 2%。6) 容差阈值选取方法：定义代理量 (score_{2/3k} − score_k)/max_score×100 作为阈值参考，跨查询平均后与语义召回曲线对照；亦可由 8-bit 压缩引入的相对误差给出保守下界，或在有 SN 时由两条曲线交点确定。7) 交叉验证：抽样 100 查询 × top-100，用 Claude Haiku 4.5 作为 Gemini 家族外的独立评判者，一致率 91.1%、Cohen's κ=0.82，并分析分歧偏向（Gemini 更宽松、Claude 更严格）。

Result: (1) MSMARCO 中每查询语义邻居数呈双峰分布：40% 查询仅有 0–15 个 SN（3 个查询无相关结果），另一峰在 90–100（实际相关文档超过 100，上界由 top-100 截断所致）。SN 的平均内积得分在各分组均高于 non-SN（0.77 vs 0.71–0.76），且 SN 相邻得分差（delta）显著更大，而 non-SN 的 delta 极小（≈0.001–0.005），说明大量无关文档近乎等距、极易因排序扰动而丢失。(2) ScANN+8bit 量化：全体查询传统 recall 0.863、srecall 0.932、trecall 0.920；少 SN 查询（<20）传统 recall 0.762 vs srecall 0.903、trecall 0.859；20–80 SN 组 0.903/0.937/0.941；≥80 SN 组 0.976/0.978/0.991，差异主要集中于少相关结果查询。top-20 检索时少 SN 组为 0.869 vs 0.901。(3) 量化误差分析：全精度与 8-bit 标量量化的内积相对误差分布在 MSMARCO/GloVe/BigANN 上均足以重排无关邻居，导致传统 recall 被惩罚，trecall 以"允许在无关结果内替换"缓解，srecall 完全规避。(4) 与 R-precision 对比：少 SN 查询中 ground truth 排名与语义相关性呈负相关（秩双列相关 −0.72，p≈0），R-precision 会把"精确 NNS 也取不到"的语义相关文档计为错误（如 k=5、R=3、第三个 SN 排名第 4 的情形），而 srecall 允许相关文档出现在 top-k 任意位置。(5) 调优收益：以传统 recall 98% 调出的配置为基线（传统 98%、trecall 98.69%、srecall 98.93%），改以 trecall 98.69% 为目标可再降 5% 成本，以 srecall 98.93% 为目标可降 14% 成本；把目标从 95% 传统 recall 换成 95% 容错 recall，成本在 BigANN（1B×128 维）约降 25%、GloVe（1M×100 维）约降 5%、MSMARCO 约降 35%；作者解释为分区型索引中召回"近等距的无关邻居"需探测更多分区、代价高昂。(6) MIRACL 泰语实验：60% 查询 SN ≤ 4，呈幂律分布；SN 的得分 delta（0.05±0.03）远大于 non-SN（0.005±0.008）；FAISS IVF 下传统/semantic/tolerant recall 分别为 0.75/0.85/0.83，趋势与 MSMARCO 一致。(7) 交叉验证：Gemini 与 Claude Haiku 4.5 的一致率 91.1%（κ=0.82，Relevant 89.5%、Not Relevant 93.3%），仅 2 个查询一致率低于 70%；分歧时 Gemini 倾向宽松判 Relevant，Claude 严格依提示词标准。
