Failure risk is defined as the probability that predicted trajectories cross predefined degradation thresholds within a fixed horizon. Using longitudinal institutional indicators, we compare DCNAR-based trajectory risk models with discrete-time hazard and Cox proportional hazards models. Results show that trajectory-aware modeling consistently outperforms Cox models and improves risk prediction for several propagation-driven institutional failures. These findings highlight the importance of modeling dynamic system interactions for reliability analysis and early detection of systemic degradation.

</details>


### [196] [Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Generating Covalent Inhibitor Candidates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20019)
*Renee Gil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套面向共价抑制剂生成的多目标强化学习（RL）流水线：以在Papyrus数据集上预训练的SMILES-LSTM（DrugEx框架）为生成模型，用策略梯度RL结合Pareto拥挤距离（PCD）同时优化合成可及性、GCNII预测的共价活性、GAT预测的残基亲和性、GNN近似的对接分数、结构重叠/Tanimoto相似度与QED等评分函数。在EGFR（Cys797）与ACHE（Ser200）上，每模型生成1万分子，10次运行中已知共价抑制剂的重发现率最高分别为0.50%与0.74%；对接后筛选出亲电基团-残基距离达5.5 Å（EGFR）和3.2 Å（ACHE）的候选物。更具意义的是流水线自发生成了训练集中不存在的三类新型亲电基团骨架：丙二烯（allene）、3-氧代-β-磺内酰胺（3-oxo-β-sultam）和α-亚甲基-β-内酯，均有独立的共价反应性文献支持，说明RL引导的生成能在化学相关的方向上外推到训练分布之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共价抑制剂的设计要求同时满足多个相互冲突的目标：结合亲和力、靶点选择性、亲电反应性，以及把反应性"弹头"（warhead）精确摆放到靶蛋白亲核残基附近的结合几何要求。虚拟筛选只能评估已有化合物库，无法探索未触及的化学空间；生成模型虽可采样新分子，但缺乏系统性的多目标优化手段。RL引导的分子生成在一般药物设计中已显示潜力，但针对共价抑制剂这一细分场景的应用仍缺乏探索，因此需要一个把共价特异性评分函数（共价活性、亲核残基反应性、亲电基团定位与几何）嵌入RL目标函数的实用化流水线。

Method: 流水线由三部分组成：(1) 生成模型——DrugEx框架中基于SMILES字符级预测的LSTM，直接使用在Papyrus（127万结构，源自ChEMBL/PubChem/PDBBind/BindingDB）上预训练的公开权重；(2) 分子评分函数集——Validity、Ertl合成可及性SA（阈值≤6）、Gil & Rowley的GCNII共价活性分类器（p≥0.75）、本文新训的GAT残基亲和性多分类器（Cys/Ser-Thr，p≥0.75）、用molgraph在DOCKSTRING的AutoDock Vina分数上训练的GNN对接近似模型（阈值≤−6.0 kcal/mol）、RDKit GetCrippenO3A三维重叠分数（≥100，参考奥希替尼/马拉硫磷）、Morgan指纹Tanimoto相似度（≥0.1，参考喹啉）、QED（无阈值）；所有原始分数先经截断函数映射到[0,1]再作为奖励，权重均等为1.0；(3) RL算法——按Šícho等的策略梯度目标（序列似然×加权奖励和）优化，并用Pareto拥挤距离在每个前沿内偏好孤立解以维持多样性。对EGFR与ACHE各训练4个模型，逐次叠加重叠、QED、相似度评分器。评价方式为每个模型生成10,000结构、按阈值筛出"理想结构"、以InChI精确匹配计算已知共价抑制剂（ProteinReactiveDB/CovalentInDB）重发现率；再按预测对接分数取前250名用DOCKSTRING实 docking 估算弹头-残基距离；亲电基团原子通过在类别平衡数据集上重训的GCNII做GNN版GradCAM定位（归一化激活>0.3），化学空间用Morgan指纹+PCA+t-SNE可视化。

Result: 对接近似模型：EGFR R²=0.86、MSE=0.52、MAE=0.54；ACHE R²=0.75、MSE=0.88、MAE=0.74，且各分子量分箱表现一致。EGFR：EGFR-1至-4分别产生7391/6776/4793/4334个理想结构，重发现36/12/24/17个，对应0.49%/0.18%/0.50%/0.39%，最佳为EGFR-3；EGFR-2偏低是因为重叠评分器强烈牵引向奥希替尼样结构，加入QED与相似度后多样性恢复；t-SNE显示丙烯酰胺主导，与训练集和参考结构一致。ACHE：ACHE-1至-4理想结构9053/1835/945/468，重发现6/0/7/0，对应0.07%/0.00%/0.74%/0.00%，最佳为ACHE-3；ACHE-1以含硼化合物为主，ACHE-2~4因马拉硫磷参考结构牵引而富含磷。几何验证：EGFR-2最佳候选近似距离3.78 Å，实 docking 确认弹头-Cys797为5.5 Å（奥希替尼晶体结构为3.35 Å）；ACHE-4最佳候选3.23 Å，实测羰基路径3.2 Å、Michael受体路径6.3 Å，且其弹头为外烯式α-亚甲基-β-内酯。规模效应：EGFR-3在1万/5万/10万生成量下重发现数单调增加（5/90/418个），ACHE-3在更大规模下还重发现了BCHE、MGLL、FAAH、血栓蛋白酶等共享丝氨酸残基的抑制剂，提示向广谱丝氨酸反应空间漂移。异常弹头：子结构检索确认3-氧代-β-磺内酰胺与α-亚甲基-β-内酯在ProteinReactiveDB中零命中，丙二烯仅1个命中（SAH水解酶的S-烯丙基抑制剂，与生成结构无结构相似性）。

Conclusion: 多目标RL流水线（预训练LSTM + 共价特异性评分器 + PCD策略梯度）能在EGFR和ACHE上生成与已知共价抑制剂化学空间重叠的结构，10,000结构运行中最高重发现率0.50%（EGFR）和0.74%（ACHE），并经对接筛选获得符合共价结合几何的候选（5.5 Å/3.2 Å）。最具价值的观察是自发涌现了训练集中不存在但有文献支持的三类新型亲电弹头（丙二烯、3-氧代-β-磺内酰胺、α-亚甲基-β-内酯），表明RL引导的生成可以沿化学上有意义的方向外推到训练分布之外。局限在于评分函数本身是受限数据上训练的ML模型，RL目标完全由这些分数定义，因此生成空间仍受训练集偏置约束；多评分器叠加会压缩可行域（如EGFR-4、ACHE-4理想结构数骤降）；对接近似与GradCAM弹头定位均为近似，误差会累积，且弹头定位只标出区域而非具体原子，距离值仅为估计；最终活性仍必须通过实 docking 或实验验证确认。该流程可作为共价药物发现的基础工具，尤其配合对高排名候选的实验筛选。

Abstract: Rational design of covalent inhibitors requires simultaneously optimizing multiple properties, such as binding affinity, target selectivity, or electrophilic reactivity. This presents a multi-objective problem not easily addressed by screening alone. Here we present a machine learning pipeline for generating covalent inhibitor candidates using multi-objective reinforcement learning (RL), applied to two targets: epidermal growth factor receptor (EGFR) and acetylcholinesterase (ACHE). A SMILES-based pretrained LSTM serves as the generative model, optimized via policy gradient RL with Pareto crowding distance to balance competing scoring functions including synthetic accessibility, predicted covalent activity, residue affinity, and an approximated docking score. The pipeline rediscovers known covalent inhibitors at rates of up to 0.50% (EGFR) and 0.74% (ACHE) in 10,000-structure runs, with candidate structures achieving warhead-to-residue distances as short as 5.5 angstrom (EGFR) and 3.2 angstrom (ACHE) after further docking-based screening. More notably, the pipeline spontaneously generates structures bearing warhead motifs absent from the training data - including allenes, 3-oxo-$β$-sultams, and $α$-methylene-$β$-lactones - all of which have independent literature support as covalent warheads. These results suggest that RL-guided generation can explore covalent chemical space beyond its training distribution, and may be useful as a tool for medicinal chemists working on covalent drug discovery.

</details>


### [197] [CHASM: Unveiling Covert Advertisements on Chinese Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20511)
*Jingyi Zheng,Tianyi Hu,Yule Liu,Zhen Sun,Zongmin Zhang,Zifan Peng,Wenhan Dong,Xinlei He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出新任务“隐蔽广告检测”（covert advertisement detection），并发布了首个面向该任务的多模态数据集 CHASM——基于小红书真实场景、人工精标的 4,992 条多模态帖子（正例 612，负例 4,380，其中 1,127 条为易混淆的真实产品分享帖）。系统评测 15 个开源/闭源 MLLM：零样本与上下文学习下均不可靠，最佳 GPT-4o 零样本 F1 仅 0.597，DeepSeek-V3 上下文学习 F1 0.592，推理型模型无显著优势；在 CHASM 上微调 Qwen2.5-7B 后 F1 提升至 0.756（超过 GPT-4o，p<0.01）。误差分析显示四类错误（证据不足、遗漏线索、语言风格、结构模式），并提出动态检测、用户行为信号、创作者画像三个研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体广告生态繁荣，但传统广告易被用户跳过，广告主转而发布伪装成日常产品体验分享的“隐蔽广告”（软广），在图中或评论区隐藏购买链接、私信引流、店铺名等线索。此类内容具有欺骗性，违反中国与美国广告必须可识别的法律要求，且带来消费者欺诈、平台信誉受损等伦理问题。已有 LLM/MLLM 内容审核研究集中于假新闻、霸凌、毒性、仇恨言论等可客观核查的有害内容，忽视了隐蔽广告这一既隐蔽又无客观参考标准的任务；迄今没有面向隐蔽广告的公开数据集、任务定义或经训练的 MLLM。该任务还有三重特殊挑战：(1) 广告线索可出现在文本、图像或评论区，本质多模态；(2) 广告主刻意伪装，隐蔽性高；(3) 平台存在大量真实用户的产品体验分享，极易被误判为广告。

Method: 1) 任务定义：隐蔽广告是“伪装为普通内容、以在不披露广告性质的前提下微妙影响受众消费决策为主要目的的推广性内容”，须同时满足两个条件——作者有从品牌获得直接金钱收益的明确推广意图（不含涨粉等间接收益），且刻意将帖子伪装成日常内容；平台/用户明确标注为广告的不算。2) 标注指南（附录A）：以“明确推广证据、帖文语言风格、图文结构”三类证据为核心，另给出 13 条隐蔽广告常见特征（价格与购买方式、图中/评论区的购买链接、引流私域、评论中被指出是广告、蹭热门标签、卖山寨/小众产品、标题党、聚焦单一品牌、正式商业化语言、少提缺点、夸张宣传语、品牌名反复强调等）。3) 数据构建三阶段（Figure 2）：(a) 采集与匿名化——用三个全新无浏览历史账号于 2024 年 9–10 月采集公开帖（标题、正文、图片、评论、发布时间），过滤掉带“赞助”标签的显性广告，使用开源工具对文本（姓名、电话、邮箱、地址、网址）与图像人脸做遮蔽，并人工抽检 30 条确认无残留；(b) 委员会式制定标注指南与金标准问题；(c) 难度感知动态标注——先判断是否与产品/付费服务相关，无关者单人直接标为非广告；产品分享类（主观性高）由三名标注者多数投票，且至少含一名资深标注者。5 名中文母语标注者（时薪 5 美元，高于当地最低工资），共 6,474 次标注，Fleiss κ=0.65；70 道金标准题（20 题作资格测试需 ≥95% 准确率，50 题随机暗埋监控质量），金标准准确率由单人标注的 78% 提升至 94%，标注成本降至全面三人投票的 43.3%。4) 评测：对 15 个 MLLM（开源小/大模型 InternVL2.5-8B、LLaVA-NeXT-8B、Qwen2.5-VL-7B、DeepSeek-VL2、DeepSeek-V3、Llama-4-Maverick；闭源 Qwen-Max、GLM-4-Flash/Plus、GPT-4o、GPT-4o-mini、Gemini-2.0；推理型 QvQ-Max、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Step-R1-V-Mini）做零样本、上下文学习两种提示下的 P/R/F1/AUC 评测，并用 LoRA（rank 8, α=32，全线性层，AdamW，lr 1e-4，wd 0.1，cosine 调度，3 epoch，4096 token 截断，bf16）在 5 折交叉验证下微调表现最好的两个开源小模型（InternVL、Qwen2.5）；另做去图像/去评论的模态消融、详细提示消融（附录E）与轻量基线（TF-IDF+LR/SVM、BERT+ResNet、CLIP）对比。

Result: 零样本：GPT-4o 最佳 F1=0.597（P=0.464, R=0.836, AUC=0.851），DeepSeek-V3 F1=0.571，Gemini 2.5 Pro R 高达 0.921 但 P 仅 0.273、F1 0.422，Qwen2.5-7B 仅 0.421，DeepSeek-VL2 几乎失效。上下文学习：DeepSeek-V3 最佳 F1=0.592，Gemini 2.5 Pro F1=0.531/AUC 0.872，GPT-4o 降至 0.521，多数模型反而下降，说明 ICL 对该任务无效。推理型 MLLM 相对非推理模型无显著 F1 优势且成本更高。轻量基线 F1 仅 0.615–0.644（BERT+ResNet 最高）。微调后：Qwen2.5 P=0.783/R=0.732/F1=0.756/AUC=0.852，InternVL P=0.681/R=0.520/F1=0.590/AUC=0.743，二者相对零样本提升显著（p<0.01），Qwen2.5 微调后超过 GPT-4o 零样本。模态消融显示去掉图像或评论均显著掉点，评论线索尤为关键。误差分析（Table 4）：GPT-4o 零样本 47.8% 错误为“证据不足”的误判，微调 Qwen2.5 该类降至 17.6%，但“遗漏线索”（尤其评论线索）升至 41.2%，“结构模式”占 17.6%，说明微调改善了事实依据对齐但结构与细微线索识别仍不足。

Conclusion: 隐蔽广告本质上是欺骗性、主观性强、跨模态（正文/图像/评论）的任务，当前主流 MLLM 无论零样本、上下文学习还是推理模型都无法可靠胜任（最佳 F1 仅约 0.6），提示词中加入详细人工判据反而更差，说明模型难以通过提示对齐人类细粒度标准；在 CHASM 上微调开源小模型可带来显著且统计显著的提升（Qwen2.5-7B F1 0.756），且成本更低、更可靠，是平台落地的更优方案。剩余核心难点是识别评论中的细微线索与图文结构差异。作者呼吁平台与社区关注这一新兴威胁，并提出三条方向：动态/时序检测（广告标签随评论区演化）、引入用户行为信号（点赞、停留时长、举报频次）、创作者画像与历史可信度；建议任何处罚性处置须经人工复核以避免误伤正常分享。局限在于仅覆盖中文单平台小红书、规模有限，且出于隐私未采集用户与创作者信息。

Abstract: Current benchmarks for evaluating large language models (LLMs) in social media moderation completely overlook a serious threat: covert advertisements, which disguise themselves as regular posts to deceive and mislead consumers into making purchases, leading to significant ethical and legal concerns. In this paper, we present the CHASM, a first-of-its-kind dataset designed to evaluate the capability of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in detecting covert advertisements on social media. CHASM is a high-quality, anonymized, manually curated dataset consisting of 4,992 instances, based on real-world scenarios from the Chinese social media platform Rednote. The dataset was collected and annotated under strict privacy protection and quality control protocols. It includes many product experience sharing posts that closely resemble covert advertisements, making the dataset particularly challenging.The results show that under both zero-shot and in-context learning settings, none of the current MLLMs are sufficiently reliable for detecting covert advertisements.Our further experiments revealed that fine-tuning open-source MLLMs on our dataset yielded noticeable performance gains. However, significant challenges persist, such as detecting subtle cues in comments and differences in visual and textual structures.We provide in-depth error analysis and outline future research directions. We hope our study can serve as a call for the research community and platform moderators to develop more precise defenses against this emerging threat.

</details>


### [198] [Continuous Semantic Caching for Low-Cost LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20021)
*Baran Atalar,Xutong Liu,Jinhang Zuo,Siwei Wang,Wei Chen,Carlee Joe-Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次为连续语义查询空间中的LLM响应缓存建立严格理论框架，通过动态ε-net离散化与核岭回归实现不确定性量化和连续邻域泛化，在切换成本优化下达到亚线性遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM缓存框架假设离散查询空间，但实际用户查询无限且连续。传统方法难以适应LLM查询嵌入空间的本质特性，导致缓存策略与真实需求脱节。

Method: 提出动态ε-net离散化耦合核岭回归方法，实现：(1)对LLM查询成本进行形式化不确定性量化；(2)在连续语义邻域内泛化部分反馈；(3)开发离线学习与在线自适应算法，专注于减少缓存响应切换成本。

Result: 在线算法实现对最优连续oracle的亚线性遗憾界，且可退化为现有离散查询模型的边界。实验表明框架能良好逼近连续最优缓存，同时降低计算和切换开销。

Conclusion: 本研究为连续空间LLM语义缓存奠定理论基础，首次实现连续查询空间下缓存优化的严格理论保证，兼具实践可行性与理论优雅性。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) become increasingly popular, caching responses so that they can be reused by users with semantically similar queries has become a vital strategy for reducing inference costs and latency. Existing caching frameworks have proposed to decide which query responses to cache by assuming a finite, known universe of discrete queries and learning their serving costs and arrival probabilities. As LLMs' pool of users and queries expands, however, such an assumption becomes increasingly untenable: real-world LLM queries reside in an infinite, continuous embedding space. In this paper, we establish the first rigorous theoretical framework for semantic LLM response caching in continuous query space under uncertainty. To bridge the gap between discrete optimization and continuous representation spaces, we introduce dynamic $ε$-net discretization coupled with Kernel Ridge Regression. This design enables the system to formally quantify estimation uncertainty and generalize partial feedback on LLM query costs across continuous semantic query neighborhoods. We develop both offline learning and online adaptive algorithms optimized to reduce switching costs incurred by changing the cached responses. We prove that our online algorithm achieves a sublinear regret bound against an optimal continuous oracle, which reduces to existing bounds for discrete query models. Extensive empirical evaluations demonstrate that our framework approximates the continuous optimal cache well while also reducing computational and switching overhead compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [199] [Statistics, Not Scale: Modular Medical Dialogue with Bayesian Belief Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20022)
*Yusuf Kesmen,Fay Elhassan,Jiayi Ma,Julien Stalhandske,David Sasu,Alexandra Kulinkina,Akhil Arora,Lars Klein,Mary-Anne Hartley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoBayes（摘要版称 BMBE）主张把"语言"与"概率推理"严格分离：LLM 只做传感器（把患者话语解析为结构化证据三元组、并把贝叶斯模块选中的特征口语化提问），全部诊断推理交给确定性的贝叶斯引擎（显式后验 + 期望信息增益选题 + 阈值化弃答），从而在更低的 token 成本下取得高于同族前沿"自主 LLM 医生"与既有诊断对话系统的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文将"LLM 混淆自然语言沟通与概率推理"定性为架构缺陷而非工程缺陷：现有对话式临床决策支持系统（Med-PaLM 2、Med-Gemini、AMIE 及端到端医疗 LLM）没有显式后验状态、没有信息论驱动的提问策略、置信度未校准、推理链不可审计，且在对抗性患者沟通、低患病率病种上崩溃，还带有校准差、跨运行不稳定、对抗幻觉、地域与种族偏见等安全代价。既有"概率-LLM 混合"（BED-LLM、DeLLMa、BIRD、APP、UoT、CIP、MedKGI）与"多智能体诊断框架"（MEDDxAgent、AgentClinic、MediQ）虽引入概率结构，却仍让 LLM 充当概率源、候选生成器、规划基底或隐式推理状态；临床数据微调路线（CoD/DiagnosisGPT 等）则牺牲了可审计性、隐私与可持续性。作者主张正确的应对不是扩规模而是"严格分离（strict separation）"：把 LLM 限制在语言接口，把全部概率状态置于可审计、可替换的外部统计引擎中。

Method: 形式化为序贯贝叶斯决策过程：疾病集 D（K）、特征集 F（N），每轮维护后验 b_t∈Δ^{K-1}，目标是在问题预算 T_max 内，当 max_d b_T(d) ≥ τ 时提交 argmax 诊断，否则弃答。两个模块：(1) 知识库——两条可互换构建路径共用同一 schema：经验 KB（DDxPlus 1.03M 训练记录共现计数 → Dirichlet-Categorical + Laplace 平滑，49 病/314 特征，先验取训练集患病频率）与 LLM 诱导 KB（GPT-5.4 与 Gemini 3.1 两阶段结构化提示：先特征目录再去重校验，再 elicit P(feature|disease)，GPT 160 特征/Gemini 89 特征，共享 45 特征上 Pearson r=0.931、Gemini 系统性高 0.055；AgentClinic-MedQA 采用"患者驱动+疾病驱动"双目录去重与连续分带 likelihood elicitation 以避免模式坍塌）。(2) 贝叶斯模块——Pearl 虚拟证据（软证据）更新：解析器给出的置信度 c∈(0,1] 将似然与中性向量混合 L_eff(d)=c·P(X_f=v|d)+(1−c)·1，c=1 退化为标准贝叶斯、c→0 证据无信息；提问采用朴素贝叶斯因子分解下的近视 EIG 策略，f*=argmax_f EIG(f)（熵减，可选 top-k 聚焦项 λ·EIG_top-k，k=3、λ=0.5、θ=0.3 时 Top-1 +12pp）；停止规则按优先级为预热 T_min=12 → 后验达 τ 提交 → 硬预算 T_max 到期则弃答；τ 为部署参数，权衡选择性准确率 Sel.Acc 与覆盖率 Cov，用 DHS_α=(α/SelAcc+(1−α)/Cov)^{-1} 汇总（α=0.5）。(3) 语言层——LLM 无权访问 b_t、熵或任何内部状态，仅按 (f,v,c) 三元组接口交互：解析采用三级级联（确定性模式匹配 → LLM 结构化分类到 V_f×L → 检测不确定线索时降级为 uncertain），置信度用 5 级序数标签映射到 {1.00,0.80,0.50,0.25,0.05}（把连续概率校准难题转成 LLM 更擅长的序数判断）；问题由 EIG 选中的特征经 verbaliser 生成，语气由粗粒度置信度指示调节；另有一次 bulk intake 调用把主诉映射为多条证据以减少追问数。评估覆盖 DDxPlus 50/100（PatientSim 与 MEDDxAgent HT-sim 两列）与 AgentClinic-MedQA 50/100，对比 6 个前沿自主 LLM 医生、CoT/DDx-CoT/Bayesian-CoT/UoT/MediQ/MEDDxAgent/AgentClinic/CoD-DiagnosisGPT-34B 与自建 Closed-World、strict-abstain、closed-world 消融，指标含 Top-1/Top-3、Sel.Acc、Cov、DHS、token/例与每例 API 成本，2000 次 bootstrap CI 与配对 bootstrap 显著性检验。

Result: 1) 对既有基线：DDxPlus 100（PatientSim）与 100（HT-sim）两列 DHS 均为 90（T-1 82/81，SA 85/87），显著高于最强非本方法基线（Bayesian-CoT 74、MEDDxAgent paper-best 62/72）；AgentClinic-MedQA 上 DHS 83（SA 74.5、Cov 94），具竞争力但低于 MEDDxAgent closed-world（88.9）与 CoT（84.1）；微调基线 CoD/DiagnosisGPT-34B 全面最差（DHS 19.8/10.7/67.2）。2) 对前沿自主医生：每个 MoBayes 变体在至少两个基准上 DHS 超过全部六个自主医生，标题配置 MoBayes+GPT-5.4-nano 三列 DHS 88.0/89.2/86.8，对应最强自主医生 Gemini 3.1 Pro 75.7/77.3/80.4。3) 成本效益：MoBayes 每例约 1.4 美分（GPT-5.4-nano），而 SA Gemini 3.1 Pro 25.0 美分、SA GPT-5.4 19.6 美分，低 10–25×；DHS–成本散点中 MoBayes 全部落在左上象限，族内替换（同一模型族用廉价传感器+引擎替换前沿自主医生）箭头一律指向"更低成本、更高 DHS"。4) 架构归因：把经验 KB 换成完全由 LLM 诱导的 KB 后，GPT 生态 MoBayes+nano Top-1 70 / DHS 83 对 SA GPT-5.4 的 72 / 84（成本约 1/10），并接近 KB oracle（76）；独立医生在自身世界模型模拟的罕见病上崩塌（罕见病 DHS 29 vs MoBayes 84），说明差距来自架构而非信息。5) 可控选择性：扫动单一 τ 给出连续准确率–覆盖率前沿，在同等覆盖率下所有传感器均压过所有自主医生点；6) 人格鲁棒性：四种对抗 persona 下 MoBayes 平均 DHS 86（最低 82，覆盖率 96–100%），自主 GPT-5.4 平均 53（覆盖率塌至 36–48%），差距 Δ+18～+41；罕见病分层上 MoBayes DHS 近平坦（95/84/83），自主医生急剧下滑（67/80/61）；疾病空间从 K=10 扩到 40，MoBayes Top-1 稳定在 78–86，自主医生 34–41。7) 消融与失败分析：先验（经验/均匀/条件）差异很小（DHS 83/83/86），均匀先验利于罕见病（+15pp）；跨 KB 迁移因特征覆盖仅 43%/59% 而下降（DHS 69/61）；83 例承诺错误中 19% 归因 KB、49% 归因 LLM 证据管线（假阳性为主、且随传感器能力下降而减少）、约 26% 归因推理（最强传感器无 diverged 失败）；效率上每轮 EIG 约 154K 次运算、单核约 50ms；数据处理不等式给出软证据信息损失上界，实测平均置信度 c̄≈0.9。作者还审计了 LLM 疾病名匹配器（250 次承诺中 1.6%–5.2% 匹配错误高估自主医生得分，无漏判），说明修正后差距更大。

Conclusion: 结论是：可靠的对话式临床决策支持不应依赖"next-token 预测即推理"和单纯扩大模型规模，而应"用 LLM 做沟通、用显式概率系统做校准推理"——把知识库、后验跟踪、EIG 提问与弃答规则全部移出 LLM，即可同时获得可审计性、逐轮可追踪的推理痕迹、隐私（患者数据不入权重）、可按人群替换统计后端而无需重训（适合低资源场景），以及连续可控的准确率–覆盖率工作点。局限包括：闭世界假设（只能诊断 KB 内病种，覆盖度是部署关键，弃答机制提供安全阀）；依赖模拟患者而非真实临床交互；无法利用患者主动提供但未被提问的额外证据（这是自主 LLM 天然能做到的）。整体定位是"临床决策支持而非自主诊断"，属于部署模式创新而非新的概率引擎或新 LLM。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as autonomous diagnostic agents, yet they conflate two fundamentally different capabilities: natural-language communication and probabilistic reasoning. We argue that this conflation is an architectural flaw, not an engineering shortcoming. We introduce BMBE (Bayesian Medical Belief Engine), a modular diagnostic dialogue framework that enforces a strict separation between language and reasoning: an LLM serves only as a sensor, parsing patient utterances into structured evidence and verbalising questions, while all diagnostic inference resides in a deterministic, auditable Bayesian engine. Because patient data never enters the LLM, the architecture is private by construction; because the statistical backend is a standalone module, it can be replaced per target population without retraining. This separation yields three properties no autonomous LLM can offer: calibrated selective diagnosis with a continuously adjustable accuracy-coverage tradeoff, a statistical separation gap where even a cheap sensor paired with the engine outperforms a frontier standalone model from the same family at a fraction of the cost, and robustness to adversarial patient communication styles that cause standalone doctors to collapse. We validate across empirical and LLM-generated knowledge bases against frontier LLMs, confirming the advantage is architectural, not informational.

</details>


### [200] [Replicable Bandits with UCB based Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20024)
*Rohan Deb,Udaya Ghai,Karan Singh,Arindam Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究随机多臂老虎机(MAB)和线性老虎机的可复制算法设计。基于UCB探索框架，提出了RepUCB（可复制批量UCB算法）和RepLinUCB（可复制线性老虎机算法），其中RepLinUCB相比之前最佳结果在 regret  bound 上改进了 O(d/ρ) 因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有老虎机算法在可复制性方面存在不足：此前工作主要基于消除法，且线性老虎机在无限动作空间下依赖离散化，导致对维度d和失败概率ρ的依赖欠优。需要开发更高效的乐观式替代方案。

Method: 1. RepUCB：可复制批量UCB算法，通过共享内部随机性实现动作序列的可复制性（概率≥1-ρ）。
2. RepRidge：可复制岭回归估计器，同时满足置信度保证和ρ-可复制性保证。
3. RepLinUCB：基于RepRidge的可复制乐观算法，用于随机线性老虎机。

Result: RepUCB获得regret: O(K²log²T/ρ² × Σ(Δ_a + log(KTlogT)/Δ_a))；RepLinUCB获得regret: Ō((d + d³/ρ)√T)，相比此前最佳保证改进了O(d/ρ)因子。

Conclusion: 乐观式算法可以显著降低可复制性的代价。RepRidge估计器本身也具有独立统计学意义，可应用于其他统计估计场景。

Abstract: We study replicable algorithms for stochastic multi-armed bandits (MAB) and linear bandits with UCB (Upper Confidence Bound) based exploration. A bandit algorithm is $ρ$-replicable if two executions using shared internal randomness but independent reward realizations, produce the same action sequence with probability at least $1-ρ$. Prior work is primarily elimination-based and, in linear bandits with infinitely many actions, relies on discretization, leading to suboptimal dependence on the dimension $d$ and $ρ$. We develop optimistic alternatives for both settings. For stochastic multi-armed bandits, we propose RepUCB, a replicable batched UCB algorithm and show that it attains a regret $O\!\left(\frac{K^2\log^2 T}{ρ^2}\sum_{a:Δ_a>0}\left(Δ_a+\frac{\log(KT\log T)}{Δ_a}\right)\right)$. For stochastic linear bandits, we first introduce RepRidge, a replicable ridge regression estimator that satisfies both a confidence guarantee and a $ρ$-replicability guarantee. Beyond its role in our bandit algorithm, this estimator and its guarantees may also be of independent interest in other statistical estimation settings. We then use RepRidge to design RepLinUCB, a replicable optimistic algorithm for stochastic linear bandits, and show that its regret is bounded by $\widetilde{O}\!\big(\big(d+\frac{d^3}ρ\big)\sqrt{T}\big)$. This improves the best prior regret guarantee by a factor of $O(d/ρ)$, showing that our optimistic algorithm can substantially reduce the price of replicability.

</details>


### [201] [Maximum Entropy Semi-Supervised Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20074)
*Julien Audiffren,Michal Valko,Alessandro Lazaric,Mohammad Ghavamzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MESSI算法，将半监督学习思想融入最大熵逆强化学习，通过成对轨迹惩罚机制利用无监督轨迹数据提升学习效果，在自动驾驶和网格世界任务中验证了算法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆强化学习（IRL）存在模糊性问题：当多条策略都能匹配专家行为时，无法确定唯一的奖励函数。MaxEnt-IRL虽然通过最大熵原理部分解决了这一问题，但在只有少量专家轨迹的情况下仍然存在局限性。作者希望利用额外可用的无监督轨迹数据来提升算法性能。

Method: MESSI算法将无监督轨迹数据整合到MaxEnt-IRL框架中，通过在轨迹上引入成对惩罚项来实现半监督学习。

Result: 在高速公路驾驶和网格世界问题上的实验表明，MESSI能够有效利用无监督轨迹数据，改善了MaxEnt-IRL的原始性能。

Conclusion: MESSI成功地将半监督学习思想融入最大熵逆强化学习框架，通过利用额外无监督轨迹数据改善了传统MaxEnt-IRL的性能。

Abstract: A popular approach to apprenticeship learning (AL) is to formulate it as an inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) problem. The MaxEnt-IRL algorithm successfully integrates the maximum entropy principle into IRL and unlike its predecessors, it resolves the ambiguity arising from the fact that a possibly large number of policies could match the expert's behavior. In this paper, we study an AL setting in which in addition to the expert's trajectories, a number of unsupervised trajectories is available. We introduce MESSI, a novel algorithm that combines MaxEnt-IRL with principles coming from semi-supervised learning. In particular, MESSI integrates the unsupervised data into the MaxEnt-IRL framework using a pairwise penalty on trajectories. Empirical results in a highway driving and grid-world problems indicate that MESSI is able to take advantage of the unsupervised trajectories and improve the performance of MaxEnt-IRL.

</details>


### [202] [Analysis of Nystrom method with sequential ridge leverage scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20077)
*Daniele Calandriello,Alessandro Lazaric,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模核岭回归（KRR）的计算效率问题，提出INK-ESTIMATE算法，通过增量计算 ridge leverage scores (RLSs) 的近似值来实现高效的Nystrom方法采样。该算法只需单次遍历数据，使用固定的小规模空间，并在任意中间步骤都能提供统计和逼近保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模核岭回归（KRR）面临的核心瓶颈是需要存储完整的核矩阵K_t，内存开销巨大。Nystrom方法通过随机采样核矩阵的列来重建近似矩阵，但采样分布的选择会显著影响统计性能与计算效率的权衡。理论上，基于ridge leverage scores (RLSs)的采样分布能提供良好的重构保证，但精确计算RLSs的复杂度与求解KRR问题相当。

Method: INK-ESTIMATE算法采用增量式sketch方法维护核矩阵K_t的小规模近似表示。在每个时间步，利用当前sketch计算RLSs的中间估计值。算法设计的关键特点是：(1) sketch更新无需访问历史列数据，支持数据流式处理；(2) 空间复杂度固定，仅依赖于核矩阵的有效维度（effective dimension）。

Result: INK-ESTIMATE实现了以下理论保证：(1) 对真实核矩阵与近似矩阵之间的距离提供强逼近保证；(2) 对近似KRR解的统计风险提供保证；(3) 所有理论保证在任意中间步骤均成立，算法具有良好的在线/增量特性。

Conclusion: 该工作为流式/sequential场景下的大规模核学习方法提供了高效的RLSs近似估计方案，通过固定小空间预算和单次数据遍历即可获得具有理论保证的近似KRR解，显著降低了Nystrom方法的计算和存储开销。

Abstract: Large-scale kernel ridge regression (KRR) is limited by the need to store a large kernel matrix K_t. To avoid storing the entire matrix K_t, Nystrom methods subsample a subset of columns of the kernel matrix, and efficiently find an approximate KRR solution on the reconstructed matrix. The chosen subsampling distribution in turn affects the statistical and computational tradeoffs. For KRR problems, recent works show that a sampling distribution proportional to the ridge leverage scores (RLSs) provides strong reconstruction guarantees for the approximation. While exact RLSs are as difficult to compute as a KRR solution, we may be able to approximate them well enough. In this paper, we study KRR problems in a sequential setting and introduce the INK-ESTIMATE algorithm, that incrementally computes the RLSs estimates. INK-ESTIMATE maintains a small sketch of K_t, that at each step is used to compute an intermediate estimate of the RLSs. First, our sketch update does not require access to previously seen columns, and therefore a single pass over the kernel matrix is sufficient. Second, the algorithm requires a fixed, small space budget to run dependent only on the effective dimension of the kernel matrix. Finally, our sketch provides strong approximation guarantees on the distance between the true kernel matrix and its approximation, and on the statistical risk of the approximate KRR solution at any time, because all our guarantees hold at any intermediate step.

</details>


### [203] [Improved large-scale graph learning through ridge spectral sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20078)
*Daniele Calandriello,Ioannis Koutis,Alessandro Lazaric,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GSQUEAK是一种在分布式流式设置下对图拉普拉斯矩阵进行稀疏化的新算法，通过维护有效电阻的小子集来实现，能够在单次遍历中以分布式方式处理边，并提供强光谱近似保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式流式设置中，当新边实时到达时，很难快速、近似地学习同时保持L的分布式表示。现有方法难以在保持图结构信息的同时实现高效在线处理。

Method: GSQUEAK算法通过维护少量有效电阻来高效稀疏化拉普拉斯矩阵，利用有效电阻与图谱性质之间的内在联系，在流式环境中实现增量更新。

Result: 该算法产生具有强光谱近似保证的稀疏化结果，能够在保持原始拉普拉斯关键谱特性的同时大幅降低图的规模。

Conclusion: GSQUEAK能够在单次遍历和分布式环境下有效处理边流，为大规模图上的分布式机器学习提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Graph-based techniques and spectral graph theory have enriched the field of machine learning with a variety of critical advances. A central object in the analysis is the graph Laplacian L, which encodes the structure of the graph. We consider the problem of learning over this Laplacian in a distributed streaming setting, where new edges of the graph are observed in real time by a network of workers. In this setting, it is hard to learn quickly or approximately while keeping a distributed representation of L. To address this challenge, we present a novel algorithm, GSQUEAK, which efficiently sparsifies the Laplacian by maintaining a small subset of effective resistances. We show that our algorithm produces sparsifiers with strong spectral approximation guarantees, all while processing edges in a single pass and in a distributed fashion.

</details>


### [204] [On the Quantization Robustness of Diffusion Language Models in Coding Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20079)
*Aarav Gupta,Gururaj Deshpande,Chandreyi Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究扩散式编码语言模型(CoDA)在训练后量化下的鲁棒性，发现其在2-4位低比特宽度下比自回归模型Qwen3-1.7B具有更小的精度衰减，表明扩散语言模型可能更适合高效部署场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型在编码任务上表现优异但内存和推理成本高昂；扩散式语言模型虽通过迭代去噪实现有界推理成本，但其训练后量化行为尚缺乏探索。

Method: 采用GPTQ和改良版HAWQ算法对扩散式编码模型CoDA进行训练后量化，并将其与自回归模型Qwen3-1.7B在统一评估框架下进行对比。

Result: 在2-4位低比特宽度下，CoDA展现出更强的量化鲁棒性，HumanEval和MBPP基准上的精度衰减更小；基于HAWQ的混合精度配置可实现精度、延迟和内存之间的平滑权衡。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型可能为高效部署提供优势，因为其具有更强的量化恢复力。

Abstract: Auto-regressive Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong performance on coding tasks, but incur high memory and inference costs. Diffusion-based language models (d-LLMs) offer bounded inference cost via iterative denoising, but their behavior under post-training quantization (PTQ) has been sparsely explored. We investigate the application and robustness of PTQ techniques, specifically GPTQ and a modified Hessian-Aware Quantization (HAWQ) algorithm, on a diffusion-based coding LLM (CoDA) and observe that these methods applied to CoDA exhibit greater robustness at low bitwidths compared to Qwen3-1.7B, its auto-regressive counterpart, under a standardized evaluation pipeline. We find that in our setup, CoDA exhibits greater robustness at low bitwidths (2-4 bits), with smaller accuracy degradation across HumanEval and MBPP benchmarks. Additionally, mixed-precision configurations derived from HAWQ provide smooth trade-offs across accuracy, latency, and memory. The results suggest that diffusion LLMs may offer advantages for efficient deployment due to more quantization-resilience.

</details>


### [205] [Concept Graph Convolutions: Message Passing in the Concept Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20082)
*Lucie Charlotte Magister,Pietro Lio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出「概念图卷积」（Concept Graph Convolution, CGC），这是首个以可解释的节点级概念表征为核心设计的图卷积层。它将节点的原始潜在表征与模糊概念编码经共享线性变换投影到同一空间，用可学习标量 η 混合后进行消息传递；边权由图结构归一化邻接权重与基于邻居概念表征的单头注意力（GAT 风格）加权组合而成，混合系数为可学习标量 γ（辅以 L2 正则引导其偏向注意力）；聚合后再经归一化 softmax 重新蒸馏出概念表征，从而可逐层追踪概念的演化。另提出完全在概念空间中运作的 pure CGC 变体。实验表明：CGC 在节点与图分类任务上精度与标准 GCN/GAT 相当甚至更优（合成数据集上普遍超越），概念完整性得分接近或优于基线，并能在每层提取子图级概念解释（如在 Mutagenicity 上发现 NO₂ 结构、在 BA-Shapes/BA-Grid 上发现与语义对应的注意力分布）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络（GNN）虽在多类任务上表现优异，但其“推理过程不透明”严重削弱了人类对预测结果的信任，导致其在医疗、司法等高风险场景难以落地。已有可解释性工作大多属于两类：一类是后验解释方法（如 GNNExplainer、GLGExplainer、LARA），它们解释的是模型已输出的结果，而非消息传递本身；另一类是概念瓶颈/概念解释方法（如 CGN、GLG），它们从消息传递之后的隐层表征中聚类抽取概念，虽能揭示“模型最后用了什么概念”，却完全无法解释“概念是如何在消息传递过程中形成与相互影响的”。导致消息传递不可解释的核心原因在于：节点的最终表征是通过邻域聚合（尤其是 GCN 中的简单平均）计算得到的，聚合后向量本身失去了可读语义；即使是引入注意力权重的 GAT/GATv2，也只是让“邻居贡献大小”变得可读，被聚合的对象——节点向量本身依然不可解释。因此，需要一种图卷积层，让消息传递直接发生在人类可读的概念空间之中，从而在每个卷积步骤都能观测到概念的演化过程。

Method: （1）表征构造：节点同时拥有原始潜在表征 u_i^l 与模糊概念编码 q_i^l（由 scaled softmax 得到，对应 CGN 的概念聚类机制）。二者经共享线性变换 W^l 投影到同一空间得 h_ui^l 与 h_qi^l，再以可学习标量 η 按 h_i^l=(1-η)h_ui^l+η h_qi^l 混合；η 施加 L2 正则（目标 η_0=0.8）以鼓励使用概念表征。（2）边权计算：结构权重用 Kipf & Welling 的归一化邻接 Ã_ij=A_ij/√(d_i d_j)；注意力权重直接作用于邻居的概念向量 α_ij=softmax_j(LeakyReLU(a^T[q_i‖q_j]))，采用单头静态注意力以保证可解释性（不用 GATv2 的动态注意力）；两者以可学习标量 γ 组合 w_ij=(1-γ)Ã_ij+γα_ij，并对 γ 加 L2 正则（目标 γ_0=0.8）鼓励依赖概念注意力。（3）消息传递：m_ij^l = w_ij h_i^l，z_j^{l+1}=Σ_{i∈N(j)∪{j}} m_ij^l + b^l，偏置项提供全局概念先验并打破对称性；最后对 z_j^{l+1} 再施加归一化 softmax 得到新概念 q_j^{l+1}，使概念空间得以跨层延续（仅第一层输入为原始特征）。（4）pure CGC：丢弃原始潜在表征，仅用概念表征进行消息传递。（5）度量：提出面向图分类的新的概念完整性（concept completeness）计算方式——将图中概念标签二值化后聚合为频率向量，再训练分类器（如决策树）从该频率向量预测图标签；节点分类则沿用 Magister et al. 的节点级概念完整性。（6）实验设置：采用 GNNExplainer 的 5 个合成节点分类数据集（BA-Shapes、BA-Community、BA-Grid、Tree-Cycles、Tree-Grid）、4 个合成图分类数据集（Grid、Grid-House、STARS、House-Colour）及 2 个真实图分类数据集（Mutagenicity、Reddit-Binary）；基线为 GCN、GAT（均按 CGN 加入归一化 softmax 以抽取概念）、R-CBMs；PyTorch 实现，5 个随机种子，SGD 训练，保持相同超参。

Result: 精度方面：CGC 在全部合成数据集上超过 GCN 与 GAT；CGC 在 11 个数据集中的 7 个上优于 pure CGC（如 BA-Shapes 98.86%、BA-Grid 99.90%、Reddit-Binary 87.55% vs GCN 72.95%），pure CGC 仅在 BA-Community 上显著领先（82.69% vs 69.56%）；GAT 因合成数据集节点特征全为 1、初始表征同质而表现极差（部分任务接近随机猜测），验证了“结构权重+概念注意力”组合的必要性；Grid-House 任务所有模型均失败（最佳为 GCN 63%）。概念完整性方面：pure CGC 在 5 个节点分类任务中的 4 个取得最高最终层完整性得分，CGC 在 BA-Shapes 上最优；图分类任务中 CGC/pure CGC/GCN 表现相近（Grid、STARS、House-Colour 上均较高，Grid-House 全部接近 50%），作者指出最终层单一指标无法体现 CGC 的真正优势——可在每一层度量完整性与提取子图解释。概念演化方面：概念完整性随层数加深而上升，说明更深层的感受野对任务更重要；但 Mutagenicity 与 Reddit-Binary 上随深度反而略有下降。过平滑分析（BA-Shapes）：超过 5 层后精度与完整性同时下滑，3 层模型完整性最高而精度仅损失约 0.5 个百分点。训练时间分析（Tree-Cycles）：Layer 0 的完整性在约 1500 epoch 后崩塌，建议在该点前选检查点；pure CGC 训练更稳定。参数分析：节点分类中 γ 多小于 0.4（结构主导，因任务几乎纯结构性），并随深度上升；图分类中 γ 在末层普遍大于 0.5，反映注意力在稀疏概念聚合中渐显重要；η 在节点分类中多数小于 0.5 但不低于约 0.25，说明模型同时依赖原始与概念表征。定性分析：可视化显示注意力分配与真实结构重要性吻合（BA-Shapes 房屋底部节点的两个房屋邻居注意力 0.48/0.34，基座邻居仅 0.18；BA-Community 房屋顶部节点的两个中间节点 0.55/0.45；BA-Grid 网格对称导致邻居注意力相同，γ=0.56）；CGC 在 Mutagenicity 第二层成功发现 NO₂ 关键基序，第三层因感受野过大而淹没该结构；还能发现稀有细粒度概念（如 House-Colour 中唯一的彩色房屋）。与 R-CBMs 相比，R-CBMs 精度略高（BA-Community 92.2% vs 82.69%），但其概念原子来自两层 GCN 的输出、不可解释，且解释形式为逻辑句，CGC 的解释为逐层节点级概念表征，二者定位不同。作者同时讨论了注意力作为解释的争议（Jain & Wallace、Wiegreffe & Pinter、Bai 等、Akula & Zhu），提醒 γ 较低时注意力分数校准不足、需谨慎解读。

Conclusion: 概念图卷积（CGC）证明可以让消息传递直接发生在可解释的概念空间中，从而在不牺牲预测精度的前提下获得对 GNN 推理过程的深层洞察：其在 11 个节点/图分类任务上的精度与标准 GCN/GAT 相当甚至更优，绕开了常见的“精度—可解释性”权衡；其核心价值在于能够在每个卷积层抽取并追踪概念演化，提供不同粒度的子图级解释（早期层对应更小的 p-hop 邻域、细粒度结构，后期层对应更具任务代表性的粗粒度概念）。pure CGC 通过完全丢弃原始潜在表征把计算强制约束在概念空间内，精度与 CGC 相当且可解释性更强，值得作为构建可解释 GNN 的首选。局限与展望：反复施加 scaled softmax 虽简单有效，却可能带来信息损失与梯度消失（实验中未成为主要瓶颈）；此外模型深度需要权衡——过深会同时降低精度与概念完整性（3 层是精度与完整性的较好折中），且训练后期可解释性会退化，需按 epoch 选择检查点。后续可探索缓解重复 softmax 的方案、在更多真实数据集上验证，并研究注意力分数的校准问题。

Abstract: The trust in the predictions of Graph Neural Networks is limited by their opaque reasoning process. Prior methods have tried to explain graph networks via concept-based explanations extracted from the latent representations obtained after message passing. However, these explanations fall short of explaining the message passing process itself. To this aim, we propose the Concept Graph Convolution, the first graph convolution designed to operate on node-level concepts for improved interpretability. The proposed convolutional layer performs message passing on a combination of raw and concept representations using structural and attention-based edge weights. We also propose a pure variant of the convolution, only operating in the concept space. Our results show that the Concept Graph Convolution allows to obtain competitive task accuracy, while enabling an increased insight into the evolution of concepts across convolutional steps.

</details>


### [206] [Energy-Based Open-Set Active Learning for Object Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20083)
*Zongyao Lyu,William J. Beksi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于开放集主动学习的双阶段能量框架，通过两个专用能量模型分别过滤未知类别样本和评估已知样本信息量，在图像和3D分类基准上验证了方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主动学习假设闭集环境（所有类别已知），但现实场景中未标注数据常包含已知和未知类别。现有方法会错误地查询未知类别样本，浪费标注预算。

Method: 提出双阶段能量框架：(1) 基于能量的已知/未知分离器，过滤可能属于未知类别的样本；(2) 基于能量的样本评分器，评估过滤后已知样本的信息量。两模型通过能量景观区分数据点，已知样本赋予较低能量，未知样本赋予较高能量。

Result: 在2D图像（CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet）和3D物体分类（ModelNet40）基准上验证了方法的有效性，实现了更优的标注效率和分类性能。

Conclusion: 该框架通过整合两个能量模型确保高效且有针对性的样本选择，在每次迭代中最大化学习效果，在开放集环境下显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Active learning (AL) has emerged as a crucial methodology for minimizing labeling costs in deep learning by selecting the most valuable samples from a pool of unlabeled data for annotation. Traditional AL operates under a closed-set assumption, where all classes in the dataset are known and consistent. However, real-world scenarios often present open-set conditions in which unlabeled data contains both known and unknown classes. In such environments, standard AL techniques struggle. They can mistakenly query samples from unknown categories, leading to inefficient use of annotation budgets. In this paper, we propose a novel dual-stage energy-based framework for open-set AL. Our method employs two specialized energy-based models (EBMs). The first, an energy-based known/unknown separator, filters out samples likely to belong to unknown classes. The second, an energy-based sample scorer, assesses the informativeness of the filtered known samples. Using the energy landscape, our models distinguish between data points from known and unknown classes in the unlabeled pool by assigning lower energy to known samples and higher energy to unknown samples, ensuring that only samples from classes of interest are selected for labeling. By integrating these components, our approach ensures efficient and targeted sample selection, maximizing learning impact in each iteration. Experiments on 2D (CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet) and 3D (ModelNet40) object classification benchmarks demonstrates that our framework outperforms existing approaches, achieving superior annotation efficiency and classification performance in open-set environments.

</details>


### [207] [Differentiable Conformal Training for LLM Reasoning Factuality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20098)
*Nathan Hittesdorf,Marco Salzetta,Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Differentiable Coherent Factuality (DCF)，通过将非可微的Coherent Factuality算法放松为完全可微的形式，使得可以学习改进的评分器，同时保持统计有效性保证。在推理数据集上实现141%的召回率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM常产生幻觉，限制了在关键应用中的可靠性。现有Conformal Prediction方法独立处理每个claim，在高可靠性要求下会错误移除近60%的真实claims。

Method: 将输出表示为依赖图，联合验证claims及其逻辑祖先；通过可微松弛使评分器可学习，同时保持原算法的保证。

Result: 在两个推理基准数据集上，DCF在维持可靠性保证的同时，claim保留率提升达141%。

Conclusion: DCF是迈向可靠 conformal LLM系统的重要一步，通过可学习评分器克服了传统方法需要手工设计的局限。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently hallucinate, limiting their reliability in critical applications. Conformal Prediction (CP) addresses this by calibrating error rates on held-out data to provide statistically valid confidence guarantees. Recent work extends CP to LLM factuality to filter out risky claims, ensuring that hallucination rates remain below a user-specified level (e.g., 10%). While prior methods treat claims independently, Coherent Factuality extends to multi-step reasoning by representing outputs as dependency graphs and jointly validating claims with their logical ancestors. A key limitation is that Coherent Factuality is not differentiable, requiring hand-crafted scorers that at high reliability levels remove nearly 60% of true claims. We introduce Differentiable Coherent Factuality (DCF), a fully differentiable relaxation that enables learning improved scorers while provably recovering the original algorithm's guarantees. Experiments on two benchmark reasoning datasets demonstrate DCF achieves up to 141% improvement in claim retention while maintaining reliability guarantees, representing a significant step towards reliable conformal LLM systems.

</details>


### [208] [Learning to Solve the Quadratic Assignment Problem with Warm-Started MCMC Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20109)
*Yicheng Pan,Ruisong Zhou,Haijun Zou,Tianyou Li,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PLMA是一个面向二次指派问题(QAP)的置换学习求解框架:先用交叉图注意力网络参数化加性能量基模型(EBM),在置换空间上以O(1)的2-swap Metropolis-Hastings步进行采样,并用"推前(pushforward)"局部改进目标在训练分布上预训练;部署阶段采用批量化的热启动MCMC微调——用上轮高质量解初始化短马尔可夫链,反复采样+2-swap改进+策略梯度更新。实验表明其在QAPLIB上平均最优性间隙仅0.06%(优于Ro-TS 0.11%、BMA 0.07%、SAWT 37.82%),在Taixxeyy上平均间隙2.56%(Ro-TS为81.01%、最坏285.64%),且计算时间远低于手工启发式;并可扩展为Bi-PLMA求解带宽最小化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAP(Koopmans-Beckmann形式)是NP-hard且不可近似的基本组合优化问题,广泛用于设施布局、图匹配、键盘设计等。现有两类方法各有明显缺陷:(1)手工启发式(Ro-TS、BMA、C-SA)依赖CPU、需要针对实例族调参,在大规模实例上效率低,且在Taixxeyy等专门针对局部搜索设计的困难实例上表现极不稳定;(2)学习类方法(QAP本体的极少,多借用图匹配文献)或构建n²节点的关联图(NGM、RGM)而扩展性差,或需完整解作为输入(SAWT),且因需在同分布实例上训练而难以泛化到结构迥异的测试集——已有方法在QAPLIB上平均间隙高达37.82%。QAPLIB/Taixxeyy包含结构差异巨大的多个实例族,不存在单一算法在所有实例上同时占优效率与效果。因此核心问题:能否构建既保留学习先验的效率与可迁移性、又能有效适应未见结构的QAP学习求解器?

Method: 整体为"预训练+部署时精调"两阶段范式。(1)概率建模:将QAP等价改写为在置换单纯形上最小化期望代价(定理1);用加性EBM p_θ(π|P) ∝ exp(⟨X_π, φ⟩)=exp(Σ_i φ_{i,π(i)}) 建模,避免Hungarian投影的不可微与多样性损失;对称2-swap提议核使MH接受率可在O(1)内计算(定理2给出无需配分函数Z_θ的、既无偏又一致的score-function梯度估计)。(2)交叉图注意力网络:对设施图F与位置图D分别做带边信息(中心化块对角)GNN编码得2n个节点嵌入,再用交叉注意力填充未知的块外关系;热图 = 点积→tanh截断→log域Sinkhorn,得到置换矩阵的对数域低秩近似。(3)预训练:定义2-swap局部改进映射T(单次Δ计算O(n),并行采样K_LS个候选取最优),以推前损失E[f(T(π);P)]训练,塑造更平坦的分布;用长链MH采样+N个样本、实例级baseline的方差缩减策略梯度更新(算法2)。(4)热启动MCMC精调(算法3):长链生成K个初始状态,每轮从每个热启动点并行采样M条长度L=⌊n/3⌋的短链,局部改进后聚合梯度做同步参数更新,并按"组内最优保留"规则生成下一轮热启动;批量(batch-wise)精调使多实例并行,共享参数更新。核心设计原则:链长动态(初始长链、后续短链)与链间组内竞争机制(兼顾质量与多样性)。(5)扩展应用:Bi-PLMA把带宽最小化化为一系列QAP子问题(Toeplitz矩阵B_m),用二分法搜索最小可行带宽m,并把热图直接参数化为可学习的n×n矩阵θ(经log-Sinkhorn),使其不依赖具体B_m,从而在子问题间自然热启动。

Result: 合成实例(n=20/50/100,几何型与均匀随机各256个):PLMA(T=200)在两类上均达到0.00%~0.03%间隙,匹配或超越Ro-TS(5k)、BMA、C-SA;如均匀随机QAP100上2193.13(间隙0.00%)耗时14m1s,比Ro-TS(5k)的3h15m快13倍以上,且优于NGM/SM/RRWM/IPFP等学习或经典启发式;零样本(T=1)结果也优于所有其他学习类方法。大规模n=200/500:PLMA分别达6639.93(-0.01%,6m15s)与42975.27(-0.12%,1h15m),与Ro-TS(3h48m/29h59m)、BMA相当或更优,远超SAWT(1.44%/1.55%)。QAPLIB(134实例):平均最优性间隙0.06%(最佳;Ro-TS 0.11%、BMA 0.07%、IPFP 2.15%、SAWT 37.82%),平均耗时2.16s,为所有对比方法中最低。Taixxeyy(10次独立运行):平均间隙2.56%、最坏平均3.84%、无异常离群点、耗时4.92s,远优于Ro-TS(81.01%,最坏285.64%,50.72s)、BMA(12.30%)与IPFP(23.87%)。消融显示:预训练相比随机初始化收敛更快更稳;去掉交叉注意力或Sinkhorn均变差;相比自回归版本AR-Seq(无法热启动)与无梯度版本GD-Free均更优;超参上L=⌊n/3⌋最优,(K,M)=(20,20)最优。带宽最小化上Bi-PLMA在质量与时间上取得最佳平衡,优于Bi-RoTS并与irCM相当;批量化精调较逐实例精调快一倍多且质量不降。

Conclusion: PLMA是首个在解质量与计算效率上同时超越主流手工启发式的学习型QAP求解器:加性EBM使置换空间采样极为廉价(常数时间2-swap),交叉图注意力以可扩展方式融合QAP的双图结构,预训练+热启动MCMC精调兼顾可迁移先验与实例自适应,因而兼具高效率、跨实例族泛化性与稳健性,并自然延伸到带宽最小化等QAP子问题场景。其局限在于:仍依赖预训练分布与测试分布的接近性(未预训练时在Taixxeyy上无法收敛到高质量解);在QAPLIB部分类别的间隙略高于BMA,在tai125e/tai175e等更大规模困难实例上仍有3.65%/9.09%的平均间隙;微调阶段无理论收敛性保证,带宽最小化上多数实例未达到最优。后续工作将引入元学习以更好地衔接预训练与精调,并将PLMA推广到带复杂约束的更广泛置换类问题。

Abstract: The quadratic assignment problem (QAP) is a fundamental NP-hard task that poses significant challenges for both traditional heuristics and modern learning-based solvers. Existing QAP solvers still struggle to achieve consistently competitive performance across structurally diverse real-world instances. To bridge this performance gap, we propose PLMA, an innovative permutation learning framework. PLMA features an efficient warm-started MCMC finetuning procedure to enhance deployment-time performance, leveraging short Markov chains to anchor the adaptation to the promising regions previously explored. For rapid exploration via MCMC over the permutation space, we design an additive energy-based model (EBM) that enables an $O(1)$-time 2-swap Metropolis-Hastings sampling step. Moreover, the neural network used to parameterize the EBM incorporates a scalable and flexible cross-graph attention mechanism to model interactions between facilities and locations in the QAP. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PLMA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across various benchmarks. In particular, PLMA achieves a near-zero average optimality gap on QAPLIB, exhibits remarkably superior robustness on the notoriously difficult Taixxeyy instances, and also serves as an effective QAP solver in bandwidth minimization.

</details>


### [209] [Meta Additive Model: Interpretable Sparse Learning With Auto Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20111)
*Xuelin Zhang,Xinyue Liu,Lingjuan Wu,Hong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Meta加性模型(MAM)，通过双层优化和MLP参数化实现数据驱动的样本权重学习，解决传统稀疏加性模型对复杂噪声敏感的问题，兼具鲁棒回归、不平衡分类和变量选择能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏加性模型依赖MSE损失，对复杂噪声（非高斯噪声、野值、噪声标签、不平衡类别）敏感；现有样本加权方法需人工预设权重函数，增加调参负担。

Method: 提出基于双层优化的Meta加性模型(MAM)，通过MLP对权重函数进行参数化，利用元数据自动学习数据驱动的样本权重

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上，MAM在多种数据损坏场景下优于现有先进加性模型。

Conclusion: MAM通过元数据驱动的权重学习框架，在保持加性模型可解释性的同时，有效提升了模型对复杂噪声的鲁棒性。理论和实验均验证了该方法的有效性。

Motivation: 传统稀疏加性模型依赖MSE损失，对复杂噪声（非高斯噪声、野值、噪声标签、不平衡类别）敏感；现有样本加权方法需人工预设权重函数，增加调参负担。

Method: 提出基于双层优化的Meta加性模型(MAM)，通过MLP对权重函数进行参数化，利用元数据自动学习数据驱动的样本权重。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上，MAM在多种数据损坏场景下优于现有先进加性模型。

Conclusion: 框架提供了计算收敛、代算法化泛化和变量选择一致性的理论保证，扩展了加性模型在鲁棒学习和不平衡分类中的应用。

Abstract: Sparse additive models have attracted much attention in high-dimensional data analysis due to their flexible representation and strong interpretability. However, most existing models are limited to single-level learning under the mean-squared error criterion, whose empirical performance can degrade significantly in the presence of complex noise, such as non-Gaussian perturbations, outliers, noisy labels, and imbalanced categories. The sample reweighting strategy is widely used to reduce the model's sensitivity to atypical data; however, it typically requires prespecifying the weighting functions and manually selecting additional hyperparameters. To address this issue, we propose a new meta additive model (MAM) based on the bilevel optimization framework, which learns data-driven weighting of individual losses by parameterizing the weighting function via an MLP trained on meta data. MAM is capable of a variety of learning tasks, including variable selection, robust regression estimation, and imbalanced classification. Theoretically, MAM provides guarantees on convergence in computation, algorithmic generalization, and variable selection consistency under mild conditions. Empirically, MAM outperforms several state-of-the-art additive models on both synthetic and real-world data under various data corruptions.

</details>


### [210] [On the Stability and Generalization of First-order Bilevel Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20115)
*Xuelin Zhang,Peipei Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次为双层极小极大优化的一阶梯度求解器提供系统性泛化分析。通过算法稳定性理论，推导了三种代表性算法的细粒度泛化界，揭示了算法稳定性、泛化差距与实际设置之间的精确权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献专注于经验效率和收敛保证，但在理解这些算法泛化能力方面存在关键的理论空白。该研究旨在填补这一空白，首次为下层为极小极大问题的双层优化一阶梯度求解器提供系统性泛化分析。

Method: 利用算法稳定性（algorithmic stability）论证方法，对三种代表性算法进行泛化分析：单时间尺度随机梯度下降上升法（S-GSDA）和两种双时间尺度随机梯度下降上升法变体。

Result: 推导出细粒度的泛化界，揭示了算法稳定性、泛化差距与实际设置之间的精确权衡关系，并通过现实任务的实证评估验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 通过算法稳定性理论建立了细粒度的泛化界，揭示了算法稳定性、泛化差距与实际设置之间的精确权衡，并通过实验验证了理论洞察。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization and bilevel minimax optimization have recently emerged as unifying frameworks for a range of machine-learning tasks, including hyperparameter optimization and reinforcement learning. The existing literature focuses on empirical efficiency and convergence guarantees, leaving a critical theoretical gap in understanding how well these algorithms generalize. To bridge this gap, we provide the first systematic generalization analysis for first-order gradient-based bilevel minimax solvers with lower-level minimax problems. Specifically, by leveraging algorithmic stability arguments, we derive fine-grained generalization bounds for three representative algorithms, including single-timescale stochastic gradient descent-ascent, and two variants of two-timescale stochastic gradient descent-ascent. Our results reveal a precise trade-off among algorithmic stability, generalization gaps, and practical settings. Furthermore, extensive empirical evaluations corroborate our theoretical insights on realistic optimization tasks with bilevel minimax structures.

</details>


### [211] [Adaptive Conformal Anomaly Detection with Time Series Foundation Models for Signal Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20122)
*Natalia Martinez Gil,Fearghal O'Donncha,Wesley M. Gifford,Nianjun Zhou,Dhaval C. Patel,Roman Vaculin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种后验自适应共形异常检测方法，无需微调即可直接利用预训练时间序列基础模型，输出可解释为假警报率的异常分数，通过加权分位数预测和自适应权重学习实现分布偏移下的稳定误报控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着时间序列基础模型日益普及，工业场景面临数据有限、缺乏训练专业知识、需要即时推理等挑战，亟需一种无需微调即可快速部署、可解释且鲁棒的后验自适应异常检测方法。

Method: 该方法采用加权分位数共形预测边界，适配预训练基础模型输出，无需额外微调即可生成可直接解释为假警报率（p值）的异常分数。通过自适应学习历史预测中的最优权重参数，实现分布偏移下的校准和稳定的误报控制，同时保留样本外保证。

Result: 实验表明该方法在合成和真实数据集上表现优异，在简单性、可解释性、鲁棒性和自适应性之间取得了良好平衡。

Conclusion: 该方法在合成和真实数据集上展现了强性能，兼具简单性、可解释性、鲁棒性和自适应性的优势，是一种可立即部署的工业异常检测解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a post-hoc adaptive conformal anomaly detection method for monitoring time series that leverages predictions from pre-trained foundation models without requiring additional fine-tuning. Our method yields an interpretable anomaly score directly interpretable as a false alarm rate (p-value), facilitating transparent and actionable decision-making. It employs weighted quantile conformal prediction bounds and adaptively learns optimal weighting parameters from past predictions, enabling calibration under distribution shifts and stable false alarm control, while preserving out-of-sample guarantees. As a model-agnostic solution, it integrates seamlessly with foundation models and supports rapid deployment in resource-constrained environments. This approach addresses key industrial challenges such as limited data availability, lack of training expertise, and the need for immediate inference, while taking advantage of the growing accessibility of time series foundation models. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets show that the proposed approach delivers strong performance, combining simplicity, interpretability, robustness, and adaptivity.

</details>


### [212] [Pairing Regularization for Mitigating Many-to-One Collapse in GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20130)
*Kuan-Yu Lin,Yu-Chih Huang,Tie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对GAN训练中的 intra-mode collapse（多对一映射导致的生成多样性丧失）问题，提出了一种配对正则化器，通过强制保持潜在变量与生成样本之间的局部一致性来缓解该问题。该方法在不同训练状态下表现不同：在易崩溃场景中提升覆盖率，在稳定训练中提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Intra-mode collapse (many-to-one mapping) has received significantly less attention compared to inter-mode collapse (mode dropping) in GAN training.

Method: Pairing regularizer jointly optimized with the generator to enforce local consistency between latent variables and generated samples.

Result: In collapse-prone regimes: pairing encourages structured local exploration, improving coverage and recall. In stabilized training: pairing refines data density by discouraging redundant mappings, improving precision without sacrificing recall.

Conclusion: Pairing regularizer effectively complements existing GAN stabilization techniques by directly addressing intra-mode collapse.

Abstract: Mode collapse remains a fundamental challenge in training generative adversarial networks (GANs). While existing works have primarily focused on inter-mode collapse, such as mode dropping, intra-mode collapse-where many latent variables map to the same or highly similar outputs-has received significantly less attention. In this work, we propose a pairing regularizer jointly optimized with the generator to mitigate the many-to-one collapse by enforcing local consistency between latent variables and generated samples. We show that the effect of pairing regularization depends on the dominant failure mode of training. In collapse-prone regimes with limited exploration, pairing encourages structured local exploration, leading to improved coverage and higher recall. In contrast, under stabilized training with sufficient exploration, pairing refines the generator's induced data density by discouraging redundant mappings, thereby improving precision without sacrificing recall. Extensive experiments on both toy distributions and real-image benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed regularizer effectively complements existing stabilization techniques by directly addressing intra-mode collapse.

</details>


### [213] [Fourier Weak SINDy: Spectral Test Function Selection for Robust Model Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20141)
*Zhiheng Chen,Urban Fasel,Anastasia Bizyaeva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fourier Weak SINDy方法，将弱形式稀疏方程学习与频谱密度估计相结合用于数据驱动方程发现。该方法使用正交正弦测试函数，将问题转化为傅里叶系数回归，并通过多窗口法选择主导频率。实验表明该方法在混沌和超混沌系统中具有良好的噪声鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统微分方程学习方法依赖数值微分，容易受噪声影响；现有方法缺乏对噪声鲁棒性和可解释性的良好平衡

Method: 该方法将弱形式稀疏回归与频谱密度估计相结合：1) 采用基于调制的正弦测试函数，弱形式稀疏回归问题简化为傅里叶系数回归；2) 通过多窗口法估计数据频谱选择主导频率

Result: 在多个混沌和超混沌常微分方程基准测试的数值实验中验证了该方法的有效性

Conclusion: 该方法在混沌和超混沌常微分方程基准测试中表现有效，成功将弱形式学习与频谱估计统一在紧凑灵活的框架中。

Abstract: We introduce Fourier Weak SINDy, a minimal noise-robust and interpretable derivative-free equation learning method that combines weak-form sparse equation learning with spectral density estimation for data-driven test function selection. By using orthogonal sinusoidal test functions inspired by their prevalence in Modulating Function-based system identification, the weak-form sparse regression problem reduces to a regression over Fourier coefficients. Dominant frequencies are then selected via multitaper estimation of the frequency spectrum of the data. This formulation unifies weak-form learning and spectral estimation within a compact and flexible framework. We illustrate the effectiveness of this approach in numerical experiments across multiple chaotic and hyperchaotic ODE benchmarks.

</details>


### [214] [Temporally Extended Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20156)
*Zeyu Shen,Peter Henderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将强化学习中的选项框架（options framework）应用于混合专家模型（MoE），通过添加控制器学习何时切换专家集合，有效将专家切换率从50%以上降至5%以下，同时保留约90%的基线准确率，解决了超大MoE模型的内存效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家模型虽然能在固定推理速度下扩展能力，但当模型超出GPU内存时，几乎每个token都切换专家的行为会导致卸载和预取等优化失效，无法有效管理内存。

Method: 基于带 deliberation costs 的 option-critic 框架，为每个MoE层添加一个控制器，该控制器学习何时切换专家集以及加载哪些专家。实验采用 gpt-oss-20b 模型，配备低秩适配器（low-rank adapters）并使用自蒸馏奖励机制。

Result: 方法将专家切换率从超过50%降至5%以下，同时在MATH、MMLU和MMMLU基准上保留了约90%的基线模型准确率。

Conclusion: 现有预训练模型可通过轻量级训练转换为时序扩展的MoE模型，deliberation cost允许模型训练者在切换率与性能之间进行权衡。这为不断增长的MoE模型的内存高效服务和持续学习提供了一条有原则的路径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts models, now popular for scaling capacity at fixed inference speed, switch experts at nearly every token. Once a model outgrows available GPU memory, this churn can render optimizations like offloading and pre-fetching ineffective. We make the case that the options framework in reinforcement learning is a perfect match to tackle this problem, and argue for temporally extended mixture-of-experts layers. Building on the option-critic framework with deliberation costs, we add a controller to each layer that learns when to switch expert sets and which to load. By applying this to gpt-oss-20b with low-rank adapters and a self-distillation reward, our method reduces switch rates from over 50% to below 5% while retaining up to 90% of base-model accuracy on MATH, MMLU, and MMMLU. This shows that even existing pre-trained models can be converted to temporally extended MoEs with lightweight training, with the deliberation cost allowing model trainers to trade off switching rates against capability. We hope this opens a principled path, grounded in the options framework, for memory-efficient serving and continual learning in ever-growing MoE models.

</details>


### [215] [SMART: A Spectral Transfer Approach to Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20161)
*Boxin Zhao,Mladen Kolar,Jinchi Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SMART（Spectral Multi-task Adaptive Regression Transfer）是一种面向低秩多任务线性回归的无源（source-free）迁移学习方法。它不要求源模型与目标模型在数值上接近（bounded-difference），而是提出"谱相似性"假设：目标的左/右奇异子空间包含于源的对应子空间内，且目标奇异向量在源奇异基下稀疏对齐。SMART仅需一个已拟合的源回归矩阵，通过源引导的结构化 ℓ1 正则化来估计目标系数矩阵，用ADMM结合Stiefel流形优化求解。理论上给出一般非渐近误差上界与无噪声源情形下的极小极大下界，实现近极小最优的Frobenius误差率；模拟与多模态单细胞（GEX→ADT）数据均显示优于RRR、SRRR、SOFAR、RSSVD等基线，且对负迁移稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务学习在目标样本量很小时性能显著退化，迁移学习虽可借力但现有方法多依赖"有界差异"假设：例如Park et al.要求目标与源的系数矩阵差在核范数下有界，这同时约束了奇异方向与效应量大小，在生物医学场景中（如不同细胞类型间基因–蛋白关联强度剧烈变化、但共表达通路/模块结构保持）过于严苛，逻辑上排除了"方向对齐但量级剧变"的情形。此外许多迁移流程需直接访问源原始数据，而隐私、同意或平台限制往往只允许共享已拟合模型或摘要表示。因此需要一个既能超越有界差异设定、又只依赖已拟合源模型的低秩多任务迁移框架。

Method: 模型：Y = X C* + E，C*低秩（C*=U*D*V*⊤），并假设存在一个"干净"源矩阵C^(0)（实际只观测到含噪的C̃^(0)=C^(0)+E^(0)）其SVD提供源奇异基。核心假设：Assumption 2.1 谱包含（col(U*)⊆col(U^(0))、col(V*)⊆col(V^(0))，r≤r0）与 Assumption 2.2 稀疏对齐（s*_u=|U*⊤U^(0)|_0、s*_v=|V*⊤V^(0)|_0 相对 rp、rq 较小）。估计量：最小化 (1/2n)||Y−XUDV⊤||_F² + λ_u|Ũ⊥ˢ(r_u)⊤UD|_1 + λ_v|Ṽ⊥ˢ(r_v)⊤VD|_1，约束 U⊤U=I_r、V⊤V=I_r、D≥0，即在源的前 r_u / r_v 个奇异方向上不加惩罚、其余方向做ℓ1收缩，从而促使目标奇异向量仅利用少数源方向。优化：ADMM框架下交替更新 U、V（Stiefel流形上的二次规划，用Pymanopt的Riemannian共轭梯度求解）、D（逐坐标闭式解 d_k=max{0,b_k/c_k}）、Ω_u/Ω_v（软阈值算子）及对偶变量，惩罚参数按 ρ←γρ 递增；收敛判据为原始残差与流形梯度驻点残差同时低于阈值。超参：r 用Bunea et al.准则，(r_u,r_v) 由K折交叉验证网格搜索，(λ_u,λ_v) 由BIC（惩罚支持集大小）选择；初始化用 ridge/Lasso 截断SVD热启动。理论：采用CANO两阶段策略（凸初始化+邻域内局部化求解），借助restricted eigenvalue条件、受限奇异值、谱隙、高斯噪声等假设；误差界分解为统计项（参数率）+ 源噪声引起的逼近项 ε_source；下界用局部度量熵（Stiefel流形覆盖数的上下界新引理）+ Fano 方法。

Result: （1）Theorem 4.6：以概率≥1−δ 有 ||Ĉ−C*||_F ≲ √α_max·η_r·√{(r(r_u+r_v+1)+s_u(r_u)+s_v(r_v))·log(12r max(p,q)/δ)/n} + α_max^{1/4}(d_1*)^{1/2}·ε_source·(log(12r max(p,q)/δ)/n)^{1/4}，其中有效自由度为 r(r_u+r_v+1)+s_u+s_v，ε_source 由源谱误差 ε^(0) 及截断水平相对最小充分水平 r_u*, r_v* 的缺口决定；源无噪声时逼近项消失。（2）Theorem 4.7（无噪声源）：极小极大风险 ≳ σ²(s*_u+s*_v)/(γ²n) + σ²[(s*_u∧⌊r0/2⌋)/n·log(0.75r0/(s*_u∧⌊r0/2⌋)) + 对称项]，与上界在对 n、s*_u、s*_v 的依赖上仅差对数因子，达到近极小最优Frobenius率。（3）模拟（3种维度模型、4组实验、100次重复）：SMART在估计精度上稳定优于RRR/SRRR/SOFAR/RSSVD，固定秩版本对超参稳健，数据驱动的全自动化版本在源噪声大时自动退化到目标-only水平，不出现负迁移。（4）多模态单细胞应用（GSE194122，NK 5434细胞为源→ILC1 552细胞为目标，248基因预测134 ADT，100次70/30划分）：SMART 归一化测试误差0.200–0.211，显著优于最好的target-only（SOFAR 0.360、Lasso 0.331）与source-only（RRR 0.331）基线，且四分位距最窄，说明其泛化与稳定性最佳。代码开源于 github.com/boxinz17/smart。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是提出一种"谱相似"而非"数值相近"的迁移学习范式，把子空间复用与效应量相似性解耦，从而能覆盖有界差异假设无法涵盖的"低维模块结构守恒但效应幅度剧烈变化"的生物医学场景，并做到只需共享拟合后的源模型。方法上以源引导的ℓ1结构化正则化+ADMM/Stiefel流形算法给出可落地实现；理论上给出非渐近误差上界与无噪声源下的近极小最优极小极大下界（新给出Stiefel流形Frobenius覆盖数的上下界）。实证显示在样本量小的目标研究与多模态单细胞基因–蛋白关联分析中均有明显提升并能规避负迁移。作者指出未来方向包括推广到广义线性/非线性模型、推断过程，以及为结构参数与正则化参数建立有理论依据的数据自适应选择机制；同时也隐含若干局限：理论结果依赖源矩阵的谱隙与无噪/低噪等条件、实际算法用的是局部（ADMM）解而非理论中的局部化全局最优、r_u/r_v 与 λ 的实际选择仍靠交叉验证与BIC而无自适应保证。

Abstract: Multi-task learning is effective for related applications, but its performance can deteriorate when the target sample size is small. Transfer learning can borrow strength from related studies; yet, many existing methods rely on restrictive bounded-difference assumptions between the source and target models. We propose SMART, a spectral transfer method for multi-task linear regression that instead assumes spectral similarity: the target left and right singular subspaces lie within the corresponding source subspaces and are sparsely aligned with the source singular bases. Such an assumption is natural when studies share latent structures and enables transfer beyond the bounded-difference settings. SMART estimates the target coefficient matrix through structured regularization that incorporates spectral information from a source study. Importantly, it requires only a fitted source model rather than the raw source data, making it useful when data sharing is limited. Although the optimization problem is nonconvex, we develop a practical ADMM-based algorithm. We establish general, non-asymptotic error bounds and a minimax lower bound in the noiseless-source regime. Under additional regularity conditions, these results yield near-minimax Frobenius error rates up to logarithmic factors. Simulations confirm improved estimation accuracy and robustness to negative transfer, and analysis of multi-modal single-cell data demonstrates better predictive performance. The Python implementation of SMART, along with the code to reproduce all experiments in this paper, is publicly available at https://github.com/boxinz17/smart.

</details>


### [216] [Cover meets Robbins while Betting on Bounded Data: $\ln n$ Regret and Almost Sure $\ln\ln n$ Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20172)
*Shubhada Agrawal,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合投注策略，在保持对对抗性数据的O(log n)最坏情况后悔界的同时，在随机数据路径上实现了O(ln ln n)的几乎必然后悔界，据称为首个展示双策略对冲价值的论文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cover的通用投资组合算法对对抗性数据的最坏情况后悔为O(ln n)，但作者希望在不牺牲对抗性数据保护的前提下，改进对随机数据的后悔界。现有方法（如Agrawal 2025）的子高斯混合在无界数据上的最坏情况后悔必须是无限的，因此需要新的对冲策略。

Method: 提出一种融合Robbins和Cover思想的混合投注策略。该策略通过对冲两种完全不同的策略实现自适应：对冲的核心是组合两个差异显著的策略以获得最佳两个世界的适应能力。

Result: 在条件均值恒为m_0且内蕴方差趋于无穷的路径（测度为1的路径集）上，后悔界为O(ln ln n)；在其补集（测度为0的路径集）上，后悔界为O(log n)。同时实现了最优投注增长率和随机数据上的几乎必然ln ln n后悔界。

Conclusion: 首次揭示了对冲两种不同策略在实现随机数据适应性与对抗性保护的双重目标中的价值。与Agrawal等人2025年的结果形成对比：其子高斯混合的最坏情况后悔必须无界，但类似对冲可同时获得最优增长率和随机数据上的几乎必然ln ln n后悔。此外，该策略印证了一个尖锐的博弈论形式的迭代对数上界定律。

Abstract: Consider betting against a sequence of data in $[0,1]$, where one is allowed to make any bet that is fair if the data have a conditional mean $m_0 \in (0,1)$. Cover's universal portfolio algorithm delivers a worst-case regret of $O(\ln n)$ compared to the best constant bet in hindsight, and this bound is unimprovable against adversarially generated data. In this work, we present a novel mixture betting strategy that combines insights from Robbins and Cover, and exhibits a different behavior: it eventually produces a regret of $O(\ln \ln n)$ on \emph{almost} all paths (a measure-one set of paths if each conditional mean equals $m_0$ and intrinsic variance increases to $\infty$), but has an $O(\log n)$ regret on the complement (a measure zero set of paths). Our paper appears to be the first to point out the value in hedging two very different strategies to achieve a best-of-both-worlds adaptivity to stochastic data and protection against adversarial data. We contrast our results to those in~\cite{agrawal2025regret} for a sub-Gaussian mixture on unbounded data: their worst-case regret has to be unbounded, but a similar hedging delivers both an optimal betting growth-rate and an almost sure $\ln\ln n$ regret on stochastic data. Finally, our strategy witnesses a sharp game-theoretic upper law of the iterated logarithm, analogous to~\cite{shafer2005probability}.

</details>


### [217] [Lever: Inference-Time Policy Reuse under Support Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20174)
*Ihor Vitenki,Noha Ibrahim,Sihem Amer-Yahia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LEVER框架，解决推理时策略复用问题：在给定预训练策略库和新复合目标的情况下，如何完全离线地构建高质量策略，无需额外环境交互。该方法在有限支持 regime 下表现出色，可匹配或超越从零训练的性能，但长时序依赖任务仍受限于离线复用的根本缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习策略通常针对固定目标训练，当任务需求变化时难以复用。现有方法在推理时策略复用方面存在不足，特别是在完全离线（无额外环境交互）场景下，如何从预训练策略库中高效构建新策略是一个开放问题。

Method: LEVER框架包含三个核心组件：1) 策略检索模块；2) 行为嵌入评估模块，用于评估检索到的策略；3) 离线Q值组合模块，通过组合预训练策略生成新策略。该框架专注于"支持有限 regime"（no value propagation），提出组合策略来平衡性能与计算成本，控制候选策略的探索。

Result: 在确定性GridWorld环境中的实验表明：1) 推理时组合可以匹配甚至超越从零训练的性能，同时提供显著的速度提升；2) 当任务需要长时序依赖和值传播时，性能会下降，揭示了离线复用的根本局限性。

Conclusion: 推理时策略复用是可行的，但其有效性关键依赖于可用转换的覆盖范围。离线复用方法在短horizon任务上表现良好，但在需要值传播的长时序依赖任务中存在本质限制。未来研究方向包括扩展支持有限场景的理论分析和更复杂环境下的验证。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) policies are typically trained for fixed objectives, making reuse difficult when task requirements change. We study inference-time policy reuse: given a library of pre-trained policies and a new composite objective, can a high-quality policy be constructed entirely offline, without additional environment interaction? We introduce lever (Leveraging Efficient Vector Embeddings for Reusable policies), an end-to-end framework that retrieves relevant policies, evaluates them using behavioral embeddings, and composes new policies via offline Q-value composition. We focus on the support-limited regime, where no value propagation is possible, and show that the effectiveness of reuse depends critically on the coverage of available transitions. To balance performance and computational cost, lever proposes composition strategies that control the exploration of candidate policies. Experiments in deterministic GridWorld environments show that inference-time composition can match, and in some cases exceed, training-from-scratch performance while providing substantial speedups. At the same time, performance degrades when long-horizon dependencies require value propagation, highlighting a fundamental limitation of offline reuse.

</details>


### [218] [Physics-Enhanced Deep Learning for Proactive Thermal Runaway Forecasting in Li-Ion Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20175)
*Salman Khan,Muhammad Zunair Zamir,Syed Sajid Ullah,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理信息LSTM（PI-LSTM），把离散化热扩散方程作为正则项嵌入LSTM损失函数，用于18650锂电池机械滥用下的温度预测与热失控三级预警；在13个电池数据集上相对标准LSTM实现RMSE降低81.9%、MAE降低81.3%，并显著优于CNN-LSTM/CNN/MLP，跨电池泛化性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂电池热失控（TR）实时预测对电池管理系统（ BMS）安全至关重要。现有两类路线各有缺陷：①纯数据驱动方法（LSTM/RNN）能捕捉复杂时序依赖，但缺乏热力学内在约束，常输出负温度、振荡等非物理行为，可靠性与可解释性差，且对新电池化学体系/工况泛化不佳、跨电池迁移易过拟合；②基于物理的热模型（共轭传热、多物理有限元、CFD）虽可解释，但计算代价高、参数标定困难、依赖难以获取的内部测量，无法实时部署。既有混合方法多依赖阻抗谱、气体传感器、内置传感网等专用/侵入式硬件，或仅在训练后做事后规则修正，缺少把物理规律直接嵌入训练损失的做法。

Method: 框架分三部分。(1)实验平台与数据集：以18650型石墨负极/LiCoO2正极（4600 mWh，18×65 mm）圆柱电芯为对象，力学试验机（100 kN）施加机械滥用，配备电压采集、距1 m热像仪（发射率0.95，热电偶标定，1 s采样），四种加载模式：圆柱/球压痕、径向压缩、轴向压缩、钉刺；采集力、位移、电压、温度、SOC、短路指示等同步数据。系统分析揭示：峰值力随轴向距离按指数衰减F_peak=F_max·exp(-λ_pos·x*)、塌落时间t_collapse=t_0+C·exp(λ_pos·x*)、能量控制加热T_peak≈T_0+α∫F du（速度依赖的对数律、压头半径与SOC的经验标度、钉刺下ΔV∝SOC、T_peak=T_0+k·ln(SOC)），并定义融合力/温度/电压降/塌落时间的综合严重度指数S（80% SOC最危险），据此给出钉刺>轴向>径向的严重度排序。(2)模型：PI-LSTM在标准LSTM（输入/遗忘/输出门，式14–19）基础上，以数据损失L_data=MSE与物理损失L_phys（预测温度一阶时间导数与热扩散项α=k/(ρc_p)二阶差分残差的均方）之和构成L_total=L_data+λL_phys；输入为多特征序列（电流、电压、温度、机械应力、SOC），滑动窗口n=50滑窗构造样本，min-max/z-score归一化，前向填充插补，Adam(lr=1e-4, batch=32)、早停；对比基线LSTM、CNN-LSTM、CNN、MLP。(3)应用：基于预测温度与失效状态构建三级TR预警（1级外部机械碰撞、2级内短路ISC、3级温度达130 °C的SEI大规模分解阈值），在Batt-1~4、Batt-8~10上验证。

Result: Table II/III/V：(1)整体测试集上PI-LSTM MAE=0.0674 °C、RMSE=0.0828 °C、R²=0.9998，相比LSTM（MAE 0.3602、RMSE 0.4573）RMSE降低81.9%、MAE降低81.3%，亦大幅优于CNN-LSTM（1.0188/1.2641）、CNN（6.6788/7.7066）、MLP（13.1394/15.1444）。(2)跨电池泛化：在未见过的Batt-9（MAE 0.0674、RMSE 0.0828）与Batt-12（MAE 0.0503、RMSE 0.0754）上误差最低，R²≥0.9997，MLP在Batt-12上R²仅0.7248，说明物理归纳偏置带来跨工况稳定性与迁移能力。(3)物理约束消融显示去掉正则项后精度明显下降并出现非物理温度振荡（负值/震荡），验证热扩散一致性项的必要性。(4)预警有效性：Batt-9、Batt-12均在TR实际发生前成功触发3级预警，实际触发温度分别为126.78 °C与125.7 °C，可提前于失控。(5)数据集统计：14,569训练点、3,642测试点，特征范围（温度25–140 °C、SOC 0–100%、力0.2–10 kN、电压2.5–4.2 V）与13种滥用条件覆盖各SOC/速度/压头半径。

Conclusion: 将热扩散方程以物理正则项形式直接嵌入LSTM损失，可在保持数据驱动灵活性的同时获得热力学一致、可解释、非物理振荡消除的温度预测，在精度、跨电池泛化与实时性上均优于纯数据驱动基线，证明物理信息深度学习是下一代BMS主动热失控预警与实时热管理的可行路径。后续方向为扩展至多维传热建模与电化学-热混合预测。此外需注意论文存在若干表述不一致之处：实验设置段落提及"UPIT"注意力编码器（3层编码、4头、d_model=64）、四元损失权重(α,β,γ,δ)与MC dropout(S=50)、70/20/10划分及NVIDIA Drive AGX上<100 ms推理延迟，与前文PI-LSTM描述（min-max归一化、14,569/3,642划分）不统一；特征表中未列"电流"却含速度/力/短路指示；峰值温度>350 °C与特征统计上限140 °C存在矛盾，物理损失中α（热扩散率）与严重度指数权重符号亦存在复用混用，部分现象拟合仅基于有限数据点的经验式。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of thermal runaway in lithium-ion batteries is essential for ensuring the safety, efficiency, and reliability of modern energy storage systems. Conventional data-driven approaches, such as Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, can capture complex temporal dependencies but often violate thermodynamic principles, resulting in physically inconsistent predictions. Conversely, physics-based thermal models provide interpretability but are computationally expensive and difficult to parameterize for real-time applications. To bridge this gap, this study proposes a Physics-Informed Long Short-Term Memory (PI-LSTM) framework that integrates governing heat transfer equations directly into the deep learning architecture through a physics-based regularization term in the loss function. The model leverages multi-feature input sequences, including state of charge, voltage, current, mechanical stress, and surface temperature, to forecast battery temperature evolution while enforcing thermal diffusion constraints. Extensive experiments conducted on thirteen lithium-ion battery datasets demonstrate that the proposed PI-LSTM achieves an 81.9% reduction in root mean square error (RMSE) and an 81.3% reduction in mean absolute error (MAE) compared to the standard LSTM baseline, while also outperforming CNN-LSTM and multilayer perceptron (MLP) models by wide margins. The inclusion of physical constraints enhances the model's generalization across diverse operating conditions and eliminates non-physical temperature oscillations. These results confirm that physics-informed deep learning offers a viable pathway toward interpretable, accurate, and real-time thermal management in next-generation battery systems.

</details>


### [219] [Structure-Aware Variational Learning of a Class of Generalized Diffusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20188)
*Yubin Lu,Xiaofan Li,Chun Liu,Qi Tang,Yiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于能量的学习框架，利用能量-耗散原理从不完整和噪声观测中推断随机梯度系统中的未知势能函数。该方法避免了直接回归控制方程，通过De Giorgi耗散泛函构造损失函数，保持了系统潜在的变分结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典方法直接回归控制方程或速度场，对噪声敏感，在观测不完整时可能失效。需要一种更具鲁棒性的数据驱动建模方法。

Method: 基于Fokker-Planck方程的能量-耗散定律，构造基于De Giorgi耗散泛函的损失函数，结合自由能与系统耗散机制，保持系统的变分结构。

Result: 在一维、二维和三维数值实验中验证了所提方法的鲁棒性，对观测时间、噪声水平、训练数据多样性和数量均表现出增强的稳定性。

Conclusion: 能量-耗散原理可作为从数据学习随机扩散动力学的可靠基础，所提框架在处理不完整和噪声观测方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Learning the underlying potential energy of stochastic gradient systems from partial and noisy observations is a fundamental problem arising in physics, chemistry, and data-driven modeling. Classical approaches often rely on direct regression of governing equations or velocity fields, which can be sensitive to noise and external perturbations and may fail when observations are incomplete. In this work, we propose a structure-aware, energy-based learning framework for inferring unknown potential functions in generalized diffusion processes, grounded in the energetic variational approach. Starting from the energy-dissipation law associated with the Fokker-Planck equation, we construct loss functions based on the De Giorgi dissipation functional, which consistently couple the free energy and the dissipation mechanism of the system. This formulation avoids explicit enforcement of the governing partial differential equation and preserves the underlying variational structure of the dynamics. Through numerical experiments in one, two, and three dimensions, we demonstrate that the proposed energy-based loss exhibits enhanced robustness with respect to observation time, noise level, and the diversity and amount of available training data. These results highlight the effectiveness of energy-dissipation principles as a reliable foundation for learning stochastic diffusion dynamics from data.

</details>


### [220] [ACT: Anti-Crosstalk Learning for Cross-Sectional Stock Ranking via Temporal Disentanglement and Structural Purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20204)
*Juntao Li,Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ACT框架，通过时序解耦和结构净化解决股票排名中不同因子间的信息干扰（串扰）问题，在CSI300和CSI500数据集上实现了最先进的排名精度和优异的投资组合收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型利用图结构传播信息提升排名准确率，但存在"串扰"问题——即预测因子间出现非预期信息干扰。这包括：时序尺度串扰（趋势、波动、冲击在共享表征中纠缠，局部模式污染跨股票学习）和结构串扰（异构关系被无差别融合，关系特异性预测信号被掩盖）。

Method: ACT框架包含三个核心模块：1) 时序解耦：将单只股票序列分解为趋势、波动、冲击三个成分，通过专属分支提取成分特异性信息；2) 渐进式结构净化编码器：在消除时序尺度串扰后，对趋势成分逐步净化结构串扰；3) 自适应融合模块：整合所有分支表征进行排名。

Result: 在CSI300和CSI500数据集上验证，ACT实现了最先进的排名精度和优异的投资组合表现，在CSI300数据集上提升高达74.25%。

Conclusion: 通过时序解耦和结构净化有效解决了跨截面股票排名中的串扰问题，显著提升了预测准确性和投资组合收益。

Abstract: Cross-sectional stock ranking is a fundamental task in quantitative investment, relying on both temporal modeling of individual stocks and the capture of inter-stock dependencies. While existing deep learning models leverage graph-based approaches to enhance ranking accuracy by propagating information over relational graphs, they suffer from a key challenge: crosstalk, namely unintended information interference across predictive factors. We identify two forms of crosstalk: temporal-scale crosstalk, where trends, fluctuations, and shocks are entangled in a shared representation and non-transferable local patterns contaminate cross-stock learning; and structural crosstalk, where heterogeneous relations are indiscriminately fused and relation-specific predictive signals are obscured. To address both issues, we propose the Anti-CrossTalk (ACT) framework for cross-sectional stock ranking via temporal disentanglement and structural purification. Specifically, ACT first decomposes each stock sequence into trend, fluctuation, and shock components, then extracts component-specific information through dedicated branches, which effectively decouples non-transferable local patterns. ACT further introduces a Progressive Structural Purification Encoder to sequentially purify structural crosstalk on the trend component after mitigating temporal-scale crosstalk. An adaptive fusion module finally integrates all branch representations for ranking. Experiments on CSI300 and CSI500 demonstrate that ACT achieves state-of-the-art ranking accuracy and superior portfolio performance, with improvements of up to 74.25% on the CSI300 dataset.

</details>


### [221] [Scaling Self-Play with Self-Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20209)
*Luke Bailey,Kaiyue Wen,Kefan Dong,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Tengyu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SGS自博弈算法，通过让LLM同时扮演Solver、Conjecturer和Guide三个角色来解决自博弈中的奖励黑客问题。在Lean4定理证明任务中，该方法展现出优异的扩展性，7B模型经200轮训练后超越671B模型的pass@4表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自博弈方法在大规模计算时无法有效扩展，会出现学习 plateau。其根本原因是Conjecturer在长期训练中学会了"黑客"其奖励机制，崩溃到人为复杂但对Solver提升无益的问题上。

Method: 提出自引导自博弈(SGS)算法，模型同时扮演Solver、Conjecturer和Guide三个角色。Guide通过评估合成问题与未解决目标问题的相关性及其清洁度和自然性，为Conjecturer提供监督信号，防止其Reward hacking导致的退化问题。

Result: 在Lean4形式化定理证明任务中，SGS在少于80轮自博弈即超越最强RL基线的渐近解决率；经过200轮自博弈后，7B参数模型的解题量超过671B参数模型的pass@4性能。

Conclusion: SGS成功解决了自博弈中的奖励黑客问题，使7B参数模型在200轮自博弈后超越了671B参数模型的性能，证明了语言模型能够评估子问题对实现目标的效用性，且该方法具有良好的扩展性。

Abstract: LLM self-play algorithms are notable in that, in principle, nothing bounds their learning: a Conjecturer model creates problems for a Solver, and both improve together. However, in practice, existing LLM self-play methods do not scale well with large amounts of compute, instead hitting learning plateaus. We argue this is because over long training runs, the Conjecturer learns to hack its reward, collapsing to artificially complex problems that do not help the Solver improve. To overcome this, we introduce Self-Guided Self-Play (SGS), a self-play algorithm in which the language model itself guides the Conjecturer away from degeneracy. In SGS, the model takes on three roles: Solver, Conjecturer, and a Guide that scores synthetic problems by their relevance to unsolved target problems and how clean and natural they are, providing supervision against Conjecturer collapse. Our core hypothesis is that language models can assess whether a subproblem is useful for achieving a goal. We evaluate the scaling properties of SGS by running training for significantly longer than prior works and by fitting scaling laws to cumulative solve rate curves. Applying SGS to formal theorem proving in Lean4, we find that it surpasses the asymptotic solve rate of our strongest RL baseline in fewer than 80 rounds of self-play and enables a 7B parameter model, after 200 rounds of self-play, to solve more problems than a 671B parameter model pass@4.

</details>


### [222] [Geometric Layer-wise Approximation Rates for Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20219)
*Shijun Zhang,Zuowei Shen,Yuesheng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立"逐层"（layer-wise）逼近理论：在固定宽度 2dN+d+2 的单一共享混合激活网络（每层 2 个 sine 通道 + ReLU 通道）中，每个中间读出 Φ_ℓ 本身就是目标函数的逼近器，且误差被 (2d+1)·ω_{f,p}(N^{−ℓ}) 控制；对 1-Lipschitz 目标即几何速率 (2d+1)N^{−ℓ}。深度因此被解释为"分辨率参数"而非单纯规模指标，并给出嵌套（可截断）的自适应精化结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络逼近理论（通用逼近与各类定量结果）几乎只针对最终输出给出误差保证，中间层的作用（渐进特征提取）缺乏定量刻画；同时深度通常需预先固定，由 ℓ 层扩展到 ℓ+1 层往往要重新设计并重训整个网络，缺乏经典逼近论中"追加基函数即完成精化"的嵌套精化原则。本文试图给深度一个精确的、依赖尺度的解释，并提供可逐层验证误差、可在达到目标精度后即停下的构造性框架。

Method: （1）几何划分：在 ℓ 层把 [0,1]^d 分为 N^{dℓ} 个边长 N^{−ℓ}−δ 的内部立方体 Q_{ℓ,β}，及宽度 O(dN^{L}δ) 的过渡区 Ω_ℓ，且各层过渡区嵌套（∪_{j≤ℓ}Ω_j = Ω_ℓ），使网络在内部立方体上"分段常值"而整体连续。（2）编码器（Prop 3.1）：用连续阶梯代理函数 h（2N−1 段线性，单隐层 ReLU 宽度 2N−1）构造二变量映射 h_ℓ（单隐层 ReLU 宽度 2N+1），逐层递推得到 Ψ_ℓ(x)=β/N^ℓ 实现格点定位；d 路并行需 d(2N+1) 个 ReLU 单元。（3）解码器（Prop 3.2）：双 sine 映射 k ↦ u_ℓ sin(v_ℓ sin(w_ℓ k)) 可在有限集上同时逼近任意指定值（由 arcsin 取目标相位 + Kronecker 逼近定理保证 sin(kw)/2π 的 Q-线性无关），仅需 2 个 sine 通道即可编码 N^{dℓ} 个胞元平均值；Λ_ℓ(y)=1+ΣN^{iℓ}y_i 将格点标签映为整数指标。（4）误差估计（Prop 3.3 与 Lemma 3.4）：用逐坐标平均算子 P_j 的 L^p 压缩性与 Jensen 不等式，把 f 与胞元平均之差控制为方向平移增量，从而被 L^p 模量 ω_{f,p} 约束。（5）残差递推：f_0=f，Γ_ℓ 为第 ℓ 级修正项，Φ_ℓ=Σ_{j≤ℓ}Γ_j，f_{ℓ+1}=f_ℓ−Γ_ℓ，对 ℓ 归纳证明三项性质（Φ_ℓ 在每胞元上常值、‖Γ_ℓ‖_∞ 受控、误差界）；最终所有 A_i、A_j^out 归并为单一宽度 2dN+d+2 的共享网络。

Result: 主定理（Thm 2.1）：对任意 N,L∈ℕ⁺ 与 f∈L^p([0,1]^d)，存在宽度固定为 2dN+d+2 的共享混合激活网络，使每层读出 Φ_ℓ=Σ_{j=0}^ℓ A_j^out∘⨂_{i=-1}^j(ϱ∘A_i) 满足 ‖f−Φ_ℓ‖_{L^p} ≤ (2d+1)ω_{f,p}(N^{−ℓ})（ω 为平移模量）。推论：L^p-Hölder（α 阶）时误差 ≤ (2d+1)λ_p N^{−αℓ}，1-Lipschitz 时 ≤ (2d+1)N^{−ℓ}（Cor 2.2）；为达精度 ε 需深度 m = max{1, ⌈α^{−1}log_N((2d+1)λ_p/ε)⌉}，参数数 O((2dN+d+2)²α^{−1}log_N((2d+1)λ_p/ε))，对 ε^{-1} 仅对数增长、对 d 多项式增长（Cor 2.3）。矩形盒推广（Thm 2.4）：尺度换成 max_i(b_i−a_i)N^{−ℓ}，点态 Hölder 情形额外出现体积因子 |Q|^{1/p}（Cor 2.5）。文中还给出 L^p 模量与经典模量的比较（ω_{f,p}≤ω_{f,q}≤ω_f；p<∞ 时 ω_{f,p}→0 不蕴含连续性，如示性函数例）以及与稠密性论证的一致性核对。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献是把深度解释为多尺度分辨率参数：Φ_ℓ=Φ_{ℓ−1}+Γ_ℓ 的代数嵌套使更早的修正项保留在后续读出中，形成有限时域的层级精化，可按需截断（自适应深度），为 multigrade deep learning（MGDL）提供了构造性的逼近理论基础，并明确了两类激活的分工：ReLU 通道负责几何定位与划分，sine 通道负责胞元数值实现。作者强调本结果纯属逼近论/表达力结论，不涉及优化、泛化、数值稳定性、有界权重或位复杂度；固定宽度的几何速率依赖双 sine 解码器，其参数可能随 N^{dℓ} 极为巨大，未来需研究解码器参数的定量控制与训练算法层面的利用。

Abstract: Depth is widely viewed as a central contributor to the success of deep neural networks, whereas standard neural network approximation theory typically provides guarantees only for the final output and leaves the role of intermediate layers largely unclear. We address this gap by developing a quantitative framework in which depth admits a precise scale-dependent interpretation. Specifically, we design a single shared mixed-activation architecture of fixed width $2dN+d+2$ and any prescribed finite depth such that each intermediate readout $Φ_\ell$ is itself an approximant to the target function $f$. For $f\in L^p([0,1]^d)$ with $p\in [1,\infty)$, the approximation error of $Φ_\ell$ is controlled by $(2d+1)$ times the $L^p$ modulus of continuity at the geometric scale $N^{-\ell}$ for all $\ell$. The estimate reduces to the geometric rate $(2d+1)N^{-\ell}$ if $f$ is $1$-Lipschitz. Our network design is inspired by multigrade deep learning, where depth serves as a progressive refinement mechanism: each new correction targets residual information at a finer scale while the earlier correction terms remain part of the later readouts, yielding a nested architecture that supports adaptive refinement without redesigning the preceding network.

</details>


### [223] [Machine Learning for Two-Stage Graph Sparsification for the Travelling Salesman Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20236)
*Bo-Cheng Lin,Yi Mei,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段图稀疏化方法用于TSP求解器。首先结合α-Nearest和POPMUSIC两种启发式方法最大化召回率，然后训练单一模型降低图密度。实验表明该方法在多种距离类型和空间分布上具有良好泛化性，优于现有神经稀疏化方法，且在大规模问题上优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能TSP求解器（如LKH）在稀疏候选图上搜索，而非全图。现有的α-Nearest和POPMUSIC启发式方法无法同时满足稀疏性和可靠性；现有机器学习方法仅在全图上操作，计算开销大且仅限欧氏距离。因此需要一种既能保证解质量又能降低图密度的稀疏化方法。

Method: 提出两阶段图稀疏化方法：Stage 1取α-Nearest和POPMUSIC的并集以最大化召回率；Stage 2训练单一模型对Stage 1结果进行密度压缩。在四种TSPLIB距离类型、五种空间分布、规模50-500的问题上进行实验评估。

Result: 两阶段方法显著降低了候选图密度，同时保持了高覆盖率；在不同距离类型和空间分布上具有良好的泛化能力；优于仅限欧氏距离的神经稀疏化方法；在较大规模问题上优势更加明显。

Conclusion: 两阶段图稀疏化方法能有效平衡TSP候选图的质量与稀疏性，为大规模TSP问题的高效求解提供了可行方案。

Abstract: High-performance TSP solvers like LKH search within a sparsified candidate graph rather than over all possible edges. Graph sparsification is non-trivial: keep too many edges and the solver wastes time; cut too many and it loses edges that belong to the optimal tour. The two leading heuristic methods, $α$-Nearest and POPMUSIC, produce high-quality candidate graphs, but no single heuristic is both sparse and reliable across all instance sizes and distributions. Machine learning methods can potentially learn better sparsification models. However, existing approaches operate on the complete graph, which is expensive and mostly restricted to Euclidean distances. To address this issue, we propose a two-stage graph sparsification approach: Stage~1 takes the union of $α$-Nearest and POPMUSIC to maximise recall; Stage~2 trains a single model to reduce density. We conducted experiments across four TSPLIB distance types, five spatial distributions, and problem sizes from 50 to 500. The two-stage approach substantially reduces candidate-graph density while retaining high coverage, generalises across distance types and distributions, outperforms recent neural sparsification methods that are restricted to Euclidean distances, and becomes increasingly valuable at larger scales where single-stage heuristics degrade.

</details>


### [224] [uLEAD-TabPFN: Uncertainty-aware Dependency-based Anomaly Detection with TabPFN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20255)
*Sha Lu,Jixue Liu,Stefan Peters,Thuc Duy Le,Craig Xie,Lin Liu,Jiuyong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 uLEAD-TabPFN，一种基于 TabPFN（预训练先验数据拟合网络）的**不确定性感知依赖型表格异常检测框架**。其核心思想是：不再用"离群点距离"而用"条件依赖违背"作为异常证据——把原始特征投影到一个与依赖结构对齐的低维隐空间，用冻结的 TabPFN 以 in-context 方式逐维估计条件均值，再用一个轻量方差网络（MLP）估计条件方差并对偏离做方差归一化，从而在异方差噪声下仍能可靠打分。在 ADBench 的 57 个数据集上与 26 个基线对比：高维场景 ROC-AUC 73.98（排名第 1，较最优基线 +2.84%、较基线均值 +19.56%），中维 ROC-AUC 80.13（第 1）；低维与整体排名略低（ROC-AUC 85.51 第 7 / 80.78 第 3）。消融表明依赖对齐编码器与 TabPFN 条件建模是主要增益来源，不确定性打分主要提升稳健性；相关性/聚类分析显示其与基线方法行为互补（平均 Spearman 相关 0.67 vs 基线间 >0.83）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格异常检测受困于高维、复杂特征依赖与异质噪声。主流方法为基于邻近性（proximity-based）的方法（如 LOF、CBLOF、Isolation Forest、kNN），只能捕捉"孤立点/低密度区"式的异常，在高维下因距离失效而退化，且无法发现那些"单看每个特征都正常、但组合起来违反依赖结构"的隐蔽异常。

依赖型异常检测（DepAD、ADMIRE、DAGnosis、PARs 等）提供了更原理化的替代路径，但存在三点关键局限：(1) 多数方法直接在原始特征空间建模条件依赖，稳健性与计算效率在高维下急剧恶化；(2) 依赖多依赖刚性的、任务专用模型（决策树、因果/逻辑结构），随特征维度与依赖复杂度上升而脆弱，且在训练数据有限时难以拟合；(3) 普遍把"依赖偏离"直接当作异常证据，忽略依赖估计本身的不确定性，导致在异方差/多模态正常数据上打分不可靠、假阳性高。

因此需要一种既能稳健表达复杂条件依赖、可扩展到高维，又能把依赖估计不确定性纳入评分的方法——这正是 PFN（预训练合成表格先验模型）与上下文学习（in-context learning）能够提供的。

Method: **问题设定**：半监督异常检测，训练集仅含正常样本，学习打分函数 s(x)，值越大越异常。

**三大模块**：

1. **代表性上下文集（RCS）构建**：为降低 in-context 推理成本，从正常训练集中采样构造紧凑参考集 C。预算 c=500；若 |X_train| ≤ c 则全用；否则用 K-means（k=100）划分训练集，预算在簇间均匀分配，簇内约一半样本取靠近质心的"中心区域"、一半取远离质心的"分散区域"，以兼顾分布覆盖。该集合构建一次后固定，并用于特征归一化（μ_C、σ_C）。

2. **依赖对齐隐空间建模**：用单层线性编码器 z = W_enc x + b_enc（d→p，p≤d；d≤100 时 p=d，d>100 时 p=100）做降维与表示对齐；用**冻结的 TabPFN** 作为条件预测器，以 RCS 为上下文，对每个隐维度 j 估计条件均值 μ̂_ij = E[Z_j | Z_{-j}=z_{i,-j}, Z_C]（逐维独立进行）。训练损失 = 依赖损失 L_dep（条件残差平方均值）+ Huber 重构正则 L_rec（线性解码器，δ=1，不与编码器权重绑定），L_rl = λ_dep·L_dep + λ_rec·L_rec（λ_dep=1, λ_rec=0.3）。编码器训练 20 epoch、Adam、batch 1024、学习率 5e-4→1e-6 调度。TabPFN 全程冻结，无需数据集特定微调。

3. **不确定性感知打分**：对每个隐维度训练一个轻量方差网络（MLP：64-ReLU-32-ReLU-1，隐层后 dropout 0.1，输出 log σ̂²），输入为其余 p-1 个隐维度，刻画异方差残差不确定性；以高斯负对数似然训练（50 epoch，Adam，lr 1e-3，weight decay 1e-4），编码器与 TabPFN 冻结。推理时异常分数为各维度方差归一化偏离的平均：s(x) = (1/p)Σ_j (z_j − μ̂_j)²/σ̂²。归一化后，低方差区域的小偏离会被判为高负对数似然（异常），高方差区域的大偏离则被容忍（正常），从而降低异方差/多模态正常数据下的假阳性。

**复杂度**：训练对 N、p、|C| 线性；推理对每个样本独立，主要成本随 p 与 |C| 线性增长；上下文固定，推理期内存恒定；无需数据集特定微调与迭代优化。

Result: 在 ADBench 全部 57 个数据集、26 个基线（ADBench 全部 14 个半监督基线 + Livernoche 等 2024 的 10 个 + FoMo-0D 与扩散方法 DDAE）、5 个随机种子下评估：

- **ROC-AUC**：高维（d>100，15 个）73.98，排名第 1，+2.84%B / +19.56%A；中维（20<d≤100，18 个）80.13，第 1，+1.47%B / +15.59%A；低维（d≤20，24 个）85.51，第 7，−5.14%B / +10.49%A；整体 80.78，第 3，−0.87%B / +14.16%A。
- **PR-AUC**：高维 40.24（第 2，−1.26%B / +45.50%A）、中维 57.37（第 3，+20.43%A）、低维 62.67（第 7，−15.82%B）、整体 55.09（第 6，+22.79%A）。
- **显著性**（Wilcoxon 符号秩）：ROC-AUC 全部数据集 17/25 显著胜出、PR-AUC 16/26；高维分组两项均达 20 次显著胜出，低维仅 6/25 与 9/26，说明优势随维度与依赖复杂度上升而放大。
